# Suttantapiṭake Khuddakanikāyassa Apadānaṃ (Dutiyo Bhāgo), Buddhavaṃso, Cariyāpiṭakaṃ

> **Source:** Syāmaraṭṭhassa Tepiṭakaṃ (Siam Rath Edition, Volume 33)
> **Edition:** Royal Thai Edition (King Rama VII)

---


<!-- Siam Rath Page 1 -->
<a id="page-1"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 1]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa    
apadānaṃ    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

Dvācattāḷīsamo bhaddālivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ bhaddālittherāpadānaṃ (411)    
[1] sumedho nāma sambuddho       aggo kāruṇiko muni    
vivekakāmo lokaggo        	himavantaṃ upāgami .

Ajjhogahetvā himavantaṃ [^1-1]    sumedho lokanāyako    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna         	nisīdi purisāsabho [^1-2] .

Samādhiṃ so samāpanno        	sumedho lokanāyako    
sattarattindivaṃ buddho         	nisīdi purisuttamo .

Khārikājaṃ [^1-3] gahetvāna      	vanamajjhogahiṃ ahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Sammajjaniṃ gahetvāna         	sammajjitvāna assamaṃ    
catudaṇḍe ṭhapetvāna         	akāsiṃ maṇḍapaṃ tadā .

Sālapupphaṃ gahetvāna         	maṇḍapaṃ chādayiṃ ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          	avandiṃ lokanāyakaṃ [^1-4] .

#[^1-1] ma. Yu. Himavaṃ .  [^1-2] ma. Yu. Purisuttamo .   [^1-3] ma. Khāribhāraṃ . Ito paraṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 2 -->
<a id="page-2"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 2]*

Yaṃ vadanti sumedhoti          bhūripaññaṃ sumedhasaṃ    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Buddhassa giramaññāya          sabbe devā samāgamama    
asaṃsayaṃ buddhaseṭṭho          dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
devasaṅghe nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me sattāhaṃ maṇḍapaṃ       dhārayi sālachādanaṃ [^2-1]    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi           suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Devabhūto manusso vā        hemavaṇṇo bhavissati    
pahūtabhogo hutvāna          kāmabhogī bhavissati .

Saṭṭhī nāgasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
suvaṇṇakacchā mātaṅgā        hemakappanivāsasā [^2-2] .

Āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi         tomaraṅkusapāṇibhi    
sāyaṃ pāto upaṭṭhānaṃ        āgacchissantimaṃ naraṃ .

Tehi nāgehi parivuto        ramissati ayaṃ naro    
saṭṭhī assasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā .

Ājānīyā ca [^2-3] jātiyā     sindhavā sīghabāhanā    
āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi         indiyācāpadhāribhi .

Parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
saṭṭhī rathasahassāni           sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā .

#[^2-1] ma. Yu. Sālachāditaṃ .    [^2-2] ma. Hemakappanavāsasā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 3 -->
<a id="page-3"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 3]*

Dīpā athopi veyyagghā       sannaddhā ussitaddhajā    
āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi         cāpahatthehi cammibhi [^3-1] .

Parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
saṭṭhī gāmasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso .

Pahūtadhanadhaññāni             susamiddhāni sabbaso    
sadā pātubhavissanti          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Hatthī assā rathā pattī       senā ca caturaṅginī    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca        cakkavatti bhavissati .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuṃ ca          devarajjaṃ karissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ [^3-2] .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
etthantaraṃ upādāya         gavesiṃ amataṃpadaṃ .

Lābhā mayhaṃ suladdhaṃ me       yamahaṃ ñāmi [^3-3] sāsanaṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^3-1] ma. Yu. Vammibhi . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^3-2] ma. Yu. Asaṅkhiyaṃ . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 4 -->
<a id="page-4"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 4]*

Namo te purisājañña         namo te purisuttama    
ñāṇaṃ paṭikittetvāna [^4-1]      pattomhi amataṃpadaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbattha sukhito homi         phalamme ñāṇakittane .

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ           	carimo vattatī bhavo    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhaddāli thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bhaddālittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ekachattiyattherāpadānaṃ (412)    
[2] candabhāgānadītīre           assamo sukato mama    
susuddhapuḷinākiṇṇo           paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Uttānakūlā nadikā          supatitthā manoramā    
macchakacchapasampannā          sasasumāranisevitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 5 -->
<a id="page-5"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 5]*

Acchā mayuradīpī ca           karavikā ca sālikā    
kujjanti sabbadā ete       sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Kokilā mañjubhāṇī ca         haṃsā ca madhurassarā    
abhikujjanti te tattha         sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Sīhā byagghā varāhā ca      bakā kokataracchayo [^5-1]    
giriduggamhi [^5-2] nādenti      sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Eṇimigā ca sarabhā          bheraṇḍā sūkarā bahū    
giriduggamhi nādenti         sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Uddālakā ca campakā        pāṭalī sinduvārikā    
adhimuttā [^5-3] asokā ca      sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Aṅkolā yūthikā ceva        sattapaṇṇā [^5-4] bimbijālikā    
kaṇikā kaṇikārā ca          pupphanti mama assame [^5-5] .

Nāgā sālā ca salaḷā       puṇḍarīkettha pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā      sobhanti mama assame .

Asaṭṭhā [^5-6] asanā cettha     mahānāmā ca pupphitā    
sālā ca kaṅgupupphā ca       sobhanti mama assame .

Ambā jambū ca tilakā        nipā [^5-7] ca sālakalyāṇī    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā      sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Asokā ca kapiṭṭhā ca        bhaginimālā [^5-8] ca pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā      sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

#[^5-1] ma. Acchakokataracchakā .  [^5-2] yu. Giriduggaṃpi .  [^5-3] ma. Yu. Atimuttā . Ito paraṃ    
#īdisameva .  [^5-4] ma. Yu. Sattasī .  [^5-5] ma. Sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .  [^5-6] ma. Yu. Ajjunā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 6 -->
<a id="page-6"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 6]*

Kadambā kadalī ceva          		isimuggā ca ropitā    
dhuvaṃ phalāni dhārenti         		sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Harītakā āmalakā           		ambā jambū vibhedakā [^6-1]    
kolā bhallātakā bellā [^6-2]  phalino mama assame .

Avidūre pokkharaṇī           		supatitthā manoramā    
mandālakehi sañchannā        padumuppalakehi ca .

Gabbhaṃ gaṇhanti padumā         	aññe pupphanti kesari    
opattakaṇṇikā ceva         	pupphanti mama assame .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā       	valajā [^6-3] muñjarohitā    
acchodakamhi  vicaraṃ          	sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Nayitā ambagandhi ca          	anukule ca ketakā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā      sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Madhubhiṃsehi savanti [^6-4]         	khīrasappimuḷālibhi    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā      sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Puḷinā sobhanā tattha         ākiṇṇā jalasevitā    
opupphā [^6-5] pupphitā setā   sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Jaṭābhārabharitā ca           ajinuttaravāsino    
vākacīradharā sabbe          sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Yugamattamapekkhantā          nipakā santavuttino    
kāmagedhe [^6-6] anapekkhā     vasanti mama assame .

#[^6-1] ma. Yu. Vibhītakā . Sabbattha īdisameva .  [^6-2] ma. Billā . Sabbattha īdisameva .

#[^6-3] ma. Balajā . Yu jalajā . Sabbattha īdisameva . [^6-4] ma. Yu. Madhubhisamhā savati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 7 -->
<a id="page-7"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 7]*

Parūḷhakacchanakhalomā          paṅkadantā rajassirā    
rajojalladharā sabbe         vasanti mama assame .

Abhiññāpāramippattā         antalikkhe carāva te    
uggacchantā nabhaṃ ete       sobhayanti mamassamaṃ .

Tehi sissehi parivuto        vasāmi pavane [^7-1] tadā    
rattindivaṃ na jānāmi         jhānaratisamappito [^7-2] .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          atthadassī mahāmuni    
tamandhakāraṃ nāsento        uppajji lokanāyako .

Atha aññataro sisso         āgañchi mama santike    
mante ajjhetukāmo so      chaḷaṅgaṃ nāma lakkhaṇaṃ .

Buddho loke samuppanno      atthadassī mahāmuni    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento         desesi amataṃpadaṃ .

Tuṭṭhahaṭṭho pamudito          dhammantaragaṇāsayo [^7-3]    
assamā abhinikkhamma          idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Buddho loke samuppanno      dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
etha sabbe gamissāma        sammāsambuddhasantike .

Ovādapaṭikārā te         saddhamme pāramiṃ gatā    
sādhūti sampaṭicchiṃsu           uttamatthaṃ gavesakā .

Jaṭābhārabharitā te          ajinuttaravāsino    
uttamatthaṃ gavesantā         nikkhamiṃsu vanā tadā .

#[^7-1] ma. Vipine . Sabbattha īdisameva .  [^7-2] ma. Yu. Sadā jhānasamappito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 8 -->
<a id="page-8"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 8]*

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          atthadassī mahāyaso    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento         desesi amataṃpadaṃ .

Setacchattaṃ gahetvāna        buddhaseṭṭhassa dhārayiṃ    
ekāhaṃ dhārayitvāna         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ avandihaṃ .

Atthadassī tu bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me chattamadhāresi        pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi           suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Imassa jāyamānassa          devatte atha mānuse    
dhārissanti sadā chattaṃ        chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasattatikappāni           devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca        cakkavatti bhavissati .

Sattasattatikkhattuñca          devarajjaṃ karissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
aṭṭhārase kappasate         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
tamandhakāraṃ nāsento        upajjissati cakkhumā .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya         nibbāyissatināsavo .

Yato ahaṃ kammamakaṃ [^8-1]        chattaṃ buddhassa dhārayiṃ    
etthantare na jānāmi       setacchattaṃ adhāritaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 9 -->
<a id="page-9"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 9]*

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ           carimo vattate bhavo    
chattadhāraṇamajjāsi           matthake [^9-1] niccakālikaṃ .

Aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ         atthadassissa tādino    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ [^9-2] vata me āsi     mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekachattiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekachattiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ tiṇasūlakachādaniyattherāpadānaṃ (413)    
[3] jātijarañca maraṇaṃ            paccavekkhiṃ ahaṃ tadā    
ekako abhinikkhamma          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Caramāno anupubbena         gaṅgātīraṃ upāgamiṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ paṭhaviṃ           gaṅgātīre sapuṇṇakaṃ [^9-3] .

Assamaṃ tattha māpetvā       vasāmi mama assame [^9-4]    
sukkato caṅkamo mayhaṃ        nānādijagaṇāyuto .

#[^9-1] ma. Yu. Chattadhāraṇamajjāpi vattate . [^9-2] yu. Sāgataṃ . Sabbattha īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 10 -->
<a id="page-10"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 10]*

Mamaṃ upenti ca sattā [^10-1]     	kujjanti ca manoharaṃ    
rammamāno saha tehi         	vasāmi assame ahaṃ .

Mama assamasāmantā          	migarājā catukkamo    
āsayā abhinikkhamma          	gajji so asanī [^10-2] viya .

Nadite migarāje ca          		hāso me upapajjatha    
migarājaṃ gavesanto          	addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Disvānāhaṃ devadevaṃ         	tissaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena       	pūjayiṃ nāgakesaraṃ .

Uggacchantaṃva suriyaṃ           	sālarājaṃva pupphitaṃ    
osadhīva virocantaṃ           		santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Tava ñāṇena sabbaññu         jotesi maṃ sadevakaṃ    
tuvaṃ [3] ārādhayitvāna     	jātiyā parimuccare .

Adassanena sabbaññū          buddhānaṃ sabbadassinaṃ    
patantivīcinirayaṃ              		rāgadosehi otthaṭā [^10-4] .

Tava dassanamāgamma           	sabbaññulokanāyakaṃ    
pamuccanti bhavā sabbe        	phusanti amataṃpadaṃ .

Yadā buddhā cakkhumanto       	uppajjanti pabhaṅkarā    
kilese jhāpayitvāna         	ālokaṃ dassayanti te .

Kittayitvāna sambuddhaṃ         	tissaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena       tiṇasūlaṃ apūjayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 11 -->
<a id="page-11"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 11]*

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          tisso lokagganāyako    
sakāsane nisīditvā          imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo maṃ pupphehi chādesi       pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi           suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Pañcavīsatikkhattuñca [^11-1]        devarajjaṃ karissati    
pañcasattatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
tassa [^11-2] kammassa nissando    pupphānaṃ pūjanāya so [^11-3] .

Sāyaṃ [^11-4] pāto ca yaṃ poso   pupphehi [^11-5] maṃ achādayi    
puññakammena saṃyutto [^11-6]      purato pātubhavissati .

Yaṃ yaṃ icchati kāmehi         taṃ taṃ pātubhavissati    
saṅkappaṃ paripūretvā         nibbāyissatināsavo .

Kilese jhāpayitvāna         sampajāno paṭissato    
ekāsane nisīditvā         arahattaṃ apāpuṇi [^11-7] .

Caṅkamanto nipajjanto        nisinno athavā ṭhito    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         viharāmi  ahaṃ tadā .

Cīvare piṇḍapāte ca         paccaye sayanāsane    
tattha me ūnatā natthi        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

So dāni patto amataṃ        santaṃ padamanuttaraṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^11-1] ma. Yu. Pañcavīsatikkhattuṃ so .   [^11-2] ma. Tassa kammanissandena . [^11-3] ma. ... Ca .

#[^11-4] ma. Yu. Sīsaṃ nhāto ca yaṃ poso .  [^11-5] ma. Pupphamākaṅkhate yadi . Yu. ... Cayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 12 -->
<a id="page-12"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 12]*

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasūlakachādaniyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇasūlakachādaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ madhumaṃsadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (414)    
[4] nagare bandhumatiyā           sūkariko ahosahaṃ    
ukkoṭakaṃ randhayitvā         madhumaṃsamhi [^12-1] okiriṃ .

Sannipātaṃ ahaṃ gantvā        ekapattaṃ ahaṃ [^12-2] gahiṃ    
pūrayitvāna taṃ pattaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ .

Yo [^12-3] vuḍḍhatherataro bhikkhu    niyyādemi ahaṃ tadā    
iminā pattapūrena           labhissaṃ [^12-4] vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Duve sampattiyo bhutvā       sukkamūlena codito    
pacchime vattamānamhi         kilese jhāpayissati .

#[^12-1] yu. Madhusappimhi ākiriṃ . [^12-2] ma. Yu. Gahe sahaṃ . [^12-3] ma. Yu. Yottha -- niyyādesi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 13 -->
<a id="page-13"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 13]*

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchihaṃ    
tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca       labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā        pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ    
annapānābhivasso me        ativassati tāvade .

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ           carimo vattate bhavo    
idhāpi annapānaṃ me         vassate sabbakālikaṃ .

Teneva madhudānena [^13-1]       sandhāvitvā bhave ahaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santikeya    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā madhumaṃsadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Madhumaṃsadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 14 -->
<a id="page-14"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 14]*

pañcamaṃ nāgapallavakattherāpadānaṃ (415)    
[5] nagare bandhumatiyā           rājuyyāne vasāmahaṃ    
mama assamasāmantā          nisīdi lokanāyako .

Nāgapallavamādāya           buddhassa abhiropayiṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          sugataṃ abhivādayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ pallavamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgapallavako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Nāgapallavakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ekadīpiyattherāpadānaṃ (416)    
[6] parinibbutamhi sugate          siddhatthe lokanāyake    
sadevamānusā sabbe         pūjenti dipaduttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 15 -->
<a id="page-15"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 15]*

Āropite ca citake         siddhatthe lokanāyake    
yathāsakena thāmena          citaṃ pūjenti satthuno .

Avidūre citakassa            dīpaṃ ujjālayiṃ ahaṃ    
yāva udeti suriyo          dīpaṃ me tāva pajjali .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        ekadīpanti ñāyati    
dīpasatasahassāni             byamhe pajjalare mama .

Udayantova suriyo           deho me jalate [^15-1] sadā    
sappabhāhi sarīrassa           āloko hoti me sadā .

Tirokuḍḍaṃ tiroselaṃ          samatiggayha pabbataṃ    
samantā yojanasataṃ           passāmi cakkhunā ahaṃ .

Sattasattatikkhattuṃ ca          devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
ekatiṃsatikkhattuṃ ca           devarajjaṃ akārayiṃ .

Aṭṭhavīsatikkhattuṃ ca           cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna         nibbatti mātukucchiyaṃ    
mātukucchigatassāpi           akkhi me na [^15-2] vinassati .

Jātiyā catuvassohaṃ          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 16 -->
<a id="page-16"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 16]*

dibbacakkhuṃ visodhesiṃ          bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbe kilesā sañchinnā      ekadīpassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tirokuḍḍaṃ tiroselaṃ          pabbatañcāpi kevalaṃ    
samatikkamma passāmi          ekadīpassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Visamā me samā honti       andhakāro na vijjati    
nāhaṃ passāmi timiraṃ          ekadīpassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dīpamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ekadīpassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekadīpiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekadīpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ ucchaṅgapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (417)    
[7] nagare bandhumatiyā           ahosiṃ māliko tadā    
ucchaṅgaṃ pūrayitvāna          agamaṃ antarāpaṇaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 17 -->
<a id="page-17"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 17]*

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
mahatā ānubhāvena          niyyāti lokanāyako .

Disvāna lokapajjotaṃ         vipassiṃ lokatāraṇaṃ    
pupphaṃ paggayha ucchaṅgā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ [^17-1] apūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ucchaṅgapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ucchaṅgapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ yāgudāyakattherāpadānaṃ (418)    
[8] atithiṃ me gahetvāna         āgañchiṃ gāmakaṃ tadā    
sampuṇṇanadikaṃ disvā          saṅghārāmaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Āraññakā dhutadharā [^17-2]       jhāyino lūkhacīvarā    
vivekābhiratā dhīrā          saṅghārāme vasanti te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 18 -->
<a id="page-18"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 18]*

Gati tesaṃ upacchinnā         suvimuttāna tādinaṃ    
piṇḍāya te na gacchanti       oruddhanadikāyatiṃ [^18-1] .

Pasannacitto sumano          vedajāto katañjalī    
taṇḍulaṃ [^18-2] paggahetvāna      yāgudānaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Pacanaṃ [^18-3] yāguṃ datvāna       pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
sakakammābhiraddhohaṃ           tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Maṇimayañca me byamhaṃ         nibbattaṃ [^18-4] tidase gaṇe    
nārīgaṇehi saṃhito [^18-5]        modāmi byamhamuttame .

Tettiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
tiṃsakkhattuṃ cakkavatti          mahārajjamakārayiṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
devaloke manusse vā       anubhotvā yasaṃ [^18-6] ahaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
saha oropite kese        sabbasampattivijjahaṃ .

Khayato vayato cāpi          sammasanto kalevaraṃ    
pure sikkhāpadānena [^18-7]      arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Sudinnaṃ me dānavaraṃ          pāṇijaṃ [^18-8] suppayojitaṃ    
teneva yāgudānena         pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Sokaṃ pariddavaṃ byādhiṃ         darathaṃ cittatāpanaṃ    
nābhijānāmi uppannaṃ         yāgudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^18-1] ma. Oruddhanadikāya hi . Yu. Oruddhanadikā yadi . [^18-2] ma. Yu. Me gahetvāna .

#[^18-3] ma. Yu. Pañcannaṃ . [^18-4] ma. Nibbatti .  [^18-5] ma. Yu. Sahito . [^18-6] ma. Sayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 19 -->
<a id="page-19"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 19]*

Yāguṃ saṅghassa datvāna        puññakkhette anuttare    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi         aho yāgusuyiṭṭhatā .

Abyādhitā rūpavatā          khippaṃ dhammaṃ [^19-1] nibujjhitā    
lābhitā annapānassa         āyu pañcamakaṃ mama .

Yo koci vedaṃ janayaṃ         saṅghe yāguṃ dadeyya so    
imāni pañca ṭhānāni         paṭiggaṇheyya paṇḍito .

Karaṇīyaṃ kataṃ sabbaṃ            bhavā ugghāṭitā mayā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā         natthi dāni punabbhavo .

So ahaṃ vicarissāmi          gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ    
namassamāno sambuddhaṃ         dhammassa ca sudhammataṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          yāgudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yāgudāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Yāgudāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 20 -->
<a id="page-20"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 20]*

Navamaṃ patthodanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (419)    
[9] vanacārī pure āsiṃ          satataṃ vanakammiko    
patthodanaṃ gahetvāna         kammantaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
vanā piṇḍāya nikkhantaṃ        disvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Parakammāyane yutto         puññañca me na vijjati    
ayaṃ patthodano atthi         bhojayissāmimaṃ muniṃ .

Patthodanaṃ gahetvāna         sayambhussa adāsahaṃ    
mama nijjhāyamānassa          paribhuñji mahāmuni .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Dvattiṃsakkhattuṃ [^20-1] devindo    devarajjamakārayiṃ    
tettiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati [^20-2] .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sukhito yasavā homi          patthodanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaranto          labhāmi amitaṃ dhanaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       patthodanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Nadīsotapaṭibhāgā            bhogā nibbattare mama    
parimetuṃ na sakkomi          patthodanassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 21 -->
<a id="page-21"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 21]*

Imaṃ khāda imaṃ bhuñja          imamhi sayane saya    
tenāhaṃ sukhito homi         patthodanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute [^21-1] ito kappe    yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          patthodanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhimeD    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā patthodanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Patthodanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ mañcadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (420)    
[10] parinibbute kāruṇike         siddhatthe lokanāyake    
vitthārike pāvacane         devamanussasakkate .

Caṇḍālo āsahaṃ tattha        āsandipīṭhakārako    
tena kammena jīvāmi         tena posemi dārake .

Āsandiṃ sukataṃ katvā         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
sayameva upagantvā          bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 22 -->
<a id="page-22"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 22]*

tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Devalokaṃ gato santo        modāmi tidase gaṇe    
sayanāni mahagghāni           nibbattanti yathicchakaṃ .

Paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
asītikkhattuṃ rājā ca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sukhito yasavā homi          mañcadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna         emi ce mānusaṃ bhavaṃ    
mahārahā susayanā           sayameva bhavanti me .

Ayaṃ pacchimako mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
ajjāpi sayanakāle          sayanaṃ upatiṭṭhahi .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dānaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          mañcadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 23 -->
<a id="page-23"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 23]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mañcadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mañcadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
bhaddālī ekachatto ca        tiṇasūlo ca maṃsado    
nāgapallavako dīpaṃ           ucchaṅgi yāgudāyako .

Patthodani mañcadado          gāthāyo gaṇitā visā    
dve satāni ca gāthānaṃ       gāthā cekā taduttari .

Bhaddālivaggo dvācattāḷīsamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 24 -->
<a id="page-24"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 24]*

Tecattāḷīso sakiṃsammajjakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sakiṃsammajjakattherāpadānaṃ (421)    
[11] vipassino bhagavato           pāṭaliṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
disvāva taṃ pādapaggaṃ         tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Sammajjaniṃ gahetvāna         bodhiṃ sammajji tāvade    
sammajjitvāna taṃ bodhiṃ        avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ .

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
namassamāno taṃ bodhiṃ         gañchiṃ paṭikuṭiṃ ahaṃ .

Cārimaggena [^24-1] gacchāmi      saranto bodhimuttamaṃ    
ajagaro maṃ pīḷeti           ghorarūpo mahabbalo .

Āsanne me kataṃ kammaṃ       phalena tosayī mamaṃ    
kalevaraṃ me gilati           devaloke ramāmahaṃ .

Anāvilaṃ mama cittaṃ           visuddhaṃ paṇḍaraṃ sadā    
sokasallaṃ na jānāmi         cittasantāpanaṃ mama .

Kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍo kilāso ca       apamāro vitacchikā    
daddu kaṇḍu ca me natthi       phalaṃ sammajjanāyidaṃ .

Soko ca pariḷāho ca        hadaye me na vijjati    
abhantaṃ [^24-2] ujukaṃ cittaṃ        phalaṃ sammajjanāyidaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 25 -->
<a id="page-25"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 25]*

Samādhiṃ [^25-1] puna pajjāmi       visuddhaṃ [^25-2] hoti mānasaṃ    
yaṃ yaṃ samādhiṃ icchāmi         so so sampajjate mama .

Rajanīye na rajjāmi          atho dosaniyesu ca    
mohanīye na muyhāmi         phalaṃ sammajjanāyidaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phalaṃ sammajjanāyidaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakiṃsammajjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sakiṃsammajjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ekadussadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (422)    
[12] nagare haṃsavatiyā            ahosiṃ tiṇahārako    
tiṇahārena jīvāmi           tena posemi dārake .

Padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
tamandhakāraṃ nāsetvā        uppajji lokanāyako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 26 -->
<a id="page-26"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 26]*

Sake ghare nisīditvā         evaṃ cintesi tāvade    
buddho loke samuppanno      deyyadhammo na vijjati .

Idaṃ me sāṭakaṃ ekaṃ         natthi me koci dāyako    
dukkho nirayasamphasso         ropayissāmi dakkhiṇaṃ .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna         sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
ekaṃ dussaṃ gahetvāna        buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekaṃ dussaṃ daditvāna         ukkuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayiṃ    
yadi buddho tuvaṃ vīra          tārehi maṃ mahāmuni .

Padumuttaro lokavidū          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamaṃ dānaṃ pakittento        akā me anumodanaṃ .

Iminā ekadussena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
kappasatasahassāni            vinipātaṃ na gacchati .

Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjaṃ karissati    
tettiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
devaloke manusase vā       saṃsaranto tuvaṃ bhave .

Rūpavā guṇasampanno          anuvattantadehavā    
akkhobhaṃ amitaṃ dussaṃ          labhissati yathicchakaṃ .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        jalajuttamanāmako [^26-1]    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro          haṃsarājāva ambare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 27 -->
<a id="page-27"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 27]*

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       ekadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paduddhāre paduddhāre        dussaṃ nibbattate mamaM    
heṭṭhā dussamhi tiṭṭhāhi      uparicchadanaṃ mama .

Cakkavāḷaṃ upādāya          sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ    
icchamāno ahaṃ ajja         dussenacchādayeyya taṃ .

Teneva ekadussena         saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇo hutvāna         saṃsarāmi bhavābhave .

Vipākaṃ ekadussassa          ajjhagaṃ [^27-1] tattha bhikkhutaṃ    
ayaṃpi [^27-2] pacchimā jāti       vipaccati idhāpi me .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ekadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekadussadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekadussadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 28 -->
<a id="page-28"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 28]*

Tatiyaṃ ekāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (423)    
[13] himavantassa avidūre          kasiko [^28-1] nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ         paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Nārado nāma nāmena        kassapo iti maṃ vidū    
suddhimaggaṃ gavesanto         vasāmi kasike tadā .

Padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
vivekakāmo sambuddho        āgañchi anilañjasā .

Vanagge gacchamānassa         disvā raṃsiṃ mahesino    
kaṭṭhamañcaṃ paññāpetvā       ajinañca apatthariṃ .

Āsanaṃ paññāpetvāna        sīse katvāna añjaliṃ    
somanassaṃ pavedetvā        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Sallakatto tuvaṃ dhīra          āturānaṃ tikicchako    
mama rāgaparetassa [^28-2]        tikicchaṃ dehi nāyaka .

Puññatthikā [^28-3] ye passanti    buddhaseṭṭhaṃ tuvaṃ muni    
dhuvatthasiddhiṃ papponti         etesaṃ ajaro bhave .

Na me deyyaṃ [^28-4] tava atthi    pavattaphalabhojahaṃ    
idaṃ me āsanaṃ atthi         nisīda kaṭṭhamañcake .

Nisīdi tattha bhagavā           achambhitova kesarī    
muhuttaṃ vītināmetvā         idaṃ vacanamabravi .

#[^28-1] ma. Gosito . Yu. Kosiko . [^28-2] ma. Yu. Rogaparetassa . [^28-3] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 29 -->
<a id="page-29"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 29]*

Vissaṭṭho hohi mā bhāyi      laddho jotiraso tayā    
yaṃ tuyhaṃ patthitaṃ sabbaṃ         paripūressatāsanaṃ [^29-1] .

Na [^29-2] thokaṃ sukataṃ puññaṃ       puññakkhette anuttare    
sakkā uddharituṃ attā        yassa cittaṃ sunīhitaṃ [^29-3] .

Iminā āsanadānena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
kappasatasahassāni            vinipātaṃ na gacchati .

Paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati    
asītikkhattuṃ rājā ca         cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sabbattha sukhito hutvā        saṃsāre saṃsarissati .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        jalajuttamanāmako    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro          haṃsarājāva ambare .Ha    
hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ          sarathaṃ sandamānikaṃ    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          ekāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kānanaṃ pavisitvāna           yadā icchāmi āsanaṃ    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          pallaṅko upatiṭṭhati .

Vārimajjhagato santo         yadā icchāmi āsanaṃ    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          pallaṅko upatiṭṭhati .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
pallaṅkasatasahassāni          parivārenti maṃ sadā .

#[^29-1] ma. Yu. Paripūrissatināgate . [^29-2] ma. Na moghaṃ taṃ kataṃ tuyhaṃ . Yu. Na thokaṃ taṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 30 -->
<a id="page-30"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 30]*

Duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse    
duve kule ca jāyāmi        khattiye cāpi brāhmaṇe .

Ekāsanaṃ daditvāna          puññakkhette anuttare    
dhammapallaṅkamaññāya [^30-1]       viharāmi anāsavo .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ekāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ sattakadambapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (424)    
[14] himavantassa avidūre          kadambo [^30-2] nāma pabbato    
tasmiṃ pabbatapassamhi [^30-3]       satta buddhā vasiṃsu te .

Kadambapupphaṃ [^30-4] disvāna       paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
satta mālāni gahetvā       puṇṇacittena [^30-5] okiriṃ .

#[^30-1] ma. Yu. ...Mādāya . [^30-2] ma. Kukkuṭo ... . [^30-3] ma. ...Pādamhi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 31 -->
<a id="page-31"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 31]*

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattakadambapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sattakadambapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ koraṇḍapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (425)    
[15] vanakammiko pure āsiṃ        pitupetāmahenahaṃ [^31-1]    
pasumārena [^31-2] jīvāmi        kusalaṃ me na vijjati .

Mama āsayasāmantā          tisso lokagganāyako    
padāni tīṇi dassesi          anukampāya cakkhumā .

Akkante ca pade disvā      tissanāmassa satthuno    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena       pade cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 32 -->
<a id="page-32"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 32]*

Koraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ    
sakoṭikaṃ [^32-1] gahetvāna       padaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
koraṇḍavaṇṇakoyeva          sappabhāso [^32-2] bhavāmahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchakatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā koraṇḍapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Koraṇḍapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ghaṭamaṇḍadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (426)    
[16] sucintitaṃ bhagavantaṃ            lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāraññaṃ           vātābādhena pīḷitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 33 -->
<a id="page-33"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 33]*

Disvā cittaṃ pasādetvāna     ghaṭamaṇḍaṃ upānayiṃ    
katattā apacitattā [^33-1] ca     gaṅgā bhāgīrasī [^33-2] ayaṃ .

Mahāsamuddā cattāro        ghaṭaṃ sampajjare mama    
ayañca paṭhavī ghorā          appamāṇā asaṅkhayā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          bhavate madhusakkharā    
catuddisā [^33-3] ime rukkhā     pādapā dharaṇīruhā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          kapparukkhā bhavanti te    
paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Ekapaññāsakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ghaṭamaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghaṭamaṇḍadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ghaṭamaṇḍadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 34 -->
<a id="page-34"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 34]*

Sattamaṃ ekadhammasavaniyattherāpadānaṃ (427)    
[17] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento         santāresi bahuṃ janaṃ .

Ahaṃ tena samayena           jaṭilo uggatāpano    
dhunanto vākacīrāni          gacchāmi ambare tadā .

Buddhaseṭṭhassa upari          gantuṃ na visahāmahaṃ    
pakkhīva selamāsajja          gamanaṃ na labhāmahaṃ .

Udake dhopayitvāna [^34-1]       evaṃ gacchāmi ambare    
na me idaṃ bhūtapubbaṃ          iriyāpathavikopanaṃ .

Handa me taṃ gavesissaṃ        appevatthaṃ labheyyahaṃ    
orohanto antalikkhā       saddamassosi satthuno .

Sarena rajanīyena            savanīyena vattunā [^34-2]    
aniccataṃ kathentassa          taññeva uggahiṃ tadā .

Aniccasaññamuggayha           agamāsiṃ mamassamaṃ    
yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna          tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Carime vattamānamhi          saddhammassavanaṃ sariṃ    
tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca .

Jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ    
tiṃsakappasahassāni            devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 35 -->
<a id="page-35"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 35]*

Ekapaññāsakkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
ekatiṃsatikkhattuñca [^35-1]        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhomi sakaṃ puññaṃ           sukhitohaṃ bhavābhave .

Anussarāmi taṃ saññaṃ          saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
na kenacihaṃ [^35-2] vijjhāmi       nibbānaṃ accutaṃ padaṃ .

Pitu gehe nisīditvā         samaṇo bhāvitindriyo    
kathaṃ so paridīpento         aniccaṃ [^35-3] tatthudāhari .

Aniccā vata saṅkhārā        uppādavayadhammino    
uppajjitvā nirujjhanti        tesaṃ vūpasamo sukho .

Saha gāthaṃ suṇitvāna          sabbasaññaṃ [^35-4] anussariṃ    
ekāsane nisīditvā         arahattamapāpuṇiṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho          dhammassavanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dhammaṃ asuṇiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          dhammassavanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^35-1] ma. Yu. Ekavīsatikkhattuñca . [^35-2] ma. Yu. Na koṭiṃ paṭivijjhāmi [^35-3] ma. Anicacatamudāhari .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 36 -->
<a id="page-36"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 36]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Atthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekadhammasavaniyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekadhammasavaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sucintitattherāpadānaṃ (428)    
[18] nagare haṃsavatiyā            ahosiṃ kasako tadā    
kasikammena jīvāmi           tena posemi dārake .

Susampannaṃ tadā khettaṃ        dhaññaṃ me phalinaṃ ahu    
pākakāleva sampatte        evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Na [^36-1] mecchannaṃ na paṭirūpaṃ     jānantassa guṇāguṇaṃ    
yohaṃ saṅghe adatvāna        aggaṃ bhuñjeyyamattanā [^36-2] .

Sambuddho asamo loke       dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
tato ca bhāvito saṅgho       puññakkhettaṃ [^36-3] anuttaraṃ .

Tattha dassāmihaṃ dānaṃ         navasassaṃ pure pure    
evāhaṃ  cintayitvāna        haṭṭho pītikamānaso [^36-4] .

Khettato dhaññamāharitvā      sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ    
upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Vanditvā satthuno pāde      idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
navasassañca sampannaṃ          idha hosi ca tvaṃ [^36-5] mune .

#[^36-1] ma. Yu. Nacchannaṃ .  [^36-2] ma. Bhuñjeyya ce tadā . Yu. Bhuñjeyya maṃ tadā .

#[^36-3] ma. Yu. Puññakkhetato anuttaro . [^36-4] ma. Yu. Piṇitamānaso . [^36-5] ma. Āyāgosi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 37 -->
<a id="page-37"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 37]*

Anukampaṃ upādāya           adhivāsehi cakkhuma    
padumuttaro lokavidū          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          idaṃ vacanamabravi    
cattāro ca paṭipannā        cattāro ca phale ṭhitā .

Esa saṅgho ujubhūto         paññāsīlasamāhito    
yajantānaṃ manussānaṃ          puññapekkhāna pāṇinaṃ .

Karotaṃ upacikaṃ [^37-1] puññaṃ       saṅghe dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ    
tasmiṃ saṅghe ca dātabbaṃ       tava sassaṃ tathetaraṃ .

Saṅghato uddisitvāna         bhikkhū netvā sakaṃ gharaṃ    
paṭiyattaṃ ghare santaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghassa dehi tvaṃ .

Saṅghato uddisitvāna         bhikkhū netvānahaṃ gharaṃ    
yaṃ ghare paṭiyattaṃ me         bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ    
tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        sovaṇṇaṃ supabhassaraṃ    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbedhaṃ           tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Ekūnavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Ākiṇṇaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ          nārīgaṇasamākulaṃ    
tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca       vasāmi tidase ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 38 -->
<a id="page-38"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 38]*

Satānaṃ tiṃsakkhattuṃ [^38-1] ca       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaranto          labhāmi amitaṃ dhanaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ          sivikaṃ sandamānikaṃ    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Navavatthaṃ navaphalaṃ             navaggarasabhojanaṃ    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Koseyyaṃ kambaliyāni         khomakappāsikāni ca    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dāsīgaṇaṃ dāsagaṇaṃ            nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Na maṃ sītaṃ va uṇhaṃ vā        pariḷāho na vijjati    
atho cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ          hadaye me na vijjati .

Imaṃ khāda imaṃ bhuñja          imamhi sayane saya    
labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ayaṃ pacchimako dāni          carimo vattate bhavo    
ajjāpi deyyadhammo [^38-2] me   mamaṃ [^38-3] toseti sabbadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 39 -->
<a id="page-39"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 39]*

Navasassaṃ daditvāna           saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Vaṇṇavā yasavā homi         mahābhogo anītiko    
mahābhakkho [^39-1] sadā homi     abhejjapariso sadā .

Sabbe maṃ apacāyanti         yekeci paṭhavissitā    
deyyadhammo ca yokoci       pure pure labhāmihaṃ .

Bhikkhusaṅghassa vā majjhe       buddhaseṭṭhassa sammukhā    
sabbepi samatikkamma          denti mameva dāyakā .

Paṭhamaṃ navasassañhi            datvā saṅghe gaṇuttame    
imānisaṃse anubhomi          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sucintito thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sucintitattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 40 -->
<a id="page-40"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 40]*

Navamaṃ soṇṇakiṃkaṇiyattherāpadānaṃ [^40-1] (429)    
[19] saddhāya abhinikkhamma          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
vākacīradharo āsiṃ           tapokammamapassito .

Atthadassī tu bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
uppajji tamhi samaye         tārayanto mahājanaṃ .

Balañca vata me khīṇaṃ          byādhinā paramenahaṃ [^40-2]    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         puḷine thūpamuttamaṃ .

Karitvā haṭṭhacittohaṃ         sahasena [^40-3] samokiriṃ    
soṇṇakiṃkaṇipupphāni           udaggamanaso ahaṃ .

Sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ         thūpaṃ paricariṃ ahaṃ    
tena cetopasādena         atthadassissa tādino .

Devalokaṃ gato santo        labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇo tatthāsi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Asītikoṭiyo mayhaṃ           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sadā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhanti         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni          bheriyo paṇavāpica    
saṅkhā ca deṇḍimā tattha      vaggū vajjanti dundubhi .

Cullāsītisahassāni           hatthiyo samalaṅkatā    
tidhappabhinnā mātaṅgā        kuñjarā saṭṭhihāyanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 41 -->
<a id="page-41"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 41]*

Hemajālābhisañchannā         upaṭṭhānaṃ karonti me    
balakāye bhave [^41-1] ceva      ūnatā me na vijjati .

Soṇṇakiṃkaṇipupphānaṃ           vipākaṃ anubhomihaṃ    
aṭṭhapaññāsakkhattuṃ ca         devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Ekasattatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ ekasataṃ        mahiyā kārayiṃ ahaṃ .

So dāni patto amataṃ        gambhīraṃ [^41-2] duddasaṃ padaṃ    
saṃyojanā parikkhīṇā          natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā soṇṇakiṃkaṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Soṇṇakiṃkaṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 42 -->
<a id="page-42"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 42]*

Dasamaṃ sovaṇṇakontarikattherāpadānaṃ [^42-1] (430)    
[20] manobhāvaniyaṃ buddhaṃ           attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ    
iriyamānaṃ brahmapathe         cittavūpasame rataṃ .

Nitiṇṇaogha sambuddhaṃ          jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ muniṃ    
upaviṭṭhaṃ [^42-2] samāpattiṃ        indriyasaṃvarappahaṃ [^42-3] .

Alāvunodakaṃ gayha           buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ    
buddhapāde dhovitvāna        alāvukamadāsahaṃ .

Āṇāpesi ca sambuddho       padumuttaranāmako    
imimodakamāhatvā           pādamūle ṭhapehi me .

Sādhūtihaṃ paṭissutvā          satthu gāravatāyava [^42-4]    
udakaṃ lāvunāhatvā          buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upānayiṃ [^42-5] .

Anumodi mahāvīro           cittaṃ nibbāpayaṃ mama    
iminā lāvudānena          saṅkappo te samijjhatu .

Paṇṇarase ito kappe        devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
tettiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Divā vā yadivā rattiṃ        caṅkamantassa tiṭṭhato    
sovaṇṇaṃ kontaraṃ gayha        tiṭṭhanti [^42-6] purato mama .

Buddhassa datvāna lāvava        labhāmi soṇṇakontaraṃ    
appakaṃpi karaṃ kāraṃ           vipulaṃ hoti tādisu [^42-7]    
#[^42-1] ma. Soṇṇakontarikat... . Yu. Suvaṇṇakattarikat... . [^42-2] ma. Upatitthaṃ    
#samāpannaṃ . [^42-3] ma. Yu. Indivaradalappabhaṃ . [^42-4] ma. Yu. ... Ca . [^42-5] ma. Upāgamiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 43 -->
<a id="page-43"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 43]*

satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ lāvumadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          alāvussa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sovaṇṇakontariko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sovaṇṇakontarikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sakiṃsammajjako thero         ekadussāsanappadā    
kadamba koraṇḍakadā          ghaṭasavanikopica    
sucintito kiṃkaṇiko           soṇṇakontarikopica    
ekagāthāsatañcettha         ekasattatimeva ca .

Sakiṃsammajjakavaggo tecattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 44 -->
<a id="page-44"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 44]*

Catucattāḷīso ekavihārivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ekavihāriyattherāpadānaṃ (431)    
[21] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma gottena       uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Nippapañco nirālambo        ākāsasamamānaso    
suññatabahulo tādi           animittarato vasi .

Āsaṃkacitto [^44-1] nillepo     asaṃsaṭṭho kule gaṇe    
mahākaruṇiko dhīro           vinayopāyakovido .

Uyyutto parakiccesu         vinayanto sadevake    
nibbānagamanaṃ maggaṃ           gatipaṅkavisosanaṃ .

Amataṃ paramassādaṃ            jarāmaraṇanivāraṇaṃ    
mahāparisamajjhe so          nisinno lokatāraṇo .

Karavikarudo nātho           brahmaghoso tathāgato    
uddharanto mahādukkhā [^44-2]     vippanaṭṭhe anāyake .

Desento virajaṃ dhammaṃ        diṭṭho me lokanāyako    
tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Pabbajitvā tadāvāhaṃ         cintento jinasāsanaṃ    
ekakova vane ramme        vasiṃ saṃsaggapīḷito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 45 -->
<a id="page-45"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 45]*

Sakkāyavūpakasso [^45-1] me      hetubhūto mamāgami    
manaso vūpakāsassa [^45-2]        saṃsaggabhayadassino .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekavihāriyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekavihāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ekasaṅkhiyattherāpadānaṃ (432)    
[22] vipassino bhagavato           mahābodhimaho ahu    
mahājanā samāgamma          pūjenti bodhimuttamaṃ .

Mahāsokarahito [^45-3] pañño     buddhaseṭṭho bhavissati    
yassāyaṃ īdiso bodhi         pūjanīyova satthuno .

Tato saṅkhaṃ gahetvāna        bodhirukkhaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ    
dhamanto sabbadivasaṃ           avandiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Āsannake kataṃ kammaṃ         devalokaṃ apāpayi    
kalevaraṃ me patitaṃ           devaloke ramāmahaṃ .

#[^45-1] ma. Sakkāyavūpakāso me . Yu. Savakāya vūpakaṭṭho me . [^45-2] yu. Vūpakaṭṭhassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 46 -->
<a id="page-46"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 46]*

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni          tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā pamoditā    
sadā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhanti         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekasattatime kappe         rājā āsi sudassano    
cāturanto vijitāvī          jambūdīpassa [^46-1] issaro .

Tato aṭṭhasatā [^46-2] turiyā     parivārenti maṃ sadā    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ           upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
mātukucchigatassāpi           vajjare bheriyo sadā .

Upaṭṭhitvāna sambuddhaṃ         anubhotvāna sampadā    
sivaṃ amalaṃ [^46-3] amaraṃ          pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekasaṅkhiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekasaṅkhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^46-1] ma. Jambūmaṇḍassa . Yu. Jambūsaṇḍassa . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^46-2] ma. Yu. Aṅgasatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 47 -->
<a id="page-47"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 47]*

Tatiyaṃ pāṭihirasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (433)    
[23] padumuttaro nāma jino        āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
vasīsatasahassehi             nagaraṃ pāvisī tadā .

Nagaraṃ pavisantassa            upasantassa tādino    
rathiyā [^47-1] paccuggamanādīsu     nigghoso āsi tāvade .

Buddhassa ānubhāvena         avajjitaaghaṭṭitā [^47-2]    
sayaṃ vīṇā pavajjanti          buddhassa pavisato puraṃ .

Buddhaseṭṭhaṃ namassāmi         padumuttaraṃ mahāmuniṃ    
pāṭihirañca passitvā         tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Aho buddho aho dhammo      aho no satthusampadā    
acetanāpi turiyā           sayameva pavajjare .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^47-1] ma. Ratanāni pajjotiṃsu . Yu. Ratanāni panādiṃsu . [^47-2] ma. Bherivajjumaghaṭṭitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 48 -->
<a id="page-48"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 48]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭihirasaññako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Pāṭihirasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ñāṇatthavikattherāpadānaṃ (434)    
[24] kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ             dīparukkhaṃva jotitaṃ    
kañcanaṃva virocantaṃ           addasaṃ dipaduttamaṃ .

Kamaṇḍalala ṭhapetvāna          vākacīrañca kuṇḍikaṃ    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā          buddhaseṭṭhaṃ thaviṃ ahaṃ .

Tamandhakāraṃ vidhamaṃ            mohajālasamākulaṃ    
ñāṇālokaṃ dassayitvā        tiṇṇo [^48-1] asi tuvaṃ muni .

Samuddharasimaṃ lokaṃ            sabbāvantaṃ anuttaraṃ    
ñāṇena te upamā natthi      yāvatā jagato [^48-2] gati .

Tena ñāṇena sabbaññū        sabbaññūti pavuccati    
vandāmi taṃ mahāvīraṃ          sabbaññutaṃ anāsavaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ thaviṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ñāṇatthumanāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 49 -->
<a id="page-49"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 49]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇatthaviko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ñāṇatthavikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ ucchukhaṇḍikattherāpadānaṃ (435)    
[25] nagare bandhumatiyā           dvārapālo ahosahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ .

Ucchukkhaṇḍikamādāya          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          vipassissa mahesino .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ ucchumadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ucchukhaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ucchukhaṇḍiko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ucchukhaṇḍikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 50 -->
<a id="page-50"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 50]*

chaṭṭhaṃ kalambadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (436)    
[26] romaso nāma sambuddho       vasati pabbatantare    
kalambaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kalambassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kalambadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kalambadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ ambāṭakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (437)    
[27] pavane buddhaṃ disvāna         sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
ambāṭakaṃ gahetvāna         sayambhussa adāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 51 -->
<a id="page-51"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 51]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambāṭakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ambāṭakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ harītakidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (438)    
[28] harītakiṃ āmalakaṃ             ambajambuvibhedakaṃ    
kolaṃ bhallātakaṃ bellaṃ        sayamevāharāmahaṃ .

Disvāna pabbhāragataṃ          jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ muniṃ    
ābādhena āpīḷitaṃ          addhaniyaṃ [^51-1] mahāmuniṃ .

Harītakiṃ gahetvāna           sayambhussa adāsahaṃ    
katamattamhi bhesajjaṃ          byādhi passambhi tāvade .

Pahīnadaratho buddho           anumodaniyaṃ [^51-2] akā    
bhesajjadāneniminā          byādhivūpasamena ca .

Devabhūto manusso vā        jāto vā aññajātiyā    
sabbattha sukhito hohi [^51-3]      mā ca te byādhimāgamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 52 -->
<a id="page-52"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 52]*

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        sayambhū aparājito    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro          haṃsarājāva ambare .

Yato harītakimadaṃ [^52-1]          sayambhussa mahesino    
imaṃ jātiṃ upādāya          byādhi me nūpapajjatha .

Ayaṃ pacchimako mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
tisso vijjā anuppattā [^52-2]   kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        bhesajjamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          bhesajjassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā harītakidāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Harītakidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ ambapiṇḍiyattherāpadānaṃ (439)    
[29] hatthīrājā tadā āsiṃ        īsādanto uruḷhavo    
vicaranto brahāraññe        addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 53 -->
<a id="page-53"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 53]*

Ambapiṇḍiṃ [^53-1] gahetvāna      adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ    
paṭiggaṇhi mahāvīro          siddhattho lokanāyako .

Mama nijjhāyamānassa          paribhuñji tadā jino    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Tato ahaṃ cavitvāna          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
eteneva upāyena         anubhotvāna sampadā .

Padhānaṃ pahitattohaṃ           upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambapiṇḍiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ambapiṇḍiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 54 -->
<a id="page-54"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 54]*

Dasamaṃ jambūphaliyattherāpadānaṃ [^54-1] (440)    
[30] padumuttarabuddhassa            lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
piṇḍāya vicarantassa          dhārato uttamaṃ yasaṃ .

Aggaphalaṃ gahetvāna          vippasannena cetasā    
dakkhiṇeyyassa dhīrassa         adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda        lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ         hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          aggadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jambūphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Jambūphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 55 -->
<a id="page-55"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 55]*

uddānaṃ    
thero ekavihārī ca         saṅkhiko pāṭihīrako    
thaviko ucchukhaṇḍī ca          kalambambāṭakappadā .

Harītakambapiṇḍī ca            jambūdo dasamo yati    
chaḷāsīti ca gāthāyo         gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi .

Ekavihārivaggo catucattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 56 -->
<a id="page-56"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 56]*

Pañcacattāḷīso vibhedakivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ vibhedakabījiyattherāpadānaṃ [^56-1] (441)    
[31] kakusandho mahāvīro          sabbadhammāna pāragū    
gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭho so        agamāsi vanantaraṃ .

Vījavījaṃ [^56-2] gahetvāna        latāya ācariṃ [^56-3] ahaṃ    
bhagavā tamhi samaye          jhāyate pabbatantare .

Disvānāhaṃ devadevaṃ         vippasannena cetasā    
dakkhiṇeyyassa dhīrassa         vījavījamadāsahaṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          yaṃ vījamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          vījavījassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vibhedakabījiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vibhedakabījiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 57 -->
<a id="page-57"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 57]*

Dutiyaṃ koladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (442)    
[32] ajinena nivatthohaṃ           vākacīradharo tadā    
bhāriyā [^57-1] pūrayitvāna       kolaṃ hāsi [^57-2] mamassamaṃ .

Tamhi kāle sikhī buddho       eko adutiyo ahu    
mamassamaṃ upāgañchi           jotento [^57-3] sabbakālikaṃ .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā        vanditvāna ca subbataṃ    
ubhohatthehi paggayha         kolaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          koladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā koladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Koladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^57-1] ma. Khāriyā . Yu. Khāribhāraṃ haritvāna . [^57-2] ma. Hāsiṃ . Yu. Kolamāharimassamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 58 -->
<a id="page-58"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 58]*

Tatiyaṃ veluvaphaliyattherāpadānaṃ [^58-1] (443)    
[33] candabhāgānadītīre           assamo sukato mama    
veluvarukkhehi ākiṇṇo       nānādumanisevito .

Sugandhaṃ veluvaṃ disvā         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ    
khāribhāraṃ pūrayitvā          tuṭṭho saṃviggamānaso .

Kakusandhaṃ upāgamma           billapakkamadāsahaṃ    
puññakkhettassa dhīrassa        vippasannena cetasā .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā veluvaphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Veluvaphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 59 -->
<a id="page-59"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 59]*

Catutthaṃ bhallātakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (444)    
[34] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ    
pavanaggena gacchantaṃ          sālarājaṃva phullitaṃ .

Tiṇasantharaṃ paññāpetvā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ ayācahaṃ    
anukampatu maṃ buddho          bhikkhaṃ icchāmi dātave .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         atthadassī mahāyaso    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          oruyha [^59-1] mama assame .

Orohitvāna sambuddho       nisīdi paṇṇasanthare    
bhallātakaṃ gahetvāna         buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Mama nijjhāyamānassa          paribhuñji tadā jino    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       abhivandiṃ tadā jinaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 60 -->
<a id="page-60"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 60]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhallātakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bhallātakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ ummāpupphiyattherāpadānaṃ [^60-1] (445)    
[35] nigrodhe haritobhāse        saṃvirūḷhamhi pādape    
ummāmālañhi [^60-2] paggayha     bodhiyā abhiropayiṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          yaṃ bodhimabhipūjayiṃ [^60-3]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          bodhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭipasambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ummāpupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ummāpupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ambāṭakiyattherāpadānaṃ (446)    
[36] supupphitaṃ sālavanaṃ            oggayha vessabhū muni    
nisīdi giriduggesu            abhijātova kesarī .

#[^60-1] ma. Uttaripupphiyatthera... . Yu. Utuḷhipupphiyatthera... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 61 -->
<a id="page-61"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 61]*

Pasannacitto sumano          ambāṭakamapūjayiṃG    
puññakkhettaṃ mahāvīraṃ [^61-1]      pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambāṭakiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Imbāṭakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ sīhāsanikattherāpadānaṃ (447)    
[37] padumuttarassa bhagavato         sabbabhūtahitesino    
pasannacitto sumano          sīhāsanamadāsahaṃ .

Devaloke manusse vā       yattha yattha vasāmahaṃ    
labhāmi vipulaṃ byamhaṃ          sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Suvaṇṇarūpiyamayā             lohitaṅkamayā bahū    
maṇimayā ca pallaṅkā         nibbattanti mamaṃ sadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 62 -->
<a id="page-62"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 62]*

Bodhiyā āsanaṃ katvā        jalajuttamanāmino    
ucce kule pajāyāmi        aho dhammasudhammatā .

Satasahasse ito kappe       sīhāsanamakāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsaniko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sīhāsanikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ pādapīṭhiyattherāpadānaṃ (448)    
[38] sumedho nāma sambuddho       kāruṇiko [^62-1] mahāmuni    
tārayitvā bahū satte        nibbuto so mahāyaso .

Sīhāsanassa sāmantā         sumedhassa mahesino    
pasannacitto sumano          pādapīṭhamakārayiṃ .

Katvāna kusalaṃ kammaṃ          sukhapākaṃ sukhudrayaṃ [^62-2]    
puññakammena saṃyutto        tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 63 -->
<a id="page-63"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 63]*

Tattha me vasamānassa         puññakammasamaṅgino    
padāni uddharantassa          soṇṇapīṭhā bhavanti me .

Lābhā tesaṃ suladdhante       ye labhanti upassuti [^63-1]    
nibbute kāraṃ katvāna        labhanti vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Mayāpi sukataṃ kammaṃ           vāṇijje supayojitaṃ    
pādapīṭhaṃ karitvāna           soṇṇapīṭhaṃ labhāmahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ disaṃ pakkamāmi          kenaci kiccayenahaṃ [^63-2]    
soṇṇapīṭhe akkamāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pādapīṭhiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Pādapīṭhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 64 -->
<a id="page-64"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 64]*

Navamaṃ vediyakārakattherāpadānaṃ [^64-1] (449)    
[39] padumuttarassa buddhassa         bodhiyā pādaputtame    
vediyaṃ sukataṃ katvā          sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Aggoḷārāni [^64-2] bhaṇḍāni     katāni akatāni ca    
antalikkhā pavassanti         vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ubhato byuḷhasaṅgāme        pakkhandanto bhayānake    
bhayabheravaṃ na passāmi         vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          byamhaṃ nibbattate subhaṃ    
sayanāni mahagghāni           vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ vedikamakārayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vediyakārako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vediyakārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 65 -->
<a id="page-65"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 65]*

Dasamaṃ bodhigharakārakattherāpadānaṃ [^65-1] (450)    
[40] siddhatthassa bhagavato          dipadindassa tādino    
pasannacitto sumano          bodhigharamakārayiṃ .

Tussitaṃ upapannomhi          vasāmi ratane ghare    
na me sītaṃ va uṇhaṃ vā       vāto gatte na samphuse .

Pañcasaṭṭhimhito kappe        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
kāsikaṃ nāma nagaraṃ           visukammena māpitaṃ .

Dasayojanaāyāmaṃ            aṭṭhayojanavitthataṃ    
na tamhi nagare atthi         kaṭṭhaṃ vallī ca mattikā .

Tiriyaṃ yojanaṃ āsi           aḍḍhayojanavitthataṃ    
maṅgalo nāma pāsādo       visukammena māpito .

Cūḷāsītisahassāni            thambhā sovaṇṇiyā ahu    
maṇimayā ca niyyūhā          chadanaṃ rūpiyaṃ ahu .

Sabbasoṇṇamayaṃ gharaṃ           visukammena māpitaṃ    
ajjhāvutthaṃ mayā etaṃ        gharadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Te sabbe anubhotvāna       devamānusake bhave    
ajja pattomhi nibbānaṃ       santipadaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            bodhigharamakārayiṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 66 -->
<a id="page-66"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 66]*

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          gharadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bodhigharakārako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bodhigharakārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
vibhedaki kolaphali            billabhallātakappado    
ummā ambāṭaki ceva        āsani pādapīṭhako .

Vedikā bodhighariko          gāthāyo gaṇitāpica    
ekūnāsītikā sabbā         asmiṃ vagge pakittitā .

Vibhedakivaggo pañcacattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 67 -->
<a id="page-67"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 67]*

Chacattāḷīso jagativaggo    
paṭhamaṃ jagatidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (451)    
[41] dhammadassissa munino          bodhiyā pādaputtame    
pasannacitto sumano          jagatiṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ .

Darito pabbatāto vā        rukkhato patito ahaṃ    
cuto patiṭṭhaṃ vindāmi [^67-1]      jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Na me corā vihesanti [^67-2]    nātimaññanti khattiyā    
sabbāmittetikkamāmi         jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbattha pūjito homi         jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         jagatiṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          jagatidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 68 -->
<a id="page-68"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 68]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jagatidāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Jagatidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ morahatthiyattherāpadānaṃ (452)    
[42] morahatthaṃ gahetvāna         upesi [^68-1] lokanāyakaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          morahatthaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Iminā morahatthena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
nibbutā [^68-2] me tayo aggī    labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Aho buddhā aho dhammā      aho no satthusampadā    
datvānahaṃ morahatthaṃ          labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Tidhaggī nibbutā mayhaṃ         bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā         natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Ekatiṃse ito kappe        yaṃ dānamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          morahatthassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime  
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 69 -->
<a id="page-69"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 69]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā morahatthiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Morahatthiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ sīhāsanabījiyattherāpadānaṃ (453)    
[43] tissassāhaṃ bhagavato          bodhirukkhaṃ avandihaṃ [^69-1]    
paggayha vījaniṃ tattha          sīhāsanamavijjahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe       sīhāsanamavijjahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          vījanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanabījiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sīhāsanabījiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ tiṇukkadhāriyattherāpadānaṃ (454)    
[44] pudumuttarassa buddhassa         bodhiyā pādaputtame    
pasannacitto sumano          tayo ukke adhārayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 70 -->
<a id="page-70"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 70]*

Satasahasse ito kappe       yāhaṃ [^70-1] ukkamadhārayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ukkadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇukkadhāriyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇukkadhāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ akkamanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (455)    
[45] kakusandhassa munino           brāhmaṇassa vusīmato    
divāvihāraṃ vajato           akkamanamadāsahaṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          yaṃ dānamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          akkamanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 71 -->
<a id="page-71"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 71]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā akkamanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Akkamanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ vanakoraṇḍiyattherāpadānaṃ (456)    
[46] siddhatthassa bhagavato          lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
vanakoraṇḍamādāya           buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ
