duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vanakoraṇḍiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vanakoraṇḍiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Vīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 72 -->
<a id="page-72"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 72]*

Sattamaṃ ekachattiyattherāpadānaṃ (457)    
[47] aṅgārajātā paṭhavī          kukkuḷānugatā mahi    
padumuttaro bhagavā           abbhokāsamhi caṅkami .

Paṇḍaraṃ chattamādāya          addhānaṃ patipajjahaṃ    
tattha disvāna sambuddhaṃ        cittaṃ [^72-1] me upapajjatha .

Marīcimophunā [^72-2] bhūmi         aṅgārāva mahī ayaṃ    
upaṭṭhanti [^72-3] mahāvātā      sarīrakāyukhepanā [^72-4] .

Sītaṃ uṇhaṃ vihanati [^72-5]         vātātapanivāraṇaṃ    
paṭiggaṇha imaṃ chattaṃ          phassayissāmi nibbutiṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         padumuttaro mahāyaso    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          paṭiggaṇhi tadā jino .

Tiṃsakappāni devindo         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuṃ ca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ           pubbe sukatamattano .

Ayaṃ me pacchimā jāti        carimo vattate bhavo    
ajjāpi setachattaṃ me        sabbakālaṃ dharīyati .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ chattamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^72-1] ma. Yu. Vitti . [^72-2] ma. Marīciyotthaṭā . Yu. Marīcivophuṭā . [^72-3] ma. Upahanti .

#yu. Upavāyanti . [^72-4] ma. Sarīrassāsukhepanā . Yu. Sarīrassānukhepanā . [^72-5] ma. Vihanantaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 73 -->
<a id="page-73"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 73]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekachattiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekachattiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ jātipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (458)    
[48] parinibbute bhagavati           padumuttare mahāyase    
pupphacaṅkoṭake [^73-1] gahetvā   sarīraṃ abhiropayiṃ .

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       nimmānaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
devalokaṃ gato santo        puññakammaṃ carāmahaṃ .

Ambarā pupphavasso me       sabbakālaṃ pavassati    
sambhavāmi [^73-2] manusse ve     rājā homi mahāyaso .

Tahiṃ kusumavasso me          abhivassati sabbadā    
kāyesu [^73-3] pupphapūjāya       vāhasā sabbadassino .

Ayaṃ pacchimako mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
ajjāpi pupphavasso me       abhivassati sabbadā .

#[^73-1] ma. Pupphavaṭaṃ sake katvā . [^73-2] ma. Yu. Saṃsarāmi manusse ce . [^73-3] ma. Tasseva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 74 -->
<a id="page-74"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 74]*

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          dehapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jātipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Jātipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sattipaṇṇiyattherāpadānaṃ [^74-1] (459)    
[49] nīharante sarīramhi           vijjamānāsu [^74-2] bherisu    
pasannacitto sumano          sattipupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          dehapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 75 -->
<a id="page-75"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 75]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattipaṇṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sattipaṇṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ gandhapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (460)    
[50] citakesu kurumānesu          nānāgandhe samāhaṭe    
pasannacitto sumano          gandhamuṭṭhiṃ apūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       citakaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhapūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Gandhapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 76 -->
<a id="page-76"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 76]*

uddānaṃ    
jagatī morahattho ca          āsani ukkadhārako    
akkami vanakoraṇḍi           chattado jātipūjako .

Sattipaṇṇiyo thero ca        dasamo gandhapūjako    
sattasaṭṭhī ca gāthāyo        gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi .

Jagatidāyakavaggo chacattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 77 -->
<a id="page-77"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 77]*

sattacattāḷīso sālapupphivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sālakusumiyattherāpadānaṃ (461)    
[51] parinibbute bhagavati           jalajuttamanāmake    
āropitamhi citake          sālapupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālakusumiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sālakusumiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (462)    
[52] jhāyamānassa bhagavato         sikhino lokabandhuno    
aṭṭha campakapupphāni          citakaṃ abhiropayiṃ .

Ekatiṃse ito kappe        yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 78 -->
<a id="page-78"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 78]*

Duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ [^78-1] phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ citakanibbāpakattherāpadānaṃ (463)    
[53] ḍayhamāne sarīramhi          vessabhussa mahesino    
gandhodakaṃ gahetvāna         citaṃ nibbāpayiṃ ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       citaṃ nibbāpayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          gandhodakassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 79 -->
<a id="page-79"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 79]*

paṭisambhidā catasso           vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā           kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakanibbāpako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Citakanibbāpakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ setudāyakattherāpadānaṃ (464)    
[54] vipassino bhagavato            caṅkamantassa sammukhā    
pasannacitto sumano           setuṃ kārāpayiṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe        yaṃ setuṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi           setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ         bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi         mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā       kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso           vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā           kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā setudāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Setudāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 80 -->
<a id="page-80"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 80]*

pañcamaṃ sumanatālavaṇṭiyattherāpadānaṃ (465)    
[55] siddhatthassa bhagavato          tālavaṇṭaṃ adāsahaṃ    
sumanehi paṭicchannaṃ           dhārayāmi mahāyasaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        tālavaṇṭaṃ adāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanatālavaṇṭiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sumanatālavaṇṭiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ avaṭaphaliyattherāpadānaṃ (466)    
[56] sataraṃsī nāma bhagavā          sayambhū aparājito    
vivekakāmo sambuddho        gocarāyābhinikkhami .

Phalahattho ahaṃ disvā         upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          adāsiṃ avaṭaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 81 -->
<a id="page-81"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 81]*

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā avaṭaphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Avaṭaphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ labujaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (467)    
[57] nagare bandhumatiyā           ārāmiko ahaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Labujaphalamādāya             buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
ākāseva ṭhito santo       paṭiggaṇhi mahāyaso .

Vittisañjanano [^81-1] mayhaṃ       diṭṭhadhamme sukhāvaho    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna         vippasannena cetasā .

Adhiggañchiṃ tadā pītiṃ          vipulaṃ sukhamuttamaṃ    
uppajjate me ratanaṃ         nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 82 -->
<a id="page-82"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 82]*

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā labujaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Labujaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ milakkhuphaladāyakattherāpadāna [^82-1] (468)    
[58] vanantare [^82-2] buddhaṃ disvā     atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          milakkhussa phalaṃ adaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 83 -->
<a id="page-83"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 83]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā milakkhuphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Milakkhuphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sayampaṭibhāṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (469)    
[59] kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva             devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            ko disvā nappasīdati .

Tamandhakāraṃ nāsetvā        santāretvā bahuṃ janaṃ    
ñāṇālokena jotantaṃ        ko disvā nappasīdati .

Vasīsatasahassehi             niyyantaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
uddharantaṃ ḷahū satte         ko disvā nappasīdati .

Āhanitvā dhammabheriṃ         maddantaṃ titthiye gaṇe    
sīhanādaṃ va nadantaṃ           ko disvā nappasīdati .

Yāvatā brahmalokamhā       āgantvāna sabrahmakā    
pucchanti nipuṇe pañhe        ko disvā nappasīdati .

Yassañjaliṃ karitvāna          ādhāvanti [^83-1] sadevakā    
tena puññaṃ anubhonti         ko disvā nappasīdati .

Sabbe janā samāgantvā      sampavārenti cakkhumaṃ    
na vikampati ajjhiṭṭho         ko disvā nappasīdati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 84 -->
<a id="page-84"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 84]*

Nagaraṃ pavīsato yassa          nadanti [^84-1] bheriyo bahū    
vinadanti gajā [^84-2] mattā      ko disvā nappasīdati .

Vīthiyā gacchato yassa         pabhā [^84-3] vā jotate sadā    
abbhunnatā samā honti       ko disvā nappasīdati .

Byāharantassa buddhassa        cakvāḷamhi suyyati    
sabbe satte viññāpeti      ko disvā nappasīdati .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayampaṭibhāṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sayampaṭibhāṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ nimittabyākaraṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (470)    
[60] ajjhogahetvā himavantaṃ       mante vācemahantadā    
catupaññāsasahassāni          sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 85 -->
<a id="page-85"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 85]*

Adhitā vedagū sabbe         chaḷaṅge pāramiṃ gatā    
sakavijjāhupatthaddhā          himavante vasanti te .

Cavitvā tusitā kāyā        devaputto mahāyaso    
uppajji mātukucchismiṃ         sampajāno paṭissato .

Sambuddhe upapajjante        dasasahassi kampatha    
andhā cakkhuṃ alabhiṃsu          uppajjantamhi nāyake .

Chappakāramakampittha [^85-1]        kevalā vasudhā ayaṃ    
nigghosasaddaṃ sutvāna         vimhayiṃsu [^85-2] mahājanā .

Sabbe janā samāgamma        agañchacha mama santike    
vasudhāyaṃ pakampittha           kiṃvipāko bhavissati .

Vidassāmi [^85-3] tadā tesaṃ      mā bhāyittha natthi vo bhayaṃ    
vissaṭṭhā hotha sabbepi       uppādoyaṃ sukhatthiko [^85-4] .

Aṭṭhahetūhi samphassā         vasudhāyaṃ pakampati    
tathā nimittā dissanti        obhāso vipulo mahā .

Asaṃsayaṃ buddhaseṭṭho          uppajjissati cakkhumā    
saññāpetvāna janataṃ         pañca sīle kathesahaṃ .

Sutvāna pañca sīlāni         buddhuppādañca dullabhaṃใ    
ubbeṅgajātā sumanā        tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā ahesu te .

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ nimittaṃ viyākariṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          byākaraṇassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^85-1] ma. Sabbākāraṃ pakampittha . Yu. Chabbikāraṃ ... . [^85-2] ma. Yu. Ubbijjiṃsu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 86 -->
<a id="page-86"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 86]*

kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nimittabyākaraṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Nimittabyākaraṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sālakusumiyo thero          pūjā nibbāpakopica    
setudo tālavaṇṭi ca         avaṭaṃ labujampado    
milakkhu paṭibhāṇī ca           veyyākaraṇiyo dijā    
dvesattati ca gāthāyo       gaṇitāyo vibhāvibhi .

Sālapupphivaggo sattacattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 87 -->
<a id="page-87"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 87]*

Aṭṭhacattāḷīso naḷamālivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ naḷamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (471)    
[61] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
pavanaggena gacchantaṃ          addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Naḷamālaṃ gahetvāna          nikkhamanto ca tāvade    
tatthaddasāsi sambuddhaṃ         oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano          naḷamālaṃ apūjayiṃ    
dakkhiṇeyyaṃ mahāvīraṃ          sabbalokānukampakaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ [^87-1] abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Naḷamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 88 -->
<a id="page-88"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 88]*

Dutiyaṃ maṇipūjakattherāpadānaṃ (472)    
[62] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
vivekakāmo sambuddho        gacchate anilañjase .

Avidūre himavantassa          mahājātassaro ahu    
tattha me bhavanaṃ āsi         puññakammena saṃyutaṃ .

Bhavanā nikkhamitvāna [^88-1]       addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ             ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Vijanaṃ [^88-2] addasaṃ pupphaṃ        pūjayissanti nāyakaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā        avandiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Mamaṃ sīsamaṇiṃ gayha            pūjayiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
imāya maṇipūjāya            vipāko hotu bhaddako .

Padumuttaro lokavidū          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā       imaṃ gāthaṃ [^88-3] abhāsatha .

So te ijjhatu saṅkappo      labhatu vipulaṃ sukhaṃ    
imāya maṇipūjāya            anubhohi mahāyasaṃ .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho [^88-4]     jalajuttamanāmako    
agamāsi buddhaseṭṭho         yassa cittaṃ paṇīhitaṃ .

Saṭṭhikappāni devindo        devarajjamakārayiṃ    
anekasatakkhattuñca           cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

#[^88-1] ma. Yu. Abhinikkhamma . [^88-2] ma. Vijinaṃ naddasaṃ pupphaṃ . Yu. ... Nāddasaṃ pupphaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 89 -->
<a id="page-89"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 89]*

Pubbakammaṃ sarantassa          devabhūtassa me sato    
maṇi nibbattate mayhaṃ         ālokakaraṇo mama .

Chaḷāsītisahassāni            nāriyo me pariggahā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā           āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā [^89-1] .

Āḷāramukhā hasulā          susaññā tanumajjhimā    
parivārenti maṃ niccaṃ         maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soṇṇamayā maṇimayā          lohitaṅkamayā tathā    
bhaṇḍā [^89-2] me sukatā honti   yadicchasi [^89-3] pilandhanā .

Kūṭāgārā guhā rammā       sayanañca mahārahaṃ    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          nibbattanti yathicchikaṃ [^89-4] .

Lābhā tesaṃ suladdhañca        ye labhanti upassutiṃ    
puññakkhettaṃ manussānaṃ        osathaṃ [^89-5] sabbapāṇinaṃ .

Mayhaṃpi sukataṃ kammaṃ           yohaṃ adakkhi nāyakaṃ    
vinipātā sumuttomhi         pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
divasañceva [^89-6] rattiñca       āloko hoti me sadā .

Tāyeva maṇipūjāya           anubhotvāna sampadā    
ñāṇāloko mayā diṭṭho      pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ maṇimabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^89-1] ma. Āmukka... . [^89-2] po. Yu. Bhaṇḍā katākatā honti . [^89-3] yu. Yadicchāya .

#po. Yadicchassa . [^89-4] po. Ma. Yu. Yadicchakaṃ . [^89-5] ma. Osadhaṃ . [^89-6] yu. Samantā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 90 -->
<a id="page-90"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 90]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maṇipūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Maṇipūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ ukkāsatikattherāpadānaṃ (473)    
[63] kosiko nāma bhagavā         cittakūṭe vasī tadā    
jhāyī jhānarato buddho        vivekābhirato muni .

Ajjhogahetvā [^90-1] himavantaṃ    nārīgaṇapurakkhato    
addasaṃ kosikaṃ buddhaṃ          puṇṇamāyeva candimaṃ .

Ukkāsate gahetvāna        parivāresahantadā    
sattarattindivaṃ buddhaṃ [^90-2]       aṭṭhamena agacchahaṃ .

Vuṭṭhitaṃ kosikaṃ buddhaṃ          sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
pasannacitto vanditvā        ekaṃ bhikkhaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda        lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
uppajjiṃ tusite kāye        ekabhikkhāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 91 -->
<a id="page-91"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 91]*

Divasañceva rattiñca          āloko hoti me sadā    
samantā yojanasataṃ           obhāsena pharāmahaṃ .

Pañcapaññāsakappamhi          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
cāturanto vijitāvī          jambūmaṇḍassa issaro .

Tadā me nagaraṃ āsi         iddhaṃ phītaṃ sunimmitaṃ    
tiṃsayojanaāyāmaṃ            vitthārena ca vīsati .

Sobhanaṃ nāma nagaraṃ           visukammena māpitaṃ    
dasasaddā vivittantaṃ          sammatāḷasamāhitaṃ .

Na tasmiṃ nagare atthi         vallī kaṭṭhañca mattikā    
sabbasoṇṇamayaṃyeva           jotate niccakālikaṃ .

Catupākāraparikkhittaṃ          tayo āsuṃ maṇimayā    
vemajjhe tālapanti [^91-1] ca     visukammena māpitā .

Dasasahassapokkharaṇī [^91-2]        padumuppalachāditā    
puṇḍarīkādisañchannā [^91-3]       nānāgandhasameritā [^91-4] .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ ukkaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ukkādhārassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^91-1] tālapantī . Yu. Tālapattī . [^91-2] ma. Yu. Dasasahassā pokkharañño .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 92 -->
<a id="page-92"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 92]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ukkāsatiko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ukkāsatikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ sumanavījaniyattherāpadānaṃ (474)    
[64] vipassino bhagavato           bodhiyā pādaputtame    
sumano bījaniṃ gayha           avījiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       avījiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          bījanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanavījaniyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sumanavījaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 93 -->
<a id="page-93"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 93]*

pañcamaṃ kummāsadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (475)    
[65] esanāya carantassa          vipassino mahesino    
rittakaṃ pattaṃ disvāna         kummāsaṃ pūrayiṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ bhikkhaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kummāsassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kummāsadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kummāsadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ kusaṭṭhakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (476)    
[66]  kassapassa bhagavato           brāhmaṇassa vusīmato    
pasannacitto sumano          kusaṭṭhakamadāsahaṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          kusaṭṭhakamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kusaṭṭhakassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 94 -->
<a id="page-94"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 94]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kusaṭṭhakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kusaṭṭhakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ giripunnāgiyattherāpadānaṃ (477)    
[67] sobhito nāma sambuddho       cittakūṭe vasī tadā    
gahetvā giripunnāgaṃ         sayambhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        sambuddhamabhipūjayiṃ [^94-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 95 -->
<a id="page-95"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 95]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā giripunnāgiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Giripunnāgiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ vallikāraphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (478)    
[68] sumano nāma sambuddho        takkarāyaṃ vasī tadā    
vallikāraphalaṃ gayha           sayambhussa adāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vallikāraphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vallikāraphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ pānadhidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (479)    
[69] anomadassī bhagavā           lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
divāvihārā nikkhamma         vīthimāruyhi cakkhumā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 96 -->
<a id="page-96"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 96]*

Pānadhiṃ sukataṃ gayha           addhānaṃ paṭipajjahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         pattikaṃ cārudassanaṃ .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā        nīharitvāna pānadhiṃ    
pādamūle ṭhapetvāna         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Abhirūha mahāvīra             sugatinda vināyaka    
ito phalaṃ labhissāmi          so me attho samijjhatu .

Anomadassī bhagavā           lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
pānadhiṃ abhirūhitvā           idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Yo pānadhiṃ me adāsi [^96-1]     pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi           suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Buddhassa giramaññāya          sabbe devā samāgatā    
udaggacittā sumanā          vedajātā katañjalī .

Pānadhimeva [^96-2] dānena       sukhitoyaṃ bhavissati    
pañcapaññāsakkhattuñca         devarajjaṃ karissati .

Sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca        cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe      okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya         nibbāyissatināsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 97 -->
<a id="page-97"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 97]*

Devaloke manusse vā       nibbattissati paññavā    
devayānapaṭibhāgaṃ            yānaṃ paṭilabhissati .

Pāsādā sivikā mayhaṃ        hatthino samalaṅkatā    
rathā vājaññasaṃyuttā         sadā pātubhavanti me .

Agārā nikkhamantopi         rathena nikkhamiṃ ahaṃ    
kesesu chijjamānesu         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Lābhā mayhaṃ suladdhaṃ me       vāṇijaṃ suppayojitaṃ    
datvāna pānadhiṃ ekaṃ         pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe      yaṃ pānadhimadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          pānadhissa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pānadhidāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Pānadhidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 98 -->
<a id="page-98"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 98]*

Dasamaṃ puḷinacaṅkamiyattherāpadānaṃ (480)    
[70] migaluddho [^98-1] pure āsiṃ      araññe kānane ahaṃ    
vātamigaṃ gavesanto          caṅkamaṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ .

Ucchaṅgena puḷinaṃ gayha        caṅkame okiriṃ ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          sugatassa sirīmato .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       puḷinaṃ okiriṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          puḷinassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puḷinacaṅkamiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Puḷinacaṅkamiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ  
naḷamāli maṇidado            ukkāsatikavījani  
kummāsañca kusaṭṭhañca         giripunnāgiyopica  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 99 -->
<a id="page-99"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 99]*

Vallikāro pānadhido         atho puḷinacaṅkamo    
gāthāyo pañcanavuti          gaṇitāyo vibhāvibhi .

Naḷamālivaggo aṭṭhacattāḷīso .

ekūnapaññāsamo paṃsukūlavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ paṃsukūlasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (481)    
[71] tisso nāmāsi bhagavā        sayambhū lokanāyako [^99-1]    
paṃsukūlaṃ ṭhapetvāna           vihāraṃ pāvisī jino .

Sajjitaṃ [^99-2] dhanumādāya        pakkhittasalilaṃ [^99-3] ahaṃ    
maṇḍalaggaṃ gahetvāna         kānanaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ .

Tatthaddasaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ            dumagge laggitaṃ tadā    
cāpaṃ tattheva nikkhippa        sīse katvāna añjaliṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano          vipulāya ca pītiyā    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         paṃsukūlaṃ avandahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ paṃsukūlamavandahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^99-1] ma. Yu. Aggapuggalo . [^99-2] ma. Vinataṃ . Yu. Tiyantaṃ . [^99-3] ma. Bhakkhatthāya cariṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 100 -->
<a id="page-100"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 100]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṃsukūlasaññako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Paṃsukūlasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ buddhasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (482)    
[72] ajjhāyiko [^100-1] mantadharo      tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
lakkhaṇe itihāse ca         sanighaṇḍusakeṭubhe .

Nadīsotapaṭibhāgā            sissā āyanti me tadā    
tesāhaṃ mantaṃ [^100-2] vācemi     rattindivamatandito .

Siddhattho nāma sambuddho      loke uppajji tāvade    
tamandhakāraṃ nāsetvā        ñāṇālokaṃ pavattayi .

Mama aññataro sisso         sissānaṃ so kathesi me    
sutvāna te etamatthaṃ        ārocesuṃ mamaṃ tadā .

Buddho loke samuppanno      sabbaññū lokanāyako    
tassānuvattati jano          lābho mayhaṃ na vijjati .

Adhiccuppattikā buddhā        cakkhumanto mahāyasā    
yannūnāhaṃ buddhaseṭṭhaṃ         passeyyaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 101 -->
<a id="page-101"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 101]*

Ajinaṃ me gahetvāna         vākacīraṃ kamaṇḍalala    
assamā abhinikkhamma          sisse āmantayiṃ ahaṃ .

Udumbarikapupphaṃva             candamhi sasakaṃ yathā    
vāyasānaṃ yathā khīraṃ          dullabhaṃ [^101-1] lokanāyakaṃ .

Buddho lokamhi uppanno      manussattaṃpi dullabhaṃ    
ubhosu vijjamānesu          savanañca sudullabhaṃ .

Buddho loke samuppanno      cakkhuṃ lacchāma no bhavaṃ    
etha sabbe gamissāma        sammāsambuddhasantikaṃ .

Kamaṇḍaludharā sabbe          kharājinanivāsino    
te [^101-2] jaṭābhārabharitā       nikkhamma pavanā tadā .

Yuttamattaṃ [^101-3] pekkhamānā     uttamatthaṃ gavesino    
āyanti [^101-4] nāgapotāva      asambhītāva kesarī .

Appatāsā [^101-5] aloluppā     nipakā santavuttino    
uñchāya caramānā te        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upāgamama .

Diyaḍḍhayojane dese [^101-6]      byādhi me upapajjatha    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^101-1] ma. Dullabho lokanāyako . [^101-2] yu. Ye . [^101-3] po. Ma. Yu. Yugamattaṃ .

#[^101-4] ma. Āsatti dosarahitā . [^101-5] ma. Appakiccā . Yu. Appabhāsā alīlatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 102 -->
<a id="page-102"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 102]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasaññako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Buddhasaññakattherassa apādānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ bhisadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (483)    
[73] oggayhāhaṃ [^102-1] pokkharaṇiṃ     nānākuñjarasevitaṃ    
uddharāmi bhisaṃ tattha          asanahetu [^102-2] ahaṃ tadā .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          padumuttarasavhayo    
rattakambaladharo [^102-3] buddho     gacchasi anilañjase .

Dhunanto paṃsukūlāni           saddaṃ assosahaṃ tadā    
uddhaṃ nijjhāyamānohaṃ         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Tattheva ṭhitako santo        āyāciṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
madhu [^102-4] bhiṃsehi savati         khīrasappi [^102-5] muḷālibhi .

Paṭiggaṇhātu me buddho       anukampāya cakkhumā    
tato kāruṇiko satthā        orohitvā mahāyaso .

Paṭiggaṇhi mama bhikkhaṃ          anukampāya cakkhumā    
paṭiggahetvā sambuddho       akā me anumodanaṃ .

#[^102-1] ma. Ogayha yaṃ pokkharaṇiṃ . [^102-2] ma. Yu. Ghāsahetu . [^102-3] ma. Yu. Rattambaradharo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 103 -->
<a id="page-103"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 103]*

Sukhī hohi [^103-1] mahāpuñña       gati tuyhaṃ samijjhatu    
iminā bhisadānena           labhassu vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Adaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        jalajuttamanāmako    
bhikkhamādāya sambuddho        ambarenāgamā jino .

Tato bhisaṃ gahetvāna         āgañchiṃ mama assamaṃ    
bhisaṃ rukkhe laggitvāna        mama dānaṃ anussariṃ .

Mahāvāto vuṭṭhahitvā        sañcālesi vanaṃ tadā    
ākāso abhinādittha         asaniyā phalantiyā .

Tato me asanipāto         matthake nipati tadā    
so [^103-2] hi nisinnako santo    tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Puññakammena saṃyutto         tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ    
kalevaramme patitaṃ           devaloke ramāmahaṃ .

Chaḷāsītisahassāni            nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhenti        bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Manussayonimāgantvā         sukhito homahaṃ tadā    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Anukampitako [^103-3] tena        devadevena tādinā    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo [^103-4]        natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ bhikkhamadadiṃ [^103-5] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^103-1] ma. Hotu . [^103-2] ma. Yu. Sohaṃ . [^103-3] yu. Anukampitattā tena . [^103-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 104 -->
<a id="page-104"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 104]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bhisadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ñāṇatthavikattherāpadānaṃ (484)    
[74] dakkhiṇe himavantassa          sukato assamo mama    
uttamatthaṃ gavesanto         vasāmi pavane tadā .

Lābhālābhena santuṭṭho       mūlena ca phalena ca    
anomasanto [^104-1] acari [^104-2]     vasāmi ekako ahaṃ .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho       loke uppajji tāvade    
catusaccaṃ pakāseti           uddharanto mahājanaṃ .

Nāhaṃ suṇomi sambuddhaṃ         napi me koci sāsati    
aṭṭhavasse atikkante        assosiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Aggiṃ dāruṃ niharitvā         sammajjitvāna assamaṃ    
khāribhāraṃ gahetvāna         nikkhamiṃ pavanā ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 105 -->
<a id="page-105"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 105]*

Ekarattiṃ vasantohaṃ          gāmesu nigamesu ca    
anupubbena candavatiṃ [^105-1]       tadāhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          sumedho lokanāyako    
uddharanto bahū satte        deseti amataṃpadaṃ .

Janakāyamatikkamma            vanditvā jinasāgataṃ [^105-2]    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā          santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Tuvaṃ satthā ca ketuva [^105-3]      dhajo yūpova [^105-3] pāṇinaṃ    
parāyano patiṭṭhā ca         dīpo ca dipaduttamo .

Ekavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Nepuñño dassane dhīro       tāresi janataṃ tuvaṃ    
natthañño tārako loke      tavuttarittaro mune .

Sakkā bhave [^105-4] kusaggena     pametuṃ sāgaruttamo [^105-5]    
na tveva tava sabbaññu        ñāṇaṃ sakkā pametave .

Tuladaṇḍe ṭhapetvāna         mahi [^105-6] sakkā dharetave    
na tveva tava paññāya        samānaṃ [^105-7] atthi cakkhuma .

Ākāso minituṃ sakkā        rajjuyā aṅgulena ca [^105-8]    
na tveva tava sabbaññu        sīlaṃ sakkā pametave .

Mahāsamudde udakaṃ           ākāso ca vasundharo [^105-9]    
parimeyyāni etāni         appameyyosi cakkhuma .

#[^105-1] po. Dhāvanto . [^105-2] ma. Jinasāgaraṃ . Yu. Jinasāsanaṃ . [^105-3] ma. Yu. Ca .

#[^105-4] ma. Theve . Yu. Have . [^105-5] ma. Sāgaruttame . [^105-6] ma. Mahiṃ . [^105-7] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 106 -->
<a id="page-106"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 106]*

Chahi gāthāhi sabbaññaña         kittayitvā mahāyasaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna         tuṇhī aṭṭhāsahaṃ tadā .

Yaṃ vadanti sumedhoti          bhūmipaññaṃ [^106-1] sumedhasaṃ .

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha    
yo me ñāṇaṃ pakittesi       vippasasannena [^106-2] cetasā .

Tamahaṃ kittayissāmi           suṇātha mama bhāsato    
sattasattati kappāni          devaloke ramissati .

Sahassakkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjaṃ karissati    
anekasatakkhattuñca           cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
devabhūto manusso vā        puññakammasamāhito .

Anūnamanasaṅkappo [^106-3]         tikkhapañño bhavissati    
tiṃsakappasahassamhi            okkākakulasambhavo .

Gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati    
agārā abhinikkhamma          pabbajissatikiñcano .

Jātiyā sattavassena         arahattaṃ pāpuṇissati [^106-4]    
yato sarāmi attānaṃ         yato pattosmi sāsanaṃ .

Etthantare na jānāmi       cetanaṃ amanoramaṃ    
saṃsaritvā bhavābhave [^106-5]       sampattānubhaviṃ ahaṃ .

#[^106-1] ma. Yu. Bhūripaññaṃ . [^106-2] po. Ma. Pasanno sehi pāṇibhi . [^106-3] yu. Anūnamatasaṅkappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 107 -->
<a id="page-107"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 107]*

Bhoge me ūnatā natthi       phalaṃ ñāṇassa thomane    
tidhaggī [^107-1] nibbutā mayhaṃ      bhavā sabbe samūhatā .

Sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo           natthi dāni punabbhavo    
tiṃsakappasahassamhi            yaṃ ñāṇamabhithomahaṃ [^107-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phalaṃ ñāṇassa thomane .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇatthaviko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ñāṇatthavikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ candanamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (485)    
[75] pañcakāmaguṇe hitvā         piyarūpe manorame    
asītikoṭiyo hitvā          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Pabbajitvāna kāyena         pāpakammaṃ vivajjayiṃ    
vacīduccaritaṃ hitvā           nadīkūle vasāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 108 -->
<a id="page-108"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 108]*

Ekekaṃ [^108-1] maṃ viharantaṃ       buddhaseṭṭho upāgami    
nāhaṃ jānāmi buddhoti        akāsiṃ paṭisantharaṃ .

Karitvā paṭisanthāraṃ          nāmagottaṃ apucchahaṃ    
devatā nusi gandhabbo        ādū [^108-2] sakko purindado .

Ko vā tvaṃ kassa vā putto   mahābrahmā idhāgato    
virocasi disā sabbā         udayaṃ suriyo yathā .

Sahassārāni cakkāni         pāde dissanti mārisa    
ko vā tvaṃ kassa vā putto   kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

Nāmagottaṃ pavedehi         saṃsayaṃ apanehi me    
namhi devo na gandhabbo      namhi sakko purindado .

Brahmabhāvo ca me natthi      etesaṃ uttamo ahaṃ    
atīto visayaṃ tesaṃ           dālayiṃ kāmabandhanaṃ .

Sabbe kilese jhāpetvā     patto sambodhimuttamaṃ    
tassa vācaṃ suṇitvāhaṃ         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Yadi buddhosi sabbaññū         nisīda tvaṃ mahāmuni    
tamahaṃ pūjayissāmi            dukkhassantakaro tuvaṃ .

Pattharitvā jinacammaṃ          adāsi satthuno ahaṃ    
nisīdi tattha bhagavā           sīhova girigabbhare .

Khippaṃ pabbatamāruyha          ambassa phalamaggahiṃ    
sālakalyāṇikaṃ pupphaṃ          candanañca mahārahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 109 -->
<a id="page-109"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 109]*

Khippaṃ paggayha taṃ sabbaṃ        upetvā [^109-1] lokanāyakaṃ    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna         sālapupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Candanaṃ anulimpitvā          avandiṃ satthuno ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          vipulāya ca pītiyā .

Ajinamhi nisīditvā           sumedho lokanāyako    
mama kammaṃ pakittesi          hāsayanto mamaṃ tadā .

Iminā phaladānena           gandhamālehi cūbhayaṃ    
pañcavīse kappasate          devaloke ramissati .

Anūnamanasaṅkappo            vasavatti bhavissati    
chabbīsatikappasate            manussattaṃ gamissati .

Bhavissati cakkavatti           cāturanto mahiddhiko    
vekaraṃ [^109-2] nāma nagaraṃ        visukammena māpitaṃ .

Hessati sabbasovaṇṇaṃ         nānāratanabhūsitaṃ    
eteneva upāyena         saṃsarissati yoniyo [^109-3] .

Sabbattha sukhito [^109-4] hutvā     devatte atha mānuse    
pacchime bhavasampatte         brahmabandhu bhavissati .

Agārā abhinikkhamma          anagāri bhavissati    
aviññattipaccayo [^109-5] hutvā    nibbāyissatināsavo .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        sumedho lokanāyako    
mama nijjhāyamānassa          pakkāmi anilañjase .

#[^109-1] yu. Upesiṃ . [^109-2] ma. Yu. Vebhāraṃ . [^109-3] ma. So bhave . Yu. Yoniso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 110 -->
<a id="page-110"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 110]*

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tusitaṃ [^110-1] upapajjahaṃ .

Tusitato cavitvāna           nibbattiṃ mātukucchiyā    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       yamhi gabbhe vasāmahaṃ .

Mātukucchigate mayi           annapānañca bhojanaṃ    
mātuyā mama chandena         nibbattati yathicchakaṃ .

Jātiyā pañcavassena         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
oropitamhi kesamhi         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Pubbakammaṃ gavesanto         orena naddasaṃ ahaṃ    
tiṃsakappasahassamhi            mama kammaṃ anussariṃ .

Namo te purisājañña         namo te purisuttama    
tava sāsanamāgamma           pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            sambuddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 111 -->
<a id="page-111"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 111]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā candanamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Candanamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ dhātupūjakattherāpadānaṃ (486)    
[76] nibbute lokanāthamhi         siddhatthe lokanāyake    
mamaññātī samānetvā        dhātupūjaṃ akāsahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dhātumabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          dhātupūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhātupūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Dhātupūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ puḷinuppādakattherāpadānaṃ (487)    
[77] pabbate himavantamhi          devalo nāma tāpaso    
tattha me caṅkamo āsi       amanussehi māpito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 112 -->
<a id="page-112"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 112]*

Jaṭābhārassa [^112-1] bharito       kamaṇḍaludharo tadā    
uttamatthaṃ gavesanto         pavanā nikkhamiṃ [^112-2] tadā .

Cullāsītisahassāni           sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ    
sakakammābhipasutā            vasanti pavane tadā .

Assamā abhinikkhamma          akaṃ puḷinacetiyaṃ    
nānāpupphaṃ samānetvā       taṃ cetiyaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       assamaṃ pavisāmahaṃ    
sabbe sissā samāgantvā     etamatthaṃ apucchisuṃ [^112-3] .

Puḷinena kato thūpo          yaṃ tvaṃ deva namassasi    
mayampi ñātumicchāma          puṭṭho ācikkha no tuvaṃ .

Diṭṭhā no [^112-4] vo mantapade   cakkhumanto mahāyasā    
te kho ahaṃ namassāmi        buddhaseṭṭhe mahāyase .

Kīdisā te mahāvīrā         sabbaññū lokanāyakā    
kathaṃvaṇṇā kathaṃsīlā           kīdisā te mahāyasā .

Battiṃsalakkhaṇā buddhā         cattāriva [^112-5] dijāpica    
nettā gopakhumā [^112-6] tesaṃ    jiñjukaphalasannibhā .

Gacchamānā ca te buddhā      yugamattañca pekkhare    
na tesaṃ jāṇu nadati          sandhisaddo na suyyati .

Gacchamānā ca sugatā         aturitāva [^112-7] gacchare    
paṭhamaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ pādaṃ           buddhānaṃ esa dhammatā .

#[^112-1] ma. Yu. Jaṭābhārena . [^112-2] yu. Abhinikkhamiṃ . [^112-3] ma. Yu. Pucchiṃsu maṃ . [^112-4] ma. ... Nu  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 113 -->
<a id="page-113"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 113]*

Asambhītā ca te buddhā       migarājāva kesarī    
nevukkaṃsenti attānaṃ        no ca vambhenti pāṇinaṃ .

Mānātimānato [^113-1] muttā     samā sabbesu pāṇisu    
anattukkaṃsakā buddhā         buddhānaṃ esa dhammatā .

Uppajjantā ca sambuddhā      ālokaṃ dassayanti te    
chabbikāraṃ [^113-2] pakāsenti [^113-3]   kevalaṃ vasudhaṃ imaṃ .

Passanti nirayañcete         nibbāti nirayo tadā    
pavassati mahāmegho          buddhānaṃ esa dhammatā .

Īdisā te mahānāgā        atulā te [^113-4] mahāyasā    
vaṇṇato anatikkantā         appameyyā tathāgatā .

Anumodiṃsu me vākyaṃ         sabbe sissā sagāravā    
tathāva [^113-5] paṭipajjiṃsu         yathāsatti yathābalaṃ .

Paṭipūjenti puḷinaṃ            te [^113-6] sakammābhilāsino    
saddahantā mama vākyaṃ        buddhattagatamānasā [^113-7] .

Tadā cavitvā tusitā         devaputto mahāyaso    
uppajji mātukucchismiṃ         dasasahassi kampatha .

Assamassāvidūramhi           caṅkamamhiṭṭhito ahaṃ    
sabbe sissā samāgantvā     āgacchacha mama santike .

Usabhova mahi nadati           migarājāva kujjati    
sasasumārova saḷati [^113-8]         kiṃ vipāko bhavissati .

#[^113-1] ma. Yu. Mānāvamānato . [^113-2] ma. Chappakāraṃ . [^113-3] yu. Pakampenti . 4-[^113-5] ma. Yu. Ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 114 -->
<a id="page-114"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 114]*

Yaṃ pakittemi sambuddhaṃ         sikatā thūpasantike    
so dāni bhagavā satthā       mātukucchimupāgami .

Tesaṃ dhammakathaṃ katvā [^114-1]      kittayitvā mahāmuniṃ    
uyyojetvā sake sisse     pallaṅke ābhujiṃ ahaṃ .

Balañca vata me khīṇaṃ          byādhiko [^114-2] paramenahaṃ    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Sabbe sissā samāgantvā     akaṃsu citakaṃ tadā    
kalevarañca me gayha         citakaṃ abhiropayuṃ .

Citakaṃ parivāretvā          sīse katvāna añjaliṃ    
sokasallaparetā te         vikandiṃsu samāgatā .

Tesaṃ lālappamānānaṃ         agamaṃ [^114-3] citakantikaṃ    
ahaṃ ācariyo tuyhaṃ          mā socittha sumedhasā .

Sadatthe vāyameyyātha        rattindivamatanditā    
mā vo pamattā ahuttha       khaṇo vo paṭipādito .

Sake sissenusāsitvā        devalokaṃ punāgamiṃ    
aṭṭhārasa [^114-4] ca kappāni      devaloke ahosahaṃ [^114-5] .

Satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
anekasatakkhattuñca           devarajjaṃ akārayiṃ .

Avasesesu kappesu          vokiṇṇaṃ [^114-6] saṃsariṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          uppādassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^114-1] ma. Vatvā . [^114-2] ma. Byādhinā . [^114-3] ma. Agamaṃ citakaṃ tadā . Yu. Agamāsiṃ ... .

#[^114-4] po. Yu. Aṭṭhārasāhaṃ kappāni . [^114-5] ma. Ramāmahaṃ . Yu. Ramiṃ ahaṃ . [^114-6] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 115 -->
<a id="page-115"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 115]*

Yathā komudike māse        bahū pupphanti pādapā    
tathevāhampi samaye          pupphitomhi mahesinā [^115-1] .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ         yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puḷinuppādako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Puḷinuppādakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ taraṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (488)    
[78] atthadassī tu bhagavā          sayambhū lokanāyako    
vinatānadiyā [^115-2] tīre [^115-3]     upagañchi tathāgato .

Udakā abhinikkhamma           kacchapo vārigocaro    
buddhaṃ taritukāmohaṃ [^115-4]        upesiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 116 -->
<a id="page-116"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 116]*

Abhirūhatu maṃ buddho           atthadassī mahāmuni    
ahaṃ taṃ tārayissāmi          dukkhassantaṃ karo tuvaṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          atthadassī mahāyaso    
ārohitvāna me piṭṭhiṃ       aṭṭhāsi lokanāyako .

Yato sarāmi attānaṃ         yato pattosmi viññutaṃ    
sukhaṃ me tādisaṃ natthi         phuṭṭhe [^116-1] pādatale yathā .

Uttaritvāna sambuddho        atthadassī mahāyaso    
nadītīramhi ṭhatvāna           imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yāvatā vattate cittaṃ        gaṅgāsotaṃ tarāmahaṃ    
ayañca kacchapo rājā        tāreti [^116-2] mama puññavā .

Iminā buddhataraṇena          mettacittavatāya ca    
aṭṭhārase kappasate         devaloke ramissati .

Devalokā idhāgantvā       sukkamūlena codito    
ekāsane nisīditvā         kaṅkhāsotaṃ tarissati .

Yathāpi bhaddake khette       bījaṃ appampi ropitaṃ    
sammā dhāraṃ [^116-3] pavassante    phalaṃ toseti kassake [^116-4] .

Tathevidaṃ buddhakhettaṃ          sammāsambuddhadesitaṃ    
sammā dhāraṃ [^116-3] pavacchante    phalaṃ maṃ tosayissati .

Padhānaṃ pahitattomhi          upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^116-1] po. Yu. Yathā pādatale muni . [^116-2] ma. Tāresi . [^116-3] ma. Yu. Dhāre . [^116-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 117 -->
<a id="page-117"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 117]*

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Taraṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ dhammarucittherāpadānaṃ [^117-1] (489)    
[79] yadā dīpaṅkaro buddho        sumedhaṃ byākari jino    
aparimeyye ito kappe      ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Imassa janikā mātā         māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma        ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Padhānaṃ padahitvāna           katvā dukkarakārikaṃ    
assatthamūle sambuddho        bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Upatisso kolito ca         aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko [^117-2]    upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 118 -->
<a id="page-118"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 118]*

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca        aggā hessanti sāvikā    
citto āḷavako ceva        aggā hessantupāsakā .

Khujjuttarā nandamātā        aggā hessantupāsikā    
bodhi imassa dhīrassa          assatthoti pavuccati .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ           asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū           namassanti katañjalī .

Tadāhaṃ māṇavo āsiṃ         megho nāma susikkhito    
sutvā byākaraṇaṃ seṭṭhaṃ       sumedhassa mahāmuni .

Saṃvissaṭṭho bhavitvāna         sumedhe karuṇālaye [^118-1]    
pabbajantañca taṃ vīraṃ          sahā [^118-2] ca anupabbajiṃ .

Saṃvuto pāṭimokkhasmiṃ         indriyesu ca pañcasu    
suddhājīvo sato dhīro        jinasāsanakārako .

Evaṃ viharamānohaṃ           pāpamittena kenaci    
niyojito anācāre         sumaggā paridhaṃsito .

Vitakkavasiko hutvā          sāsanato apakkamiṃ    
pacchā tena kumittena        payutto mātughātanaṃ .

Akariṃnantariyañca [^118-3]          ghātayiṃ duṭṭhamānaso    
tato cuto mahāvīciṃ          upapanno sudāruṇaṃ .

Vinipātaṃ gato santo         saṃsariṃ dukkhito ciraṃ    
na puno addasaṃ dhīraṃ          sumedhaṃ narapuṅgavaṃ .

#[^118-1] ma. Yu. Karuṇāsaye . [^118-2] ma. Sahāva . Yu. Saheva . [^118-3] ma. Akariṃ ānantariyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 119 -->
<a id="page-119"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 119]*

Asmiṃ kappe samuddasmiṃ        maccho āsiṃ timiṅgalo    
disvāhaṃ sāgare nāvaṃ        gocaratthamupāgamiṃ .

Disvā maṃ vāṇijā bhītā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussaruṃ    
gotamoti mahāghosaṃ          sutvā tehi udīritaṃ .

Pubbasaññaṃ saritvāna          tato kālaṃ kato ahaṃ    
sāvatthiyaṃ kule iddhe [^119-1]     jāto brāhmaṇajātiyaṃ .

Āsiṃ dhammaruci nāma          sabbapāpajigucchako    
disvāhaṃ lokapajjotaṃ         jātiyā sattavassiko .

Mahājetavanaṃ gantvā         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
upemi buddhaṃ tikkhattuṃ         rattiyā divasassa ca .

Tadā [^119-2] disvā muni āha     sara [^119-3] dhammarucīti maṃ    
tatohaṃ avacaṃ buddhaṃ           pubbakammaṃ vibhāvitaṃ [^119-4] .

Suciraṃ satapuññalakkhaṇaṃ    
patipubbe na visuddhipaccayā [^119-5]    
ahamajja supekkhanaṃ vata    
tava passāmi nirupamaṃ viggahaṃ .

Suciraṃ vihitaṃ [^119-6] nu te mayā    
sucirakkhena niravisositā [^119-7]    
suciraṃ amalaṃ visodhitaṃ    
nayanaṃ ñāṇamayaṃ mahāmune .

#[^119-1] yu. Ucce . [^119-2] yu. Disvā . [^119-3] ma. Yu. Ciraṃ . [^119-4] ma. Yu. Pabhāvitaṃ . 5  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 120 -->
<a id="page-120"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 120]*

Cirakālasamaṅgito [^120-1] tayā    
avinaṭṭho punantaraṃ ciraṃ    
punarajja samāgato tayā    
nahi nassanti katāni gotama .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammaruci thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Dhammarucittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ sālamaṇḍapiyattherāpadānaṃ (490)    
[80] ajjhogahetvā sālavanaṃ       sukato assamo mama    
sālapupphehi sañchanno        vasāmi pavane tadā .

Piyadassī tu [^120-2] bhagavā        sayambhū aggapuggalo    
vivekakāmo sambuddho        sālavanamupāgami .

Assamā abhinikkhamma          pavanaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
mūlaphalaṃ gavesanto           āhiṇḍāmi vane tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 121 -->
<a id="page-121"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 121]*

Tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
sunisinnaṃ samāpannaṃ           virocantaṃ mahāvane .

Catudaṇḍe ṭhapetvāna         buddhassa upari ahaṃ    
maṇḍapaṃ sukataṃ katvā          sālapupphehi chādayiṃ .

Sattāhaṃ dhārayitvāna         maṇḍapaṃ sālachāditaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ avandahaṃ .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye          vuṭṭhahitvā samādhito [^121-1]    
yugamattaṃ pekkhamāno         nisīdi purisuttamo .

Sāvako varuṇo nāma         piyadassissa satthuno    
vasīsatasahassehi             upagañchi vināyakaṃ .

Piyadassī tu bhagavā           lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā         sitaṃ pātukari jino .

Anuruddho upaṭṭhāko         piyadassissa satthuno    
ekaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā          apucchittha mahāmuniṃ .

Ko nu kho bhagavā hetu       sitakammassa satthuno    
kāraṇe vijjamānamhi         satthā pātukare sitaṃ .

Sattāhaṃ pupphachadanaṃ [^121-2]        yo me dhāresi māṇavo    
tassa kammaṃ saritvāna         sitaṃ pātukariṃ ahaṃ .

Anokāsaṃ [^121-3] na passāmi      yantaṃ [^121-4] puññaṃ vipaccati    
devaloke manusse vā       okāsova na sammati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 122 -->
<a id="page-122"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 122]*

Devaloke vasantassa         puññakammasamaṅgino    
yāvatā parisā tassa         sālachannā bhavissati .

Tattha dibbehi naccehi        gītehi vāditehi ca    
ramissati sadā santo         puññakammasamāhito .

Yāvatā parisā tassa         gandhagandhi bhavissati    
sālassa pupphavasso ca        pavassissati tāvade .

Tato cutoyaṃ manujo          mānusaṃ āgamissati    
idhāpi sālachadanaṃ            sabbakālaṃ dharissati [^122-1] .

Idha naccañca gītañca          sammatāḷasamāhitaṃ    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Uggacchante ca suriye        sālavassampavassati    
puññakammena saṃyuttaṃ          vassati sabbakālikaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya         nibbāyissatināsavo .

Dhammaṃ abhisamentassa          sālachadanaṃ [^122-2] bhavissati    
citake jhāyamānassa          chadanaṃ tattha hessati .

Vipākaṃ kittayitvāna          piyadassī mahāmuni    
parisāya dhammaṃ desesi        tappento dhammavuṭṭhiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 123 -->
<a id="page-123"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 123]*

tiṃsakappāni devesu          devarajjamakārayiṃ    
saṭṭhī ca sattakkhattuṃ ca        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Devalokā idhāgantvā       labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ    
idhāpi sālachadanaṃ            maṇḍapassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ayaṃ pacchimako mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
idhāpi sālachadanaṃ            hessati sabbakālikaṃ .

Mahāmuniṃ tosayitvā          gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
pattompi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ         hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate         yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālamaṇḍapiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .T    
sālamaṇḍapiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 124 -->
<a id="page-124"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 124]*

uddānaṃ    
paṃsukūlaṃ buddhasaññī            bhisado ñāṇakittako    
candanī dhātupūjī ca           puḷinuppādakopica .

Taraṇo dhammaruciko           sālamaṇḍapiyo tathā    
gāthāsatāni dve honti      ūnavīsatimeva ca .

Paṃsukūlavaggo ekūnapaññāsamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 125 -->
<a id="page-125"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 125]*

paññāsamo kiṃkaṇipupphavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tīṇikiṃkaṇipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (491)    
[81] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ           nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           vipassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Tīṇikiṃkaṇipupphāni             paggayha abhiropayiṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhipūjetvā         gacchāmi dakkhiṇāmukho .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇikiṃkaṇipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇikiṃkaṇipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 126 -->
<a id="page-126"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 126]*

Dutiyaṃ paṃsukūlapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (492)    
[82] himavantassa avidūre          udabbalo [^126-1] nāma pabbato    
tatthaddasaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ            dumaggamhi vilambitaṃ .

Tīṇi kiṃkaṇipupphāni            ocinitvānahaṃ tadā    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena       paṃsukūlaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          pūjetvā arahaddhajaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṃsukūlapūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Paṃsukūlapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 127 -->
<a id="page-127"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 127]*

Tatiyaṃ koraṇḍapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (493)    
[83] vanakammiko pure āsiṃ        pitupitāmahenahaṃ [^127-1]    
pasumārena jīvāmi           kusalaṃ me na vijjati .

Mama āsayasāmantā          tisso lokagganāyako    
tīṇi padāni dassesi          anukampāya cakkhumā .

Akkamante [^127-2] pade disvā    tissassa nāma satthuno    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena       pade cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Koraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ    
caṅkoṭakaṃ [^127-3] gahetvāna      padaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
koraṇḍakachavi homi           sappabhāso [^127-4] bhavāmahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ    
#[^127-1] ma. Pitumātumatenahaṃ . [^127-2] ma. Yu. Akkante ca ... . [^127-3] ma. Sakosakaṃ . [^127-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 128 -->
<a id="page-128"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 128]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā koraṇḍapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Koraṇḍapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ kiṃsukapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (494)    
[84] kiṃsukaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā          paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna         ākāse abhipūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti    
itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃsukapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kiṃsukapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 129 -->
<a id="page-129"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 129]*

Pañcamaṃ upaḍḍhadussadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (495)    
[85] padumuttarabhagavato            sujāto nāma sāvako    
paṃsukūlaṃ gavesanto           saṅkāre rathiyā [^129-1] tadā .

Nagare haṃsavatiyā            paresaṃ bhatiko [^129-2] ahaṃ    
upaḍḍhadussaṃ datvāna          sirasā abhivādayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tettiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
sattasattatikhattuñca           cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
upaḍḍhadussadānena           modāmi akutobhayo .

Icchamāno ahaṃ [^129-3] ajja      sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ    
khomadussehi chādeyyaṃ        aḍḍhadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          aḍḍhadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 130 -->
<a id="page-130"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 130]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upaḍḍhadussadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Upaḍḍhadussadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ghatamaṇḍadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (496)    
[86] sucintitaṃ bhagavantaṃ            lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāraññaṃ           vātābādhena pīḷitaṃ .

Disvā cittaṃ pasādetvā      ghatamaṇḍaṃ upānayiṃ    
katattā upacitattā [^130-1] ca     gaṅgā bhāgīrasī ayaṃ .

Mahāsamuddā cattāro        ghataṃ sampajjare mama    
ayañca paṭhavī ghorā          appamāṇā asaṅkhayā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          bhavanti [^130-2] te madhusakkharā    
catuddisā [^130-3] ime rukkhā     pādapā dharaṇīruhā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          kapparukkhā bhavanti te    
paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Ekapaññāsakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ghatamaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 131 -->
<a id="page-131"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 131]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghatamaṇḍadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ghatamaṇḍadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ udakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (497)    
[87] padumuttarabuddhassa            bhikkhusaṅghe anuttare    
pasannacitto sumano          pānighaṭaṃ [^131-1] apūrayiṃ .

Pabbatagge dumagge vā       ākāse vātha bhūmiyaṃ    
yadā pānīyamicchāmi          khippaṃ nibbattate mamaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          udakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anupattā       kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 132 -->
<a id="page-132"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 132]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Udakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ puḷinathūpiyattherāpadānaṃ (498)    
[88] himavantassavidūre            yamako [^132-1] nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ         paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Nārado nāma nāmena        jaṭilo uggatāpano    
catuddasasahassāni            sissā paricaranti maṃ .

Paṭisallīnako santo          evaṃ cintesahantadā    
mahājanā [^132-2] maṃ pūjenti      nāhaṃ pūjemi kiñcanaṃ .

Na me ovādako atthi       vattā koci na vijjati    
anācariyupajjhāyo           vane vāsaṃ upemahaṃ .

Upāsamāno yamakaṃ [^132-3]        garucittaṃ upaṭṭhahe    
so me ācariyo natthi       vanavāso niratthako .

Āyāgaṃ me gavesiyaṃ [^132-4]      garubhāvaniyaṃ tathā    
sāsassayo [^132-5] vasissāmi      na koci garahissati .

Uttānakūlā nadikā          supatitthā manoramā    
susuddhapuḷinākiṇṇā           avidūre mamassamaṃ .

#[^132-1] po. Yu. Samaṅgo . [^132-2] ma. Sabbo jano maṃ pūjeti . Yu. Sabbajano . [^132-3] po.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 133 -->
<a id="page-133"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 133]*

Nadiṃ amarikaṃ nāma            upagantvā [^133-1] ahantadā    
saṅkaḍḍhitvāna [^133-2] puḷinaṃ       akaṃ puḷinacetiyaṃ .

Ye te ahesuṃ sambuddhā      bhavantakaraṇā munī    
tesaṃ etādiso thūpo        taṃ nimittaṃ karomahaṃ .

Karitvā puḷine [^133-3] thūpaṃ       sovaṇṇaṃ māpayiṃ ahaṃ    
soṇṇakiṃkaṇipupphāni           sahasse tīṇi pūjayiṃ .

Sāyaṃ pātaṃ namassāmi         pītijāto [^133-4] katañjalī    
sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ         vandiṃ puḷinacetiyaṃ .

Yadā kilesā jāyanti        vitakkā gehanissitā    
sarāmi sukataṃ thūpaṃ            paccavekkhāmi tāvade .

Upanissāya vihariṃ [^133-5]         satthavāhaṃ vināyakaṃ    
kilese saṃvareyyāsi [^133-6]      na yuttaṃ tava mārisa .

Saha āvajjite thūpe         gāravaṃ hoti me tadā    
kuvitakke vinodesiṃ          nāgo tuttaṭṭito [^133-7] yathā .

Evaṃ viharamānaṃ maṃ           maccurājābhimaddatha    
tattha kālaṃ kato santo       brahmalokaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna          tidive [^133-8] upapajjahaṃ    
asītikkhattuṃ devindo         devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikhattuñca           cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

#[^133-1] ma. Upagantavānahantadā . [^133-2] ma. Yu. Saṃvaḍḍhayitvā . [^133-3] ma. Puḷinaṃ .

#[^133-4] ma. Vedajāto . Yu. Vittijāto . [^133-5] ma. Viharaṃ . [^133-6] ma. Yu. Saṃvaseyyāsi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 134 -->
<a id="page-134"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 134]*

Tesaṃ kiṃkaṇipupphānaṃ           vipākaṃ anubhomahaṃ    
bāvīsatisahassāni            parivārenti maṃ bhave .

Thūpassa pariciṇṇattā          rajojallaṃ na limpati    
gatte sedā na muñcanti      sappabhāso bhavāmahaṃ .

Aho me sukato thūpo        sudiṭṭhāmarikā nadī    
thūpaṃ katvāna puḷinaṃ [^134-1]        pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Kusalaṃ kattukāmena           jantunā sāragāhinā [^134-2]    
natthi khettaṃ akhettaṃ vā      paṭipattiva sārakā [^134-3] .

Yathāpi balavā poso         aṇṇavaṃ taritussaho [^134-4]    
parittaṃ kaṭṭhamādāya          pakkhandeyya mahāsaraṃ .

Imāhaṃ kiṭṭhaṃ [^134-5] nissāya      tarissāmi mahodadhiṃ    
ussāhena viriyena          tareyya udadhiṃ naro .

Tatheva me kataṃ kammaṃ         parittaṃ thokakañca [^134-6] yaṃ    
taṃ [^134-7] kammaṃ upanissāya       saṃsāraṃ samatikkamiṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         sukkamūlena codito    
sāvatthiyaṃ pure jāto        mahāsāle suaddhake .

Saddhā mātāpitā mayhaṃ       buddhassa saraṇaṃ gatā    
ubho diṭṭhasutā [^134-8] ete     anuvattanti sāsanaṃ .

Bodhipappaṭikaṃ gayha           soṇṇathūpaṃ akārayuṃ    
sāyaṃ pātaṃ namassanti         sakyaputtassa sammukhā .

#[^134-1] yu. Pubine . [^134-2] po. Yu. Pāragāminā . [^134-3] ma. Sādhakā . Yu. Sādhikā . [^134-4] ma. Yu.

#taritussahe . [^134-5] po. Ma. Yu. Kaṭṭhaṃ . [^134-6] po. Yu. Thokakañcanaṃ . [^134-7] yu. Kataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 135 -->
<a id="page-135"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 135]*

Uposathamhi divase           soṇṇathūpaṃ vinīharuṃ    
buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ kittentā      tiyāmaṃ vītināmayuṃ .

Samā [^135-1] disvānahaṃ thūpaṃ       sariṃ puḷinacetiyaṃ    
ekāsane nisīditvā         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Bāvīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Gavesamāno taṃ dhīraṃ          dhammasenāpatiddasaṃ    
agārā nikkhamitvāna         pabbajiṃ tassa santike .

Jātiyā sattavassena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho          guṇamaññāya cakkhumā .

Dārakeneva santena         kiriyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ mayā    
kataṃ me karaṇīyajja           sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Sabbaverabhayātīto           sabbasaṅgātigo isi    
sāvako te mahāvīra         soṇṇathūpassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 136 -->
<a id="page-136"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 136]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puḷinathūpiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Puḷinathūpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ naḷakuṭikadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (499)    
[89] himavantassavidūre            hāriko [^136-1] nāma pabbato    
sayambhū nārado nāma         rukkhamūle vasi tadā .

Naḷāgāraṃ karitvāna          tiṇena chādayiṃ ahaṃ    
caṅkamaṃ sodhayitvāna          sayambhussa adāsahaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        naḷakuṭikāya nimmitaṃ    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbiddhaṃ [^136-2]        tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Catuddasesu kappesu          devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
ekasattatikkhattuñca          devarajjaṃ akārayiṃ .

Catuttiṃsatikkhattuñca [^136-3]        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Dhammapāsādamāruyha          suññāgāravarūpagaṃ    
yathicchakāhaṃ vihare           sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          naḷakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^136-1] ma. Hārito . Yu. Bhāriko . [^136-2] ma. Sabbattha ...Mubbedhaṃ . [^136-3] po. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 137 -->
<a id="page-137"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 137]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ    
paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷakuṭikadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Naḷakuṭikadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ piyālaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (500)    
[90] migaluddho pure āsiṃ         vivane [^137-1] vicaraṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ .

Piyālaphalamādāya            buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
puññakhettassa vīrassa         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 138 -->
<a id="page-138"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 138]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piyālaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Piyālaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kiṃkaṇi paṃsukūlañca             varakoraṇḍakiṃsukaṃ    
upaḍḍhadussi ghatado           udakaṃ thūpakārako .

Naḷāgāri ca navamo          piyālaphaladāyako    
satamekañca gāthānaṃ          navakañca taduttari .

Kiṃkaṇipupphavaggo paññāsamo .

Atha vagguddānaṃ    
metteyyavaggo bhaddāli      sakiṃsammajjakopica    
ekavihārī vibhedako         jagati sālapupphiyo .

Naḷamāli paṃsukūlaṃ             kīkaṇipupphiko tathā    
asīti dve ca gāthāyo       catuddasā satāni ca .

Metteyyavaggadasakaṃ pañcamaṃ satakaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 139 -->
<a id="page-139"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 139]*

Ekapaññāso kaṇikāravaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tīṇikaṇikārapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (501)    
[91] sumedho nāma sambuddho       dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
vivekakāmo sambuddho        himavantaṃ upāgami .

Ajjhogahetvā himavantaṃ       aggo kāruṇiko muni    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna         nisīdi purisuttamo .

Vijjādharo tadā āsiṃ        antalikkhacaro ahaṃ    
tisulaṃ sukataṃ gayha            gacchāmi ambare tadā .

Pabbatagge yathā aggi        puṇṇamāyeva candimā    
vane obhāsate buddho       sālarājāva phullito .

Vanato [^139-1] nikkhamitvāna       buddharaṃsābhidhāvare    
naḷaggivaṇṇasaṅkāsā          disvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Vicinaṃ addasaṃ pupphaṃ           kaṇikāraṃ devagandhikaṃ    
tīṇi pupphāni ādāya         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Buddhassa ānubhāvena         tīṇi pupphāni me tadā    
uddhaṃvaṇṭā adhopattā        chāyaṃ kubbanti satthuno .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 140 -->
<a id="page-140"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 140]*

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        kaṇikārīti ñāyati    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbiddhaṃ           tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Sahassakaṇḍaṃ [^140-1] sattageṇḍu [^140-2]  dhajālu haritāmayo    
satasahassaniyyūhā            byamhe pātubhaviṃsu me .

Soṇṇamayā maṇimayā          lohitaṅkamayāpica    
phalikāpica pallaṅkā          yenicchakā yathicchakaṃ .

Mahārahañca sayanaṃ            tulikaṃ [^140-3] vikatissakaṃ    
uddhalomikaekantaṃ           bibbohanasamāyutaṃ .

Bhavanā nikkhamitvāna          caranto devacārikaṃ    
yadā icchāmi gamanaṃ          devasaṅghapurakkhato .

Pupphassa heṭṭhā tiṭṭhāmi      upari chadanaṃ mama    
samantā yojanasataṃ           kaṇikārehi chāditaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni          sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ    
parivārenti maṃ niccaṃ         rattindivamatanditā .

Tattha naccehi gītehi         tāḷehi vāditehi ca    
ramāmi khiḍḍāratiyā          modāmi kāmakāmihaṃ .

Tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca       modāmi tidase tadā    
nārīgaṇehi sahito           modāmi byamhamuttame .

Satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 141 -->
<a id="page-141"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 141]*

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto saṅkhayaṃ    
bhavābhave saṃsaranto          mahābhogaṃ [^141-1] labhāmahaṃ .Cha    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse .

Aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
duve kule pajāyāmi         khattiye cāpi brāhmaṇe .

Nīce kule na jāyāmi [^141-2]     buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ          sivikaṃ sandamānikaṃ .

Labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
dāsīgaṇaṃ dāsagaṇaṃ            nāriyo samalaṅkatā .

Labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
koseyyakambaliyāni          khomakappāsikāni ca .

Labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
navavatthaṃ navaphalaṃ             navaggarasabhojanaṃ .

Labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
ime [^141-3] khāda ime [^141-3] bhuñja  imamhi sayane saya .

Labhāmi sabbamevetaṃ          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ
