sabbattha pūjito homi         yaso accuggato [^141-4] mama .

Mahesakkho [^141-5] sadā homi     abhejjapariso sadā    
ñātīnaṃ uttamo homi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^141-1] yu. Mahābhoge . [^141-2] yu. Jānāmi . [^141-3] ma. Imaṃ . [^141-4] ma. Abbhuggato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 142 -->
<a id="page-142"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 142]*

Sītaṃ uṇhaṃ na jānāmi         pariḷāho na vijjati    
atho cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ          hadaye me na vijjati .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇo hutvāna         saṃsarāmi bhavābhave    
vevaṇṇiyaṃ [^142-1] na jānāmi      buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna         sukkamūlena codito    
sāvatthiyaṃ pure jāto        mahāsāle suaddhake .

Pañcakāmaguṇe hitvā         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
jātiyā sattavassohaṃ         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Upasampādayi buddho          guṇamaññāya cakkhumā    
taruṇo pūjanīyohaṃ            buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dibbacakkhuṃ visuddhaṃ me         samādhikusalo ahaṃ    
abhiññāpāramippatto         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā anuppatto        iddhipādesu kovido    
saddhamme [^142-2] pāramippatto    buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi            yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 143 -->
<a id="page-143"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 143]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇikaṇikārapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇikaṇikārapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ekapattadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (502)    
[92] nagare haṃsavatiyā            kumbhakāro ahosahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Sukataṃ mattikāpattaṃ           buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
pattaṃ datvā bhagavato         ujubhūtassa tādino .

Bhave nibbattamānohaṃ         soṇṇathāle labhāmahaṃ    
rūpimaye ca sovaṇṇe         taṭṭike [^143-1] ca maṇīmaye .

Pāṭiyā [^143-2] paribhuñjāmi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ    
yasasāva [^143-3] janānañca        aggabhūto [^143-4] ca homahaṃ .

Yathāpi bhaddake khette       vījaṃ appampi ropitaṃ    
sammā dhāraṃ pavassante [^143-5]    phalaṃ toseti kassakaṃ .

Tathevidaṃ pattadānaṃ           buddhakhettamhi ropitaṃ    
pītidhāre pavassante         phalaṃ me [^143-6] tosayissati .

Yāvatā khettā vijjanti      saṅghāpica gaṇāpica    
buddhakhettasamo natthi         sukhado [^143-7] sabbapāṇinaṃ .

#[^143-1] po. Yu. Taṭṭake . [^143-2] ma. Yu. Pātiyo . [^143-3] ma. Yu. Yasānañaca dhanānañca .

#[^143-4] yu. Pattabhūto . [^143-5] ma. Yu. Pavacchante . Yu. Sammā dhāre pavecchante . [^143-6] ma. Maṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 144 -->
<a id="page-144"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 144]*

namo te purisājañña         namo te purisuttama    
ekapattaṃ daditvāna          pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ pattamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          pattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santake    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekapattadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekapattadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kāsumārikaphaladāyaka2ttherāpadānaṃ (503)    
[93] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ           nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
kāsumārikamādāya           buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 145 -->
<a id="page-145"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 145]*

kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāsumārikaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kāsumārikaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ avaṭaphaliyattherāpadānaṃ (504)    
[94] sahassaraṃsī bhagavā            sayambhū aparājito    
vivekā vuṭṭhahitvāna         gocarāyābhinikkhami .

Phalahattho ahaṃ disvā         upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          avaṭaṃ adadiṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 146 -->
<a id="page-146"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 146]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā avaṭaphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Avaṭaphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ cāraphaliyattherāpadānaṃ [^146-1] (505)    
[95] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            cāraphalamadāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cāraphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Cāraphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 147 -->
<a id="page-147"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 147]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ mātuluṅgaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (506)    
[96] kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ             puṇṇamāyeva candimaṃ    
jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva            addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Mātuluṅgaphalaṃ gayha           adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ    
dakkhiṇeyyassa vīrassa         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mātuluṅgaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mātuluṅgaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ ajjelaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (507)    
[97] ajino [^147-1] nāma sambuddho     himavante vasi tadā    
caraṇena ca sampanno         samādhikusalo muni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 148 -->
<a id="page-148"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 148]*

Kumbhamattaṃ gahetvāna         añjaliṃ [^148-1] jīvajīvakaṃ    
chattapaṇṇaṃ gahetvāna         adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ajjelaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ajjelaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ amoraphaliyattherāpadānaṃ [^148-2] (508)    
[98] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            amoraṃ [^148-3] adadiṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .8    
kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 149]*

svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā amoraphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Amoraphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ tālaphaliyattherāpadānaṃ (509)    
[99] sataraṃsī nāma bhagavā          sayambhū aparājito    
vivekā vuṭṭhahitvāna         gocarāyābhinikkhami .

Phalahattho ahaṃ disvā         upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano          tālaphalamadāsahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 150]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tālaphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tālaphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ nālikeraphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (510)    
[100] nagare bandhumatiyā           ārāmiko ahaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Nālikeraphalaṃ gayha           buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
ākāse ṭhitako santo       paṭiggaṇhi mahāyaso .

Pītisañjanano [^150-1] mayhaṃ        diṭṭhadhammasukhāvaho    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna         vippasannena cetasā .

Adhigañchiṃ tadā pītiṃ           vipulañca sukhuttamaṃ    
uppajjateva ratanaṃ           nibbattassa tahiṃ [^150-2] tahiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dibbacakkhuṃ visuddhaṃ me         samādhikusalo ahaṃ    
abhiññāpāramippatto         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 151]*

tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nālikeraphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Nālikeraphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kaṇikārañca patto ca         kāsumāri tathāvaṭo    
cāraphali mātuluṅgo          ajjelāmorameva ca .

Tālaṃ tathā nālikeraṃ         gāthāyo gaṇitāpiha    
ekaṃ gāthāsataṃ hoti         ūnādhikavivajjitaṃ .

Kaṇikāravaggo ekapaññāso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 152]*

Dvepaññāso phaladāyakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ kurañjiyaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (511)    
[101] migaluddho pure āsiṃ         vivane vicaraṃ ahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ .

Kurañjiyaphalaṃ gayha            buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
puññakkhettassa dhīrassa [^152-1]     pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kurañjiyaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kurañjiyaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 153]*

Dutiyaṃ kapiṭṭhaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (512)    
[102] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            kapiṭṭhaṃ adadiṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kapiṭṭhaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kapiṭṭhaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kosumbaphaliyattherāpadānaṃ [^153-1] (513)    
[103] kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva             devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            kosumbamadadintadā .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 154]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kosumbaphaliyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kosumbaphaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ketakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (514)    
[104] vinatānadiyā [^154-1] tīre        vihāsi purisuttamo    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ .

Madhugandhassa pupphena          ketakassa ahaṃ tadā    
pasannacitto sumano          buddhaseṭṭhassa pūjayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 155]*

tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ketakapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ketakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ nāgapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (515)    
[105] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ            nāgapupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Nāgapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 156]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ ajjunapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (516)    
[106] candabhāgānadītīre           ahosiṃ kinnaro tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano          vedajāto katañjalī    
gahetvā ajjunapupphaṃ         sayambhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā kinnaraṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjamakārayiṃ    
dasakkhattuṃ cakkavatti          mahārajjamakārayiṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sukhette [^156-1] vappitaṃ vījaṃ      sayambhusmiṃ ahosi [^156-2] me .

Kusalaṃ vijjate mayhaṃ          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
pūjāraho ahaṃ ajja          sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 157]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ajjunapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ajjunapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ kuṭajapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (517)    
[107] himavantassa avidūre          accalo [^157-1] nāma pabbato    
buddho sudassano nāma        vasanto [^157-2] pabbatantare .

Pupphaṃ hemavantaṃ gayha         vehāsaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Pupphaṃ kuṭajamādāya           sire katvānahantadā [^157-3]    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ          sayambhussa mahesino .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭajapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kuṭajapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 158]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ ghosasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (518)    
[108] migaluddho pure āsiṃ         araññe vivane ahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           devasaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Catusaccaṃ pakāsentaṃ          desentaṃ amataṃpadaṃ    
assosiṃ madhuraṃ dhammaṃ          sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ghose cittaṃ pasādesiṃ        asamappaṭipuggale [^158-1]    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       uttariṃ duttaraṃ bhavaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          ghosasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghosasaññako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ghosasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 159]*

Navamaṃ sabbaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (519)    
[109] varuṇo nāma nāmena         brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
chaḍḍetvā dasa puttāni       vanamajjhogahiṃ tadā .

Assamaṃ sukataṃ katvā          suvibhattaṃ manoramaṃ    
paṇṇasālaṃ karitvāna          vasāmi pavane ahaṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamuddharitukāmo so          āgañchi mama assamaṃ .

Yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi          obhāso vipulo ahu    
buddhassa ānubhāvena         pajjali pavanaṃ tadā .

Disvāna pāṭihiriyaṃ [^159-1]        buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino    
pattapuṭaṃ gahetvāna          phalena pūrayiṃ ahaṃ .

Upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ         saha [^159-2] khārimadāsahaṃ    
anukampāya me buddho        idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Khāribhāraṃ gahetvāna         pacchato ehi me tuvaṃ    
paribhutteva [^159-3] saṅghamhi       puññaṃ [^159-4] tava bhavissati .

Puṭakantaṃ gahetvāna          bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Tattha dibbehi naccehi        gītehi vāditehi ca    
puññakammena saṃyutto [^159-5]      anubhomi yasaṃ [^159-6] ahaṃ .

#[^159-1] ma. ...Taṃ pāṭihīraṃ . Yu. Disvānahaṃ pāṭihīraṃ . [^159-2] yu. Sākhārikaṃ adāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 160]*

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yāvatā caturo dīpā         sasamuddā sapabbatā    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna         issaraṃ kārayiṃ [^160-1] ahaṃ .

Yāvatā ye pakkhigaṇā        ākāse upatanti ce [^160-2]    
tepi maṃ vasamanventi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi          yakkhā bhūtā ca rakkhasā    
kumbhaṇḍā garuḷā cāpi        pācariyaṃ [^160-3] upenti me .

Kummā [^160-4] soṇā madhukarā     ḍaṃsā ca makasā ubho    
tepi maṃ vasamanventi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Supaṇṇā nāma sakuṇā         pakkhijātā mahabbalā    
tepi maṃ saraṇaṃ yanti          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yepi dīghāyukā nāgā        iddhimanto mahāyasā    
tepi maṃ vasamanventi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sīhā byagghā ca dīpi ca       acchakokataracchakā    
tepi maṃ vasamanventi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Osadhitiṇavāsī ca            ye ca ākāsavāsino    
sabbe maṃ saraṇaṃ yanti         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sududdasaṃ sunipuṇaṃ             gambhīraṃ supakāsitaṃ    
phussayitvā [^160-5] viharāmi       phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^160-1] ma. Yu. Kārayāmahaṃ . [^160-2] yu. Te . [^160-3] ma. Yu. Pāricariyaṃ . [^160-4] yu. Kumbhasoṇā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 161]*

Vimokkhe aṭṭha phussitvā      viharāmi anāsavo    
ātāpī nipako cāpi [^161-1]      phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ye phalaṭṭhā buddhaputtā       khīṇadosā mahāyasā    
ahaṃ aññataro tesaṃ          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Abhiññāpāramiṃ gantvā        sukkamūlena codito    
sabbāsave pariññāya         viharāmi anāsavo .

Tevijjā iddhipattā ca       buddhaputtā mahāyasā    
dibbasotasamāpannā          tesaṃ aññataro ahaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sabbaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 162]*

Dasamaṃ padumadhāriyattherāpadānaṃ (520)    
[110] himavantassa avidūre          romaso nāma pabbato    
buddhobhisambhavo nāma         abbhokāse vasi [^162-1] tadā .

Bhavanā nikkhamitvāna          padumaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ    
ekāhaṃ dhārayitvāna         bhavanaṃ punarāgamiṃ .

Ekatiṃse ito kappe        yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumadhāriyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Padumadhāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kurañjiyaṃ kapiṭṭhañca           kosumbaṃ atha ketakaṃ    
nāgapupphajjunañceva          kuṭaji ghosasaññako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 163]*

Thero ca sabbaphalado         tathā padumadhāriyo    
asīti cetthe gāthāyo       tisso gāthā taduttari .

Phaladāyakavaggo dvepaññāso .

tepaññāso tiṇadāyakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (521)    
[111] himavantassa avidūre          lambako nāma pabbato    
tattheva [^163-1] tisso sambuddho   abbokāsamhi caṅkami .

Migaluddho pure [^163-2] āsiṃ      araññe kānane ahaṃ    
disvānāhaṃ devadevaṃ         tiṇamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ .

Nisīdanatthaṃ buddhassa           datvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivāditvā         pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Aciraṃ gatamattaṃ [^163-3] maṃ         migarājā aheṭhayi [^163-4]    
sīhena ghāṭito [^163-5] santo     tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Āsanne me kataṃ kammaṃ       buddhaseṭṭhe anāsave    
sumutto saravegova [^163-6]       devalokaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Yūpo tattha subho āsi        puññakammābhinimmito    
sahassakaṇḍo sattageṇḍu       dhajālu haritāmayo .

#[^163-1] yu. Tatthopatisso . [^163-2] yu. Tadā . [^163-3] ma. Yu. Gatamattassa . [^163-4] ma. Apoṭhayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 164]*

Pabhā niddhāvate tassa        sataraṃsīva uggato    
ākiṇṇo devakaññāhi        āmodiṃ kāmakāmihaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna         sukkamūlena codito    
āgantvāna manussattaṃ        pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe        nisīdanamakāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          tiṇamuṭṭhe idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇamuṭṭhidāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ veccakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (522)    
[112] vipassino bhagavato           lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
ekaṃ [^164-1] veccaṃ mayā dinnaṃ    pasannena sapāṇinā .

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ          dibbayānaṃ samajjhagaṃ    
tena veccakadānena [^164-2]      pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 165]*

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ veccamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          veccadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā veccakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Veccakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ saraṇagamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (523)    
[113] āruyhamhi [^165-1] tadā nāvaṃ     bhikkhu cājīviko [^165-2] ahaṃ    
nāvāya bhijjamānāya         bhikkhu me saraṇaṃ adā .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ so me saraṇaṃ adā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          saraṇagamane phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 166]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Saraṇagamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ abbhañjanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (524)    
[114] nagare bandhumatiyā           rājuyyāne vasāmahaṃ    
cammavāsī [^166-1] tathā āsiṃ      kamaṇḍaludharo ahaṃ .

Addasaṃ virajaṃ [^166-2] buddhaṃ        sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
ātāpinaṃ [^166-3] pahitattaṃ        jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ isiṃ [^166-4] .

Sabbakāmasamiddhiñca           oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ    
disvā pasanno sumano        abbhañjanamadāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          abbhañjanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 167]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abbhañjanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Abbhañjanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ supaṭadāyakattherāpadānaṃ [^167-1] (525)    
[115] divāvihārā nikkhanto        vipassī lokanāyako    
lahuṃ supaṭikaṃ [^167-2] datvā        kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ supaṭikamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          supaṭassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ baddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supaṭadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Supaṭadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ daṇḍadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (526)    
[116] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha          veḷaḷa chetvānahaṃ tadā    
ālambanaṃ karitvāna          saṅghassa adadiṃ ahaṃ [^167-3] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 168]*

Tena cittappasādena         subbate abhivādayiṃ [^168-1]    
ālambadaṇḍaṃ datvāna         pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ daṇḍamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          daṇḍadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā daṇḍadāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Daṇḍadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tevīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Sattamaṃ girinelapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (527)    
[117] migaluddho pure āsiṃ         vivane vicaraṃ ahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ .

Tasmiṃ mahākāruṇike          sabbasattahite rate    
pasannacitto sumano          nelapupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 169]*

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā girinelapūjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Girinelapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ bodhisammajjakattherāpadānaṃ (528)    
[118] ahaṃ pure bodhipattaṃ          ujjhitaṃ cetiyaṅgaṇe    
taṃ gahetvāna chaḍḍesiṃ        alabhiṃ vīsatiṃ guṇe .

Tassa kammassa tejena        saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte cāpi mānuse .

Devalokā cavitvāna         āgantvā mānusaṃ bhavaṃ    
duve kule pajāyāmi         khattiye cāpi brāhmaṇe .

Aṅgapaccaṅgasampanno         ārohapariṇāhavā    
abhirūpo suci homi           sampuṇṇako [^169-1] anūnako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 170]*

Devaloke manusse vā       jāto vā yattha katthaci    
bhave suvaṇṇavaṇṇo ca         uttattakanakūpamo .

Mudu [^170-1] maddavā siniddhā      sukhumā sukumārikā    
chavi me sabbadā hoti        bodhipatte suchaḍḍite .

Yato kutoci gatīsu           sarīre samudāgate    
na limpati rajojallaṃ          vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Uṇhe vātātape tassa       aggitāpena vā pana [^170-2]    
gatte sedā na muñcanti      vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍo kilāso ca       tilakā piḷakā tathā    
na honti kāye daddu ca      vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
rogā na honti kāyasmiṃ      vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
na honti cittajā pīḷā       vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
amittā na bhavantassa         vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
anūnabhogo bhavati            vipāko pattachaḍḍite .

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
aggirājūhi corehi          na hoti udakā [^170-3] bhayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 171]*

Aparampi guṇantassa           nibbattati bhavābhave    
dāsī dāsā anucarā         honti cittānuvattakā .

Yamhi āyuppamāṇamhi         jāyate mānuse bhave    
tato na hāyate āyu        tiṭṭhate yāvatāyukaṃ .

Abbhantarā ca [^171-1] bāhirā     negamā ca saraṭṭhakā    
anuyuttā honti sabbepi      vuddhikāmā sukhicchakā .

Bhogavā yasavā homi         sirimā ñātipakkhavā    
avero [^171-2] gatasantāso      bhavehi [^171-3] sabbadā bhave .

Devā manussā asurā        gandhabbā yakkharakkhasā    
sabbe te parirakkhanti        bhave saṃsarato sadā .

Devaloke manusse vā [^171-4]    anubhotvā ubho yase    
avasāne ca nibbānaṃ         sivaṃ patto anuttaraṃ .

Sambuddhamuddisitvāna          bodhiṃ vā tassa satthuno    
yo puññaṃ pasave poso       tassa kiṃ nāma dullabhaṃ .

Magge phale ca āgame       jhānābhiññāguṇesu ca    
aññesaṃ adhiko hutvā        nibbāyati [^171-5] anāsavo .

Purehaṃ bodhiyā pattaṃ         chaḍḍetvā haṭṭhamānaso    
imehi vīsataṅgehi           samaṅgī homi sabbadā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^171-1] yu. Bahicarā . [^171-2] ma. Yu. Apetabhayasantāso . [^171-3] yu. Bhavesaṃ sabbato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 172]*

svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bodhisammajjako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bodhisammajjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ āmaṇḍaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (529)    
[119] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā        caṅkami lokanāyako .

Khāribhāraṃ gahetvāna         āharanto phalaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           caṅkamantaṃ mahāmuniṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano          sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā        āmaṇḍamadadiṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          āmaṇḍassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       vīharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 173]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āmaṇḍaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Āmaṇḍaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ sugandhattherāpadānaṃ (530)    
[120] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena [^173-1]     uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Anubyañjanasampanno          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
byāmappabhāparivuto          raṃsijālasamutthato .

Assāsetā yathā cando      suriyo viya pabhaṅkaro    
nibbāpetā yathā megho      sāgarova guṇākaro .

Dharaṇīriva sīlena             himavāva samādhinā    
ākāso viya paññāya        asaṅgo anilo yathā .

Anūno [^173-2] yathāvihāro       parisāsu visārado    
saccāni sampakāseti         uddharanto mahājanaṃ .

Tadāhaṃ [^173-3] bārāṇasiyaṃ        seṭṭhiputto mahāyaso    
āsabhaṃ [^173-4] dhanadhaññassa        pahutassa bahū mamaṃ [^173-5] .

Jaṅghavihāraṃ vicaraṃ            migadāyaṃ upeccahaṃ    
addasaṃ nāthasatthāraṃ [^173-6]       desentaṃ amataṃ padaṃ .

#[^173-1] ma. Gottena . [^173-2] ma. Yu. Sakadāci mahāvīro . [^173-3] ma. Yu. Tadā hi . [^173-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 174]*

Visaṭṭhakantavacanaṃ             karavikasamassaraṃ    
haṃsadundubhinigghosaṃ [^174-1]         viññāpentaṃ mahājanaṃ .

Disvā devātidevantaṃ        sutvā ca madhuraṃ saraṃ [^174-2]    
pahāyānappake bhoge        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Evaṃ pabbajitovāhaṃ          na cirena bahussuto    
ahosiṃ dhammakathiko           vicittapaṭibhāṇavā .

Mahāparisamajjhehaṃ            satthunova [^174-3] punappunaṃ    
vaṇṇayiṃ hemavaṇṇassa          vaṇṇaṃ vaṇṇavisārado .

Esa khīṇāsavo buddho        anīgho chinnasaṃsayo    
sabbakammakkhayaṃ patto         vimutto [^174-4] upadhikkhaye    
esa so bhagavā buddho       esa sīho anuttaro .

Sadevakassa lokassa          brahmacakkaṃ pavattako    
danto dametā santo ca      sametā nibbuto isi    
nibbāpetā ca assattho      assāsetā mahājanaṃ .

Vīro sūro ca vikkanto [^174-5]    pañño kāruṇiko vasī    
vijitāvī vijito [^174-6] ca        appagabbho anālayo .

Aneñjo acalo dhīro        amoho asamo muni    
dhorayho usabho nāgo       sīho sakko garūsupi .

Virāgo vimalo brahmā       vādīsūro raṇañjaho    
akhilo ca visallo ca         asamo vusabho suci .

#[^174-1] ma. Haṃsarutehi nigghosaṃ . [^174-2] ma. Yu. Giraṃ . [^174-3] ma. Yu. Haṭṭhacitto . [^174-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 175]*

Brāhmaṇo samaṇo nātho      bhisakko sallasandhuto [^175-1]    
yodho buddho sutāsuto       acalo mudito sito .

Katadantā [^175-2] vahattā ca      kattā netā pakāsitā    
sampahaṃsitā settā [^175-3] ca     chettā sotā pasaṃsitā .

Avilo [^175-4] ca visallo ca      anīgho akathaṃkathī    
anejo virajo khattā        gandhā vattā [^175-5] pakāsitā .

Tāretā atthakāretā [^175-6]    kāretā sampadāritā    
pāpetā sahitā hantā [^175-7]    tāpitā [^175-8] ca visositā .

Saccaṭṭhitosamasamo [^175-9]        asahāyo dayālayo    
accheramanto 10 akuho     katāvī isi sattamo .

Nitiṇṇakaṅkho nimmāno        appameyyo anūpamo    
sabbavākyapathātīto          sabbaneyyantito 1[^175-1] jino .

Sataraṃsīvare 1[^175-2] tasmiṃ        pasādo amatāvaho    
tasmā buddhe ca dhamme ca     saṅghe saddhā mahatthikā .

Guṇehi evamādīhi           tilokasaraṇuttamaṃ    
vaṇṇento parisamajjhe        kathiṃ dhammakathaṃ ahaṃ .

Tato cutohaṃ tusite          anubhotvā mahāsukhaṃ    
tato cuto manussesu         jāto homi sugandhiko .

Nissāso mukhagandho ca        dehagandho tatheva me    
so 1[^175-3] ca gandhova satataṃ     sabbagandhova hoti me .

#[^175-1] ma. Yu. Sallakattako . [^175-2] ma. Dhātā dhatā ca santi ca . Yu. Tātā tantā ca hantā ca .

#[^175-3] ma. Bhettā . Yu. Bhottā . [^175-4] ma. Yu. Akhilo . [^175-5] yu. Mettā .

#[^175-6] po. Yu. ... Atha tāretā . [^175-7] ma. Kantā . [^175-8] ma. Yu. Hantā ātāpī tāpaso .

#[^175-9] ma. Yu. Samacitto . 10 ma. Accherasatto . Yu. Accherasanto .

#1[^175-1] ma. Saccaneyyantagū . Yu. Saccaneyyantago . 1[^175-2] ma. Yu. Sattasāravare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 176]*

Mukhagandho sadā mayhaṃ         padumuppalacampako    
atikanto [^176-1] sadā vāti      sarīro ca tatheva me .

Guṇatthavassa sabbantaṃ          phalantaṃ paramabbhutaṃ    
ekaggamanasā sabbe         bhāsitassa [^176-2] suṇātha me .

Guṇaṃ buddhassa vatvāna         hitāyanaṃ [^176-3] sukhāvahaṃ    
sucitto [^176-4] homi sabbattha     saṅgho vīrasamāsuto [^176-5] .

Yasassī sukhito kanto         jutimā piyadassano    
vattā aparibhūto ca          niddoso paññavā tathā .

Khīṇe pāsusi [^176-6] nibbānaṃ      sulabhaṃ buddhabhattinaṃ [^176-7]    
tesaṃ hetuṃ pavakkhāmi         taṃ suṇātha yathātathaṃ .

Santaṃ yasaṃ bhagavato           vicinā [^176-8] abhivādayaṃ    
yattha [^176-9] yatthupapannopi       yasassī tena homahaṃ .

Dukkhassantakaraṃ buddhaṃ          dhammaṃ santaṃ asaṅkhataṃ    
vaṇṇayaṃ sukhado āsiṃ          sattānaṃ sukhito tato .

Guṇaṃ vadanto buddhassa         buddhapītisamāyuto    
sakanti 10 parakanti ca       āsiṃ tena ca kantimā .

Jino yo 1[^176-1] titthikātiṇṇo   abhibhuyya kutitthiye    
guṇaṃ vadanto thomesiṃ 1[^176-2]     nāyakaṃ jutimā tato .

Piyaṅkāriṃ janassāpi          sambuddhassa guṇaṃ vadaṃ    
saradiva sasaṅkohaṃ            tenāsiṃ piyadassano .

#[^176-1] ma. Parisanto ... . Yu. Atisanto . [^176-2] ma. Yu. Vaṇṇayissaṃ . [^176-3] ma. Hitāya ca na    
#sadisaṃ . Yu. ... Janasandhisu . [^176-4] ma. Yu. Sukhito . [^176-5] ma. Vīrasamāyuto . Yu. Rasaddho    
#visamāyuto . [^176-6] ma. Āyusi . Yu. Khīṇevāyupi . [^176-7] ma. Ya. Buddhabhattino . [^176-8] ma. Yu.

#vidhinā . [^176-9] ma. Tattha tatthūpapannopi . 10 ma. Yu. Sakantiṃ parakantiñca janayiṃ ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 177]*

Yathāsattivasenāhaṃ           sabbavācāhi santhaviṃ    
sugataṃ tena vaṅgīso          vicittapaṭibhāṇavā .

Ye bālā vimatiṃ pattā       paribhonti mahāmuniṃ    
niggahiṃ te sadhammena         paribhūtena tenahaṃ .

Buddhavaṇṇehi sattānaṃ         kilese apanesahaṃ    
nikkilesamano homi          tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Sotūnaṃ buddhimajaniṃ            buddhānussatidesako    
tenāpicāsiṃ [^177-1] sappañño     nipuṇatthavipassako .

Sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo           tiṇṇasaṃsārasāgaro    
vasī [^177-2] ca anupādāno       pāpuṇissāmi nibbutiṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          yamahaṃ santhaviṃ jinaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sugandho thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sugandhattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 178]*

uddānaṃ    
tiṇado veccado ceva        saraṇabbhañjanampado    
supaṭo daṇḍadāyī ca          nelapūjī tatheva ca .

Bodhisammajjakomaṇḍo         sugandho dasamo dijo    
gāthāsataṃ satevīsaṃ           gaṇitaṃ cettha sabbaso .

Tiṇadāyakavaggo tepaññāso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 179]*

Catupaññāsamo kaccāyanavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ mahākaccāyanattherāpadānaṃ (531)    
[121] padumuttaro nāma jino        anejo ajitañjino [^179-1]    
satasahasse kappānaṃ          ito uppajji nāyako .

Vīro kamalapattakkho          sasaṅkavimalānano    
kañcanatacasaṅkāso [^179-2]        ravidittisamappabho .

Sattanettamanoharī [^179-3]        varalakkhaṇabhūsito    
sabbavākyapathātīto          manujāmarasakkato .

Sambuddho bodhayaṃ satte       vātiho [^179-4] madhurassaro    
karuṇānaddhasantāno          parisāsu visārado .

Deseti madhuraṃ dhammaṃ          catusaccupasaṃhitaṃ    
nimugge mohapaṅkamhi         samuddharati pāṇino .

Tadā ekacaro hutvā        tāpaso himavālayo    
nabhasā mānusaṃ lokaṃ          gacchanto jinamaddasaṃ .

Upecca santikaṃ tassa         assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ    
vaṇṇayantassa dhīrassa          sāvakassa mahāguṇaṃ .

Saṅkhittena mayā vuttaṃ        vitthārena pakāsayaṃ    
parisaṃ mañca toseti          yathā kaccāyano ayaṃ .

#[^179-1] ma ajitaṃ jayo . Yu. Ajitañjayo . [^179-2] ma. Kanakācalasaṅkāso . [^179-3] ma. Yu. ...Hārī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 180]*

Nāhaṃ evamidhekaccaṃ          aññaṃ passāmi sāvakaṃ    
tasmā tadagge esaggo      evaṃ dhāretha bhikkhavo .

Tadāhaṃ vimhito hutvā        vākyaṃ sutvā manoramaṃ    
himavantaṃ gamitvāna           āhitvā pupphasañcayaṃ .

Pūjetvā lokasaraṇaṃ          taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ    
tadā mamāsayaṃ ñatvā         byākāsi [^180-1] saraṇālayo .

Passathetaṃ isivaraṃ            niddhantakanakattacaṃ    
udaggalomamānasaṃ [^180-2]         acalaṃ pañjaliṃ ṭhitaṃ .

Hāsaṃ supuṇṇanayanaṃ            buddhavaṇṇagatāsayaṃ    
dhammajaṃ [^180-3] uggahadayaṃ         amatāsittasannibhaṃ .

Kaccānassa guṇaṃ sutvā        taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ [^180-4] ṭhito    
anāgatamhi addhāne         gotamassa mahāmune .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
kaccāno nāma nāmena       hessati satthusāvako .

Bahussuto mahāñāṇī          adhippāyavidū muni [^180-5]    
pāpuṇissati taṃ ṭhānaṃ          yathāyaṃ byākato mayā .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .O    
duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse    
aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi [^180-6]      buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^180-1] ma. ... Saraṇañjaho . [^180-2] ma. Udaggalomaṃ pīṇaṃsaṃ . Yu. ... Pīṇasaṃ . [^180-3] yu.

#dhammapaṭiggahavaraṃ . [^180-4] ma. Yu. Patthayaṃ . [^180-5] po. Ma. Yu. Mune . [^180-6] ma. Yu. Na  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 181]*

Duve kule pajānāmi         khattiye cāpi brāhmaṇe    
nīce kule na jāyāmi        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave jāto       ujjeniyaṃ pure rame    
pajjotassa sacaṇḍassa [^181-1]      purohitanujātino [^181-2] .

Putto tipitivacchassa [^181-3]       nipuṇo vedapāragū    
mātā ca candanapadumā [^181-4]     kaccānohaṃ varattaco .

Vīmaṃsanatthaṃ buddhassa           bhūmipālena pesito    
disvā mokkhapuradvāraṃ        nāyakaṃ guṇasañcayaṃ .

Sutvāna vimalaṃ vākyaṃ         gatipaṅkavisodhanaṃ    
pāpuṇiṃ amataṃ santaṃ           sesehi [^181-5] saha sattahi .

Adhippāyavidū jāto          sugatassa mahāmate    
ṭhapito etadagge ca         susamiddhamanoratho .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākaccāyano thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mahākaccāyanattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^181-1] po. Yu. Acaṇḍassa . [^181-2] ma. Purohitadijādhino . [^181-3] ma. Yu. Tiriṭivacchassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 182]*

Dutiyaṃ vakkalittherāpadānaṃ (532)    
[122] ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako    
anomanāmo amito          nāmena padumuttaro .

Padumākāravadano            padumāmalasucchavī    
lokenānūpalitto ca         toyena padumaṃ yathā .

Dhīro padumapattakkho          kanto ca padumaṃ yathā    
padumuttaragandho ca           tasmā so padumuttaro .

Lokajeṭṭho ca nimmāno      andhānaṃ nayanūpamo    
santaveso guṇanidhi           karuṇāmatisāgaro .

Sa kadāpi mahāvīro          brahmāsurāsuraacchito [^182-1]    
sadevamanujākiṇṇe [^182-2]        janamajjhe januttamo [^182-3] .

Vadanena sugandhena           madhurena sutena [^182-4] ca    
rañjayaṃ parisaṃ sabbaṃ           santhavi sāvakaṃ sakaṃ .

Saddhādhimutto sumati          mama dassanasālayo    
natthi etādiso añño       yathāyaṃ bhikkhu vakkali .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            nagare brāhmaṇassatrajo    
hutvā sutvā ca taṃ vākyaṃ     taṇṭhānaṃ abhirocayiṃ .

Sasāvakantaṃ vimalaṃ            nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
sattāhaṃ bhojayitvāna         dussehi chādayiṃ tadā .

#[^182-1] ma. Brahmāsurasuraccito . Yu. Brahmāmarasuraccito . [^182-2] yu. Sadevamanujākiṇṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 183]*

Nipacca sirasā tassa          anantaguṇasāgare    
nimuggo pītisampuṇṇo         imaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Yo so tayā santhavito       idha [^183-1] saddhādhimutto isi    
bhikkhu saddhāvataṃ aggo        tādisohaṃ [^183-2] mahāmuni .

Evaṃ vutte mahāvīro        anāvaraṇadassano    
imaṃ vākyaṃ udīrayi [^183-3]        parisāya mahāmuni .

Passathetaṃ māṇavakaṃ           pītamaṭṭhanivāsanaṃ    
hemayaññopacitaṅgaṃ           jananettamanoharaṃ .

Eso anāgataddhāne        gotamassa mahesino    
aggo saddhādhimuttānaṃ        sāvakoyaṃ bhavissati .

Devabhūto manusso vā        sabbasantāpavajjito    
sabbabhogaparibyuḷho          sukhito saṃsarissati .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
vakkali nāma nāmena         hessati satthusāvako .

Tena kammavisesena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Sabbattha sukhito hutvā        saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
sāvatthiyaṃ pure jāto        kule aññatare ahaṃ .

#[^183-1] ma. Ito sattamake muni . Yu. ...Sattamakehani . [^183-2] ma. Yu. Tādiso homahaṃ mune .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 184]*

Nonitaṃ sukhumālaṃ maṃ           ṭhitaṃ [^184-1] pallavakomalaṃ    
mātā [^184-2] uttānasayanaṃ       pisācabhayatajjitā .

Pādamūle mahesissa          sayāpesi [^184-3] dinamānasā    
imaṃ dadāmi te nātha         saraṇaṃ hohi nāyaka .

Tadā paṭiggahi so maṃ         bhītānaṃ saraṇo muni    
jālinā cakkaṅkitena [^184-4]      mudukomalapāṇinā .

Tadā pabhūti jātohaṃ [^184-5]       arakkheyyena rakkhito    
sabbabyādhivinimutto [^184-6]       sukhena parivutthito [^184-7] .

Sugatena vinābhūto           ukkaṇṭhāmi muhuttakaṃ    
jātiyā sattavassohaṃ         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Sabbapāramisambhūtaṃ            nīlakkhinayanaṃ varaṃ [^184-8]    
rūpaṃ sabbaṃ subhākiṇṇaṃ          atitto viharāmahaṃ [^184-9] .

Buddharūparatiṃ ñatvā           tadā ovadi maṃ jino    
alaṃ vakkali kiṃ rūpe          ramase bālanandite .

Yo hi passati saddhammaṃ        so maṃ passati paṇḍito    
apassamāno saddhammaṃ         maṃ passaṃpi na passati .

Anantādīnavo kāyo         visarukkhasamūpamo    
āvāso sabbarogānaṃ        puñjo dukkhassa kevalo .

Nibbindiya tato rūpe         khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ    
passaṃ 10 sabbakilesānaṃ      sukhenantaṃ gamissasi .

#[^184-1] ma. Yu. Jātapallavakomalaṃ . [^184-2] ma. Yu. Mandaṃ . [^184-3] ma. Sāyesuṃ . Yu. Sāyeyyuṃ .

#[^184-4] yu. Saṅkhalaṅkena . [^184-5] ma. Yu. Tenāhaṃ . [^184-6] ma. Sabbaveravinimutto . Yu.

#sabbūpadhivinimutto . [^184-7] ma. Parivuddhito . Yu. Parivaḍḍhito . [^184-8] yu. Paraṃ . [^184-9] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 185]*

Evaṃ tenānusiṭṭhohaṃ         nāyakena hitesinā    
gijjhakūṭaṃ samāruyha           jhāyāmi girikandare [^185-1] .

Ṭhito pabbatapādamhi          assāsayaṃ [^185-2] mahāmuni    
vakkalīti jināvoca [^185-3]        taṃ sutvā mudito ahaṃ .

Pakkhandiṃ selapabbhāre        anekasataporise    
tadā buddhānubhāvena         sukheneva mahiṃ gato .

Punāpi dhammaṃ deseti         khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ    
tamahaṃ dhammamaññāya           arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Sumahāpurisamajjhe            tadā maṃ caraṇantako [^185-4]    
aggaṃ saddhādhimuttānaṃ         paññāpeti mahāmati .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vakkali thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vakkalittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^185-1] yu. Giriniddare . [^185-2] ma. Assāsayi . Po. Mamahāsaṃ . Yu. Mamāhasa . [^185-3] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 186]*

Tatiyaṃ mahākappinattherāpadānaṃ (533)    
[123] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
udito ajatākāse [^186-1]       raviva saradambare .

Vacanābhāya bodheti          veneyyapadumāni so    
kilesapaṅkaṃ soseti          matiraṃsīhi nāyako .

Titthiyānaṃ yase [^186-2] hanti      vajiratā [^186-3] yathā ravi    
sabbattha [^186-4] sampakāseti      rattindivaṃ [^186-5] divākaro .

Guṇānaṃ āyatibhūto           ratanānaṃva sāgaro    
pajunnoriva [^186-6] bhūtānaṃ        dhammameghena vassati .

Akkhadasso tadā āsiṃ        nagare haṃsasavhaye    
upecca dhammamassosiṃ         jalajuttamanāmino .

Ovādakassa bhikkhūnaṃ          sāvakassa satāvino [^186-7]    
guṇaṃ pakāsayantassa           hāsayantassa [^186-8] me manaṃ .

Sutvā pītito [^186-9] sumano      nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
sasissaṃ bhojayitvāna          taṇṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Tadā haṃsasamabhāgo           haṃsadundubhinissano 10    
passathetaṃ mahāmattaṃ          vinicchayavisāradaṃ .

Mama pādamūle patitaṃ          samuddhaggatanuruhaṃ 1[^186-1]    
jūmuttavaṇṇaṃ 1[^186-2] ruciraṃ        pasannayanānanaṃ .

#[^186-1] yu. Jaradākāse . [^186-2] ma. Yu. Yaso . [^186-3] yu. Khajjotābhā . [^186-4] ma. Saccatthābhaṃ .

#[^186-5] ma. Yu. Ratanaṃ va . [^186-6] yu. Pajunnopiva bhūtāni . [^186-7] ma. Yu. Katāvino . [^186-8] mA.Ja    
#tappayantassa . Yu. Vāsayantassa . [^186-9] ma. Yu. Patīto . 10 yu. ...Nīvaco .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 187]*

Parivārena mahatā           rājayuttaṃ mahāyasaṃ    
eso kathāvino ṭhānaṃ        pattheti muditāya [^187-1] so .

Iminā piṇḍapātena          cāgena paṇidhīhi ca    
kappasatasahassāni            nupapajjati duggatiṃ .

Devesu devasobhāgyaṃ        manussesu mahaggataṃ    
anubhotvāvasesena          nibbānaṃ pāpuṇissati .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
kappino nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Tatohaṃ sukataṃ kāraṃ           katvāna jinasāsane    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Devamanussarajjāni           suttaso anusāsiya    
bārāṇasīsamāsanne          jāto keṇiyajātiyā .

Satasahassaparivāro           sapajāpatiko ahaṃ    
pañcapaccekabuddhānaṃ          satāni samupaṭṭhahiṃ .

Temāsaṃ bhojayitvāna         acchādampi [^187-2] ticīvaraṃ    
tato cutā mayaṃ sabbe        ahumha tidasūpagā .

Puno sabbe manussattaṃ        āgatamha tato cutā    
kukkuṭamhi pure jātā        himavantassa passato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 188]*

Kappino nāmahaṃ āsiṃ         rājaputto mahāyaso    
sesāmaccakule jātā        mameva parivārayuṃ .

Mahārajjasukhaṃ patto          sabbakāmasamiddhimo [^188-1]    
vāṇijehi samakkhātaṃ          buddhuppādamahaṃ suṇiṃ .

Buddho loke samuppanno      asamo ekapuggalo    
so pakāseti saddhammaṃ        amataṃ sukhamuttamaṃ .

Suyuttā tassa sissā ca       sumuttā ca anāsavā    
sutvā nesaṃpi [^188-2] vacanaṃ       sakkaritvāna vāṇije .

Pahāya [^188-3] rajjaṃ sāmacco     nikkhamiṃ buddhamāmako    
nadiṃ disvā mahācandaṃ         pūritaṃ samatittikaṃ .

Appatitthaṃ anālambaṃ          duttaraṃ sīghabāhiniṃ    
guṇaṃ saritvā buddhassa         sotthinā samatikkamiṃ .

Bhavasotaṃ sace buddho         tiṇṇo lokantagū vidū    
etena saccavajjena         gamanaṃ me samijjhatu .

Yadi santigamo maggo         mokkhadaṃ [^188-4] santikaṃ sukhaṃ    
etena saccavajjena         gamanaṃ me samijjhatu .

Saṅgho ce tiṇṇakantāro      puññakhetto anuttaro    
etena saccavajjena         gamanaṃ me samijjhatu .

Saha kate saccavare          maggā apagataṃ jalaṃ    
tato sukhena uttiṇṇo        nadītīre manorame .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 189]*

Nisinnaṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ          udentaṃva pabhaṅkaraṃ    
jalantaṃ hemaselaṃva           dīparukkhaṃva jotitaṃ .

Sasiṃva [^189-1] tārāsahitaṃ         sāvakehi purakkhataṃ    
vāsavaṃ viya vassantaṃ          devena [^189-2] jananandanaṃ .

Vanditvāna sahāmacco        ekamantaṃ upāvisiṃ    
tato [^189-3] ajjhāsayaṃ ñatvā     buddho dhammamadesayi .

Sutvāna dhammaṃ vimalaṃ          avocumha mayaṃ jinaṃ    
pabbājehi mahāvīra          otiṇṇamha [^189-4] bhave mayaṃ .

Svākkhāto bhikkhave dhammo    dukkhantassa [^189-5] karāya vo    
caratha brahmacariyaṃ            iccāha muni sattamo .

Saha vācāya sabbepi         bhikkhuvesadharā mayaṃ    
ahumha upasampannā          sotāpannāva sāsane .

Tato jetavanaṃ gantvā        anusāsi vināyako    
anusiṭṭho jinenāhaṃ          arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Tato bhikkhusahassāni          anusāsimahaṃ tadā    
mamānusāsanakarā            tepi  āsuṃ anāsavā .

Jino tasmiṃ guṇe tuṭṭho       etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ    
bhikkhuovādakānaggo         kappinoti mahājane [^189-6] .

Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ         phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto saravegova          kilese jhāpayiṃ mama .

#[^189-1] po. Sasaṃva . Yu. Sasīva . [^189-2] ma. Desanā jaladantaraṃ . Yu. Desanā jalanandanaṃ .

#[^189-3] ma. Yu. Tato no āsayaṃ . [^189-4] ma. Nibbindāmha . Yu. Nibbiṇṇāmha . [^189-5] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 190]*

kalesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākappino thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mahākappinattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ dabbamallaputtattherāpadānaṃ (534)    
[124] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbalokavidū muni    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji cakkhumā .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 191]*

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ             seṭṭhiputto mahāyaso    
upetvā lokapajjotaṃ        assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ .

Senāsanāni bhikkhūnaṃ          paññāpentaṃ sasāvakaṃ    
kittayantassa vacanaṃ           suṇitvā mudito ahaṃ .

Adhikāraṃ sasaṅghassa           katvā tassa mahesino    
nipacca sirasā pāde         taṇṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Tadā [^191-1] hi so mahāvīro     mama kammaṃ pakittayi    
yoyaṃ sasaṅghaṃ bhojesi         sattāhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Soyaṃ kamalapattakkho          sīhaṃso kanakattaco    
mama pādamūle nipati [^191-2]       patthayaṃ [^191-3] ṭhānamuttamaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Sāvako tassa buddhassa        dabbo nāmena vissuto    
senāsanapaññāpako          aggo hessatiyaṃ tathā [^191-4] .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca          devarajjamakārayiṃ    
tathā [^191-5] pañcasatakkhattuṃ       cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

#[^191-1] yu. Tadāhaṃ sa . [^191-2] yu. Patito . [^191-3] yu. Patthayi . [^191-4] ma. Yu. Tadā . [^191-5] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 192]*

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sabbattha sukhito āsiṃ         tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Ekanavute ito kappe       vipassī nāma nāyako    
uppajji cārunayano [^192-1]       sabbadhammavipassako .

Duṭṭhacitto upavadiṃ           sāvakaṃ tassa tādino    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇaṃ            suddhoti ca vijāniya .

Tasseva naravīrassa           sāvakānaṃ mahesinaṃh    
salākaṃ ca gahetvāna         khīrodanamadāsahaṃ .

Imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Sāsanaṃ jotayitvāna [^192-2]       abhibhuyya kutitthiye    
veneyye vinayitvāna        nibbuto so sasāvako .

Sasisse nibbute nāthe       atthamentamhi sāsane    
devā kandiṃsu saṃviggā        muttakesā rudamukhā .

Nibbāyissati dhammakkho        na passissāma subbate    
na suṇissāma saddhammaṃ         aho no appapuññatā .

Tadāyaṃ paṭhavī sabbā          acalā sā pulāpulī [^192-3]    
sāgaro ca sasokova         vinadī karuṇaṃ giraṃ .

Catuddisā dundubhiyo          nādayiṃsu amānusā    
samantato asaniyo           patiṃsu [^192-4] ca bhayāvahā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 193]*

Ukkā patiṃsu nabhasā          dhūmaketu padissatha [^193-1]    
sadhūmā [^193-2] jālavaṭṭā ca      raviṃsu karuṇaṃ migā .

Uppāde dāruṇe disvā      sāsanatthaṅgasūcake    
saṃviggā bhikkhavo satta        cintayimha mayaṃ tadā .

Sāsanena vināmhākaṃ [^193-3]      jīvitena alaṃ mayaṃ    
pavisitvā mahāraññaṃ          yuñjāma jinasāsanaṃ .

Addasamha tadāraññe         ubbiddhaṃ selapabbataṃ [^193-4]    
nisseṇiyā tamāruyha         nisseṇiṃ pātayimhase .

Tadā ovadi no thero       buddhuppādo sudullabho    
saddhā [^193-5] vā [^193-6] dullabhā laddhā    
thokaṃ sesañca sāsanaṃ .

Nipatanti khaṇātītā           anante dukkhasāgare    
tasmā payogo kattabbo      yāva [^193-7] tiṭṭhati sāsanaṃ .

Arahā āsi so thero       anāgāmi tadānugo    
susīlā itare yuttā         devalokaṃ agamhase .

Nibbuto tiṇṇasaṃsāro         suddhāvāse ca ekako    
ahañca pukkusāti ca          sabhiyo bāhiyo tathā .

Kumārakassapo ceva          tattha tatthūpagā mayaṃ    
saṃsārabandhanā muttā         gotamenānukampitā .

Mallesu kusinārāyaṃ          jāto gabbheva me sato    
mātā matā [^193-8] citakārūḷhā [^193-9]    
tato nipatito ahaṃ .

#[^193-1] ma. Yu. ... Ca dissati . [^193-2] yu. Sabbathalajasattā ca . [^193-3] yu. Vinā sammā .

#[^193-4] ma. Selamuttamaṃ . [^193-5] ma. Saddhāti . [^193-6] yu. Vo . [^193-7] ma. Yu. Yāva  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 194]*

patito dabbapuñjamhi          tato dabboti vissuto    
brahmacāriphalenāhaṃ          vimutto sattavassiko .

Khīrodanaphalenāhaṃ            pañcaṅgehi upāgato    
khīṇāsavopavādena           pāpehi bahu codito .3    
ubho puññañca pāpañca        vītivattomhi dānahaṃ    
patvāna paramaṃ santiṃ          viharāmi anāsavo .

Senāsanaṃ paññāpayiṃ          hāsayitvāna subbate    
jino tasmiṃ guṇe tuṭṭho       etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dabbamallaputto thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Dabbamallaputtattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ kumārakassapattherāpadānaṃ (535)    
[125] ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako    
sabbalokahito dhīro          padumuttaranāmako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 195]*

Tadāhaṃ brāhmaṇo hutvā      vissuto vedapāragū    
divāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ            addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Catusaccaṃ pakāsentaṃ          bodhayantaṃ sadevakaṃ    
sasāvakaṃ [^195-1] cittakathikaṃ        vaṇṇayantaṃ mahājane .

Tadā muditacittohaṃ           nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
nānārattehi vatthehi        alaṅkaritvāna maṇḍapaṃ .

Nānāratanapajjotaṃ           sasaṅghaṃ bhojayiṃ tahiṃ    
bhojayitvāna sattāhaṃ         nānaggarasabhojanaṃ .

Nānācittehi pupphehi        pūjayitvā sasāvakaṃ    
nipacca pādamūlamhi           taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ ahaṃ .

Tadā munivaro āha          karuṇo [^195-2] karuṇālayo    
passathetaṃ dijavaraṃ            padumānanalocanaṃ .

Pītipāmujjabahulaṃ             samuddhaggatanuruhaṃ [^195-3]    
hāsāvahaṃ [^195-4] visālakkhaṃ       mama [^195-5] sāsanasālayaṃ .

Mama pādamūle patitaṃ          ekavattaṃ sumānasaṃ    
esa pattheti taṃ ṭhānaṃ        vicittakathikattanaṃ [^195-6] .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
kumārakassapo nāma          hessati satthusāvako .

#[^195-1] ma. Yu. Vicittakathikānaggaṃ . [^195-2] ma. Yu. Karuṇekarasāsayo . [^195-3] ma. Yu.

#samuggatatanūruhaṃ . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^195-4] ma. Hāsamhitavisālakkhaṃ . [^195-5] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 196]*

Vicittapupphadussānaṃ           ratanānañca vāhasā    
vicittakathikānaṃ so           aggattaṃ pāpuṇissati .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Paribbhamiṃ bhavābhave           raṅgamajjhe yathā naṭo    
sākhāmigatrajo hutvā        migiyā kucchimokkamiṃ .

Tadā mayi kucchigate          vajjhavāro [^196-1] upaṭṭhito    
sākhena cattā me mātā     nigrodhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā .

Tena sā migarājena         maraṇā parimocitā    
pariccajitvāna sappāṇaṃ [^196-2]     mamevaṃ ovadī tadā .

Nigrodhameva seveyya        na sākhamūpasaṃvase    
nigrodhasmiṃ mataṃ seyyo       yañce sākhasmi jīvitaṃ .

Tenānusiṭṭhā migayūthapena [^196-3]    
ahañca mātā catare [^196-4] ca tassovādaṃ [^196-5]    
āgamma rammaṃ tusitādhivāsaṃ    
gatā [^196-6] pavāsaṃ sagharaṃ yatheva .

Puno kassapadhīrassa           atthamentamhi sāsane    
āruyha selasikharaṃ           yuñjitvā jinasāsanaṃ .

Idāni [^196-7] hi rājagahe       jāto seṭṭhikule ahaṃ [^196-8]    
āpannagabbhā [^196-9] me mātā   pabbaji anagāriyaṃ .

#[^196-1] yu. Vajjhavāraṃ upaṭṭhitā . [^196-2] yu. Saṃpāṇaṃ . [^196-3] yu. Migayovādena . [^196-4] ma. Ca    
#tathetare ca . [^196-5] yu. Tassovādena . [^196-6] yu. Gato . [^196-7] ma. Yu. Idānāhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 197]*

Sagabbhantaṃ viditvāna          devadattamupānayuṃ    
so avoca vināsetha         pāpikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ imaṃ .

Idānipi munindena           jinena anukampitā    
sukhinī jananī [^197-1] mayhaṃ         mātā bhikkhunūpassaye .

Taṃ viditvā mahipālo         kosalo maṃ aposayi    
kumāraparihārena            nāmenāhañca kassapo .

Mahākassapamāgamma           ahaṃ komārakassapo    
vammikassadisaṃ [^197-2] kāyaṃ        sutvā buddhena desitaṃ .

Tato cittaṃ vimucci me        anupādāya sabbaso    
pāyāsiṃ damayitvāhaṃ          etadaggaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumārakassapo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kumārakassapattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catuvīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 198]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ bāhiyattherāpadānaṃ (536)    
[126] ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako    
mahappabho tilokaggo         nāmena padumuttaro .

Khippābhiññassa bhikkhussa        guṇaṃ kittayato mune    
sutvā udaggacittohaṃ         kāraṃ katvā mahesino .

Datvā sattāhikaṃ dānaṃ        sasissassa mune ahaṃ    
abhivādiya sambuddhaṃ           taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ tadā .

Tato maṃ byākari buddho       etaṃ passatha brāhmaṇaṃ    
patitaṃ pādamūle me          pinasampannavekkhaṇaṃ [^198-1] .

Hemayaññopacitaṅgaṃ           avadātaṃ tanuttacaṃ    
palambabimbatamboṭṭhaṃ          sītatiṇhasamandijaṃ .

Guṇakāmaṃ bahutaraṃ             samuddhaggatanuruhaṃ    
guṇoghāyatanībhūtaṃ [^198-2]          pītisamphullitānanaṃ .

Eso patthayate [^198-3] ṭhānaṃ     khippābhiññassa bhikkhuno    
anāgate mahāvīro          gotamo nāma hessati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
bāhiyo nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Tadā tuṭṭho ca vuṭṭhāya       yāvajīvaṃ mahāmuniṃ    
kāraṃ katvā cuto saggaṃ       agā sabhavanaṃ yathā .

#[^198-1] ma. Cariyaṃ paccavekkhaṇaṃ . Yu. Pīnasaṃ paccavekkhaṇaṃ . [^198-2] po. Yu. Guṇe... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 199]*

Devabhūto manusso vā        sukhito tassa kammuno    
vāhasā saṃsaritvāna          sampattimanubhosahaṃ .

Puno kassapadhīrassa           atthaṅgatamhi [^199-1] sāsane    
āruyha selasikharaṃ           yuñjitvā jinasāsanaṃ .

Visuddhasīlo sappañño         jinasāsanakārako    
tato cutā pañca janā        devalokaṃ agamhase .

Tatthāhaṃ [^199-2] bāhiyo jāto    bhārukacche puruttame    
tato nāvāya pakkhanno [^199-3]    sāgaraṃ appasiddhikaṃ [^199-4] .

Tato nāvā abhijjittha        gantvāna katipāhakaṃ    
tadā bhiṃsanake ghore         patito makarālaye [^199-5] .

Tadāhaṃ vāyamitvāna          santaritvā mahodadhiṃ    
suppārakaṃ paṭṭanavaraṃ          sampatto mandavedito [^199-6] .

Dārucīraṃ nivāsetvā         gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ    
tadāha so jano tuṭṭho       arahāyamidhāgato .

Imaṃ annena pānena         vatthena sayanena ca    
bhesajjena ca sakkatvā       hessāma sukhitā mayaṃ .

Paccayānaṃ tadā lābhī         tehi sakkatapūjito    
arahāhanti [^199-7] saṅkappaṃ       uppādesiṃ ayoniso .

Tato me cittamaññāya        codayi pubbadevatā    
na tvaṃ upāyamaggaññū         kuto hi arahā bhave .

#[^199-1] ma. Atthamentamhi . [^199-2] ma. Yu. Tatohaṃ . [^199-3] ma. Pakkhando . Po. Yu. Pakkhanto .

#[^199-4] ma. Yu. Appasiddhiyaṃ . [^199-5] ma. Yu. Makarākare . [^199-6] ma. Yu. Mandavedhito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 200]*

Codito tāya saṃviggo        tadāhaṃ paripucchi taṃ    
ke vā ete kuhiṃ loke     arahanto naruttamā .

Sāvatthiyaṃ kosalamandire jino    
pahūtapañño varabhūrimedhaso    
so sakyaputto arahā anāsavo    
deseti dhammaṃ arahattapattiyā .

Tadassa sutvā vacanaṃ supīṇito    
nidhiṃva laddhā kapaṇo [^200-1] suvimhito    
udaggacitto arahattamuttamaṃ    
sudassanaṃ daṭṭhumanantagocaraṃ .

Tadā tato [^200-2] nikkhamitvāna satthuno    
sadā jinaṃ passāmi vimalānanaṃ    
upecca rammaṃ vijitavhayaṃva taṃ [^200-3]    
dije apucchiṃ kuhiṃ lokanandano .

Tato avocuṃ naradevavandito    
puraṃ paviṭṭho asanesanāya so    
paccehi [^200-4] khippaṃ munidassanussuko    
upecca vandāhi tamaggapuggalaṃ .

Tatohaṃ tuvaṭaṃ gantvā         sāvatthiṃ puramuttamaṃ    
vicarantaṃ tamaddakkhiṃ           piṇḍatthaṃ apihāgidhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 201]*

Pattapāṇiṃ alolakkhaṃ          jotayantaṃ [^201-1] idhāmataṃ    
sirinilayasaṅkāsaṃ             ravidittiharānanaṃ .

Taṃ samecca nipaccāhaṃ         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
kupathe vippanaṭṭhassa          saraṇaṃ hohi gotama .

Pāṇasantāraṇatthāya          piṇḍāya vicarāmahaṃ    
na te dhammakathākālo        iccāha muni sattamo .

Tadā punappunaṃ buddhaṃ          āyāciṃ dhammalālaso    
so [^201-2] me dhammamadesesi     gambhīraṃ suññataṃ padaṃ .

Tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         pāpuṇiṃ āsavakkhayaṃ    
parikkhīṇāyuko santo         aho satthānukampako [^201-3] .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa sāsane    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Evaṃ thero viyākāsi        bāhiyo dāruciriyo    
saṅkārakūṭe patito          bhūtādiṭṭhāya [^201-4] gāviyā .

Attano pubbacaritaṃ           kittayitvā mahāmati    
parinibbāyi so dhīro [^201-5]      sāvatthiyaṃ puruttame .

#[^201-1] ma. Pācayantaṃ pītākaraṃ . Yu. Bhājayantaṃ idhāmataṃ . [^201-2] ma. Yo . [^201-3] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 202]*

Nagarā nikkhamanto taṃ         dasvāna isi sattamo    
dārucīradharaṃ dhīraṃ             bāhiyaṃ [^202-1] bāhitātapaṃ [^202-2] .

Bhūmiyaṃ patitaṃ daṇḍaṃ            indaketuva pātitaṃ    
gatāyuṃ [^202-3] sukkhakilesaṃ        jinasāsanakārakaṃ .

Tato āmantayi satthā        sāvake sāsane rate    
gaṇhatha hutvā [^202-4] jhāpetha     tanana sabrahmacārino .

Thūpaṃ karotha pūjetha           nibbuto so mahāmati    
khippābhiññānamesaggo        sāvako me vacokaro .

Sahassamapi ce gāthā         anatthapadasañhitā    
ekaṃ gāthāpadaṃ seyyo       yaṃ sutvā upasammati .

Yattha āpo ca paṭhavī         tejo vāyo na gādhati    
na tattha sukkā jotanti       ādicco nappakāsati .

Na tattha candimā bhāti        tamo tattha na vijjati    
yadā ca attanāvedi         muni monena brāhmaṇo .

Atha rūpā arūpā ca          sukhadukkhā vimuccati    
iccevaṃ abhaṇi nātho         tilokasaraṇo munīti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bāhiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bāhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 203]*

Sattamaṃ mahākoṭṭhikattherāpadānaṃ (537)    
[127] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbalokavidū muni    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji cakkhumā .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi [^203-1] .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            brāhmaṇo vedapāragū    
upetvā [^203-2] sabbalokaggaṃ [^203-3]  assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ .

Tadā so sāvakaṃ dhīro        pabhinnapaṭisambhidaṃ [^203-4]    
atthe dhamme nirutte ca      paṭibhāṇe ca kovidaṃ .

Ṭhapesi etadaggamhi          taṃ sutvā mudito ahaṃ    
sasāvakaṃ jinavaraṃ             sattāhaṃ bhojayiṃ tadā .

#[^203-1] ma. Yu. Patiṭṭhapi . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^203-2] ma. Upecca . [^203-3] yu. Sattapāraṅgaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 204]*

Dussehi chādayitvāna         sasissaṃ buddhasāgaraṃ [^204-1]    
nipacca pādamūlamhi           taṇṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ ahaṃ .

Tato avoca lokaggo        passathetaṃ dijuttamaṃ    
vinataṃ pādamūlamhi [^204-2]         kamalodarasappabhaṃ .

Seṭṭhaṃ [^204-3] buddhassa bhikkhussa    ṭhānaṃ patthayate ayaṃ    
tāya saddhāya cāgena        saddhammassavanena [^204-4] ca .

Sabbattha sukhito hutvā        saṃsaritvā bhavābhave    
anāgatamhi addhāne         lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
koṭṭhiko nāma nāmena       hessati satthusāvako .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
mettacitto paricariṃ          yato [^204-5] paññāsamāhito    
tena kammavipākena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca          devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
sabbattha sukhito āsiṃ         tassa kammassa vāhasā .

#[^204-1] ma. Buddhisāgaraṃ . [^204-2] ma. Pādamūlaṃ me . [^204-3] ma. Yu. Buddhaseṭṭhassa . [^204-4] yu. Tena  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 205]*

Duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse    
aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi [^205-1]      suciṇṇassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Duve kule pajāyāmi         khattiye vāpi brāhmaṇe    
nīce kule na jāyāmi        suciṇṇassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         brahmabandhu ahosahaṃ    
sāvatthiyaṃ vippakule          pacchā jāto mahaddhane [^205-2] .

Mātā candavatī nāma         pitā me assalāyano    
yadā me pitaraṃ buddho        vinayi sabbasuddhiyā [^205-3] .

Tadā pasanno sugate         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
moggallāno ācariyo       upajjhā sārisambhavo .

Kesesu chijjamānesu         diṭṭhi chinnā samūlikā    
nivāsento ca kāsāvaṃ       arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe ca me mati    
pabhinnā tena lokaggo       etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Asandiṭṭhaṃ viyākāsiṃ          upatissena pucchito    
paṭisambhidāsu tenāhaṃ         aggo sambuddhasāsane .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 206]*

paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākoṭṭhiko thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mahākoṭṭhikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ uruvelakassapattherāpadānaṃ (538)    
[128] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbalokavidū muni    
ito satasahasassamhi          kappe uppajji cakkhumā .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇo  .

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            brāhmaṇo sādhusammato    
upecca lokapajjotaṃ         assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 207]*

Tadā mahāparisati            mahāpurisasāvakaṃ    
ṭhapentaṃ etadaggamhi         sutvāna mudito ahaṃ .

Mahatā parivārena           nimantetvā mahājinaṃ    
brāhmaṇānaṃ sahassena        saha dānamadāsahaṃ .

Mahādānaṃ daditvāna          abhivādiya nāyakaṃ    
ekamantaṃ ṭhito haṭṭho        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Tayi saddhāya me vīra         adhikāraguṇena ca    
parisā mahatī hotu           nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ .

Tadā avoca parisaṃ           gajagajjitasussaro    
karavikarudo [^207-1] satthā        etaṃ passatha brāhmaṇaṃ .

Hemavaṇṇaṃ mahābāhuṃ          kamalānanalocanaṃ    
uddhaggatanujaṃ haṭṭhaṃ           saddhāvantaṃ guṇe mama .

Esa patthayi [^207-2] taṃ ṭhānaṃ      sīhasarassa [^207-3] bhikkhuno    
anāgatamhi addhāne         lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
kassapo nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Ito dvenavute kappe       ahu satthā anuttaro    
anūpamo asadiso            phusso lokagganāyako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 208 -->
<a id="page-208"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 208]*

So ve [^208-1] sabbatamaṃ hantvā   vijaṭetvā mahājaṭaṃ    
vassate amataṃ vuṭṭhiṃ          tappayanto sadevakaṃ .

Tadā mayaṃ [^208-2] bārāṇasiyaṃ      rājāmaccā [^208-3] āhumhase    
bhātaromha tayo sabbe       saṃvissaṭṭhāva rājino .

Vīraṅgarūpā balino           saṅgāme aparājitā    
tadā kupitapaccanto          amhe āha mahīpati .

Etha gantvāna paccantaṃ       sādhetvā avanibalaṃ [^208-4]    
khemaṃ me vijitaṃ katvā        punarethāti sāsatha [^208-5] .

Tato mayaṃ avocumha          yadi deyyāsi nāyakaṃ    
upaṭṭhānāya amhākaṃ         sādhayissāma te [^208-6] tato .

Tato mayaṃ laddhavarā          bhūmipālena pesitā    
nikkhittasatthaṃ paccantaṃ         katvā punarupecca [^208-7] taṃ .

Yācitvā satthupaṭṭhānaṃ        rājānaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
munivaraṃ [^208-8] labhitvāna         yāvajīvaṃ yajimha [^208-9] taṃ .

Mahagghāni ca vatthāni         paṇītāni rasāni ca    
senāsanāni rammāni         bhesajjāni hitāni ca .

Datvā sasaṅghamunino          dhammenuppāditāni no    
sīlavanto kāruṇikā          bhāvanāyuttamānasā .

Sadā paricaritvāna           mettacittena nāyakaṃ    
nibbute tamhi lokagge       pūjaṃ katvā yathābalaṃ .

#[^208-1] ma. Ca . [^208-2] ma. Yu. Hi . [^208-3] ma. Rājāpaccā . [^208-4] po. Avadhibalaṃ .

#ma. Aṭavībalaṃ . Yu. Sodhetvā avidhībalaṃ . [^208-5] ma. Yu. Bhāsatha . [^208-6] ma. Vo . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 209]*

Tato cutā tāvatiṃsaṃ [^209-1]       gatā tattha mahāsukhaṃ
