anubhūtā mayaṃ sabbe          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Mālākāro [^209-2] yathāladdho    dasseti vikatiṃ bahuṃ    
tathā bhave bhavantohaṃ         videhādhipatī ahu .

Guṇācelassa vākyena        micchādiṭṭhihatāsayo [^209-3]    
narakamaggamāruḷho           rucāya mama dhītuyā .

Ovādaṃ nādayitvāna         brahmunā nāradenahaṃ    
bahudhā sāsito [^209-4] santo     diṭṭhiṃ hitvāna pāpikaṃ .

Pūrayitvā visesena          dasakammapathe ahaṃ    
hitvāna dehamagamiṃ           saggaṃ sabhavanaṃ yathā .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         brahmabandhu ahosahaṃ    
bārāṇasiyaṃ phītāya           jāto vippakule [^209-5] ahaṃ .

Maccubyādhijarābhīto          ogāhetvā mahāvanaṃ    
nibbānaṃ padamesanto         jaṭilesu paribbajiṃ .

Tadā dve bhātaro mayhaṃ      pabbajiṃsu mayā saha    
uruvelāya māpetvā        assamaṃ nīvasiṃ ahaṃ .

Kassapo nāma gottena       uruvelāya [^209-6] nīvasiṃ    
tato me āsi paññatti       uruvelāsu [^209-7] kassapo .

Nadīsakāse bhātā me        nadīkassapasavhayo    
āsippakāso nāmena        gayāyaṃ gayakassapo .

#[^209-1] ma. Yu. Santusitaṃ . [^209-2] ma. Yu. Māyākāro yathā raṅge . [^209-3] ma. Micchādiṭṭhigatāsayo .

#[^209-4] po. Bahuṃ vā sāsite sante . Ma. ...Saṃsito . Yu. Bahuṃ va . [^209-5] ma. Yu. ...

#vippamahākule . [^209-6] ma. Uruvelanivāsiko . Yu. Uruvelānivāsitā . [^209-7] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 210 -->
<a id="page-210"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 210]*

Dve satāni kaniṭṭhassa [^210-1]     tīṇi majjhassa bhātuno    
mama pañcasatānūnā           sissā sabbe mamānugā .

Tadā upecca maṃ buddho       katvāna [^210-2] vividhāni me    
pāṭihirāni lokaggo         vinesi narasārathi .

Sahassaparivārena            ahosiṃ ehibhikkhuko    
teheva saha sabbehi         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Te ca [^210-3] aññeva bahavo     yasasā [^210-4] maṃ parivārayuṃ    
sāsituñca samatthohaṃ          tato maṃ isi sattamo .

Mahāparisabhāvasmiṃ            etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ    
aho buddhe kataṃ kāraṃ        saphalaṃ me ajāyatha .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uruvelakassapo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Uruvelakassapattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^210-1] yu. Nadīkassa . [^210-2] yu. Katvā nānāvidhāni . [^210-3] ma. Te cevaññe ca bahavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 211]*

navamaṃ rādhattherāpadānaṃ (539)    
[129] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbalokavidū muni    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji cakkhumā .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjata tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
upecca taṃ naravaraṃ           assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ .

Paññāpentaṃ mahāvīraṃ         parisāsu visāradaṃ    
paṭibhāṇeyyakaṃ bhikkhuṃ          etadagge vināyakaṃ .

Tadāhaṃ kāraṃ katvāna         sasaṅghe lokanāyake    
nipacca sirasā pāde         taṇṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 212]*

Tato maṃ bhagavā āha         siṅginikkhasamappabho    
sarena rajanīyena            kilesamalahārinā .

Sukhī bhavassu dīghāyu           sijjhataṃ paṇidhī tava    
sasaṅghe me kataṃ kāraṃ        atīva vipulaṃ tayā .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
rādhoti nāmadheyyena        hessati satthu sāvako .

[1]    
paṭibhāṇeyyakānaggaṃ          paññāpessati nāyako    
taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ .

Mettacitto paricariṃ          yato [^212-2] paññāsamāhito    
tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca .

Jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ    
satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca          devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sabbattha sukhito āsiṃ         tassa kammassa vāhasā    
pacchime bhavasampatte         giribbajapuruttame .

Jāto vippakule niddhe       vikalacchādanāsane    
kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ pādāsiṃ          sārīputtassa tādino .

#[^212-1] ma. Sa tehetuguṇe tuṭṭho sakyaputto narāsabho . Yu. Sake tehetuguṇe tuṭṭho  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 213]*

Yadā jiṇṇo ca vuddho ca      tadārāmamupāgamiṃ    
pabbājenti [^213-1] na maṃ keci    jiṇṇaṃ dubbalathāmakaṃ .

Tena dino vivaṇṇaṅgo        soko cāsiṃ tadā ahaṃ    
disvā mahākāruṇiko         mamamāha mahāmuni .

Kimatthaṃ putta sokaṭṭo        brūhi te cittajaṃ rujaṃ    
pabbajjaṃ na labhe vīra         svākkhāte tava sāsane .

Tena sokena dinomhi        saraṇaṃ hohi nāyaka    
tadā bhikkhū samānetvā       apucchi muni sattamo .

Imassa adhikāraṃ ye          sarantā [^213-2] byāharantu te    
sārīputto tadāvoca         kāramassa sarāmahaṃ .

Kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ dāpesi          piṇḍāya carato mamaṃ    
sādhu sādhu kataññūsi          sārīputta imaṃ tuvaṃ .

Pabbājehi dijaṃ vuḍḍhaṃ         hessati [^213-3] pūjaniyo ayaṃ    
tato alatthaṃ pabbajjaṃ         kammavācopasampadaṃ .

Na cireneva kālena         pāpuṇiṃ āsavakkhayaṃ    
sakkaccaṃ munino vākyaṃ        suṇāmi mudito yato    
paṭibhāṇeyyakānaggaṃ          tato maṃ ṭhapayi jino .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 214]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rādho thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Rādhattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ mogharājattherāpadānaṃ (540)    
[130] padumuttaro [^214-1] lokavidū       sabbalokahito muni    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji cakkhumā .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 215]*

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            kule aññatare ahu [^215-1]    
parakammāyane yutto         natthi me kiñci sandhanaṃ .

Paṭikkamanasālāyaṃ            vasanto katabhūmiyaṃ    
aggiṃ ujjālayiṃ tattha         ḍayhakaṇhā silāmahi .

Tadā parisati nātho          catusaccaṃ pakāsako    
sāvakaṃ sampakittesi          lūkhacīvaradhārakaṃ .

Tassa tamhi guṇe tuṭṭho       paṭipajja [^215-2] tathāgataṃ    
lūkhacīvaradhāraggaṃ             patthayiṃ ṭhānamuttamaṃ .

Tadā avoca bhagavā          sāvake padumuttaro    
passathetaṃ purisakaṃ            kucelaṃ tanudehakaṃ .

Pītippasannavadanaṃ             saddhādhanasamanvitaṃ    
udaggatanujaṃ haṭṭhaṃ            acalaṃ sālapiṇḍitaṃ .

Eso pattheti taṃ ṭhānaṃ       saccasenassa bhikkhuno    
lūkhacīvaradhārissa             tassa vaṇṇagatāsayo [^215-3] .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       nipacca sirasā jinaṃ    
yāvajīvaṃ subhaṃ kammaṃ           karitvā jinasāsane .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ [^215-4] agañchahaṃ .

Paṭikkamanasālāyaṃ            bhūmidāhanakammunā [^215-5]    
samasahassaṃ niraye            aḍayhiṃ vedanāṭṭito .

#[^215-1] ma. Yu. Sabbattha ahuṃ . [^215-2] ma. Yu. Paṇipacca . [^215-3] ma. Vaṇṇasitāsayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 216]*

Tena kammāvasesena         pañca jātisatānihaṃ    
manusso kulajo hutvā        pāṭiyā [^216-1] lakkhaṇaṅkito .

Pañca jātisatāneva          kuṭṭharogasamappito    
mahādukkhaṃ anubhaviṃ            tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Imamhi bhaddake kappe        upariṭṭhaṃ [^216-2] yasassinaṃ    
piṇḍapātena tappesiṃ         pasannamānaso ahaṃ .

Tena kammavisesena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         ajāyiṃ jattiye kule    
pituno accayenāhaṃ          mahārajjasamappito .

Kuṭṭharogābhibhūtohaṃ           rattiyaṃ [^216-3] na sukhaṃ labhe    
moghaṃ rajjasukhaṃ yasmā         mogho rājā tato ahaṃ .

Kāyassa dosaṃ disvāna        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
bāvarissa dijaggassa          sissattaṃ ajjhupāgamiṃ .

Mahatā parivārena           upecca naranāyakaṃ    
apucchiṃ nipuṇaṃ pañhaṃ           vāgisaṃ [^216-4] vādisūdanaṃ .

Ayaṃ loko paro loko       brahmaloko sadevako    
diṭṭhinte [^216-5] nābhijānāmi     gotamassa yasassino .

Evaṃ abhikkantadassāviṃ        atthī pañhena āgamaṃ    
kathaṃ lokaṃ avekkhantaṃ         maccurājā na passati .

#[^216-1] ma. Jātiyā . Yu. Tatiyā karaṇaṅkito . [^216-2] yu. Upatiṭṭhaṃ . [^216-3] ma. Na ratiṃ ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 217]*

Suññato lokaṃ avekkhassu      mogharājā sadā sato    
attānudiṭṭhiṃ ūhacca          evaṃ maccuttaro siyā .

Evaṃ lokaṃ avekkhantaṃ        maccurājā na passati    
iti maṃ abhaṇi buddho          sabbarogatikicchako .

Saha gāthāvasānena          kesamassuvivajjito    
kāsāvavatthavasano           āsiṃ bhikkhu tathārahaṃ .

Saṅghikesu vihāresu          na vasiṃ rogapīḷito    
mā vihāro padussīti         vācāyābhisupīḷito [^217-1] .

Saṅkārakuṭā āhatvā        susānā rathikāhi ca    
tato saṅghāṭiṃ karitvāna       dhārayiṃ lūkhacīvaraṃ .

Mahābhisakko tasmiṃ me        guṇe tuṭṭho vināyako    
lūkhacīvaradhārīnaṃ              etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Puññapāpaparikkhīṇo           sabbarogavivajjito    
sikhīva anupādāno           nibbāyissamanāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 218]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mogharājā thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mogharājattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kaccāno vakkalī thero       mahākappinasavhayo    
dabbo kumāranāmo ca        bāhiyo koṭṭhiko vasī .

Uruvelakassapo rādho        mogharājā ca paṇḍito    
tīṇi gāthāsatānettha         dvāsaṭṭhi ceva piṇḍitā .

Kaccāyanavaggo catupaññāso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 219]*

Pañcapaññāso bhaddiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ lakuṇṭakabhaddiyattherāpadānaṃ (541)    
[131] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū [^219-1]    
ito satasahassamhi           loke [^219-2] uppajji nāyako [^219-3] .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            seṭṭhiputto mahaddhano    
jaṅghavihāraṃ vicaraṃ            saṅghārāmaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tadā so lokapajjoto       dhammaṃ desesi nāyako    
madhurassarānaṃ [^219-4] pavaraṃ        sāvakaṃ abhikittayi .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       kāraṃ katvā mahesino    
vanditvā satthuno pāde      taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Tadā buddho viyākāsi        saṅghamajjhe vināyako    
anāgatamhi addhāne         lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
bhaddiyo nāma nāmena        hessati satthu sāvako .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

#[^219-1] ma. Yu. Sabbadhammesu cakkhumā . [^219-2] ma. Yu. Kappe . [^219-3] yu. Cakkhumā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 220]*

Dvenavute ito kappe       phusso uppajji nāyako    
durāsado dupasaho           sabbalokuttamo jino .

Caraṇena ca sampanno         brahmā [^220-1] uju patāpavā    
hitesī sabbasattānaṃ          bahuṃ mocesi bandhanā .

Nandārāmavare [^220-2] tassa      ahosiṃ pussakokilo    
gandhakuṭisamāsanne           ambarukkhe vasāmahaṃ .

Tadā piṇḍāya gacchantaṃ        dakkhiṇeyyaṃ jinuttamaṃ    
disvā cittaṃ pasādetvā      mañjunā abhikūjahaṃ [^220-3] .

Rājuyyānaṃ tadā gantvā      supakkaṃ kanakattacaṃ    
ambapiṇḍaṃ gahetvāna         sambuddhassopanāmayiṃ .

Tadā me cittamaññāya        mahākāruṇiko jino    
upaṭṭhākassa hatthato         pattaṃ paggaṇhi nāyako .

Adāsiṃ [^220-4] haṭṭhacittohaṃ       ambapiṇḍaṃ mahāmune    
patte pakkhippa pakkhehi       pañjaliṃ katvāna mañjunā .

Sarena rajanīyena            savanīyena mañjunā [^220-5]    
vassanto buddhapūjatthaṃ         niddaṃ [^220-6] gantvā nipajjahaṃ .

Tadā muditacittaṃ maṃ           buddhapemagatāsayaṃ    
sakuṇagghi upāgantvā         ghātayi duṭṭhamānaso [^220-7] .

Tato cutohaṃ tusite          anubhotvā mahāsukhaṃ    
manussayonimāgañchiṃ           tassa kammassa vāhasā .

#[^220-1] ma. Yu. Brahā . [^220-2] ma. Nandārāmavane . [^220-3] ma. Mañjunābhinikūjahaṃ . [^220-4] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 221]*

Imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma gottena       uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Sāsanaṃ jotayitvā so        abhibhuyya kutitthiye    
vinayitvāna veneyye        nibbuto so sasāvako .

Nibbute tamhi lokagge       pasannā janatā bahū    
pūjanatthāya buddhassa          thūpaṃ kubbanti satthuno .

Sattayojanikaṃ thūpaṃ            sattaratanabhūsitaṃ [^221-1]    
kāressāma mahesissa        iccevaṃ mantayanti te .

Kikino kāsirājassa          tadā senāya nāyako    
hutvāhaṃ appamāṇassa         pamāṇaṃ cetiye vadiṃ .

Tadā te mama vākyena       cetiyaṃ yojanuggataṃ    
akaṃsu naradhīrassa             nānāratanabhūsitaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jāto seṭṭhikule ahaṃ    
sāvatthiyaṃ puravare           iddhe phīte mahaddhane .

Purappavese sugataṃ           disvā vimhitamānaso    
pabbajitvāna na ciraṃ          arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Cetiyassa pamāṇaṃ yaṃ          akariṃ tena kammunā    
lakuṇṭakasarīrohaṃ             jāto paribbhavāraho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 222]*

Sarena madhurenāhaṃ           pūjetvā isisattamaṃ    
mañjussarāna bhikkhūnaṃ          aggattaṃ anupāpuṇiṃ .

Phaladānena buddhassa          guṇānussaraṇena ca    
sāmaññaphalasampanno          viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā lakuṇṭakabhaddiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Lakuṇṭakabhaddiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ kaṅkhārevatattherāpadānaṃ (542)    
[132] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Sīhahanu brahmagiro           haṃsadundubhisāvano [^222-1]    
nāgavikkantagamano           candasūrādikappabho .

Mahāmati mahāvīro           mahājhāyī mahāgati [^222-2]    
mahākāruṇiko nātho         mahātamavidhaṃsano [^222-3] .

#[^222-1] sī. Haṃsadundubhinissaro . Ma. Sahaṃdundubhinissano . Yu. Haṃsadundarabhinissavano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 223]*

Sa kadāci tilokaggo         veneyyaṃ vinayaṃ bahuṃ    
dhammaṃ deseti sambuddho       sattacittavidū [^223-1] muni .

Jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ vīraṃ           upasantaṃ anāvilaṃ    
vaṇṇayanto parisati           toseti janataṃ jino .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            brāhmaṇo vedapāragū    
dhammaṃ sutvāna mudito         taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Tadā jino viyākāsi         saṅghamajjhe vināyako    
mudito hohi tvaṃ brahme      lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
revato nāma nāmena        hessati satthu sāvako .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātohaṃ koliye pure    
khattiye kulasampanne         iddhe phīte mahaddhane .

Yadā kapilavatthusmiṃ           buddho dhammaṃ adesayi    
tadā pasanno sugate         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Kaṅkhā me bahulā āsi       kappākappe tahiṃ tahiṃ    
taṃ sabbaṃ vinayi buddho         desetvā dhammamuttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 224]*

Tatohaṃ tiṇṇasaṃsāro          tadā [^224-1] jhānasukhe rato    
viharāmi tadā buddho         maṃ disvā etadabravi .

Yākāci kaṅkhā idha vā huraṃ vā    
sakavediyā vā paravediyā vā    
(2) jhāyino tā pajahanti sabbā    
ātāpino brahmacariyaṃ carantā .

Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ         phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
samutto saravegova          kilese jhāpayiṃ mama .

Tato maṃ [^224-3] jhānanirataṃ        disvā lokantagū muni    
jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aggo         paññāpesi mahāmati .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṅkhārevato thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kaṅkhārevatattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 225]*

Tatiyaṃ sīvalittherāpadānaṃ (543)    
[133] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Sīlantassa asaṅkheyyaṃ         samādhi vajirūpamo    
asaṅkheyyaṃ ñāṇavaraṃ          vimutti ca anopamā .

Manujāmarunāgānaṃ [^225-1]         brahmānañca samāgame    
samaṇabrāhmaṇākiṇṇe         dhammaṃ desesi nāyako .

Sasāvakaṃ mahālābhiṃ           puññavantaṃ jutindharaṃ    
ṭhapesi etadaggamhi          parisāsu visārado .

Tadāhaṃ khattiyo āsiṃ         nagare haṃsasavhaye    
sutvā jinassa taṃ vākyaṃ       sāvakassa guṇaṃ bahuṃ .

Nimantayitvā sattāhaṃ         bhojayitvā sasāvakaṃ    
mahādānaṃ daditvāna          taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Tadā maṃ vinataṃ pāde         disvāna purisāsabho    
sarena mahatā dhīro          imaṃ vacanamabravi .

Tato jinassa vacanaṃ           sotukāmā mahājanā    
devatā atha gandhabbā [^225-2]     brahmāno [^225-3] ca mahiddhikā .

Samaṇabrāhmaṇā ceva         namassiṃsu katañjalī    
namo te purisājañña         namo te purisuttama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 226]*

Khattiyena mahādānaṃ          dinnaṃ sattāhakādhikaṃ [^226-1]    
sotukāmā phalaṃ tassa         byākarohi mahāmuni .

Tato avoca bhagavā          suṇātha mama bhāsitaṃ    
appameyyamhi buddhamhi        sasaṅghamhi patiṭṭhitā [^226-2] .

Dakkhiṇādāya [^226-3] ko vattā    appameyyaphalā hi sā    
apicesa mahābhogo          ṭhānaṃ pattheti uttamaṃ .

Lābhī vipulalābhānaṃ           yathā bhikkhu sudassano    
tathāhampi bhaveyyanti         lacchasetaṃ anāgate .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
sīvalī nāma nāmena          hessati satthusāvako .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       vipassī lokanāyako    
uppajji cārunayano [^226-4]       sabbadhammavipassako .

Tadāhaṃ bandhumatiyā           kulassaññatarassa ca    
dayito vuṭṭhito [^226-5] ceva      āsiṃ kammantabyāvaṭo [^226-6] .

Tadā aññataro puggo [^226-7]     vipassissa mahesino    
pariveṇaṃ [^226-8] akāresi        mahantamiti vissutaṃ    
#[^226-1] ma. Sattāhikampi vo . Yu. Satatāhikaṃ vibho . [^226-2] yu. Saṅghamhi suppatiṭṭhitā .

#[^226-3] ma. Dakkhiṇā tāya . [^226-4] ma. Cārudassano . [^226-5] ma. Passito . Yu. Patthito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 227]*

Niṭṭhite ca mahādānaṃ         daduṃ khajjakasaññutaṃ    
navaṃ dadhiṃ madhuñceva           vicinaṃ neva addasuṃ .

Tadāhantaṃ [^227-1] gahetvāna      navadadhiṃ madhumpica    
kammasāmigharaṃ gacchiṃ           tamesantā [^227-2] mamaddasuṃ .

Sahassamapi datvāna           nālabhittha [^227-3] satadvayaṃ    
tatohameva cintesiṃ          netaṃ hessati orakaṃ .

Yathā ime janā sabbe       sakkaronti tathāgataṃ    
ahampi kāraṃ karissāmi        sasaṅghe lokanāyake .

Tadāhameva netvāna [^227-4]      dadhiṃ madhuñca ekato    
madditvā lokanāthassa        sasaṅghassa adāsahaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Punāhaṃ bārāṇasiyaṃ           rājā hutvā mahāyaso    
sattukassa tadā duṭṭho [^227-5]     dvārarodhaṃ akārayiṃ .

Tadā tapassino ruddhā        ekāhaṃ rakkhitā ahuṃ    
tato tassa vipākena         pāpiṭṭhaṃ [^227-6] nirayaṃ bhusaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātohaṃ lokiye pure    
suppavāsāva [^227-7] me mātā    mahālilicchavi me pitā .

Khattiye puññakammena         dvārarodhassa vāhasā    
sattavassāni nivasiṃ           mātukucchimhi dukkhito .

#[^227-1] yu. Tadābhattaṃ . [^227-2] yu. Tamesantaṃ tamaddasuṃ . [^227-3] ma. Nālabhiṃsu ca taṃdvayaṃ .

#[^227-4] ma. Yu. Tadāhamevaṃ cintetvā . [^227-5] yu. Buddho . [^227-6] ma. Pāpatiṃ . Yu. Pāpattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 228]*

sattāhaṃ dvāramuḷhohaṃ        mahādukkhasamappito    
mātā me chandadānena       evaṃ āsi sudukkhitā .

Suvatthitohaṃ nikkhanto         buddhena anukampito    
nikkhantadivaseyeva           pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Upajjhāyo sārīputto me     moggallāno mahiddhiko    
kese oropayanto me      anusāsi mahāmati .

Kesesu chijjamānesu         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
devā nāgā manussā ca      paccaye upanenti me .

Padumuttaranāmañca            vipassiñca vināyakaṃ    
yaṃ pūjayiṃ pamudito            paccayehi visesato .

Tato tesaṃ visesena         kammānaṃ vipuluttamaṃ    
lābhaṃ labhāmi sabbattha         vane gāme jale thale .

Revataṃ dassanatthāya          yadā yāti vināyako    
tiṃsabhikkhusahassehi            saha lokagganāyako .

Tadā devopanītehi          mamatthāya mahāmati    
paccayehi mahāvīro          sasaṅgho lokanāyako .

Upaṭṭhito mayā buddho        gantvā revatamaddasa    
tato jetavanaṃ gantvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Lābhīnaṃ sīvalī aggo          mama sissesu bhikkhavo    
sabbalokahito satthā         kittayi parisāsu maṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 229]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīvalī thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sīvalittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ vaṅgīsattherāpadānaṃ (544)    
[134] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
ito satasahassasamhi          kappe uppajji nāyako .

Yathāpi sāgare ummi [^229-1]      gagane viya tārakā    
evaṃ pāvacanaṃ tassa          arahantehi cittitaṃ  .

Sadevāsūranāgehi           manujehi purakkhato    
samaṇabrāhmaṇākiṇṇe         janamajjhe jinuttamo .

Pabhāhi anurañjanto          loke lokantagū jino    
vacanena vibodhento         veneyyapadumāni so .

Vesārajjehi sampanno       catūhi purisuttamo    
pahīnabhayasārajjo            khemappatto visārado .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 230]*

Āsabhaṃ pavaraṃ ṭhānaṃ           buddhabhūmiñca kevalaṃ    
paṭijānāti lokaggo         natthi sañcodako kvaci .

Sīhanādamasambhītaṃ             nadato tassa tādino    
devo naro vā brahmā vā   paṭivattā na vijjati .

Desento pavaraṃ dhammaṃ        santārento sadevakaṃ    
dhammacakkaṃ pavatteti          parisāsu visārado .

Paṭibhāṇavataṃ aggaṃ            sāvakaṃ sādhusammataṃ    
guṇaṃ bahuṃ pakittetvā         etadagge ṭhapesi taṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            brāhmaṇo sādhusammato    
sabbavedavidū jāto          vaṅgīso [^230-1] vādisūdano .

Upecca taṃ mahāvīraṃ          sutvā taṃ dhammadesanaṃ    
pītivaraṃ paṭilabhiṃ              sāvakassa guṇe rato .

Nimantayitvā sugataṃ           sasaṅghaṃ lokanandanaṃ    
sattāhaṃ bhojayitvāhaṃ         dussehi chādayiṃ tadā .

Nipacca sirasā pāde         katokāso katañjalī    
ekamantaṃ ṭhito haṭṭho        santhaviṃ jinamuttamaṃ .

Namo te vādisūdana [^230-2]       namo te isisattama [^230-3]    
namo te sabbalokagga        namo te abhayaṃkara .

Namo te māramathana          namo te diṭṭhisūdana    
namo te santisukhada          namo te saraṇaṃkara [^230-4] .

#[^230-1] ma. Yu. Vāgīso . [^230-2] ma. Vādimaddana . Yu. Vālisaddūra . [^230-3] yu. Isisuttama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 231]*

Anāthānaṃ bhavaṃ nātho         bhītānaṃ abhayappado    
vissāsaṃ [^231-1] bhūmisantānaṃ       saraṇaṃ saraṇesinaṃ .

Evamādīhi sambuddhaṃ          santhavitvā mahāguṇaṃ    
avocaṃ vādisūrassa [^231-2]        gatiṃ pappomi bhikkhuno .

Tadā avoca bhagavā          anantapaṭibhāṇavā    
yo so buddhaṃ abhojesi [^231-3]    sattāhaṃ sahasāvakaṃ .

Guṇañca me pakittesi         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
eso patthayate ṭhānaṃ        vādisūrassa [^231-4] bhikkhuno .

Anāgatamhi addhāne         lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ    
devamanussasampattiṃ           anubhotvā anappakaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
vaṅgīso nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
paccayehi upaṭṭhāsiṃ          mettacitto tathāgataṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ [^231-5] agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        paribbājakule [^231-6] ahaṃ    
pacchā jāto yadā āsiṃ      jātiyā sattavassiko .

#[^231-1] ma. Vissāmabhūmi santānaṃ . Yu. Vissānabhūmisantānaṃ . 2-[^231-4] ma. Vādisūdassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 232]*

Sabbavedavidū jāto          vādasatthavisārado    
vaggussaro [^232-1] cittakathī       paravādappamaddano .

Vaṅge jātoti vaṅgīso       vacane issaroti vā    
vaṅgīso iti me nāmaṃ        aggampi [^232-2] lokasammataṃ .

Yadāhaṃ viññutaṃ patto         ṭhito paṭhamayobbane    
tadā rājagahe ramme        sārīputtañca addasaṃ [^232-3] .

Pañcavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Piṇḍāya vicarantaṃ taṃ          pattapāṇiṃ susaṃvutaṃ    
alolakkhiṃ mitabhāṇiṃ           yugamattaṃ udikkhataṃ [^232-4] .

Taṃ disvā vimhito hutvā      avocamananucchavaṃ    
kaṇikāraṃva nicitaṃ             cittaṃ gāthāpadaṃ ahaṃ .

Ācikkhi so me satthāraṃ      sambuddhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
tadā so paṇḍito dhīro       uttaraṃ samavoca me .

Virāgasahitaṃ vākyaṃ           katvā duddasamuttamaṃ    
vicittapaṭibhāṇehi            tosito tena tādinā .

Nipacca sirasā pāde         pabbājehīti [^232-5] abraviṃ    
tato maṃ sa mahappañño        buddhaseṭṭhamupānayi .

Nipacca sirasā pāde         nisīdiṃ satthu santike    
mamāha vadataṃ seṭṭho         saccaṃ vaṅgīsa kacci [^232-6] te .

#[^232-1] ma. Vādissaro . [^232-2] ma. Yu. Abhavī . [^232-3] ma. Sārīputtamahaddasaṃ .

#yu. Sārīputtamathaddasaṃ . [^232-4] ma. Nidakkhitaṃ . Yu. Nirikkhitaṃ . [^232-5] ma. Pabbājehītimaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 233]*

[1]    
mataṃ [^233-2] sīsaṃva viditaṃ          sugatiduggatimataṃ    
tuyhaṃ vijjāvisesena         sace sakkosi vācaya .

Āmoti [^233-3] me paṭiññāte    tīṇi sīsāni dassayi    
atho nirayadevesu [^233-4]        upapanne avācayiṃ .

Tadā khīṇāsavasseva [^233-5]       sīsaṃ dassesi nāyako    
tatohaṃ vihatārambho          pabbajjaṃ samayācisaṃ .

Pabbajitvāna sugataṃ           santhavāmi yahiṃ [^233-6] tahiṃ    
tato maṃ kavicittoti [^233-7]       ujjhāyanti hi bhikkhavo .

Tato vīmaṃsanatthaṃ me          āha buddho vināyako    
takkitānaṃ [^233-8] imā gāthā     ṭhānaso paṭibhanti vā [^233-9] .

Na kabyacittohaṃ 10 vīra      ṭhānaso paṭibhanti me    
tenahi dāni vaṅgīsa          ṭhānaso santhavāhi maṃ .

Tadāhaṃ santhaviṃ dhīraṃ           gāthāhi isisattamaṃ    
so 1[^233-1] ṭhānaso tadā tuṭṭho  jino agge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Paṭibhāṇena cittena          aññesamatimaññahaṃ    
pesalo 1[^233-2] tena saṃviggo    arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Paṭibhāṇavataṃ aggo           añño koci na vijjati    
yathāyaṃ bhikkhu vaṅgīso         evaṃ dhāretha bhikkhavo .

Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ         phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto saravegova          kilesā 1[^233-3] jhāpitā mama .

#1. Ma. Kiñci sippanti tassāhaṃ jānāmīti ca abraviṃ . [^233-2] ma. Yu. Matasīsaṃ vanacchuddhaṃ    
#api bālasavatthikaṃ . [^233-3] yu. Āmāti . [^233-4] ma. Nirayanaradevesu . [^233-5] yu.

#paccekabuddhassa . [^233-6] ma. Tahiṃ tahiṃ . [^233-7] ma. Kabbavittosi . [^233-8] ma. Takkikā    
#panimā gāthā . [^233-9] ma. Taṃ . 10 ma. Kabbavittohaṃ . 1[^233-1] ma. Ṭhānaso me .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 234]*

kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīso thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vaṅgīsattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ nandakattherāpadānaṃ (545)    
[135] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Hitāya sabbasattānaṃ          sukhāya vadataṃ varo    
atthāya purisājañño         paṭipanno sadevake .

Yasaggapatto sirimā          kittivaṇṇabhaṭo jino    
pūjito sabbalokassa          disā sabbā suvissuto .

Uttiṇṇavicikiccho so         vītivattakathaṃkatho    
paripuṇṇamanasaṅkappo          patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Anuppannassa maggassa         uppādetā naruttamo    
anakkhātañca akkhāsi         asañjātañca sañjanī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 235]*

Maggaññū maggavidū ca          maggakkhāyī narāsabho    
maggassa kusalo satthā        sārathīnaṃ naruttamo .

Tadā mahākāruṇiko          dhammaṃ desesi nāyako    
nimugge mohapaṅkamhi [^235-1]      samuddharati pāṇino .

Bhikkhunīnaṃ ovadane           sāvakaṃ jeṭṭhasammataṃ [^235-2]    
vaṇṇayaṃ etadaggamhi          paññāpesi mahāmuni .

Taṃ sutvāhaṃ pamudito          nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
bhojayitvā sasaṅghantaṃ         patthayiṃ ṭhānamuttamaṃ .

Tadā pamudito nātho         maṃ avoca mahāisi    
sukhī bhavassu dīghāyu [^235-3]        lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
nandako nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ [^235-4] agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jāto seṭṭhikule ahaṃ    
sāvatthiyaṃ puravare           iddhe phīte mahaddhane .

Purappavese sugataṃ           disvā vimhitamānaso    
jetārāmapaṭiggāhe         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

#[^235-1] ma. Kāmapaṅkamhi . [^235-2] ma. Yu. Seṭṭhasammataṃ . [^235-3] ma. Dīghāvu . [^235-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 236]*

na cireneva kālena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
tatohaṃ tiṇṇasaṃsāro          sāsito sabbadassinā .

Bhikkhunīnaṃ dhammakathaṃ            paripucchā kariṃ ahaṃ    
sāsitā tā mayā sabbā      abhaviṃsu anāsavā .

Satāni pañcanūnāni           tadā tuṭṭho mahāhito    
bhikkhunīnaṃ ovadataṃ            aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapesi maṃ .

Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ         phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto saravegova          kilesā jhāpitā mama .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nandako thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Nandakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ kāḷudāyittherāpadānaṃ (546)    
[136] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 237]*

Nāyakānaṃ varo satthā        guṇāguṇavidū jino    
kataññūkatavedī ca            titthe yojeti pāṇino .

Sabbaññū tena ñāṇena        tulayitvā dayāsayo    
deseti pavaraṃ dhammaṃ          anantaguṇasañcayo .

Sa kadāci mahāvīro          anantajanataṃ sudhī    
deseti madhuraṃ dhammaṃ          catusaccūpasañhitaṃ .

Sutvāna taṃ dhammavaraṃ          ādimajjhantasobhanaṃ    
pāṇasatasahassānaṃ            dhammābhisamayo ahu .

Ninnāditā tadā bhūmi         gajjiṃsu ca payodharā    
sādhukāraṃ pavattiṃsu           devabrahmanarāsurā .

Aho kāruṇiko satthā        aho saddhammadesanā    
aho bhavasamuddasmiṃ           nimugge uddhari jino .

Evaṃ [^237-1] saṃvegajātesu       sanarāmarabrahmesu    
kulappasādakānaggaṃ           sāvakaṃ vaṇṇayi jino .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            jātomaccakule ahu    
pāsādiko dassanīyo         pahūtadhanadhaññavā .

Haṃsārāmamupeccāhaṃ          vanditvā taṃ tathāgataṃ    
suṇitvā madhuraṃ dhammaṃ          kāraṃ katvā ca tādino .

Nipacca pādamūlehaṃ           imaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
kulappasādakānaggo          yo tava sāsane mune [^237-2] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 238]*

Tādisohaṃ mahāvīra [^238-1]        buddhaseṭṭhassa sāsane    
tadā mahākāruṇiko          siñcantovāmatena maṃ .

Āha maṃ putta uttiṭṭha        lacchasetaṃ manorathaṃ    
kathaṃ nāma jine kāraṃ         katvāna viphalo siyā .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
udāyī nāma nāmena         hessati satthusāvako .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
mettacitto paricariṃ          paccayehi vināyakaṃ .

Tena kammavipākena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        ramme kāpilavatthave    
jāto mahāmaccakule         suddhodano mahīpati [^238-2] .

Tadā ajāyi siddhattho        ramme lumbinikānane    
hitāya sabbalokassa          sukhāya ca narāsabho .

Tadaheva ahaṃ jāto          saha teneva vaḍḍhito    
piyo sahāyo dayiko         vissaṭṭho [^238-3] nītikovido .

Ekūnatiṃso vayasā           nikkhanto pabbajittha so [^238-4]    
chavassāni [^238-5] vināmetvā     āsi buddho vināyako .

#[^238-1] ma. Yu. Tādisohomahaṃ vīra . [^238-2] ma. Yu. Suddhodanamahīpate . [^238-3] ma. Viyatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 239]*

Jetvā sasenakaṃ māraṃ        khepayitvāna āsave    
bhavaṇṇavaṃ taritvāna           buddho āsi sadevake .

Isivhayaṃ patanaṃ gantvā [^239-1]     vinetvā pañcavaggiye    
tato vinesi bhagavā          gantvā gantvā tahiṃ tahiṃ .

Veneyye vinayanto so      saṅgaṇhanto sadevakaṃ    
upecca magadhe giriṃ          viharittha tadā jino .

Tadā suddhodanenāhaṃ         bhūmipālena pesito    
gantvā disvā dasabalaṃ        pabbajitvārahā ahuṃ .

Tadā mahesiṃ yācitvā        pāpayiṃ kapilavhayaṃ    
tato purāhaṃ gantvāna        pasādesiṃ mahākulaṃ .

Jino tasmiṃ guṇe tuṭṭho       mamāha purisāsabho [^239-2]    
kulappasādakānaggaṃ           paññāpesi vināyako .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāḷudāyī thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kāḷudāyittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 240]*

Sattamaṃ abhayattherāpadānaṃ (547)    
[137] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammesu pāragū [^240-1]    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Saraṇāgamane keci [^240-2]        nivesesi tathāgato    
keci [^240-3] sīle nivesesi      dasakammapathuttame .

Deti kassaci so dhīro        sāmaññaphalamuttamaṃ    
samāpattī tathā aṭṭha         tisso vijjā pavecchati .

Chaḷabhiññāsu yojeti          kañci sattaṃ naruttamo    
deti kassaci so nātho       catasso paṭisambhidā .

Bodhaneyyaṃ pajaṃ disvā        asaṅkheyyepi yojane    
khaṇena upagantvāna          vineti narasārathi .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ             ahosiṃ brāhmaṇatrajo    
pāragū sabbavedānaṃ          veyyākaraṇasammato .

Niruttiyā ca kusalo          nighaṇḍe [^240-4] ca visārado    
padako keṭubhavidū            chandovicittakovido [^240-5] .

Jaṅghavihāraṃ vicaraṃ            haṃsārāmaṃ upeccahaṃ    
addasaṃ pavaraṃ [^240-6] seṭṭhaṃ       mahājanapurakkhataṃ .

Desentaṃ virajaṃ dhammaṃ         paccanikamatī ahaṃ    
upetvā tassa vākyāni [^240-7]   sutvāna vimalānahaṃ .

#[^240-1] ma. Yu. Cakkhumā . 2-[^240-3] ma. Kiñci . Yu. Kañci . [^240-4] ma. Nighaṇṭumhi . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 241]*

Byāhataṃ punaruttaṃ vā         apatthaṃ vā niratthakaṃ    
nāddasaṃ tassa munino         tato pabbajito ahaṃ .

Na cireneva kālena         sabbatthāpi visārado [^241-1]    
nipuṇe buddhavacane           ahosiṃ gaṇisammato [^241-2] .

Tadā catasso gāthāyo       ganthayitvā subyañjanā    
santhavitvā tilokaggaṃ         desayissaṃ dine dine .

Virattosi mahāvīro          saṃsāre sabhaye vasaṃ    
karuṇāya na nibbāyi          tato kāruṇiko muni .

Puthujjanova yo satto [^241-3]     na kilesavaso ahu    
sampajāno satiyutto         tasmā eso acintiyo .

Dubbalāni kilesāni          yassāsayagatāni me    
ñāṇaggiparidaḍḍhāni           na khīyiṃsu tadabbhutaṃ .

Yo sabbalokassa garu         loke [^241-4] yassa tathā garu    
tathāpi lokācariyo          loko tassānuvattako .

Evamādīhi sambuddhaṃ          kittayiṃ [^241-5] dhammadesanaṃ    
yāvajīvaṃ karitvāna           gato saggaṃ tato cuto .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Devaloke mahārajjaṃ         dibbānubhosahantadā [^241-6]    
cakkavattimahārajjaṃ           bahusonubhaviṃ ahaṃ .

#[^241-1] ma. Sabbasattavisārado . Yu. Sabbasatthavisārado . [^241-2] ma. Guṇisammato .

#[^241-3] ma. Santo . [^241-4] ma. Yu. Loko . [^241-5] ma. Yu. Kittayaṃ . [^241-6] ma. Pādesiṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 242]*

Duve bhave pajāyāmi         devatte atha mānuse    
aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi         kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Duve kule pajāyāmi         khattiye atha brāhmaṇe    
nīce kule na jānāmi        kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        giribbajapuruttame    
raññohaṃ bimbisārassa         putto nāmena cābhayo .

Pāpamittavasaṃ gantvā         nigaṇṭhena samohito [^242-1]    
pesito nāṭaputtena         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upeccahaṃ .

Pucchitvā nipuṇaṃ pañhaṃ         sutvā byākaraṇuttamaṃ    
pabbajitvāna na ciraṃ          arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Kittayitvā jinavaraṃ           kittito homi sabbadā    
sugandhadehavadano            āsiṃ sukhasamappito .

Tikkhahāsalahuppañño          mahāpañño tathevahaṃ    
vicittapaṭibhāṇo ca           tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Abhitthavitvā padumuttarāhaṃ    
pasannacitto asamaṃ sayambhuṃ    
nāgacchi kappāni apāyabhūmiṃ    
sataṃsahassāni phalena tassa .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 243]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abhayo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Abhayattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ lomasatiyattherāpadānaṃ [^243-1] (548)    
[138] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Tadāhaṃ candano ceva         pabbajitvāna sāsane    
āpāṇakoṭiyaṃ [^243-2] kammaṃ       pūrayitvāna sāsane .

Tato cutā santusitaṃ          upapannā ubho mayaṃ    
tattha dibbehi naccehi        gītehi vāditehi ca .

Rūpādidasahaṅgehi            abhibhotvāna sesake    
yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna          anubhotvā [^243-3] mahāsukhaṃ .

Tato cavitvā tidasaṃ          candano upapajjatha    
ahaṃ kapilavatthusmiṃ            ajāyiṃ [^243-4] sākiyatrajo .

Yadā udāyitherena          ajjhiṭṭho lokanāyako    
anukampiya sakyānaṃ           upesi kapilavhayaṃ .

#[^243-1] ma. Yu. Somasakaṅkiyat... . [^243-2] ma. Yu. Āpāṇakoṭikaṃ dhammaṃ . [^243-3] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 244]*

Tadātimānino sakyā         na buddhassa guṇaññuno    
paṇamanti na sambuddhaṃ          jātithaddhā anādarā .

Tesaṃ saṅkappamaññāya         ākāse caṅkami jino    
pajjunno viya vassittha        pajjalittha yathā sikhī .

Dassetvā rūpamatulaṃ          puna antaradhāyatha    
ekopi hutvā bahudhā        ahosi punarekako .

Andhakāraṃ pakāsañca          dassayitvā anekadhā    
pāṭiheraṃ karitvāna          vinayi ñātake muni .

Cātuddīpo mahāmegho        tāvadeva pavassatha [^244-1]    
tadā hi jātakaṃ buddho        vessantaramadesayi .

Tadā te khattiyā sabbe      nihantvā jātijaṃ madaṃ    
upesuṃ saraṇaṃ buddhaṃ           āha suddhodano tadā .

Idaṃ tatiyaṃ tava bhūripañña    
pādāni vandāmi samantacakkhu    
yadā hi jāto paṭhavī pakampayi    
yadā ca taṃ najjahi jambuchāyā .

Tadā buddhānubhāvantaṃ         disvā vimhitamānaso    
pabbajitvāna tattheva         nivasiṃ mātupūjako .

Candano devaputto maṃ        upagantvā apucchatha [^244-2]    
bhaddekarattassa tadā         saṅkhepavitthāraṃ nayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 245]*

Coditohaṃ tadā tena         upecca naranāyakaṃ    
bhaddekarattaṃ sutvāna         saṃviggo vanamāmako .

Tadā  mātaramāpucchi         vane vacchāmi ekako    
sukhumāloti me mātā        vārayi [^245-1] te tadā vacaṃ .

Sabbaṃ [^245-2] kusaṃ poṭakilaṃ        usīraṃ muñjapabbajaṃ    
urasā panudissāmi           vivekamanubrūhayaṃ .

Tadā vanaṃ paviṭṭhohaṃ          saritvā jinasāsanaṃ    
bhaddekarattamovādaṃ          arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Atītaṃ nānvāgameyya         nappaṭikaṅkhe anāgataṃ    
yadatītampahīnantaṃ             appattañca anāgataṃ .

Paccuppannañca yo dhammaṃ       tattha tattha vipassati    
asaṃhiraṃ asaṅkuppaṃ            taṃ viddhā anubrūhaye .

Ajjeva kiccamātappaṃ         ko jaññā maraṇaṃ suve    
na hi no saṅgarantena        mahāsenena maccunā .

Evaṃ vihārimātāpiṃ          ahorattamatanditaṃ    
taṃ ve bhaddekarattoti        santo ācikkhate muni .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 246]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā lomasatiyo thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Lomasatiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ vanavacchattherāpadānaṃ (549)    
[139] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Tadāhaṃ pabbajitvāna          tassa buddhassa sāsane    
yāvajīvaṃ caritvāna           brahmacaraṃ [^246-1] tato cuto .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tato cuto araññamhi         kapoto āsahaṃ tahiṃ    
vasate guṇasampanno          bhikkhu jhānarato sadā .

Mettacitto kāruṇiko        sadā pamuditānano    
upekkhako mahāvīro         appamaññāsu kovido .

Vinīvaraṇasaṅkappo            sabbasattahitāsayo    
vissaṭṭho na cirenāsiṃ        tasmiṃ sugatasāvake .

Upecca pādamūlamhi          nisinnassa tadāssame    
kadāci āmisaṃ [^246-2] deti       dhammaṃ desesi cekadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 247]*

Tadā vipulapemena           upāsitvā jinatrajaṃ    
tato cuto gato saggaṃ        pavāso sagharaṃ yathā .

Saggā cuto manussesu        nibbatto puññakammunā    
agāraṃ chaḍḍayitvāna          pabbajiṃ bahuso ahaṃ .

Samaṇo tāpaso dijo [^247-1]      paribbājo tathevahaṃ    
hutvā vasiṃ araññamhi         anekasatayo [^247-2] ahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        ramme kāpilavatthave    
vacchagotto dijo tassa       jāyāya ahamokkamiṃ .

Mātu me dohaḷo āsi       tirokucchigatassa me    
jāyamānasamīpamhi            vanavāsāya nicchayo .

Tato me ajani mātā        ramaṇīye vanantare    
gabbhato nikkhamantaṃ maṃ         kāsāyena paṭiggahuṃ .

Tato kumāro siddhattho       jāto sakyakuladdhajo    
tassa mitto piyo āsiṃ       saṃvissaṭṭho sumāniyo [^247-3] .

Sattasārehi nikkhante [^247-4]     ohāya vipulaṃ yasaṃ    
ahaṃpi pabbajitvāna           himavantaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Vanālayaṃ bhāvaniyaṃ            kassapaṃ dhutavādakaṃ [^247-5]    
disvā sutvā jinuppādaṃ       upesiṃ narasārathiṃ .

So me dhammaṃ adesesi       sabbatthaṃ sampakāsayaṃ    
tatohaṃ pabbajitvāna          vanameva punokkamaṃ [^247-6] .

#[^247-1] ma. Yu. Vippo . [^247-2] ma. Yu. Anekasataso . [^247-3] yu. Sumānigo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 248]*

Tatthappamatto viharaṃ          chaḷabhiññā apassayiṃ    
aho suladdhalābhomhi         sumittenānukampito .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vanavaccho thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Vanavacchattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ cūḷasugandhattherāpadānaṃ (550)    
[140] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Anubyañjanasampanno          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
byāmappabhāparivuto          raṃsijālasamogato [^248-1] .

Assāsetā yathā cando      suriyova pabhaṅkaro    
nibbāpetā yathā megho      sāgarova guṇākaro .

Dharaṇīriva sīlena             himavāva samādhinā    
ākāso viya paññāya        asaṅgo anilo yathā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 249]*

Tadāhaṃ bārāṇasiyaṃ           upapanno mahākule    
pahūtadhanadhaññasmiṃ             nānāratanasañcaye .

Mahatā parivārena           nisinnaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
upecca dhammamassosiṃ         amataṃva [^249-1] manorahaṃ  [^249-2] .

Dvattiṃsalakkhaṇadharo           sunakkhattova candimā    
anubyañjanasampanno          sālarājāva phullito .

Raṃsijālaparikkhitto           dittova kanakācalo    
byāmappabhāparivuto          sataraṃsīva [^249-3] divākaro .

Soṇṇānano jinavaro         rammaṇīva [^249-4] siluccayo    
karuṇāpuṇṇahadayo            guṇena [^249-5] viya sāgaro .

Lokavissutakitti ca           sineruva naguttamo    
yasasā vitato dhīro          ākāsasadiso muni .

Asaṅgacitto sabbattha         anilo viya nāyako    
patiṭṭhā sabbabhūtānaṃ          mahīva muni sattamo .

Anūpalitto lokena          toyena padumaṃ yathā    
kuvādagacchadahano            aggikkhandhova so [^249-6] vasi .

Agado viya sabbattha          kilesavisanāsako    
gandhamādanaselova           guṇagandhavibhūsito .

Guṇānaṃ ākaro dhīro         ratanānaṃva sāgaro    
sindhuva [^249-7] ca narājañño [^249-8]   kilesamalahārako .

#[^249-1] yu. Amataṃ ca . [^249-2] ma. Manoharaṃ . [^249-3] yu. Sarasmiṃva . [^249-4] ma. Samaṇīva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 250]*

Vijayīva mahāyodho           mārasenāppamaddano [^250-1]    
cakkavattīva so rājā        bojjhaṅgaratanissaro .

Mahābhisakkasaṅkāso          dosabyādhitikicchako    
sallakatto yathā seṭṭho [^250-2]   diṭṭhigaṇḍaviphālako .

So tadā lokapajjoto       sanarāmarasakkato    
parisāsu narādicco          dhammaṃ desayate jino .

Dānaṃ datvā mahābhogo       sīlena sugatūpago    
bhāvanāya ca nibbāti         iccevamanusāsatha .

Desanantaṃ mahassādaṃ          ādimajjhantasobhanaṃ    
suṇanti parisā sabbā         amataṃva mahārasaṃ .

Sutvā sumadhuraṃ dhammaṃ          pasanno jinasāsane    
sugataṃ saraṇaṃ gantvā          yāvajīvaṃ namassahaṃ .

Munino gandhakuṭiyā           ubbaṭṭesiṃ [^250-3] tadā mahiṃ    
catujjātena gandhena         māse aṭṭha dine svahaṃ .

Paṇidhāya sugandhantaṃ [^250-4]        sarīrassa vigandhino    
tadā jino viyākāsi         sugandhatanulābhitaṃ .

Yoyaṃ gandhakuṭibhūmiṃ            gandhena ubbaṭo [^250-5] sakiṃ    
tena kammavipākena          upapanno yahiṃ tahiṃ .

Sugandhadeho sabbattha         bhavissati ayaṃ naro    
guṇagandhāyuto hutvā         nibbāyissatināsavo .

#[^250-1] ma. Mārasenāvamaddano . [^250-2] ma. Vejjo . [^250-3] ma. Opuñjesiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 251]*

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jāto vippakule ahaṃ    
gabbhaṃ me vasato mātā       dehenāsi sugandhikā .

Yadā ca mātukucchimhā        nikkhamāmi tadā puraṃ [^251-1]    
sāvatthi sabbagandhehi         vāsitā viya vāyatha .

Pupphavassañca surabhi           dibbagandhaṃ manoramaṃ    
dhūpāni ca mahagghāni          upavāyiṃsu tāvade .

Devā ca sabbagandhehi        dhūpapupphehi taṃ gharaṃ    
vāsayiṃsu sugandhena           yasmiṃ jāto ahaṃ ghare .

Yadā ca taruṇo bhaddo        paṭhame yobbane ṭhito    
tadā sesaṃ [^251-2] saparisaṃ        vinetvā narasārathi .

Tehi sabbehi sahito [^251-3]      sāvatthipuramāgato    
tadā buddhānubhāvantaṃ         disvā pabbajito ahaṃ .

Sīlaṃ samādhipaññañca           vimuttiñca anuttaraṃ    
bhāvetvā caturo dhamme      pāpuṇiṃ āsavakkhayaṃ .

Yadā pabbajito cāhaṃ         yadā ca arahaṃ [^251-4] ahaṃ    
nibbāyissaṃ yadā cāhaṃ        gandhavasso tadā ahu .

Sarīragandho ca seṭṭhotiseti [^251-5] me    
mahārahaṃ candanaṃ campakuppalaṃ    
#[^251-1] ma. Yu. Purī . [^251-2] ma. Yu. Selaṃ . [^251-3] ma. Parivuto . [^251-4] ma. Arahā ahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 252]*

Tatheva gandhe itare ca sabbaso [^252-1]    
pasayha vāyāmi gato yahiṃ tahiṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cūḷasugandho thero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Cūḷasugandhattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
bhaddiyo revato thero       mahālābhī ca sīvalī    
vaṅgīso nandako ceva        kāḷudāyī tathābhayo .

Lomaso vanavaccho ca         sugandho dasamo kato    
tīṇi gāthāsatā tattha         soḷasā ca taduttari .

Bhaddiyavaggo pañcapaññāso .

Atha vagguddānaṃ    
kaṇikāravhayo vaggo         phalado tiṇadāyako    
kaccāno bhaddiyo vaggo      gāthāyo gaṇitā vasā [^252-2] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 253]*

nava gāthāsatāni ca          caturāsītimeva ca    
chapaññāsaṃ pañcasatā          apadānā pakāsitā .

Saha uddānagāthāhi          cha sahassāni hontimā    
dve satāni ca gāthānaṃ       aṭṭhārasa taduttari .

Ettāvatā buddhāpadānañca paccekabuddhāpadānañca therāpadānañca    
samattāni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 254]*

Therīapadānaṃ    
paṭhamo sumedhāvaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sumedhātheriyāpadānaṃ (1)    
atha therikāpadānāni suṇātha    
[141] bhagavati  konāgamane         saṅghārāmamhi nivesamhi [^254-1]    
sakhiyo tisso janiyo         vihāradānaṃ adamhase [^254-2] .

Dasakkhattuṃ satakkhattuṃ          [3] satānaṃ ca satakkhattuṃ    
devesu upapajjimha          ko vādo mānuse bhave .

Devesu mahiddhikā hutvā [^254-4]   mānusakamhi ko vādo    
sattaratanassa mahesī          itthīratanaṃ ahaṃ bhaviṃ .

Tattha [^254-5] sañcitaṃ kusalaṃ        susamiddhakulappajā    
dhanañjānī ca khemā ca        ahaṃpica tayo janā .

Ārāmaṃ sukataṃ katvā         sabbāvayavamaṇḍitaṃ    
buddhappamukhasaṅghassa           niyyādetvā pamoditā .

Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi          tassa kammassa vāhasā    
devesu aggataṃ pattā        manussesu tatheva ca .

Imasmiṃyeva kappasmiṃ          brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

#[^254-1] ma. Yu. Navanivesamhi . [^254-2] ma. Yu. Adāsimha . [^254-3] ma. Dasasatakkhattuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 255]*

Upaṭṭhāko mahesissa         tadā āsi narissaro    
kāsirājā kikī nāma         bārāṇasīpuruttame .

Tassāsuṃ satta dhītaro         rājakaññā sukhe ṭhitā    
buddhupaṭṭhānaniratā           brahmacariyaṃ cariṃsu tā .

Tāsaṃ sahāyikā hutvā        sīlesu susamāhitā    
datvā dānāni sakkaccaṃ       agāreva vattaṃ cariṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsūpagā ahaṃ .

Tato cutā yāmāsaggaṃ [^255-1]     tatohaṃ tusitaṃ gatā    
tato ca nimmānaratiṃ          vasavattipuraṃ gatā [^255-2] .

Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi          puññakammasamāhitā    
tattha tattheva rājūnaṃ         mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Tato cutā manussatte        rājūnaṃ cakkavattinaṃ    
maṇḍalīnañca rājūnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Sampattiṃ anubhotvāna         devesu mānusesu ca    
sabbattha sukhitā hutvā        nekajātīsu saṃsariṃ .

So hetu ca so pabhavo       taṃ [^255-3] mūlaṃ sāsane khamaṃ    
taṃ paṭhamaṃ samodhānaṃ           taṃ dhammaratāya nibbutaṃ [^255-4] .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

#[^255-1] ma. Yu. Yāmamagaṃ . [^255-2] ma. Yu. Tato . [^255-3] yu. Taṃ mūlaṃ sā ca sāsane khanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 256]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa [^256-1] santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ sumedhā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sumedhātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ mekhaladāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (2)    
[142] siddhatthassa bhagavato          thūpakāramakāsahaṃ [^256-2]    
mekhalikā mayā dinnā        navakammassa satthuno .

Niṭṭhite ca mahāthūpe         mekhalaṃ punadāsahaṃ    
lokanāthassa munino          pasannā sehi pāṇibhi .

Catunavute ito kappe        mekhalaṃ adadiṃ tadā [^256-3]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          thūpakārassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^256-1] ma. Yu. Mama buddhassa... . [^256-2] ma. Thūpakārāpikā ahuṃ . Yu. Thūpaṃ kārāpitā ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 257]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ mekhaladāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Mekhaladāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ maṇḍapadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (3)    
[143] konāgamanabuddhassa           maṇḍapo kārito mayā    
thūpañca [^257-1] pavaramadaṃ          buddhassa lokabandhuno .

Yaṃ yaṃ janapadaṃ yāmi           nigame rājadhāniyo    
sabbattha pūjitā homi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ maṇḍapadāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Maṇḍapadāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ saṅkamanadātheriyāpadānaṃ [^257-2] (4)    
[144] koṇḍaññassa [^257-3] bhagavato      lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
rathiyaṃ paṭipannassa            tārayantassa pāṇino .

#[^257-1] ma. Dhuvaṃ ticīvaraṃ dāsiṃ . Yu. Dhuvañca cīvaramadaṃ . [^257-2] ma. Saṅkamanatātheriyāpadānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 258]*

Gharato nikkhamitvāna          avakujja [^258-1] nipajjahaṃ    
anukampako lokajeṭṭho [^258-2]    sirasi akkami tadā [^258-3] .

Akkamitvāna sirasi           agamā lokanāyako    
tena cittappasādena         tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ saṅkamanadā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Saṅkamanadātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ naḷamālikātheriyāpadānaṃ (5)    
[145] candabhāgānadītīre           ahosiṃ kinnarī tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ           sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ .

Pasannacittā sumanā          vedajātā katañjalī    
naḷamālaṃ gahetvāna          sayambhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā kinnarīdehaṃ          agacchiṃ tidasaṃ gaṇaṃ [^258-4] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 259]*

Chattiṃsadevarājūnaṃ            mahesittamakārayiṃ    
dasannaṃ cakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Vedayitvāna kusalaṃ [^259-1]        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā [^259-2] sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā         natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Catunavute ito kappe        yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ naḷamālikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Naḷamālikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ekapiṇḍapātadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (6)    
[146] nagare bandhumatiyā           bandhumā nāma khattiyo    
tassa rañño ahaṃ [^259-4] bhariyā    ekaccaṃ vādayāmahaṃ [^259-5] .

#[^259-1] ma. Saṃvejetvāna me cittaṃ . Yu. Saṃvedayitvā kusalaṃ . [^259-2] yu. Bhavā saṅghāṭitā mamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 260]*

Rahogatā nisīditvā          evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
ādāya gamanīyaṃ hi           kusalaṃ natthi me kataṃ .

Mahābhitāpaṃ kaṭukaṃ [^260-1]         ghorarūpaṃ sudāruṇaṃ    
nirayaṃ nūna gacchāmi           ettha me natthi saṃsayo .

Rājānaṃ upagantvāna [^260-2]      idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
ekaṃ me samaṇaṃ dehi         bhojayissāmi khattiya .

Adāsi me mahārājā        samaṇaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ    
tassa pattaṃ gahetvāna        paramannena tappayiṃ .

Pūjayitvā paramannaṃ           gandhālepaṃ akāsahaṃ    
jālena pidahitvāna          pītacolena [^260-3] chādayiṃ .

Ārammaṇaṃ mama etaṃ          sarāmi yāvajīvitaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tiṃsānaṃ devarājūnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ    
manasā patthitaṃ mayhaṃ          nibbattati yathicchakaṃ [^260-4] .

Vīsānaṃ cakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ    
upacitattā [^260-5] hutvāna       saṃsarāmi bhavābhave [^260-6] .

Sabbabandhanamuttāhaṃ           apetā me upādikā    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
#[^260-1] yu. Dukkhaṃ . [^260-2] ma. Yu. Upasaṅkamma . [^260-3] ma. Vatthayugena . Yu. Mahānelena .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 261]*

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ ekapiṇḍapātadāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekapiṇḍapātadāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ kaṭacchubhikkhadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (7)    
[147] piṇḍacāraṃ carantassa          tissanāmassa satthuno    
kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ paggayha          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Paṭiggahetvā sambuddho       tisso lokagganāyako    
vīthiyā saṇṭhito satthā        akā me anumodanaṃ .

Kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ datvāna          tāvatiṃsaṃ gamissasi    
chattiṃsadevarājūnaṃ            mahesittaṃ karissasi .

Paññāsacakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittaṃ karissasi    
manasā patthitaṃ sabbaṃ          paṭilacchasi sabbadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 262]*

Sampattiṃ anubhotvāna         pabbajissasikiñcanā    
sabbāsave pariññāya         nibbāyissasināsavā .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho        tisso lokagganāyako    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro          haṃsarājāva ambare .

Sudinnameva [^262-1] me dānaṃ      suyiṭṭhā yāgasampadā    
kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ datvāna          pattāhaṃ acalaṃ padaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          bhikkhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ kaṭacchubhikkhadāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Kaṭacchubhikkhadāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sattaüppalamālikātheriyāpadānaṃ (8)    
[148] nagare aruṇavatiyā           aruṇo [^262-2] nāma khattiyo    
tassa rañño ahaṃ bhariyā       nagulaṃ pādayāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 263]*

Satta mālā gahetvāna       uppalā [^263-1] devagandhikā    
nisajja pāsādavare          evaṃ cintesi tāvade .

Kiṃ me imāhi mālāhi        sirasā ropitāhi me    
varaṃ me buddhaseṭṭhassa        ñāṇamhi abhiropitaṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ paṭimānentī         dvārāsanne nisīdahaṃ    
yadā ehiti [^263-2] sambuddho     pūjayissaṃ mahāmuniṃ .

Kakudho vilasantova           migarājāva kesarī    
bhikkhusaṅghena sahito          āgacchi vīthiyā jino .

Buddhassa raṃsiṃ disvāna         haṭṭhā saṃviggamānasā    
dvāraṃ apāpuṇitvāna         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Satta uppalapupphāni          suvitthiṇṇāni [^263-3] ambare    
chādiṃ [^263-4] karontā buddhassa    matthake dhārayanti te .

Udaggacittā sumanā          vedajātā katañjalī    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Mahānelassa chādanaṃ          dhārenti mama muddhani    
dibbagandhaṃ pavāyati [^263-5]        sattuppalānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kadāci nīyamānāya           ñātisaṅghena me tadā    
yāvatā parisā mayhaṃ         mahānelaṃ dharīyati .

Sattatidevarājūnaṃ            mahesittaṃ akārayiṃ    
sabbattha issarā hutvā       saṃsarāmi bhavābhave .

#[^263-1] yu. Uppalī . [^263-2] yu. Etiha . [^263-3] ma. Parikiṇṇāni . Yu. Paritthiṇṇāni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 264]*

tesaṭṭhicakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittaṃ akārayiṃ    
sabbe maṃ anuvattanti         ādeyyavacanā ahaṃ .

Uppalasseva me vaṇṇo       gandho ceva pavāyati    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Iddhipādesu kusalā          bojjhaṅgabhāvanāratā    
abhiññāpāramippattā         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satipaṭṭhānakusalā            samādhijhānagocarā    
sammappadhānamanuyuttā         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ         yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ sattaüppalamālikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sattaüppalamālikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 265]*

Navamaṃ pañcadīpikātheriyāpadānaṃ (9)    
[149] nagare haṃsavatiyā            cārinī [^265-1] āsahaṃ tadā    
ārāmeneva ārāmaṃ        carāmi kusalatthikā .

Kālapakkhamhi divase          addasaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā       bodhimūle nisīdahaṃ .

Garucittaṃ upaṭṭhetvā         sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
somanassaṃ pavedetvā        evaṃ cintesi tāvade .

Yadi buddho amitaguṇo         asamappaṭipuggalo    
dassetu pāṭihīraṃ me         bodhi obhāsatu ayaṃ .

Saha āvajjite mayhaṃ         bodhi pajjali tāvade    
sabbasovaṇṇamayā āsi        disā sabbā virocati .

Sattarattindivaṃ tattha          bodhimūle nisīdahaṃ    
sattame divase patte        dīpapūjaṃ akāsahaṃ .

Āsanaṃ parivāretvā         pañcadīpāni pajjalala    
yāva udeti suriyo          dīpā me pajjalala tadā .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        pañcadīpāti [^265-2] vuccati    
satayojanamubbedhaṃ [^265-3]         saṭṭhiyojanavitthataṃ .

#[^265-1] ma. Yu. Cārikī . [^265-2] yu. Pañcadīpīti . [^265-3] ma saṭṭhiyojanamubbedhaṃ tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 266]*

Asaṅkheyyāni dīpāni         parivāre jalanti me    
yāvatā devabhavanaṃ           dīpālokena jotati .

Puritthābhimukhā santi [^266-1]       yadi icchāmi passituṃ    
uddhaṃ adho ca tiriyaṃ          sabbaṃ passāmi cakkhunā .

Yāvatā abhikaṅkhāmi          daṭṭhaṭha sukatadukkaṭaṃ [^266-2]    
tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthi          rukkhesu pabbatesu vā .

Asītidevarājūnaṃ             mahesittamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ cakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
dīpasatasahassāni             parivāre jalanti me .

Devalokā cavitvāna         uppajjiṃ [^266-3] mātukucchiyaṃ    
mātu kucchagataṃ santaṃ [^266-4]       akkhi me na nimīlati .

Dīpasatasahassāni             puññakammasamaṅgino [^266-5]    
sūtighareva jalanti            pañcadīpānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         mānasaṃ vinivattayiṃ    
ajarāmaraṃ [^266-6] sītibhāvaṃ        nibbānaṃ passayiṃ [^266-7] ahaṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassāhaṃ         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho          guṇamaññāya gotamo .

Maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā        pāsādesu guhāsu vā    
#[^266-1] ma. Parammukhā nisīditvā . [^266-2] ma. Sukatadukkaṭe . Yu. Dassuṃ sukatadukkaṭe . [^266-3] ma.

#yu. Upajja . [^266-4] ma. Yu. Kucchagatā santī . [^266-5] ma. Samaṅagitā . Yu. Samaṅginā . [^266-6] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 267]*

Suññāgāre pajjhāyantā [^267-1]   pañcadīpā jalanti me .

Dibbacakkhu visuddhaṃ me         samādhikusalā ahaṃ    
abhiññāpāramippattā         pañcadīpānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sabbavositavosānā          katakiccā anāsavā    
pañcadīpā mahāvīra           pāde vandāmi cakkhumā .

Satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dīpamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          pañcadīpānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ pañcadīpikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Pañcadīpikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ udakadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (10)    
[150] nagare bandhumatiyā           āsiṃ [^267-2] udakahārikā    
udahārena jīvāmi           tena posemi dārake .

Deyyadhammo ca me natthi      puññakkhette anuttare    
koṭṭhakaṃ upasaṅkamma          udakaṃ paṭṭhapesahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 268]*

Tena kammena sukatena        tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ    
tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        udahārena nimmitaṃ .

Accharānaṃ sahassassa          ahaṃ hi pavarā tadā    
dasaṭṭhānehi tā sabbā       abhibhomi tadā ahaṃ .

Paññāsaṃ devarājūnaṃ          mahesittamakārayiṃ    
vīsaticakkavattīnaṃ             mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          udakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pabbatagge dumagge vā       antalikkhe ca bhūmiyaṃ    
yadā udakamicchāmi           khippaṃ paṭilabhāmahaṃ .

Avuṭṭhikā disā atthi [^268-1]      santattā khuppitā [^268-2] hi me    
mama saṅkappamaññāya          mahāmegho pavassati .

Kadāci nīyamānāya           ñātisaṅghena me tadā    
yadā icchāmahaṃ vassaṃ         mahāmegho ajāyatha .

Uṇhaṃ vā pariḷāho vā       sarīre [^268-3] me na vijjati    
kāye ca me rajo natthi      udakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Visuddhamanasā ajja           apetamanapāpikā    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā [^268-4]    
#[^268-1] ma. Yu. Natthi . [^268-2] ma. Kutitāpi ca . Yu. Santattakaṭhitāna ca . [^268-3] yu. Athameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 269]*

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          udakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ udakadāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Udakadāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sumedhā mekhaladadā          maṇḍapasaṅkamantathā    
naḷamālinī piṇḍadadā          kaṭacchuuppalappadā .

Dīpaüdakadāyī ca             gāthāyo gaṇitāviha    
ekagāthā satañceva         sattarasaṃ taduttari .

Sumedhāvaggo paṭhamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 270]*

Dutiyo ekuposathavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ekuposathikātheriyāpadānaṃ (11)    
[151] nagare bandhumatiyā           bandhumā nāma khattiyo    
divase puṇṇamāya so         upavasi [^270-1] uposathaṃ .

Ahaṃ tena samayena           kumbhadāsī ahu [^270-2] tahiṃ    
disvā sarājikaṃ senaṃ         evāhaṃ cintayiṃ tadā .

Rājāpi rajjaṃ chaḍḍetvā      upavasi [^270-1] uposathaṃ    
saphalaṃ nūna taṃ kammaṃ           janakāyo pamodito .

Yoniso paccavekkhitvā       duggatiñca [^270-3] daliddataṃ    
mānasaṃ sampahaṃsitvā          upavasiṃ [^270-4] uposathaṃ .

Ahaṃ uposathaṃ katvā          sammāsambuddhasāsane    
tena kammena sukatena        tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        uddhaṃ [^270-5] yojanamuggataṃ    
kūṭāgāravarūpetaṃ            mahāsanaṃ subhūsitaṃ .

Accharā satasahassā          upatiṭṭhanti maṃ sadā    
aññādeva [^270-6] atikkamma      atirocāmi sabbadā .

Catusaṭṭhidevarājūnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ    
tesaṭṭhicakkavattīnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ .

#[^270-1] yu. Upapajji . [^270-2] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ . [^270-3] ma. Yu. Daggaccañca . [^270-4] yu. Upapajjiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 271]*

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇā hutvāna         bhavesu saṃsarāmahaṃ    
sabbattha pavarā homi         uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ          rathayānañca kevalaṃ [^271-1]    
labhāmi sabbametampi [^271-2]       uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sovaṇṇamayaṃ rūpimayaṃ           athopi phalikāmayaṃ    
lohitaṅkamayañceva           sabbaṃ paṭilabhāmahaṃ .

Koseyyakambalakāni [^271-3]       khomakappāsikāni ca    
mahagghāni ca vatthāni         sabbaṃ paṭilabhāmahaṃ .

Annapānaṃ khādanīyaṃ           vatthasenāsanāni ca    
sabbametaṃ paṭilabhe           uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Varagandhañca mālañca          cuṇṇakañca vilepanaṃ    
sabbametaṃ paṭilabhe           uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kūṭāgārañca pāsādaṃ         maṇḍapaṃ hammiyaṃ guhaṃ    
sabbametaṃ paṭilabhe           uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassāhaṃ         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
[4]    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          uposathassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^271-1] ma. Sīvikaṃ . [^271-2] ma. Sabbamevetaṃ . [^271-3] ma. Yu. ...Kambaliyāni . [^271-4] ma. Yu. Kilesā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 272 -->
<a id="page-272"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 272]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ ekuposathikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ekuposathikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ salaḷapupphikātheriyāpadānaṃ (12)    
[152] candabhāgānadītīre           ahosiṃ kinnarī tadā    
athaddasaṃ devadevaṃ           caṅkamantaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Ocinitvā salaḷapupphaṃ         buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
upasiṅghi mahāvīro           salaḷaṃ devagandhikaṃ [^272-1] .

Paṭiggahetvā sambuddho       vipassī lokanāyako    
upasiṅghi mahāvīro           pekkhamānāya me tadā .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna         vanditvā dipaduttamaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā        tato pabbatamāruhiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 273 -->
<a id="page-273"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 273]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salaḷapupphikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Salaḷapupphikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .Z    
tatiyaṃ modakadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (13)    
[153] nagare bandhumatiyā           kumbhadāsī ahosahaṃ    
mama bhāgaṃ gahetvāna         gacchaṃ udakahārike [^273-1] .

Panthamhi samaṇaṃ disvā         santacittaṃ samāhitaṃ    
pasannacittā sumanā          modake tīṇidāsahaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
ekūnatiṃsa [^273-2] kappāni        vinipātaṃ na agañchahaṃ .

Sampattiñca karitvāna         sabbaṃ anubhaviṃ ahaṃ    
modake tīṇi datvāna         pattāhaṃ acalaṃ padaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 274]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā modakadāyikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Modakadāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ekāsanadāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (14)    
[154] nagare haṃsavatiyā            ahosiṃ mālikā [^274-1] tadā    
mātā ca me pitā ceva      kammantaṃ agamaṃsu te .

Majjhantikamhi suriye          addasaṃ samaṇaṃ ahaṃ    
vīthiyā anugacchantaṃ           āsanaṃ paññapesahaṃ .

Goṇakacittakādīhi [^274-2]         paññāpetvāhamāsanaṃ [^274-3]    
pasannacittā sumanā          imaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Santattā kaṭhitā bhūmi         sūro majjhantiko [^274-4] ṭhito    
mātulā ca na vāyanti        kālo cevatthamehiti [^274-5] .

Paññattamāsanamidaṃ            tavatthāya mahāmuni    
anukampaṃ upādāya           nisīda mama āsane .

Nisīdi tattha samaṇo           sudanto suddhamānaso    
tassa pattaṃ gahetvāna        yathārandhaṃ adāsahaṃ .

#[^274-1] ma. Bālikā . [^274-2] ma. Yu. Goṇakāvikatikāhi . [^274-3] ma. Paññāpetvā mamāsanaṃ . Yu. ...


<!-- Siam Rath Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 275]*

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ        āsanena sunimmitaṃ    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbedhaṃ           tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Soṇṇamayā maṇimayā          athopi phalikāmayā    
lohitaṅkamayā ceva          pallaṅkā vividhā mama .

Tūlikāvikatīhi [^275-1] ca          kaṭissācittakāhi [^275-2] ca    
uddhaṃ ekantalomi ca         pallaṅkā [^275-3] me susaṇṭhitā .

Yadā icchāmi gamanaṃ          hāsakhiḍḍāsamappitā [^275-4]    
saha pallaṅkaseṭṭhena         gacchāmi mama patthitaṃ .

Asītidevarājūnaṃ             mahesittamakārayiṃ    
sattaticakkavattīnaṃ            mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsarantī           mahābhogaṃ labhāmahaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi       ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ .

Duve bhave saṃsarāmi          devatte atha mānuse    
aññe bhave na jānāmi       ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ .

Duve kule pajāyāmi         khattiye cāpi brāhmaṇe    
uccākulikā [^275-5] sabbattha      ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ .

Domanassaṃ na jānāmi         cittasantāpanaṃ mama    
vevaṇṇiyaṃ na jānāmi         ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ .

#[^275-1] yu. ...Vikatikāhi ca . [^275-2] ma. Kaṭissacittakāhi ca . Yu. Kaṭṭhissāvikatikāhi ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 276 -->
<a id="page-276"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 276]*

Dhātiyo maṃ upaṭṭhanti         khujjā celāvakā [^276-1] bahū    
aṅgena aṅgaṃ gacchāmi        ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ .

Aññā [^276-2] mameva nhāpenti   aññā bhojenti bhojanaṃ    
aññā maṃ alaṅkaronti        aññā ramenti maṃ sadā .

Aññā gandhaṃ vilimpanti        ekāsanaphalaṃ idaṃ    
maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā        suññāgāre vasantiyā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya          pallaṅko upatiṭṭhati    
ayaṃ pacchimako mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo .

Ajjāpi rajjaṃ chaḍḍetvā      pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
satasahasse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā .
