Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi          tassa kammassa vāhasā    
sañjalanti [^341-1] mahādīpā       tattha tattha gatāya me .

Tirokuḍḍaṃ tiroselaṃ          samatiggayha pabbataṃ    
passāmahaṃ yadicchāmi          dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Visuddhanayanā homi           yasasā pajjalāmahaṃ [^341-2]    
saddhā paññavatī [^341-3] ceva      dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātā vippakule ahaṃ    
pahūtadhanadhaññamhi             mudite rājapūjite .

Ahaṃ sabbaṅgasampannā         sabbābharaṇabhūsitā    
purappavese sugataṃ           vātapāne ṭhitā ahaṃ .

Disvā jalantaṃ yasasā         devamanussasakkataṃ    
anubyañjanasampannaṃ           lakkhaṇehi vibhūsitaṃ .

Udaggacittā sumanā          pabbajjaṃ samarocayiṃ    
na cireneva kālena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
paracittāni jānāmi          satthusāsanakārikā .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
khepetvā āsave sabbe     visuddhāsiṃ sunimmalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 342 -->
<a id="page-342"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 342]*

Pariciṇṇo mayā satthā        kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ    
ohito garuko bhāro        bhavanetti samūhatā .

Yassatthāya pabbajitā         agārasmā anagāriyaṃ    
so me attho anuppatto     sabbasaṃyojanakkhayo .

Tato mahākāruṇiko          etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ    
dibbacakkhukānaṃ [^342-1] aggā      sakulāti naruttamo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ sakulā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sakulātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ nandātheriyāpadānaṃ (25)    
[165] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Ovādako viññāpako        tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 343]*

Anukampako kāruṇiko         hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe      pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi [^343-1] .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi          suññataṃ titthiyehi ca    
vicittaṃ arahantehi           vasībhūtehi tādibhi .

Ratanānaṭṭhapaññāsaṃ           uggato [^343-2] so mahāmuni    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Vassasatasahassāni            āyu vijjati [^343-3] tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so       tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ             jātā seṭṭhikule ahu    
nānāratanapajjote          mahāsukhasamappitā .

Upetvā taṃ mahāvīraṃ         assosiṃ dhammadesanaṃ    
amataṃ paramassādaṃ            paramatthaṃ nivedakaṃ .

Tadā nimantayitvāna          sasaṅghaṃ lokanāyakaṃ [^343-4]    
datvā tassa mahādānaṃ        pasannā sehi pāṇibhi .

Jhāyinīnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ            aggaṭṭhānaṃ apatthayiṃ    
nipacca sirasā vīraṃ           sasaṅghaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Tadā adantadamako           tilokasaraṇo pabhū    
byākāsi narasundharo [^343-5]      lacchasetaṃ supatthitaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

#[^343-1] ma. Yu. Patiṭṭhapi . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^343-2] ma. Uggato va mahāmuni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 344]*

Tassa dhammesu dāyādā       orasā dhammanimmitā    
nandāti nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvikā .

Taṃ sutvā muditā hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
mettacittā paricariṃ          paccayehi vināyakaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tato cutā yāmamagaṃ          tatohaṃ tusitaṃ gatā [^344-1]    
tato ca nimmānaratiṃ          vasavattipuraṃ gatā .

Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi          tassa kammassa vāhasā    
tattha tattheva rājūnaṃ         mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Tato cutā manussatte        rājūnaṃ cakkavattinaṃ    
maṇḍalīnañca rājūnaṃ           mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Sampattiṃ anubhotvāna         devesu manujesu ca    
sabbattha sukhitā hutvā        nekakappesu saṃsariṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         purasmiṃ [^344-2] kapilavhaye    
rañño suddhodanassāhaṃ        dhītā āsiṃ aninditā .

Raṃsiriva [^344-3] rūpiniṃ disvā       nanditaṃ āsi taṃ kulaṃ    
tena nandāti me nāmaṃ       sundarā [^344-4] pavarā ahaṃ .

Yuvatīnañca sabbāsaṃ           kalyāṇīti ca vissutā    
tasmiṃpi nagare ramme         ṭhapetvā taṃ yasodharaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 345]*

Jeṭṭho bhātā tilokaggo     pacchimo [^345-1] arahā tathā    
ekakinī [^345-2] gahaṭṭhāhaṃ        mātuyā paricoditā .

Sākiyamhi kule jātā        putte buddhānujā tuvaṃ    
nandenapi vinā bhūtā         agāre kiṃ nu lacchasi [^345-3] .

Jarāvasānaṃ yobbaññaṃ         rūpaṃ asucisammataṃ    
rogantamapicārogyaṃ          jīvitaṃ maraṇantikaṃ .

Idaṃpi te subhaṃ rūpaṃ           sasikantaṃ [^345-4] manoharaṃ    
bhūsanānaṃ [^345-5] alaṅkāraṃ        sirisaṅketasannibhaṃ .

Pūjitaṃ lokasāraṃva            nayanānaṃ rasāyanaṃ    
puññānaṃ kittijananaṃ           okkākakulanandanaṃ .

Na cireneva kālena         jarā samabhibhossati [^345-6]    
pahāya [^345-7] gehaṃ gārayhaṃ      cara dhammamanindite .

Sutvāhaṃ mātu vacanaṃ          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
dehena na tu cittena        rūpayobbanalolitā [^345-8] .

Mahatā ca payattena          jhānajjhena saraṃ mamaṃ    
kātuñca vadate mātā        na cāhaṃ tattha ussukā .

Tato mahākāruṇiko          disvā maṃ kamalānanaṃ [^345-9]    
nibbindanatthaṃ rūpasmiṃ          mama cakkhupathe jino .

Sakena ānubhāvena          itthiṃ māpesi sobhanaṃ 10    
dassanīyaṃ suruciraṃ             mamatopi surūpiniṃ .

#[^345-1] yu. Majjhimo . [^345-2] ma. Yu. Ekākinī . [^345-3] ma. ... Nu acchasi . Yu. ... Na    
#acchasi . [^345-4] yu. Passa kantaṃ ... . [^345-5] yu. Maṃ bhūsanaṃ alaṅkāraṃ . [^345-6] ma. Samadhisessati .

#yu. Samadhihessati . [^345-7] ma. Yu. Vihāya gehaṃ kāruññe . [^345-8] ma. Yu. ...Lāḷitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 346]*

Tamahaṃ vimhitā disvā         ativimhitadehiniṃ [^346-1]    
cintayiṃ saphalaṃ meti           nettalābhañca mānusaṃ .

Tamahaṃ ehi subhaṇe [^346-2]        yenattho taṃ vadehi me    
kulante nāmagottañca        vada me yadi te piyaṃ .

Na pañhakālo [^346-3] subhaṇe      ucchaṅge maṃ nivesaya [^346-4]    
sīdantiva mamaṅgāni           pasuppaya [^346-5] muhuttakaṃ .

Tato sīsaṃ mamaṅke sā        katvā sayi susobhaṇā [^346-6]    
tassā lalāte [^346-7] patitā     luddhā paramadāruṇā .

Saha tassā nipātena         pīḷakā upapajjatha    
pagghariṃsu pabhinnā ca          kuṇapā pubbalohitā .

Sambhinnaṃ [^346-8] vadanañcāpi       kuṇapapūtigandhikaṃ [^346-9]    
uddhumātaṃ vinīlañca           sabbañcāpi 10 sarīrakaṃ .

Sā pavedhitasabbaṅgī          nissasantī mahuṃ mahuṃ    
vedayantī sakaṃ dukkhaṃ          karuṇaṃ paridevayi .

Dukkhena dukkhitā homi        phusayantī ca vedanā    
mahādukkhe nimuggamhi         saraṇaṃ hohi me sakhī .

Kuhiṃ vadanasobhante           kuhinte tuṅganāsikā    
tambabimbavaroṭṭhante         vadanante kuhiṃ gataṃ .

Kuhiṃ sasinibhaṃ vaṇṇaṃ            kambugivā kuhiṃ gatā    
dāmāmālañca te kaṇṇaṃ       vevaṇṇaṃ samupāgataṃ .[^346-2]    
#[^346-1] yu. Avinicchitadehiniṃ . [^346-2] ma. Yu. Subhage . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^346-3] ma. Vañcakālo .

#[^346-4] ma. Nivāsaya . [^346-5] yu. Passayissaṃ . [^346-6] ma. Yu. Sulocanā . [^346-7] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 347]*

Makulapadumākārā [^347-1]         kalasāva [^347-2] payodharā    
pabhinnā pūtikuṇapā           duṭṭhagandhajamāgatā [^347-3] .

Vedimajjhā [^347-4] puthussoṇi      suṇāva nītakibbisā    
jātā amajjhabharitā          aho rūpaṃ asassataṃ .

Sabbaṃ sarīrasañjātaṃ           pūtigandhaṃ bhayānakaṃ    
susānamiva jegucchaṃ [^347-5]        ramante yattha bāliyā [^347-6] .

Tadā mahākāruṇiko          bhātā me lokanāyako    
disvā saṃviggacittaṃ maṃ         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Āturaṃ kuṇapaṃ pūtiṃ            passa nande samussayaṃ    
asubhāya cittaṃ bhāvehi        ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ .

Yathā idaṃ tathā etaṃ         yathā etaṃ tathā idaṃ    
duggandhaṃ pūtikaṃ vāti          bālānaṃ abhinanditaṃ .

Evametaṃ avekkhanti         rattindivamatanditā    
tato sakāya paññāya         abhinibbijja dakkhasi .

Tatohaṃ āsi [^347-7] saṃviggā      sutvā gāthā subhāsitā    
tatraṭṭhitāvahaṃ santī          arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Yattha yattha nisinnāhaṃ         tadā jhānaparāyanā    
jino tasmiṃ guṇe tuṭṭho       etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

#[^347-1] ma. Yu. Makuḷakhārakākārā . [^347-2] ma. Kalikāva . [^347-3] ma. Duṭṭhagandhittamāgatā .

#yu. ...Gandhitavamāgatā . [^347-4] ma. Vedimajjhāva susussoṇī . [^347-5] ma. Bībhacchaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 348]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ nandā bhikkhunī janapadakalyāṇī imā gāthāyo    
abhāsitthāti .

Nandātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ soṇātheriyāpadānaṃ (26)    
[166] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Tadā [^348-1] seṭṭhikule jātā    sukhitā sajjitā [^348-2] piyā    
upetvā taṃ munivaraṃ          assosiṃ madhuraṃ vacaṃ .

Āraddhaviriyānaggaṃ           vaṇṇeti [^348-3] bhikkhuniṃ jino [^348-4]    
taṃ sutvā muditā hutvā       kāraṃ katvāna satthuno .

Abhivādiya sambuddhaṃ           taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ tadā    
anumodi mahāvīro           sijjhataṃ paṇidhi tava .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 349]*

Tassa dhammesu dāyādā       orasā dhammanimmitā    
soṇāti nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvikā .

Taṃ sutvā muditā hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tathā jinaṃ    
mettacittā paricariṃ          paccayehi vināyakaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātā seṭṭhikule ahaṃ    
sāvatthiyaṃ puravare           iddhe phīte mahaddhane .

Yadā ca yobbanaṃ pattā       gantvā patikulaṃ ahaṃ    
dasa puttāni ajaniṃ           surūpāni visesato .

Sukhe ṭhitā ca te sabbe      jananettamanoharā    
amittānampi rucitā          mama [^349-1] pageva te piyā .

Tato mayhaṃ akāmāya         dasaputtapurakkhato    
pabbajittha sa me bhattā       devadevassa sāsane .

Tadekikā vicintesiṃ          jīvitenālamatthu me    
jināya [^349-2] patiputtehi        buddhāyapi [^349-3] varākiyā .

Ahaṃpi tattha gacchissaṃ          sampatto yattha me pati    
evāhaṃ cintayitvāna         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Tato mamaṃ [^349-4] bhikkhuniyo       ekaṃ bhikkhunupassaye    
vihāya gañchumovādaṃ          tāpehi udakaṃ iti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 350]*

Tadā udakamānetvā [^350-1]      okiritvāna kumbhiyā    
culle ṭhapetvā āsinā      tato cittaṃ samādahiṃ .

Khandhe aniccato disvā       dukkhato ca anattato    
khepetvā āsave sabbe     arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Tadāgantvā bhikkhuniyo        uṇhodakamapucchisuṃ    
tejodhātuṃ adhiṭṭhāya         khippaṃ santāpayiṃ jalaṃ .

Vimhitā tā jinavaraṃ          etamatthamasāvayuṃ [^350-2]    
taṃ sutvā mudito nātho       imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Yo ca vassasataṃ jīve         kusīto hīnavīriyo    
ekāhaṃ jīvitaṃ seyyo        viriyārabhato daḷhaṃ .

Ārādhito mahāvīro         mayā suppaṭipattiyā    
āraddhaviriyānaggaṃ           mamāha sa mahāmuni .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ soṇā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Soṇātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 351]*

Sattamaṃ bhaddakāpilānītheriyāpadānaṃ (27)    
[167] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna [^351-1] pāragū    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Tadā [^351-2] hi haṃsavatiyaṃ         videho nāma nāmako    
seṭṭhī pahūtaratano           tassa jāyā ahosahaṃ .

Kadāci so narādiccaṃ         upecca saparijjano    
dhammaṃ assosi buddhassa        sabbadukkhakkhayāvahaṃ [^351-3] .

Sāvakaṃ dhutavādānaṃ           aggaṃ kittesi nāyako    
sutvā sattāhikaṃ dānaṃ        datvā buddhassa tādino .

Nipacca sirasā pāde         taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ [^351-4]    
sa hāsayanto parisaṃ          tadā hi narapuṅgavo .

Seṭṭhino anukampāya         imā gāthā abhāsatha    
lacchase patthitaṃ ṭhānaṃ         nibbuto hohi puttaka .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo       oraso dhammanimmito    
kassapo nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Taṃ sutvā mudito hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
mettacitto paricari          paccayehi vināyakaṃ .

#[^351-1] yu. Sabbadhammesu cakkhumā . [^351-2] ma. Yu. Tadāhu . [^351-3] ma. Sabbadukkhabhayappahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 352]*

Sāsanaṃ jotayitvāna          so madditvā kutitthiye    
veneyyaṃ vinayitvāna [^352-1]      nibbuto so sasāvako .

Nibbute tamhi lokagge       pūjanatthāya satthuno    
ñātimitte samānetvā       saha tehi akārayi .

Sattayojanikaṃ thūpaṃ            ubbedhaṃ ratanāmayaṃ    
jalantaṃ sataraṃsīva             sālarājaṃva phullitaṃ .

Sattasatasahassāni            cātiyo [^352-2] satta kārayiṃ    
naḷaggi viya jotante [^352-3]      rataneheva sattahi .

Gandhatelehi pūretvā        dīpāni ujjalayiṃ tahiṃ    
pūjanatthāya mahesissa         sabbasattānukampino [^352-4] .

Sattasatasahassāni            puṇṇakumbhāni kārayiṃ    
rataneheva puṇṇāni          pūjanatthāya mahesino .

Majjhe aṭṭhaṭṭhakumbhīnaṃ         ussitā kañcanagghiyo    
atirocanti vaṇṇena          sāradeva divākaro .

Catudvāresu sobhanti         toraṇā ratanāmayā    
ussitā phalakā rammā        sobhanti ratanāmayā .

Virocanti parikkhāyo [^352-5]      avaṭaṃsā sunimmitā    
ussitāni paṭākāni          ratanāni virocare .

Surattaṃ sukatañcetaṃ [^352-6]        cetiyaṃ ratanāmayaṃ    
atirocati vaṇṇena           sasajjhāva divākaro .

#[^352-1] ma. Vinayitvā ca . [^352-2] ma. Pātiyo tattha kārayi . Yu. ... Kāriyiṃ . [^352-3] ma. Jotantī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 353]*

Thūpassa me disā tisso [^353-1]    haritālena pūrayiṃ [^353-2]    
ekā [^353-3] manosilāyekā     añjanena ca ekikā .

Pūjaṃ etādisaṃ rammaṃ          kāretvā varavādino [^353-4]    
adāsiṃ dānaṃ saṅghassa         yāvajīvaṃ yathābalaṃ .

Sahāhaṃ [^353-5] seṭṭhinā tena     tāni puññāni sabbaso    
yāvajīvaṃ karitvāna           sahāva sugatiṃ gatā .

Sampattiyonubhutvāna [^353-6]       devatte atha mānuse    
chāyā viya sarīrena          saha teneva saṃsariṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe       vipassī nāma nāyako    
uppajji cārunayano          sabbadhammavipassako .

Tadā hi bandhumatiyā          brāhmaṇo sādhusammato    
addho santo guṇenāsi       dhanena ca suduggato .

Tadāpi tassāhaṃ āsiṃ         brāhmaṇī samacetasā    
kadāci so dijavaro          saṅgamesi mahāmuniṃ .

Nisinnaṃ janakāyamhi           desentaṃ amataṃpadaṃ    
sutvā dhammaṃ pamudito         adāsi ekasāṭakaṃ .

Gharaṃ ekena vatthena         gantvānetaṃ mamabravi    
anumoda mahāpuññe          dinnaṃ buddhassa sāṭakaṃ .

Tadāhaṃ añjaliṃ katvā         anumodiṃ suviditā    
sudinno sāṭako sāmi        buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino .

#[^353-1] ma. Yu. Vedayo . [^353-2] ma. Pūrayi . [^353-3] ma. Yu. Ekaṃ manosilāyekaṃ ... Ekikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 354]*

Sukhito sajjito hutvā        saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
bārāṇasīpure ramme         rājā āsi mahīpati .

Tadā tassa mahesīhaṃ          itthīgumbassa uttamā    
tassāpi [^354-1] dutiyikā āsiṃ     pubbasinehena uttari [^354-2] .

Piṇḍāya vicarante so        aṭṭha paccekanāyake    
disvā pamudito hutvā        datvā piṇḍaṃ mahārahaṃ .

Buddhe [^354-3] nimantayitvāna      katvā ratanamaṇḍapaṃ    
kammārehi katamaṭṭhaṃ [^354-4]       sovaṇṇaṃ satahatthakaṃ [^354-5] .

Samānetvāna te sabbe      tesaṃ dānamadāsi so    
senāsane [^354-6] paviṭṭhānaṃ      pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Taṃpi dānaṃ sahādāsiṃ          kāsirājenahantadā    
punāhaṃ bārāṇasiyaṃ           jātā kāsikagāmake .

Kuṭumbikakule phīte           sukhito so sabhātuko    
jeṭṭhassa bhātuno jāyā      ahosiṃ supatibbatā .

Paccekabuddhaṃ disvāna         mama bhattukaniyaso    
bhātu bhattaṃ tassa datvā       āgate tamhi pāvadi .

Nābhinandittha so dānaṃ        tato tassa adāsahaṃ    
buddhānāniya taṃ annaṃ         puno tasseva so adā .

Taṃ dānaṃ chaḍḍayitvāna         duṭṭhā buddhassahaṃ tadā    
pattaṃ kalalapuṇṇantaṃ           adāsiṃ tassa tādino .

#[^354-1] ma. Yu. Tassāti dayitā āsiṃ . [^354-2] ma. Bhattuno . [^354-3] ma. Puno . [^354-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 355]*

Dāne ca gahaṇe ceva        apace padusepica    
samacittamukhaṃ [^355-1] disvā        tadāhaṃ saṃvijiṃ bhusaṃ .

Puno pattaṃ gahetvāna        sodhayitvā sugandhinā    
pasannacittā pūretvā        saghataṃ sakkaraṃ adaṃ .

Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi          surūpā homi dānato    
buddhassa apakārena          duggandhā madanena ca [^355-2] .

Puno kassapadhīrassa           niṭṭhāpitamhi cetiye [^355-3]    
sovaṇṇaṃ iṭṭhakaṃ varaṃ          adāsiṃ muditā ahaṃ .

Catujjātena gandhena         temayitvā tamiṭṭhakaṃ [^355-4]    
muttā duggandhadosamhā       sabbaṅgasusamāgatā [^355-5] .

Sattapātisahassāni           ratanehi ca sattahi    
kāretvā ghatapūrāni         vaṭṭīni ca sahassaso .

Pakkhipetvā padīpetvā       ṭhapayiṃ satta pantiyo    
pūjanatthaṃ lokanāthassa         vippasannena cetasā .

Tadāpi tasmiṃ puññasmiṃ         bhāginīhaṃ visesato    
puno kāsīsu sañjāto        sumitto iti vissuto .

Tassāhaṃ bhariyā āsiṃ         sukhitā sajjitā piyā    
tadā paccekamunino          adāsi ghanaveṭṭhanaṃ .

Tassāpi bhāginī āsiṃ         moditvā dānamuttamaṃ    
punopi kāsiraṭṭhamhi          jāto koliyajātiyā .

#[^355-1] yu. Samacittasukhaṃ disvā mahāsaṅghaṃ cajiṃ bhusaṃ . [^355-2] ma. Yu. Vadanena ca .

#[^355-3] ma. Nidhāyantamhi . Yu. Niṭṭhāyantamhi . [^355-4] ma. Yu. Nicayitvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 356]*

Tadā koliyaputtānaṃ          satehi saha pañcahi    
pañca paccekabuddhānaṃ         satāni samupaṭṭhahi [^356-1] .

Temāsaṃ vāsayitvāna [^356-2]      adāsi [^356-3] ca ticīvaraṃ    
jāyā tassa tadā āsiṃ       puññakammapathānugā .

Tato cuto ahu rājā        nando nāma mahāyaso    
tassāpi mahesī āsiṃ         sabbakāmasamiddhinī .

Tato [^356-4] ahu cavitvāna       brahmadatto mahīpati    
padumāvatiputtānaṃ            paccekamuninaṃ tadā .

Satāni pañcanūnāni           yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ    
rājuyyāne nivāsetvā      nibbutāni ca pūjayiṃ .

Cetiyāni ca kāretvā       pabbajitvā ubho mayaṃ    
bhāvetvā appamaññāyo      brahmalokaṃ agamhase .

Tato cuto mahātitthe        sujāto pipphalāyano    
mātā sumanadevī ca          kosigotto dijo pitā .

Ahaṃ madde janapade          sākalāyaṃ puruttame    
kapilassa dijassāsiṃ           dhītā mātā sucīmati .

Ghanakañcanabimbena            nimminitvāna maṃ pitā    
adā kassapadhīrassa           kāmehi [^356-5] vajjitassa ca .

Kadāci so taruṇiko [^356-6]       gantvā kammantapekkhako    
kākādikehi khajjante        pāṇe disvāna saṃviji .

#[^356-1] yu. Samupaṭṭhahiṃ . [^356-2] ma. Tappayitvāna . [^356-3] yu. Adaṃsu ca ticīvare . [^356-4] ma. Tato  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 357]*

Gharepāhaṃ [^357-1] tile jāte     disvānātapatāpane [^357-2]    
kimikākehi khajjante         saṃvegamalabhiṃ tadā .

Tadā so pabbaji dhīro        ahaṃ taṃ anupabbajiṃ    
pañcavassāni nivasiṃ           paribbājapathe [^357-3] ahaṃ .

Yadā pabbajitā āsi         gotamī jinaposikā    
tadāhaṃ tamupāgantvā         buddhena anusāsitā .

Na cireneva kālena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
aho kalyāṇamittattaṃ         kassapassa sirīmato .

Putto buddhassa dāyādo      kassapo susamāhito    
pubbe nivāsaṃ so [^357-4] vedi    saggāpāyañca passati .

Atho jātikkhayaṃ patto        abhiññāvosito muni    
etāhi tīhi vijjāhi         tevijjo hoti brāhmaṇo .

Tatheva bhaddakāpilānī         tevijjā maccuhāyinī    
dhārentī antimaṃ dehaṃ        jetvā māraṃ savāhanaṃ .

Disvā ādīnavaṃ loke        ubho pabbajitā mayaṃ    
tayamhā [^357-5] khīṇāsavā dantā   sītibhūtāmha nibbutā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^357-1] ma. Yu. Ghare vāhaṃ . [^357-2] yu. ...Tāpite . [^357-3] ma. ...Vate . [^357-4] ma. Yu. Yo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 358 -->
<a id="page-358"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 358]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ bhaddakāpilānī bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bhaddakāpilānītheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ yasodharātheriyāpadānaṃ (28)    
[1]    
[168] purakkhatā bhikkhunīhi           satehi sahassehi sā [^358-2]    
mahiddhikā mahāpaññā         sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā        satthuno cakkalakkhaṇe    
nisinnā ekamantamhi         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Aṭṭhasattativassāhaṃ           pacchimā [^358-3] vattayi vayā    
pabbhāramhi anuppattā        ārocemi mahāmuniṃ .

Paripakko vayo mayhaṃ         parittaṃ mama [^358-4] jīvitaṃ    
pahāya vo gamissāmi         kataṃ me saraṇamattano .

Vayamhi [^358-5] pacchime kāle     maraṇaṃ uparuddhati    
ajja rattiṃ mahāvīra          pāpuṇissāmi nibbutiṃ .

Natthi jāti jarā byādhi       maraṇañca mahāmune    
ajarāmarābhayapuraṃ [^358-6]         gamissāmi asaṅkhataṃ .

Yāvatā parisā nāma         samupāsanti [^358-7] satthuno    
aparādhaṃ pajānanti           khamantaṃ sammukhā mune .

#[^358-1] ma. Ekasmiṃ samaye ramme .Pe. Nikkhamitvā sakassamā .

#[^358-2] yu. Saha pañcahi . [^358-3] ma. Pacchimo vattate vayo . Yu. Pacchimo vattati vayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 359]*

Saṃsaritvā [^359-1] ca saṃsāre      khalitañce mamantayi [^359-2]    
ārocemi [^359-3] mahāvīra       aparādhaṃ khamassu me .

Iddhiñcāpi nidassehi         mama sāsanakārike    
parisānañca sabbāsaṃ          kaṅkhaṃ chindassu sāsane [^359-4] .

Yasodharā ahaṃ vīra           agāre te pajāpati    
sākiyamhi kule jātā        itthīaṅge [^359-5] patiṭṭhitā .

Thīnaṃ satasahassānaṃ            navutīnaṃ chaḷuttari    
agāre te ahaṃ vīra         pāmokkhā sabbamissarā .

Rūpācāraguṇūpetā           yobbanaṭṭhāpi [^359-6] sabbadā    
sabbā maṃ apacāyanti         devatā viya mānusā .

Kaññāsahassapamukhā [^359-7]        sakyaputtanivesane    
samānasukhadukkhā tā          devatā viya nandane .

Kāmadhātumatikkantā [^359-8]       paṇḍitā [^359-9] rūpadhātuyā    
rūpena sadisā natthi          ṭhapetvā lokanāyakaṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā        iddhiṃ dassesi satthuno    
nekā nānāvidhākārā       mahāiddhīpi dassayi .

Cakkavāḷasamaṃ kāyaṃ           sīsaṃ uttarato kuru    
ubho pakkhā duve dīpā       jambūdīpaṃ sarīrato .

Dakkhiṇañca saraṃ piñchaṃ          nānāsākhā tu pattakā    
candañca sūriyañcakkhī          merupabbatato sikhaṃ .

#[^359-1] yu. Saṃsarantañca . [^359-2] yu. Tvaṃ mayi . [^359-3] yu. Āroceva . [^359-4] ma. Yāvatā .

#[^359-5] yu. Itthī atho . [^359-6] ma. Yu. Yobbanaṭṭhā piyaṃ vadā . [^359-7] ma. Yu. ...Satasahassa... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 360 -->
<a id="page-360"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 360]*

Cakkavāḷagiriṃ tuṇḍaṃ           jambūrukkhaṃ samūlakaṃ    
vījamānā upāgantvā        vandate lokanāyakaṃ .

Hatthivaṇṇaṃ tathevassaṃ          pabbataṃ jalajantathā [^360-1]    
candañca suriyaṃ meruṃ          sakkavaṇṇañca dassayi .

Yasodharā ahaṃ vīra           pāde vandāmi cakkhuma    
sahassalokadhātūnaṃ            phullapaddhena [^360-2] chādayi .

Brahmavaṇṇañca māpetvā      dhammaṃ desesi suññataṃ    
yasodharā ahaṃ vīra           pāde vandāmi cakkhuma .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā         natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mayhaṃ mahāvīra          uppannaṃ tava santike .

Buddhānaṃ [^360-3] lokanāthānaṃ      saṅgamaṃ te sudassitaṃ [^360-4]    
adhikāraṃ bahuṃ mayhaṃ           tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Yaṃ mayhaṃ purimaṃ [^360-5] kammaṃ       kusalaṃ sarase mune    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          puññaṃ upacitaṃ mayā .

Abhabbaṭṭhāne vajjitvā       vārayitvā anācaraṃ    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          sañcattaṃ [^360-6] jīvitaṃ mayā .

#[^360-1] ma. Jaladhiṃ . [^360-2] yu. Phullapaccena chādayiṃ . [^360-3] ma. Yu. Pubbānaṃ . [^360-4] ma. Nidassitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 361]*

Nekakoṭisahassāni           bhariyatthāyadāsi maṃ    
na tattha vimanā homi         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           upakārāyadāsi maṃ    
na tattha vimanā homi         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           bhojanatthāyadāsi maṃ    
na tattha vimanā homi         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           jīvitāni pariccajiṃ    
bhayamokkhaṃ karissanti          dadāmi [^361-1] mama jīvitaṃ    
aṅgagate alaṅkāre         vatthe nānāvidhe bahū    
itthībhaṇḍe na guyhāmi        tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Dhanadhaññapariccāgaṃ            gāmāni nigamāni ca    
khettaṃ puttā ca dhītā ca      paricattaṃ [^361-2] mahāmuni .

Hatthī assaṃ gavañcāpi         dāsiyo paricārikā    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          paricattā asaṅkhayā .

Yaṃ mayhaṃ paṭimantesi          dānaṃ dassāmi yācake    
vimanaṃ me na passāmi         dadato dānamuttamaṃ .

Nānāvidhaṃ bahuṃ dukkhaṃ          saṃsāre ca bahuvidhe    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          anubhuttaṃ [^361-3] asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sukhappattānumodāmi          na ca dukkhesu dummanā    
sabbattha tusitā homi         tuyhatthāya mahāmuni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 362]*

Anumaggena sambuddho         yaṃ dhammaṃ abhinīhari    
anubhotvā sukhadukkhaṃ          patto bodhiṃ mahāmuni .

Brahmadevañca sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
aññesaṃ lokanāthānaṃ         saṅgamante bahuṃ mayā .

Adhikāraṃ bahuṃ mayhaṃ           tuyhatthāya mahāmuni    
gavesantī [^362-1] buddhadhammaṃ       ahante paricārikā .

Kappasatasahasse ca           caturo ca asaṅkhaye    
dīpaṅkaro mahāvīro          uppajji lokanāyako .

Paccantadesavisaye           nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
tassa āgamanaṃ maggaṃ          sodhenti tuṭṭhamānasā .%    
tena kālena so āsi       sumedho nāma brāhmaṇo    
maggañca paṭiyādesi          āyato sabbadassino .

Tena kālenahaṃ āsiṃ         kaññā brāhmaṇasambhavā    
sumittā nāma nāmena        upagañchiṃ samāgamaṃ .

Aṭṭha uppalahatthāni          pūjanatthāya satthuno    
ādāya janassa majjhe        addasaṃ isimuttamaṃ [^362-2] .

Cirānugataṃ dayitaṃ [^362-3]          atikkantaṃ [^362-4] manoharaṃ    
disvā tadā amaññissaṃ        saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ mama .

Parakkamantaṃ saphalaṃ            addasaṃ isino tadā    
pubbakammena sambuddhe        cittañcāpi pasīdi me .

#[^362-1] ma. Gavesato . Yu. Gavesantā . [^362-2] ma. Yu. Isimuggataṃ . [^362-3] yu. Dassitaṃ . [^362-4] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 363]*

Bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādesiṃ        ise udaggamānase [^363-1]    
deyyaṃ aññaṃ na passāmi       demi pupphāni te ise .

Pañca hatthā tava hontu       tayo hontu mamaṃ ise    
tena saddhiṃ samā hontu       bodhitthāya [^363-2] tava ise .

Catutthaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Isi gahetvā pupphāni        āgacchantaṃ mahāyasaṃ    
pūjesi janassa majjhe         bodhitthāya mahāisiṃ .

Passitvā janassa majjhe       dīpaṅkaramahāmuni    
viyākāsi mahāvīro          isimuggatamānasaṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe      dīpaṅkaramahāmuni    
mama kammaṃ viyākāsi          ujubhāvaṃ mahāmuni .

Samacittā samakammā          samakārī bhavissati    
piyā hessati kammena        tuyhatthāya mahāise .

Sudassanā supiyā ca          manāpā [^363-3] piyavādinī    
tassa dhammesu dāyādā       viharissati [^363-4] iddhikā .

Yathāpi bhaṇḍasāmuggaṃ          anurakkhanti sāmino    
evaṃ kusaladhammānaṃ           anurakkhissate ayaṃ .

Tassa taṃ [^363-5] anukampanti       pūrayissati pāramī    
sīhova pañjaraṃ hitvā [^363-6]      pāpuṇissati bodhiyaṃ .

#[^363-1] ma. Yu. Uggatamānase . [^363-2] ma. Bodhatthāya . Yu. Bodhanatthāya . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .

#[^363-3] yu. Manasā . [^363-4] yu. Piyā hessatiṃ iddhikā . [^363-5] ma. Te . [^363-6] ma. Bhetvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 364 -->
<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 364]*

Aparimeyye ito kappe      yaṃ maṃ buddho viyākari    
taṃ vācaṃ anumodantī          evaṃkārī bhaviṃ ahaṃ .

Tassa kammassa sukatassa        tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
devamānusakaṃ yoniṃ           anubhotvā asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sukhadukkhaṃ anubhotvāna [^364-1]      devesu mānusesu ca    
pacchime bhavasampatte         ajāyiṃ sākiye kule .

Rūpavatī bhogavatī             yasasīlavatī tato    
sabbaṅgasampadā homi         kulesu patisakkatā [^364-2] .

Lābhasilokasakkāraṃ           lokadhammasamāgamaṃ    
cittañca dukkhitaṃ natthi         vasāmi akutobhayā .

Vuttaṃ hetaṃ bhagavatā          rañño antepure tadā    
khattiyānaṃ pure vīra          upakārañca [^364-3] niddisi .

Upakārā ca yā nārī        yā ca nārī sukhe dukkhe    
atthakkhāyī ca yā nārī       yā nārī cānukampikā .

Pañcakoṭisatā buddhā         navakoṭisatāni ca    
etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Adhikāraṃ mahā [^364-4] mayhaṃ       mahārāja [^364-5] suṇohi me    
ekādasakoṭisatā           honti [^364-6] lokagganāyakā .

Etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

#[^364-1] ma. Sukhadukkhenubhotvāhaṃ . Yu. Sukhadukkhenubhotvāna . [^364-2] ma. Abhisakkatā . Yu.

#atisakkatā . [^364-3] yu. Upakāratthañca . [^364-4] yu. Sadā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 365 -->
<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 365]*

Vīsakoṭisatā buddhā          tiṃsakoṭisatāni ca    
etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me    
cattāḷīsakoṭisatā           pañcakoṭisatāni [^365-1] ca .

Etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Saṭṭhikoṭisatā buddhā         sattatikoṭisatāni ca    
etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me    
asītikoṭisatā buddhā         navutikoṭisatāni ca .

Etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Koṭisatasahassāni            honti lokagganāyakā    
etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me    
navakoṭisahassāni            apare lokanāyakā .

Etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Koṭisatasahassāni            pañcāsīti mahesinaṃ    
pañcāsītikoṭisatā           sattavīsati koṭiyo [^365-2] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 366]*

Etesaṃ devadevānaṃ         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Paccekabuddhā vītarāgā [^366-1]    aṭṭhamatthakakoṭiyo [^366-2]    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Khīṇāsavā vītamalā           asaṅkheyyā buddhasāvakā    
adhikāraṃ mahā mayhaṃ          mahārāja suṇohi me .

Evaṃ dhammesu ciṇṇānaṃ        sadā saddhammacārino    
dhammacārī sukhaṃ seti          asmiṃ loke paramhi ca .

Dhammañcare sucaritaṃ           na taṃ duccaritaṃ care    
dhammacārī sukhaṃ seti          asmiṃ loke paramhi ca .

Nibbinditvāna saṃsāre        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
sahassaparivārena            pabbajitvā akiñcanā .

Agāraṃ vijahitvāna           pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte        catusaccaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca           paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
upanenti bahūneke [^366-3]       sāgareyeva [^366-4] ummiyo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^366-1] yu. Dhūtarāgā . [^366-2] ma. Aṭṭhaṭṭhamakakoṭiyo . Yu. Aṭṭhaaṭṭhamakoṭiyo . [^366-3] ma. Bahū  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 367]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ           sampattiṃ ca bahubbidhaṃ    
visuddhabhāvasampattā          labhāmi sabbasampadā .

Yo [^367-1] dadāti sakattānaṃ      puññatthāya mahesino    
sahāyasampadā honti         nibbānapadamasaṅkhataṃ .

Parikkhīṇaṃ atītañca            paccuppannaṃ anāgataṃ    
sabbakammaṃ mamaṃ khīṇaṃ           pāde vandāmi cakkhumāti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ yasodharā bhikkhunī bhagavato sammukhā imā gāthāyo    
abhāsitthāti .

Yasodharātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ dasasahassatheriyāpadānaṃ (29)    
[169] kappe satasahasse ca         caturo ca asaṅkhaye    
dīpaṅkaro nāma jino         uppajji lokanāyako .

Dīpaṅkaro mahāvīro          viyākāsi vināyako    
sumedhañca sumittañca          samānasukhadukkhataṃ .

Sadevakañca passanto         vicaranto sadevakaṃ    
tesaṃ pakittane amhe [^367-2]     upagamma samāgamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 368]*

Amhaṃ sabbapati hoti [^368-1]       anāgatasamāgame    
sabbāva tuyhaṃ bhariyā         manāpā piyavādikā .

Dānaṃ sīlamayaṃ sabbaṃ           bhāvanā [^368-2] ca subhāvitā    
dīgharattamidaṃ [^368-3] sabbaṃ         paricattaṃ mahāmune .

Gandhamālaṃ vilepanaṃ           dīpañca ratanāmayaṃ    
yaṅkiñci patthitaṃ sabbaṃ         paricattaṃ mahāmuni .

Aññaṃ vāpi kataṃ kammaṃ         paribhogañca mānusaṃ    
dīgharattaṃ hi no sabbaṃ         paricattaṃ mahāmune .

Anekajātisaṃsāraṃ            bahupuññaṃ hi no kataṃ    
issaraṃ anubhotvāna          saṃsaritvā bhavābhave .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         sakyaputtanivesane    
nānākulupapannāyo          accharā kāmavaṇṇinī .

Lābhaggena yasaṃ pattā        pūjitā sabbasakkatā    
lābhiyo annapānānaṃ         sadā sammānitā mayaṃ .

Agāraṃ pajahitvāna           pabbajimhanagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte        sabbā pattāmha nibbutiṃ .

Lābhiyo annapānānaṃ         vatthasenāsanāni ca    
upenti paccayā sabbe       sadā sakkatapūjitā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāma anāsavā .

#[^368-1] ma. Hohi . Yu. Sabbā patī honti . [^368-2] yu. Bhāvanaṃ ca subhāvitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 369 -->
<a id="page-369"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 369]*

Svāgataṃ vata no āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ yasodharīpamukhāni dasa bhikkhunīsahassāni bhagavato sammukhā    
imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Dasasahassatherīnaṃ apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ aṭṭhārasatherīsahassānamapadānaṃ (30)    
[170] aṭṭhārasasahassāni           bhikkhunī sākiyasambhavā    
yasodharīpamukhāni             sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamama .

Aṭṭhārasasahassāni [^369-1]        sabbā honti mahiddhikā    
vandanti munino pāde        ārocenti yathābalaṃ .

Jāti khīṇā jarā byādhi       maraṇañca mahāmune [^369-2]    
anāsavaṃ padaṃ santaṃ           amataṃ yāma nāyakaṃ [^369-3] .

Khalitañca pure atthi          sabbāsampi mahāmuni    
aparādhaṃ pajānanti           khama amhaṃ vināyaka .

Iddhiñcāpi nidassetha         mama sāsanakārikā    
parisānañca sabbāsaṃ          kaṅkhaṃ chindatha yāvatā .

Yasodharā [^369-4] mahāvīra        manāpā piyadassanā    
#[^369-1] po. Yasodharāsahassāni . Yu. Yasodharīyasahassāni . [^369-2] ma. Yu. Mahāmuni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 370]*

Sabbā tuyhaṃ mahāvīra         agārasmiṃ pajāpati .

Thīnaṃ satasahassānaṃ            navutīnaṃ chaḷuttari    
agāre te mayaṃ vīra         pāmokkhā sabbaïssarā .

Rūpācāraguṇūpetā           yobbanaṭṭhā piyaṃvadā    
sabbāyo [^370-1] apacāyanti [^370-2]   devatā viya mānusā .

Aṭṭhārasasahassāni           sabbā sākiyasambhavā    
yasodharī [^370-3] sahassāni        pāmokkhā issarā tadā .

Kāmadhātumatikkantā [^370-4]       saṇṭhitā rūpadhātuyā    
rūpena sadisā natthi          sahassānaṃ mahāmune [^370-5] .

Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā        iddhiṃ desesu satthuno    
nekā nānāvidhākārā       mahāiddhīpi [^370-6] dassayuṃ .

Cakkavāḷasamaṃ kāyaṃ           sīsaṃ uttarato kuru    
ubho pakkhā duve dīpā       jambūdīpaṃ sarīrato .

Dakkhiṇañca saraṃ [^370-7] piṇchaṃ       nānāsākhā tu pattakā    
candañca suriyañcakkhī          merupabbatato sikhaṃ .

Cakkavāḷagiriṃ tuṇḍaṃ           jambūrukkhaṃ samūlakaṃ    
vījamānā [^370-8] upāgantvā     vandante [^370-9] lokanāyakaṃ .

Hatthivaṇṇaṃ tathevassaṃ          pabbataṃ jalajantadā 10    
candañca suriyaṃ meruṃ          sakkavaṇṇañca dassayuṃ .

#[^370-1] ma. Yu. Sabbā no . [^370-2] po. Apasahanti . [^370-3] ma. Yasodharā . Yu. Yasovatī .

#[^370-4] ma. ...Matikkamma . [^370-5] ma. Yu. Mahāmuni . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^370-6] yu. Mahāiddhiṃ    
#vidassayuṃ . [^370-7] po. Sasirañca . [^370-8] po. Yu. Vījamānaṃ . [^370-9] ma. Yu. Vandantī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 371]*

Yasodharā [^371-1] mayaṃ vīra        pāde vandāma cakkhuma    
tava cirappabhāvena [^371-2]        nipphannā naranāyaka .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homa          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homa mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāma         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ amhaṃ mahāvīra          uppannaṃ tava santike .

Buddhānaṃ [^371-3] lokanāthānaṃ      saṅgamaṃ no nidassitaṃ    
adhikārā bahū amhe         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Yaṃ amhaṃ purimaṃ kammaṃ          kusalaṃ sarase mune    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          puññānupacitāni [^371-4] no .

Abhabbaṭṭhāne vajjetvā      vārayimha [^371-5] anācaraṃ    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          sañcattaṃ [^371-6] jīvitampi no .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           bhiriyatthāyadāsi no    
na tattha vimanā homa         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           upakārāyadāsi no    
na tattha vimanā homa         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Nekakoṭisahassāni           bhojanatthāyadāsi no    
na tattha vimanā homa         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

#[^371-1] yu. Yasovaṇṇā . [^371-2] yu. Vīrappabhāvena . [^371-3] ma. Yu. Pubbānaṃ . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .

#[^371-4] po. Puññaṃ upacitaṃ mayā . [^371-5] yu. Pācayimha anāvaraṃ . [^371-6] ma. Yu. Cattāni jīvitāni  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 372]*

Nekakoṭisahassāni           jīvitāni cajimhase [^372-1]    
bhayamokkhaṃ karissāma          jīvitāni cajimhase [^372-2] .

Aṅgagate alaṅkāre         vatthe nānāvidhe bahū    
itthībhaṇḍe na guyhāma        tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Dhanadhaññapariccāgaṃ            gāmāni nigamāni ca    
khettaṃ puttañca [^372-3] dhītañca     paricattaṃ mahāmune .

Hatthiassaṃ [^372-4] gavañcāpi       dāsiyo paricārikā    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          paricattaṃ asaṅkhayaṃ .

Yaṃ amhe paṭimantesi         dānaṃ dassāma yācake    
vimanaṃ no na passāma         dadato dānamuttamaṃ .

Nānāvidhaṃ bahuṃ dukkhaṃ          saṃsāre ca bahubbidhe    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          anubhuttaṃ [^372-5] asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sukhaṃ pattānumodāma          na ca dukkhesu dummanā    
sabbattha tusitā homa         tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Anumaggena sambuddho         yaṃ [^372-6] dhammaṃ abhinīhari    
anubhotvā sukhadukkhaṃ          patto bodhiṃ mahāmune .

Brahmadevañca sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
aññesaṃ lokanāthānaṃ         saṅgamantehi no bahu [^372-7] .

Adhikāraṃ bahu amhaṃ [^372-8]        tuyhatthāya mahāmune    
gavesato [^372-9] buddhadhammaṃ 10   mayante paricārikā .

#1-[^372-2] yu. Cajimha no . [^372-3] ma. Puttā ca jītā ca paricattā ... . [^372-4] ma. Hatthīassā    
#gavācāpi . [^372-5] yu. Paricattaṃ . [^372-6] yu. Saddhammaṃ . [^372-7] ma. Bahū . Yu. Bahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 373]*

Kappe ca satasahasse         caturo ca asaṅkhaye    
dīpaṅkaro mahāvīro          uppajji lokanāyako .

Paccantadesavisaye           nimantetvā tathāgataṃ    
tassa āgamanaṃ maggaṃ          sodhenti tuṭṭhamānasā .

Tena kālena so āsi       sumedho nāma brāhmaṇo    
maggañca paṭiyādesi          āyato sabbadassino .

Tena kālena ahumha [^373-1]      sabbā brāhmaṇasambhavā    
phalukajātipupphāni            āharimha samāgamaṃ .

Tasmiṃ so samaye buddho       dīpaṅkaro mahāyaso    
viyākāsi mahāvīro          isimuggatamānasaṃ .

Calati ravati paṭhavī [^373-2]         saṅkampati sadevake    
tassa kammaṃ pakittento       isimuggatamānasaṃ .

Devakaññā manussā ca        mayañcāpi [^373-3] sadevakā    
nānāpūjaniyaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ           pūjayitvāna patthayuṃ .

Tesaṃ buddho viyākāsi        jotidīpasanāmako    
ajja ye patthitā atthi       te bhavissanti sammukhā .

Aparimeyye ito kappe      yaṃ no buddho viyākari    
taṃ vācaṃ anumodantī [^373-4]       evaṃkārī ahumha no .

Tassa kammassa sukatassa        tattha cittaṃ pasādayuṃ    
devamānusakaṃ yoniṃ           anubhotvā asaṅkhayaṃ .

#[^373-1] po. Āsumha . Yu. Āsimha . [^373-2] ma. Yu. Puthavī . [^373-3] po. Amhevāpi sadevatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 374]*

Sukhadukkhe anubhotvāna        devesu mānusesu ca    
pacchime bhavasampatte         jātāmha sākiye kule .

Rūpavatī bhogavatī             yasasīlavatī tato    
sabbaṅgasampadā homa         kulesu atisakkatā .

Lābhasilokasakkāraṃ           lokadhammasamāgamaṃ    
cittañca dukkhitaṃ natthi         vasāma akutobhayā .

Vuttaṃ hetaṃ bhagavatā          rañño antepure tadā    
khattiyānaṃ pure tāsaṃ [^374-1]      upakārañca niddisi .

Upakārā ca yā nārī        yā ca nārī sukhe dukkhe    
atthakkhāyī ca yā nārī       yā ca nārīnukampikā .

Dhammañcare sucaritaṃ           na taṃ duccaritaṃ care    
dhammacārī sukhaṃ seti          asmiṃ loke paramhi ca .

Agāraṃ pajahitvāna [^374-2]        pabbajimhānagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte        catusaccaṃ phusimha no .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca           paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
upanenti bahū amhe         sāgarasseva ummiyo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāma anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata no āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 375]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ           sampattiñca [^375-1] bahubbidhaṃ    
visuddhabhāvaṃ sampattā         labhāma sabbasampadā .

Yā dadanti sakattānaṃ         puññatthāya mahesino    
sahāyasampadā honti         nibbānaṃ [^375-2] padamasaṅkhataṃ .

Parikkhīṇaṃ atītañca            paccuppannaṃ anāgataṃ    
sabbakammaṃpi [^375-3] no khīṇaṃ       pāde vandāma cakkhumā .

Nibbānāya vadantīnaṃ          kiṃ vo vakkhāma uttariṃ    
sattasaṅkhatadāso [^375-4] yo      pappotha amataṃpadanti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ yasodharāpamukhāni aṭṭhārasa bhikkhunīsahassāni bhagavato sammukhā    
imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Aṭṭhārasatherīsahassānaṃ apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kuṇḍalā gotamī ceva         dhammadinnā ca sakulā    
varanandā ca soṇā ca        kāpilānī yasodharā .

Dasasahassabhikkhunī             aṭṭhārasasahassakā    
satāni ceva cattāri         aṭṭhasattatimeva ca .

Kuṇḍalakesavaggo [^375-5] tatiyo .

#[^375-1] po. Ma. Yu. Sampattī ca bahubidhā . [^375-2] ma. Nibbānapadamasaṅkhataṃ . Yu.

#nibbānamatasaṅkhataṃ . [^375-3] yu. Saccaṃ kammaṃpi ... . [^375-4] ma. Sattasaṅgatadosaṃ hi . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 376]*

Catuttho khattiyakaññāvaggo    
paṭhamaṃ aṭṭhārasasahassakhattiyakaññātherīnamapadānaṃ (31)    
[171] bhavā sabbe parikkhīṇā        bhavasandhi [^376-1] vimocitā    
sabbāsavā ca no natthi       ārocema mahāmune .

Parikammañca [^376-2] kusalaṃ         yaṅkiñci sādhu [^376-3] patthitaṃ    
paribhogaṃ [^376-4] mayā dinnaṃ       tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Buddhapaccekabuddhānaṃ          sāvakānañca patthitaṃ    
paribhogaṃ mayā dinnaṃ          tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Uccānīcaṃ mayā kammaṃ         bhikkhūnaṃ sādhu patthitaṃ    
uccākulaparikkammaṃ           katamhehi [^376-5] mahāmune .

Teneva sukkamūlena          coditā kammasampadā    
mānusikamatikkantā           jāyiṃsu khattiye kule .

Upacite [^376-6] kate kamme      jātiyā cāpi [^376-7] ekato    
pacchime ekato jātā       khattiyā kulasambhavā .

Rūpavatī bhogavatī             lābhasakkārapūjitā    
antepure mahāvīra          devānaṃ iva nandane .

Nibbinditvā agāramhā       pabbajimhānagāriyaṃ    
katipāhaṃ upādāya           sabbā pattāmha nibbutiṃ .

#[^376-1] ma. Bhavā santi ... . [^376-2] ma. Yu. Purimaṃ kusalaṃ kammaṃ . [^376-3] po. Yu. Sabbaṃ .

#[^376-4] ma. Paribhogamayaṃ . Yu. Paribhogaṃ ayaṃ . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^376-5] ma. Katametaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 377]*

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca           paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
upanenti bahū amhe         sadā sakkatapūjitā .

Kilesā jhāpitā amhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāma anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata no āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ yasavatīpamukhāni khattiyakaññā bhikkhuniyo aṭṭhārasasahassāniśa    
bhagavato sammukhā imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Aṭṭhārasasahassakhattiyakaññātherīnaṃ apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ caturāsītisahassabrāhmaṇakaññātherīnamapadānaṃ (32)    
[172] cūḷāsītisahassāni            brāhmaṇakulasambhavā [^377-1]    
sukhumālahatthapādā           pure tuyhaṃ mahāmune .

Vessa suddakule jātā       devā nāgā ca kinnarā    
catuddīpā bahū kaññā         pure tuyhaṃ mahāmune .

Kāci [^377-2] pabbajitā atthi      sabbadassāvino bahū    
devā ca kinnarā nāgā      bujjhissanti [^377-3] anāgate .

Anubhotvā yasaṃ sabbaṃ         patvāna sabbasampadā  
#[^377-1] ma. Yu. Brāhmaññakulasambhavā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^377-2] yu. Keci . Ito paraṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 378]*

Tumhaṃ [^378-1] pasādaṃ paṭiladdhā     bujjhissanti anāgate .

Amhe brāhmaṇadhītā tu       brahmaññakulasambhavā    
lakkhaṇā [^378-2] ca mahāvīra       pāde vandāma cakkhumā .

Upāhatā bhavā sabbe        mūlataṇhā samūhatā    
samucchinnā anusayā          puññasaṅkhāradālitā .

Samādhigocarā sabbā         samāpattivasī tathā [^378-3]    
jhānena dhammaratiyā          viharissāma no sadā .

Bhavanetti avijjā ca         saṅkhārāpi ca khepitā    
sududdasaṃ padaṃ gantvā         anujānimha [^378-4] nāyaka .

Upakārā [^378-5] mamaṃ tumhe      dīgharattaṃ gataddhino    
catunnaṃ [^378-6] saṃsayaṃ chetvā      sabbā gacchatha [^378-7] nibbutiṃ .

Vanditvā munino pāde       katvā iddhivikubbanaṃ    
kāci dassenti ālokaṃ       andhakāraṃ [^378-8] tathāparaṃ .

Dassenti candasuriye         sāgarañca samacchakaṃ    
sineruṃ paribhaṇḍañca           dassenti pārichattakaṃ .

Tāvatiṃsañca bhavanaṃ            yāmaṃ dassenti iddhiyā    
tussitā [^378-9] nimmitā devā    vasavattī mahissarā .

Brahmāno kāci dassenti     caṅkamañca mahārahaṃ    
brahmavaṇṇañca māpetvā      dhammaṃ desenti suññataṃ .

Nānāvikubbanaṃ katvā         iddhiṃ dassiya satthuno    
#[^378-1] yu. Tvayi . [^378-2] ma. Yu. Pekkhato no ... . [^378-3] ma. Katā . [^378-4] ma. Anujānātha .

#[^378-5] ma. Yu. Katāvino . [^378-6] yu. Bahunnaṃ . [^378-7] ma. Gacchantu . [^378-8] ma. Andhakāramathāparā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 379]*

Dassayiṃsu balaṃ sabbā          pāde vandiṃsu satthuno .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homa          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homa mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāma         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ amhaṃ mahāvīra          uppannaṃ tava santike .

Buddhānaṃ lokanāthānaṃ         saṅgamantehi [^379-1] dassitaṃ    
adhikārā [^379-2] bahū amhe      tuyhatthāya mahāmune .

Yaṃ amhaṃ [^379-3] purimaṃ kammaṃ       kusalaṃ sara taṃ muni    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          puññānupacitāni no .

Satasahasse ito kappe       padumuttaro mahāmuni    
puraṃ haṃsavatī nāma            sambuddhassa kulālayaṃ [^379-4] .

Dvārena haṃsavatiyaṃ           gaṅgā sandati sabbadā    
ubbāḷhā [^379-5] nadiyā bhikkhū    gamanaṃ na labhanti te .

Divasaṃ dve [^379-6] tayo ceva     sattāhaṃ māsikaṃ tato    
cātumāsampi sampuṇṇaṃ         gamanaṃ na labhanti te .

Tadā ahu sattasāro         jajjiyo [^379-7] nāma raṭṭhako    
oratīre [^379-8] bhikkhavo disvā   setuṃ [^379-9] gaṅgāya kārayi .

#[^379-1] ma. Saṅgamaṃ no nidassitaṃ . Yu. Saṅgamaṃ te nidassi . [^379-2] ma. Adhikāraṃ bahuṃ amhaṃ .

#[^379-3] ma. Yaṃ amhehi kataṃ kammaṃ . [^379-4] ma. Yu. Kulāsayaṃ . [^379-5] yu. Uddālanadivā bhikkhu .

#[^379-6] po. Divañceva rattiyā ceva . [^379-7] ma. Yu. Jaṭilo . [^379-8] ma. Yu. Oruddhe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 380]*

Tadā satasahassehi           setuṃ gaṅgāya kārayiṃ    
saṅghassa pārime [^380-1] tīre     vihārañca akārayi .

Itthiyo purisā ceva         uccānīcakulāni ca    
tassa setuṃ vihāraṃ [^380-2] ca      samabhāgaṃ akaṃsu te .

Amhe aññe ca manujā       vippasannena cetasā    
tassa dhammesu dāyādā       nagare janapadesu ca .Da    
itthī pumā kumārā ca        bahū ceva kumārikā    
setuno ca vihārassa         vālukā ākiriṃsu te .

Vīthisammajjanaṃ katvā          kadalīpuṇṇaghaṭaddhajā [^380-3]    
dhūpaṃ [^380-4] cuṇṇañca mālañca      kāraṃ katvāna satthuno .

Setuvihāre katvāna         nimantetvā vināyakaṃ    
mahādānaṃ daditvāna          sambodhiṃ abhipatthayiṃ .

Padumuttaro mahāvīro         garuko [^380-5] sabbapāṇinaṃ    
anumodaniyaṃ katvā [^380-6]        byākāsi mahāmuni .

Satasahasse atikkante        kappo hessati bhaddako    
bhavābhave sukhaṃ [^380-7] laddhā      pāpuṇissati bodhiyaṃ .

Ye [^380-8] keci hatthakammañca     katāvī naranāriyo    
anāgatasmi addhāne         sabbe hessanti sammukhā .

Tena kammavipākena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
uppannā devabhavane [^380-9]      tuyhante 10 paricārikā .

#[^380-1] ma. Yu. Orime . [^380-2] yu. Vihāre . [^380-3] ma. Kadalīpuṇṇaghaṭe dhaje . Yu. Kadalīpuṇṇakuṭadhajā .

#[^380-4] yu. Dhūma... . [^380-5] ma. Yu. Tārako . [^380-6] ma. Yu. Kāsi jaṭilassa . [^380-7] ma. Yu. Nubhotvāna .

#[^380-8] ma. Kāci hatthaparikammaṃ . Yu. Keci hatthapadaṃ kammaṃ . [^380-9] ma. Yu. Devabhavanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 381]*

Dibbasukhaṃ asaṅkheyyaṃ          mānusañca asaṅkhiyaṃ    
anubhonti [^381-1] ciraṃ kālaṃ       saṃsaritvā bhavābhave .

Satasahasse ito kappe       sukataṃ kammasampadaṃ    
sukhumālimanussesu [^381-2]         atho devapure vare .

Rūpabhogayasañceva [^381-3]         atho kittisukhaṃ piyaṃ    
labhāma satataṃ sabbaṃ           sukataṃ kammasampadaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         jāyāma [^381-4] brāhmaṇe kule    
sukhumālahatthapādā           sakyaputtanivesane .

Sabbakālampi paṭhaviṃ           na passāma nalaṅkataṃ    
cikkhallaṃ [^381-5] bhūmiṃ gamanaṃ        na passāma mahāmuni .

Agārāvasante amhe        sakkāraṃ sabbakālikaṃ    
upanenti sadā sabbaṃ         pubbakammaphalena [^381-6] no .

Agāraṃ pajahitvāna           pabbajitvānagāriyaṃ    
saṃsārapathaṃ nitiṇṇā           natthi [^381-7] dāni punabbhavo .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca           paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
upanenti sadā amhe        sahassāni tato tato .

Kilesā jhāpitāmhākaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāma anāsavā .

#[^381-1] ma. Tuyhaṃ te paricārema saṃsarimha bhavābhave . Yu. Tuyhaṃ vo paricāre ca .

#[^381-2] ma. Yu. ... Manussānaṃ . [^381-3] ma. Rūpabhogayaseceva atho kittiṃ ca sakkataṃ .

#[^381-4] ma. Jātāmha . Po. Yu. Ajātā . [^381-5] ma. Cikkhallabhūmimasuciṃ . [^381-6] yu. ...Phalaṃ kato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 382]*

Svāgataṃ vata no āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ caturāsīti brāhmaṇakaññābhikkhunīsahassāni bhagavato sammukhā    
imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Caturāsītisahassabrāhmaṇakaññātherīnaṃ apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ uppaladāyikātheriyāpadānaṃ (33)    
[173] nagare aruṇavatiyā           aruṇo nāma khattiyo    
tassa rañño ahaṃ bhariyā       ekaccaṃ vādayāmahaṃ .

Rahogatā nisīditvā          evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
kusalaṃ me kataṃ natthi          ādāya gamiyaṃ mama .

Mahābhitāpaṃ kaṭukaṃ            ghorarūpaṃ sudāruṇaṃ    
nirayaṃ nūna gacchāmi           ettha me natthi saṃsayo .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna         pahaṃsetvāna mānasaṃ    
rājānaṃ upagantvāna         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Itthī nāma mayaṃ deva         purisā na [^382-1] bhavāma no    
ekaṃ me samaṇaṃ dehi         bhojayissāmi khattiya .

Adāsi me tadā rājā       samaṇaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ    
tassa pattaṃ gahetvāna        paramannena pūrayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 383]*

Pūretvā paramaṃ annaṃ         saha [^383-1] sugandhalepanaṃ    
mahācelena chādetvā       adāsiṃ tuṭṭhamānasā .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Sahassadevarājūnaṃ            mahesittamakārayiṃ    
sahassacakkavattīnaṃ            mahesittamakārayiṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ            gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
nānāvidhaṃ bahuṃ aññaṃ [^383-2]       tassa kammaphalaṃ tato .

Uppalasseva me vaṇṇo       abhirūpā sudassanā    
itthī sabbaṅgasampannā        abhijātā jutindharā .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         ajāyiṃ sākiye kule    
nārīsahassapāmokkhā         suddhodanasutassahaṃ .

Nibbinditvā agārehaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
sattamīrattimappattā [^383-3]       catusaccaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca           paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
parimetuṃ na sakkomi          piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yaṃ mayhaṃ purimaṃ [^383-4] kammaṃ       kusalaṃ sarase muni    
tuyhatthāya mahāvīra          paricattaṃ bahuṃ mayā [^383-5] .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi          piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^383-1] po. Sahagandhavilepanaṃ . Yu. Sahassaṃ gandhalepanaṃ . [^383-2] yu. Puññaṃ . [^383-3] ma. ...


<!-- Siam Rath Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 384]*

Duve gatī pajānāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi         piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ucce kule pajānāmi        mahāsāle [^384-1] mahaddhane    
aññe kule na jānāmi       piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaritvā          sukkamūlena coditā    
amanāpaṃ na passāmi          somanassassidaṃ [^384-2] phalaṃ .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmuni .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mama mahāvīra           uppannaṃ tava santike .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ uppaladāyikā bhikkhunī bhagavato sammukhā imā    
gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Uppaladāyikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 385]*

Catutthaṃ siṅgālamātātheriyāpadānaṃ (34)    
[174] padumuttaro nāma jino        sabbadhammāna pāragū    
ito satasahassamhi           kappe uppajji nāyako .

Tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā            jātāmaccakule ahu    
nānāratanapajjote          iddhe phīte mahaddhane .

Pitunā saha gantvāna         mahājanapurakkhatā    
dhammaṃ buddhassa sutvāna        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Pabbajitvāna kāyena         pāpakammaṃ vivajjayiṃ    
vacīduccaritaṃ hitvā           ājīvaṃ parisodhayiṃ .

Buddhe pasannā dhamme ca      saṅghe ca tibbagāravā    
saddhammassavane yuttā        buddhadassanasālayā [^385-1] .

Aggaṃ saddhādhimuttānaṃ [^385-2]      assosiṃ bhikkhuniṃ tadā    
taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayitvāna         tisso sikkhā apūrayiṃ .

Tato maṃ sugato āha         karuṇānugatāsayo    
yassa saddhā tathāgate        acalā supatiṭṭhitā .

Sīlañca yassa kalyāṇaṃ         ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ    
saṅghe pasādo yassatthi       ujubhūtañca dassanaṃ .

Adaliddoti taṃ āhu          amoghaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ    
tasmā saddhañca sīlañca        pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 386]*

Anuyuñjetha medhāvī          saraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ    
taṃ sutvāhaṃ pamuditā          apucchiṃ paṇidhiṃ mama .

Tadā anomo amito         byākarittha vināyako    
buddhassa [^386-1] pasanno kalyāṇī   lacchasetaṃ supatthitaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe       okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena        satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādā       orasā dhammanimmitā    
siṅgālakassa yā [^386-2] mātā    hessati satthu sāvikā .

Taṃ sutvā muditā hutvā       yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ    
mettacittā paricariṃ          paṭipattīhi nāyakaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        giribbajapuruttame    
jātā seṭṭhikule phīte       mahāratanasañcaye .

Putto siṅgālako nāma       mamāsi [^386-3] vipathe rato    
diṭṭhigahaṇapakkhanno           disāpūjanatapparo .

Nānādisā namassati [^386-4]       piṇḍāya nagaraṃ vajaṃ    
taṃ disvā avadi buddho        magge ṭhatvā vināyako .

Tassa desayato dhammaṃ         panādo vimhayo [^386-5] ahu    
dvekoṭinaranārīnaṃ           dhammābhisamayo ahu .

#[^386-1] ma. Yu. Buddhe pasannā . [^386-2] ma. Yu. Mātāti . [^386-3] yu. Matāsī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 387]*

Tadā taṃ [^387-1] parisaṃ gantvā     sutvā sugatabhāsitaṃ    
sotāpattiphalaṃ patvā         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Na cireneva kālena         buddhadassanasālayā    
anussatiṃ taṃ bhāvetvā        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Dassanatthāya buddhassa         sabbadā ca vajāmahaṃ    
atittāyeva passāmi         rūpaṃ nayananandanaṃ .

Sabbapāramisambhūtaṃ            lakkhinilayanaṃ paraṃ [^387-2]    
rūpaṃ sabbasubhākiṇṇaṃ           atittā viharāmahaṃ [^387-3] .

Jino tasmiṃ guṇe tuṭṭho       etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ    
siṅgālakassa yā mātā       aggā saddhādhimuttikā [^387-4] .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsave [^387-5] pariññāya      natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mama mahāvīra           uppannaṃ tava santike .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^387-1] ma. Tadāhaṃ . [^387-2] ma. Yu. Varaṃ . [^387-3] yu. Bhayāmahaṃ . [^387-4] yu. Saṅgavimuttikā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 388]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ siṅgālamātā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Siṅgālamātātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ sukkātheriyāpadānaṃ (35)    
[175] ekanavute ito kappe       vipassī nāma nāyako    
uppajji cārunayano          sabbadhammavipassako .

Tadāhaṃ bandhumatiyaṃ            jātā aññatare kule    
dhammaṃ sutvāna munino         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Bahussutā dhammadharā          paṭibhāṇavatī tathā    
vicittakathikā cāpi           jinasāsanakārikā .

Tadā dhammakathaṃ katvā         hitāya janataṃ sadā [^388-1]    
tato cutāhaṃ tusitaṃ           upapannā yasassinī .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       sikhī viya sikhī jino    
tapanto yasasā lokaṃ [^388-2]      uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Tadāpi pabbajitvāna          buddhasāsanakovidā    
jotetvā jinavākyāni       tatopi tidivaṅgatā .

Ekattiṃse [^388-3] ito kappe    vessabhū nāma nāyako    
uppajjittha mahāyānī         tadāpica tathevahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 389 -->
<a id="page-389"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 389]*

Pabbajitvā dhammadharā         jotayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ    
gantvā marupuraṃ rammaṃ         anubhomi [^389-1] mahāsukhaṃ .

Imamhi bhaddake kappe        kakusandho jinuttamo    
uppajji narasaddūlo [^389-2]       tadāpica tathevahaṃ .

Pabbajiṃ [^389-3] jinasāsanaṃ         jotayitvā yathāyukaṃ [^389-4]    
tato cutāhaṃ tidasaṃ           agaṃ sabhavanaṃ yathā .

Imasmiṃyeva kappamhi          konāgamananāyako    
uppajji vadataṃ [^389-5] varo       sabbasattānamuttamo .

Tadāpi pabbajitvāna          sāsane tassa tādino    
bahussutā dhammadharā          jotayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappamhi          kassapo muni sattamo [^389-6]    
uppajji lokasaraṇo          araṇo maraṇantagū .

Tassāpi naradhīrassa           pabbajitvāna sāsane    
pariyāpuṭasaddhammā           paripucchāvisāradā .

Susīlā lajjinī ceva          tīsu sikkhāsu kovidā    
bahuṃ dhammakathaṃ katvā          yāvajīvaṃ mahāmune .

Tena kammavipākena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        giribbajapuruttame .

Jātā seṭṭhikule phīte       mahāratanasañcaye .

#[^389-1] ma. Yu. Anubhosiṃ . [^389-2] po. Narasadduno . Ma. Narasaraṇo . Yu. Varasaddūlo .

#[^389-3] ma. Yu. Pabbajitvā munimataṃ . [^389-4] yu. Yathāsukhaṃ . [^389-5] yu. Uppajji ca dīpavaro .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 390 -->
<a id="page-390"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 390]*

Yadā bhikkhusahassena          pareto [^390-1] lokanāyako    
upāgami rājagahaṃ            sahassakkhehi [^390-2] vaṇṇito .

Danto dantehi saha purāṇajaṭilehi    
vippamutto vippamuttehi    
siṅginikkhasavaṇṇo    
rājagahaṃ pāvisi bhagavā .

Disvā buddhānubhāvantaṃ        sutvā ca [^390-3] guṇasañcayaṃ    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā      pūjayiṃ taṃ mahabbalaṃ [^390-4] .

Aparena ca kālena          dhammadinnāya santike    
agārā nikkhamitvāna         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Kesesu chijjamānesu         kilese jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ    
uggahiṃ sāsanaṃ sabbaṃ          pabbajitvā na cirenahaṃ .

Tato dhammaṃ adesesiṃ         mahājanasamāgame    
dhamme desiyamānamhi         dhammābhisamayo ahu .

Nekapāṇasahassānaṃ           taṃ viditvā vijimhito [^390-5]    
abhippasanno me yakkho       gamitvāna [^390-6] giribbajaṃ .

Kiṃ me katā rājagahe manussā  madhadha pītāva acchare    
ye sukkaṃ na payirupāsanti      desentiṃ amataṃpadaṃ .

Tañca pana appaṭivāniyaṃ        asecanakamojavaṃ    
pivanti maññe sappaññā       valāhakamivaddhagū .

#[^390-1] ma. Parivuto . [^390-2] ma. Yu. Sahassakkhena . [^390-3] ma. Va . [^390-4] ma. Yu. Yathābalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 391 -->
<a id="page-391"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 391]*

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mama mahāvīra           uppannaṃ tava santike .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ sukkā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sukkātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ    
chaṭṭhaṃ abhirūpanandātheriyāpadānaṃ (36)    
[176] ekanavute ito kappe       vipassī nāma nāyako    
uppajji cārunayano [^391-1]       sabbadhammesu cakkhumā .

Tadāhaṃ bandhumatiyā           iddhe phīte mahākule  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 392 -->
<a id="page-392"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 392]*

Jātā surūpā [^392-1] dayitā      pūjanīyā [^392-2] janassa ca .

Upagantvā mahāvīraṃ          vipassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
dhammaṃ sutvāna saraṇaṃ          upesiṃ naranāyakaṃ .

Sīlesu saṃvutā hutvā         nibbute ca naruttame    
dhātuthūpassa upari            soṇṇachattaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Muttacāgā sīlavatī           yāvajīvaṃ tato cutā    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ [^392-3] agañchahaṃ .

Tadā dasahi ṭhānehi          abhibhotvā [^392-4] asesato    
rūpasaddehi gandhehi          rasehi phussanehi ca .

Āyunāpica [^392-5] vaṇṇena       sukhena yasasāpica    
tathevādhipateyyena          adhigayha virocahaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātāhaṃ kapilavhaye    
dhītā khemakasakkassa [^392-6]       nandā nāmena vissutā .

Abhirūpasampadampi             āhu mekanti sūcakaṃ    
yadāhaṃ yobbanaṃ pattā        rūpavaṇṇavibhūsitā .

Idha [^392-7] mamatte sakyānaṃ      vivādo sumahā ahu    
pabbājesi tato tāto       mā sakyā vinassuṃ [^392-8] iti .

Pabbajitvāna [^392-9] tathāgataṃ      rūpadessiṃ naruttamaṃ    
sutvāna nopagacchāmi         mama rūpena gabbhitā .

#[^392-1] po. Yu. Surūpadassitā . [^392-2] po. Sajanassa cahaṃ sadā . Yu. Sajanassā-janassa ca .

#[^392-3] ma. Tāvatiṃsūpagā ahaṃ . [^392-4] ma. Yu. Abhibhotvāna sesake . [^392-5] po. Yākāsi pañcavaṇṇena .

#[^392-6] po. Sukhasakkassa . [^392-7] ma. Tadā mamatthe . Yu idaṃ me matthesakyānaṃ . Sakyānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 393 -->
<a id="page-393"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 393]*

Ovādaṃpi na gacchāmi         buddhadassanabhīrukā [^393-1]    
tadā jino upāyena         upanetvā sasantikaṃ .

Tissitthiyo [^393-2] nidassesi      iddhiyā maggakovido    
accharārūpasadisaṃ [^393-3]          taruṇiṃ jarikaṃ [^393-4] mataṃ .

Tayo disvā susaṃviggā        virattāse [^393-5] kalevare    
aṭṭhāsiṃ bhavanibbindā [^393-6]      tato [^393-7] maṃ āha [^393-8] nāyako .

Āturaṃ asuciṃ pūtiṃ            passa nande samussayaṃ    
uggharantaṃ paggharantaṃ          bālānaṃ abhipatthitaṃ [^393-9] .

Asubhāya cittaṃ bhāvehi        ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ    
yathā idaṃ tathā etaṃ         yathā etaṃ tathā idaṃ .

Evametaṃ avekkhantī         rattindivamatanditā    
tato sakāya paññāya         abhinibbijja vacchasi .

Tassā me appamattāya       vicinantīdha yoniso    
yathābhūtaṃ ayaṃ kāyo          diṭṭho santarabāhiro .

Atha nibbindihaṃ 10 kāye     ajjhattañca virajjahaṃ    
appamattā visaṃyuttā         upasantamhi nibbutā .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune 1[^393-1] .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
#[^393-1] ma. ...Bhīrutā . [^393-2] po. Yu. Tissotthiyo . [^393-3] yu. Accharārūpasadisā taruṇi jarikā    
#matā . [^393-4] po. Ma. Jaritaṃ . [^393-5] po. Imasmiṃpi ... . [^393-6] yu. ... Nibbinnā .

#[^393-7] ma. Yu. Tadā . [^393-8] po. Atha . [^393-9] ma. Abhinanditaṃ . 10 ma. Yu. Nibbindahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 394 -->
<a id="page-394"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 394]*

Sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mama mahāvīra           uppannaṃ tava santike .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ abhirūpanandā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Abhirūpanandātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ aḍḍhakāsītheriyāpadānaṃ (37)    
[177] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Tadāhaṃ pabbajitvāna          tassa buddhassa sāsane    
saṃvutā pāṭimokkhamhi         indriyesu ca pañcasu .

Mattaññū nīcaāsane          yuttā jāgariyepica [^394-1]    
vasantī yuttayogāhaṃ [^394-2]       bhikkhuniṃ vigatāsavaṃ .

Akkosiṃ duṭṭhacittāhaṃ         gaṇiketi sakiṃ tadā    
teneva pāpakammena         nirayamhi apaccisaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 395 -->
<a id="page-395"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 395]*

Teneva [^395-1] kammasesena      ajāyiṃ gaṇikākule    
bahusovaparādhīnā [^395-2]         pacchimāya ca jātiyā .

Kāsiseṭṭhikule jātā        brahmacariyaphalenahaṃ [^395-3]    
accharā viya devesu         ahosiṃ rūpasampadā .

Disvāna [^395-4] dassanīyaṃ maṃ       giribbajapuruttame    
gaṇikatte nivesesuṃ          akkosanaphalena me [^395-5] .

Sāhaṃ suṇitvāna saddhammaṃ       buddhaseṭṭhassa bhāsitaṃ [^395-6]    
pubbavāsanasampannā          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Tadupasampadatthāya [^395-7]         gacchantī [^395-8] jinasantikaṃ    
magge dhutte ṭhite disvā [^395-9]  labhiṃ dūtopasampadaṃ .

Sabbakammaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ           puññapāpaṃ tatheva ca    
sabbasaṃsāramuttiṇṇā          gaṇikattañca khepitaṃ .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ mama mahāvīra           uppannaṃ tava santike .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

#[^395-1] ma. Tena kammāvasesena . [^395-2] yu. ...Parādhitā . [^395-3] ma. Brahmacārībalenahaṃ .

#[^395-4] yu. Disvātidassanīyaṃ . [^395-5] ma. ...Balena me . [^395-6] ma. Yu. Buddhaseṭṭhena desitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 396 -->
<a id="page-396"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 396]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ aḍḍhakāsikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Aḍḍhakāsītheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ puṇṇikātheriyāpadānaṃ (38)    
[178] vipassino bhagavato           sikhino vessabhussa ca    
kakusandhassa munino           konāgamanatādino .

Kassapassa ca buddhassa         pabbajitvāna sāsane    
bhikkhunī sīlasampannā          nipakā saṃvutindriyā .

Bahussutā dhammadharā          dhammatthaparipucchitā [^396-1]    
uggahetvā [^396-2] ca dhammānaṃ    sotā payirupāsikā [^396-3] .

Desentī janamajjhehaṃ         ahosiṃ jinasāsanaṃ [^396-4]    
bāhusaccena tenāhaṃ         pesalā atimaññisaṃ .

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        sāvatthiyaṃ puruttame    
anāthapiṇḍino gehe         jātāhaṃ kumbhadāsiyā .

Gatā udakahāriyaṃ            sotthiyaṃ dijamaddasaṃ    
sītaṭṭaṃ toyamajjhamhi          taṃ disvā idamabraviṃ .

#[^396-1] ma. ...Paṭipucchikā . Yu. Atthatthaparipucchikā . [^396-2] ma. Yu. Uggahetā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 397 -->
<a id="page-397"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 397]*

Udakahārī [^397-1] ahaṃ sīte       sadā udakamotariṃ    
ayyānaṃ daṇḍabhayabhītā         vācārosabhayaṭṭitā [^397-2] .

Kassa brāhmaṇa tvaṃ bhīto      sadā udakamotari    
vedhamānehi gattehi         sītaṃ vedayase bhusaṃ .

Jānantī vata maṃ bhoti [^397-3]      puṇṇike paripucchasi [^397-4]    
karontaṃ [^397-5] kusalaṃ kammaṃ       nudantaṃ kammapāpakaṃ .

Yo vuḍḍho [^397-6] daharo cāpi    pāpakammaṃ pakubbati    
udakābhisecanā sopi         pāpakammā pamuccati .

Uttarantassa akkhāsiṃ         dhammatthasahitaṃ padaṃ    
tañca sutvā susaṃviggo [^397-7]     pabbajitvārahā ahu .

Pūrentī ūnakasataṃ            jātā dāsikule yato    
tato puṇṇāti nāmaṃ me       bhujissaṃ [^397-8] maṃ akaṃsu te .

Seṭṭhiṃ tatonumodetvā [^397-9]    pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
na cireneva kālena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
#[^397-1]  ma. Udahārī . Yu. Udabhārī . [^397-2] ma. Yu. Vācādosabhayaṭṭitā . [^397-3] yu. Hoti .

#[^397-4] yu. Paripucchiyaṃ . [^397-5] ma. Rundantaṃ . Yu. Niddhantaṃ . [^397-6] ma. Yo ce vuḍḍho daharo    
#vā . Yu. Yo ca vuddho daharo vā . [^397-7] ma. Yu. Sasaṃviggo . [^397-8] yu. Bhujissañca ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 398 -->
<a id="page-398"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 398]*

Ñāṇaṃ me vimalaṃ suddhaṃ         buddhaseṭṭhassa vāhasā .

Bhāvanāya mahāpaññā         suteneva sutāvinī    
mānena nīcakulajā           na hi kammaṃ panassati [^398-1] .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        viharāmi anāsavā .

Nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ puṇṇikā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Puṇṇikātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ    
navamaṃ ambapālītheriyāpadānaṃ (39)    
[179] yo raṃsirucitāveḷo [^398-2]       phusso nāma mahāmuni    
tassāhaṃ bhaginī āsiṃ          ajāyiṃ khattiye kule .

Tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāhaṃ         vippasannena cetasā    
mahādānaṃ daditvāna          patthayiṃ rūpasampadaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe       sikhī lokagganāyako    
uppanno lokapajjoto       tilokasaraṇo jino .

Tadā aruṇapure ramme        brahmaññakulasambhavā    
vimuttacittaṃ kupitā           bhikkhuniṃ abhisāpayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 399]*

Vesikā ca [^399-1] anācārā     jinasāsanadūsikā    
evaṃ akkosayitvāna         tena pāpena kammunā .

Dāruṇaṃ nirayaṃ gantvā         mahādukkhasamappitā    
tato cutā manussesu         upapannā tapassinī .

Dasajātisahassāni            gaṇikattaṃ akārayiṃ    
tato pāpā na muccissaṃ       bhutvā [^399-2] duṭṭhavisaṃ yathā .

Brahmavesamasevissaṃ [^399-3]       kassape jinasāsane    
tena kammavipākena          ajāyiṃ tidase pure .

Pacchime bhavasampatte         ahosiṃ opapātikā    
ambasākhantare jātā        ambapālīti tenahaṃ .

Parivutā pāṇakoṭīhi          pabbajiṃ jinasāsane    
pattāhaṃ acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ          dhītā buddhassa orasā .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya [^399-4] sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ me vimalaṃ suddhaṃ         buddhaseṭṭhassa vāhasā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

#[^399-1] ma. Yu. Va . [^399-2] yu. Bhuttā . [^399-3] yu. Brahmaceraṃ asevissaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 400 -->
<a id="page-400"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 400]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ ambapālī bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Ambapālītheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ selātheriyāpadānaṃ (40)    
[180] imamhi bhaddake kappe        brahmabandhu mahāyaso    
kassapo nāma nāmena        uppajji vadataṃ varo .

Sāvatthiyaṃ puravare           upāsakakule ahaṃ    
pasutā naṃ jinavaraṃ            disvā sutvā ca desanaṃ .

Taṃ vīraṃ saraṇaṃ gantvā         sīlāni ca samādayiṃ [^400-1]    
kadāci so mahāvīro         mahājanasamāgame .

Attano abhisambodhiṃ          pakāsesi narāsabho    
ananussutadhammesu            pubbe dukkhādikesu ca .

Cakkhu ñāṇañca paññā ca       vijjālokaṃ [^400-2] ca āsi me    
taṃ sutvā uggahetvāna       paripucchāmi [^400-3] bhikkhavo .

Tena kammena sukatena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 401 -->
<a id="page-401"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 401]*

Pacchime ca bhave dāni        jātā seṭṭhimahākule    
upecca buddhaṃ saddhammaṃ        sutvā maccupasaṃhitaṃ [^401-1] .

Pabbajitvā na cireneva       sabbatthāni [^401-2] vicintayaṃ    
khepetvā āsave sabbe     arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Iddhīsu ca vasī homi          dibbāya sotadhātuyā    
cetopariyañāṇassa           vasī homi mahāmune .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi         dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā           natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Atthadhammaniruttīsu            paṭibhāṇe tatheva ca    
ñāṇaṃ me vimalaṃ suddhaṃ         buddhaseṭṭhassa vāhasā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgīva bandhanaṃ chetvā        viharāmi anāsavā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi        buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso          vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā          kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ selā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Selātheriyā apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 402 -->
<a id="page-402"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 402]*

Uddānaṃ [^402-1]    
khattiyabrāhmaṇī ceva         tathā uppaladāyikā    
siṅgālamātā sukkā ca       abhirūpanandā ceva    
aḍḍhakāsikā ceva puṇṇā ca    ambapālī selā bhikkhunīti .

Khattiyakaññāvaggo catuttho .

Apadānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

#[^402-1] ma. Atha vagguddānaṃ    
#       sumedhā ekūposathā         kuṇḍalakesī khattiyā    
#       sahassaṃ tisatā gāthā         sattatāḷīsa piñḍitā    
#       saha uddānagāthāhi          gaṇitāyo vibhāvibhi    
#       sahassaṃ tisataṃ gāthā          satta paññāsameva cāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 403]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa    
buddhavaṃso    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

Ratanacaṅkamanakaṇḍo    
[1] brahmā ca lokādhipatī samahpati [^403-1]    
katañjalī anadhivaraṃ ayācatha    
santīdha sattā apparajakkhajātikā    
desehi dhammaṃ anukampimaṃ pajanti .

[^403-2] [bhagavā lokādhipatī naruttamo    
katañjalībrahmagaṇehi yācito    
santīdha dhīrāpparajakkhajātikā    
desetu dhammaṃ anukampimaṃ pajaṃ .

Desetu sugato dhammaṃ         desetu amataṃpadaṃ    
lokānamanukampāya           dhammaṃ desetu nāyaka] .

Sampannavijjācaraṇassa tādino    
jutindharassantimadehadhārino    
tathāgatassappaṭipuggalassa    
uppajji kāruññatā sabbasatte .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 404 -->
<a id="page-404"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 404]*

[^404-1] [taṃ sutvā bhagavā satthā      idaṃ vacanamabravi]    
apārutā te amatassa dvārā    
ye sotavanto pamuñcantu saddhaṃ
