Tatthāsi rukkho [^471-4] vipulo      abhirūpo brahā [^471-5] suci    
tamajjhapatvā nisīdi            mahāsoṇassa heṭṭhato .

Tattha ñāṇavaruppajji           anantaṃ vajirūpamaṃ    
tena vicini saṅkhāre          ukkujjamavakujjataṃ [^471-6] .

Tattha sabbakilesāni           asesamabhivāhayi    
pāpuṇi kevalaṃ bodhiṃ           buddhañāṇe [^471-7] ca cuddasa .

#[^471-1] ma. Yu. Sesake anusāsitvā . [^471-2] ma. Vaddhapattaṃva . Yu. Vuḍḍhaṃ pattaṃva .

#[^471-3] ma. Āmukkamālābharaṇo . Yu. Āmuttamalayābharaṇo . [^471-4] ma. Yu. Yasavipulo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 472 -->
<a id="page-472"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 472]*

Pāpuṇitvāna sambodhiṃ          dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Mahādoṇaṃ nāgarājaṃ           vinayanto mahāmuni    
pāṭiheraṃ tadākāsi           dassayanto sadevake .

Tadā devamanussānaṃ           tamhi dhammappakāsane    
navutikoṭisahassāni [^472-1]         tariṃsu sabbasaṃsayaṃ .

Yamhi kāle mahāvīro         ovadi sakamatrajaṃ    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         nāradassa mahesino    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamo āsi samāgamo .

Yadā buddho buddhaguṇaṃ          sanidānaṃ pakāsayi    
navutikoṭisahassāni            samiṃsu vimalā tadā .

Yadā verocano nāgo        dānaṃ dadāti satthuno    
tadā samiṃsu jinaputtā          asītisatasahassino [^472-2] .

Ahantena samayena            jaṭilo uggatāpano    
antalikkhacaro āsiṃ           pañcābhiññāsu pāragū .

Tadāpāhaṃ asamasamaṃ            sasaṅghaṃ saparijanaṃ    
annapānena tappetvā        candanenābhipūjayiṃ .

Sopi maṃ tadā byākāsi        nārado lokanāyako  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 473 -->
<a id="page-473"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 473]*

Aparimeyye ito kappe       buddho [^473-1] loke bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyatta varamaggena           bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 474 -->
<a id="page-474"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 474]*

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            assamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhā titthaṃ gahetvāna       uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo hāsetvāna mānasaṃ    
adhiṭṭhahiṃ vattaṃ uggaṃ           dasapāramipūriyā .

Nagaraṃ dhaññavatī nāma           sudevo nāma khattiyo    
anomā nāma janikā          nāradassa mahesino .

Navavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
jito vijitābhirāmo           tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Ticattāri sahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vijitasenā nāma sā nārī      nanduttaro nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         padasā gamanena nikkhami    
sattāhaṃ padhānacāraṃ           acari purisuttamo [^474-1] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 475 -->
<a id="page-475"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 475]*

Brahmunā yācito santo       nārado lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          dhanañjuyyānamuttame .

Bhaddasālo jitamitto          ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
vāseṭṭho nāmupaṭṭhāko       nāradassa mahesino .

Uttarā phaggunī ceva          ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           mahāsoṇoti vuccati .

Uggarindo vasabho ca          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
indavarī [^475-1] ca gaṇḍī ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Aṭṭhāsītiratanāni             accuggato mahāmuni    
kañcanagghikasaṅkāso           dasasahassī virocati [^475-2] .

Tassa byāmappabhākāyo [^475-3]     niddhāvati disodisaṃ    
nirantaraṃ divā rattiṃ           yojanaṃ pharate sadā [^475-4] .

Na keci tena samayena         samantā yojane janā    
ukkā padīpe ujjalenti       buddharaṃsīhi otthatā .

Navutivassasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Yathā uḷubhi gaganaṃ             vicittaṃ upasobhati    
tatheva sāsanaṃ tassa           arahantehi sobhati .

Saṃsārasotaṃ tāraṇāya          sesake paṭipannake    
#[^475-1] ma. Indāvarī ca vaṇḍī ca . Yu. Indāvarī ca caṇḍī ca . [^475-2] yu. Virocatha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 476 -->
<a id="page-476"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 476]*

Dhammasetuṃ daḷhaṃ katvā         nibbuto so narāsabho .

Sopi buddho asamasamo         tepi khīṇāsavā atulatejā    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Nārado jinavusabho            nibbuto sudassane pure    
tatheva thūpavaro [^476-1]           catuyojanamuggatoti .

Nāradabuddhavaṃso navamo .

Dasamo padumuttarabuddhavaṃso    
[11] nāradassa aparena            sambuddho dipaduttamo    
padumuttaro nāma jino         akkhobbho sāgarūpamo .

Maṇḍakappo ca [^476-2] so āsi     yamhi buddho ajāyatha    
ussannakusalā janatā          tamhi kappe ajāyatha .

Padumuttarassa bhagavato          paṭhame dhammadesane    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             dhammābhisamayo ahu .

Tato paraṃpi vassante          tappayante ca pāṇine    
sattattiṃsasahassānaṃ            dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yamhi kāle mahāvīro         ānandaṃ upasaṅkami    
pitu santikaṃ upagantvā         āhani amatadundubhiṃ .

Āhate amatabherimhi          vassante dhammavuṭṭhiyo    
paññāsasatasahassānaṃ           tatiyābhisamayo ahu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 477 -->
<a id="page-477"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 477]*

Ovādako viññāpako         tārako sabbapāṇinaṃ    
desanākusalo buddho          tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         padumuttarassa satthuno    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamo āsi samāgamo .

Yadā buddho asamasamo         vasi [^477-1] vebhārapabbate    
navutikoṭisahassānaṃ            dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Puna cārikaṃ pakkante          gāmanigamaraṭṭhato    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyo āsi samāgamo .

Ahantena samayena            jaṭilo nāma raṭṭhiko    
sambuddhappamukhaṃ saṅghaṃ           sabhattaṃ dussamadāsihaṃ .

Sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       saṅghamajjhe nisīdiya    
satasahasse ito kappe        ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 478 -->
<a id="page-478"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 478]*

imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhā titthaṃ gahetvāna       uttaranti mahānadiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 479 -->
<a id="page-479"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 479]*

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhahiṃ    
akāsiṃ [^479-1] uggaṃ daḷhaṃ dhitiṃ      dasapāramipūriyā .

Byāhatā titthiyā sabbe       vimanā dummanā tadā    
na tesaṃ keci paricaranti        raṭṭhato nicchubhanti te .

Sabbe tattha samāgantvā       upagacchacha buddhasantike    
tuvaṃ nātho mahāvīra           saraṇaṃ hohi cakkhumā .

Anukampako kāruṇiko          hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sampatte titthiye sabbe       pañcasīle patiṭṭhahi [^479-2] .

Evaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi           suññataṃ titthiyehi taṃ    
vicittaṃ arahantehi            vasībhūtehi tādihi .

Nagaraṃ haṃsavatī nāma            ānando nāma khattiyo    
sujātā nāma janikā          padumuttarasatthuno [^479-3] .

Navavassa [^479-4] sahassāni         agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
nārī [^479-5] bāhano yasavatī       tayo pāsāda muttamā .

Ticattāri sahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vasuladattā [^479-6] nāma nārī      uttaro [^479-7] nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         pāsādenābhinikkhami    
sattāhaṃ padhānacāraṃ           acari purisuttamo .

#[^479-1] yu. Akāsi maggaṃ . [^479-2] ma. Patiṭṭhati . [^479-3] yu. Padamuttaramahesino .

#[^479-4] ma. Yu. Dasa ... . [^479-5] ma. Naravāhano yasovasavattī . Yu. Nāravāhano yasovasavattī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 480 -->
<a id="page-480"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 480]*

Brahmunā yācito santo       padumuttaro vināyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          mithiluyyānamuttame .

Devilo [^480-1] ca sujāto ca      ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sumano nāmupaṭṭhāko          padumuttarassa satthuno [^480-2] .

Amitā asamā ceva           ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           salaḷoti pavuccati .

Amito [^480-3] ceva tisso ca      ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
hatthā ceva sucittā [^480-4] ca     ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Aṭṭhapaññāsaratanaṃ             accuggato mahāmuni    
kañcanagghikasaṅkāso           dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Kuḍḍā kavāṭā bhitti ca        rukkhā nagasiluccayo [^480-5]    
na tassāvaraṇaṃ atthi           samantā dvādasayojane .

Vassasatasahassāni             āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Santāretvā bahujaññaṃ [^480-6]      chinditvā sabbasaṃsayaṃ    
jalitvā aggikkhandhova         nibbuto so sasāvako .

Padumuttaro jino buddho        nandārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa thūpavaro         dvādasamubbedhayojanoti .

Padumuttarabuddhavaṃso dasamo .

#[^480-1] ma. Yu. Devalo . [^480-2] ma. Yu. Mahesino . [^480-3] ma. Yu. Vitiṇṇo . [^480-4] ma. Yu. Vicittā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 481 -->
<a id="page-481"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 481]*

Ekādasamo sumedhabuddhavaṃso    
[12] padumuttarassa aparena          sumedho nāma nāyako    
durāsado uggatejo          sabbalokuttamo jino [^481-1] .

Pasannanetto sumukho          brahā uju patāpavā    
hitesī sabbasattānaṃ           bahū mocesi bandhanā .

Yadā buddho pāpuṇitvā        kevalaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
sudassanamhi nagare            dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi .

Tassāpi abhisamayā tīṇi         ahesuṃ dhammadesane    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Punāparaṃ kumbhakaṇṇaṃ            yakkhañca [^481-2] damayī jino    
navutikoṭisahassānaṃ            dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Punāparaṃ amitayaso            catusaccaṃ pakāsayi    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         sumedhassa mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Sudassanamhi nagare [^481-3]         upagañchi jino yadā    
tadā khīṇāsavā bhikkhū          samiṃsu satakoṭiyo .

Punāparaṃ devakūṭe            bhikkhūnaṃ kaṭhinatthate    
tadā navutikoṭīnaṃ             dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

#[^481-1] ma. Muni . Yu. Sabbalokuttaro muni . [^481-2] ma. Yu. Yakkhaṃ so . [^481-3] ma. Sudassanaṃ nāma  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 482 -->
<a id="page-482"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 482]*

Punāparaṃ dasabalo             yadā carati cārikaṃ    
tadā asītikoṭīnaṃ             tatiyo āsi samāgamo .

Ahantena samayena            uttaro nāma māṇavo    
asītikoṭiyo mayhaṃ            ghare sanniccitaṃ dhanaṃ .

Kevalaṃ sabbaṃ datvāna          sasaṅghe lokanāyake [^482-1]    
saraṇaṃ tassa upagañchiṃ           pabbajjaṃ abhirocayiṃ .

Sopi maṃ tadā [^482-2] byākāsi     karonto anumodanaṃ    
tiṃsakappasahassamhi             ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattha rukkhamūlamhi           bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissati maṃ jinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 483 -->
<a id="page-483"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 483]*

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhā titthaṃ gahetvāna       uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ [^483-1] vattamadhiṭṭhāsī       dasapāramipūriyā .

Suttantaṃ vinayañcāpi           navaṅgasatthusāsanaṃ    
sabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitvāna          sobhayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 484 -->
<a id="page-484"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 484]*

Tatthappamatto viharanto        nisajjaṭṭhānacaṅkame    
abhiññāpāramiṃ [^484-1] patvā       brahmalokamagañchihaṃ .

Sudassanaṃ nāma nagaraṃ           sudatto nāma khattiyo    
sudattā nāma janikā          sumedhassa mahesino .

Navavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
sucando kañcano sirivaḍḍho      tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tisoḷasasahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sumanā nāma sā nārī         punabbo [^484-2] nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         hatthiyānena nikkhami    
anūnakaṃ aṭṭhamāsaṃ             padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       sumedho lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          sudassanuyyānamuttame .

Saraṇo ca sabbakāmo ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sāgaro nāmupaṭṭhāko         sumedhassa mahesino .

Rāmā ceva surāmā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           nimbarukkhoti [^484-3] pavuccati .

Uruvelā [^484-4] ceva yasavā ca    ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
yasā [^484-5] nāma sirivā ca       ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Aṭṭhāsītiratanāni             accuggato mahāmuni    
obhāseti disā sabbā        cando tāragaṇe yathā .

#[^484-1] ma. Yu. Abhiññāsu pāramiṃ gantvā . [^484-2] ma. Punabbasu nāma . Yu. Sumitto nāma .

#[^484-3] ma. Mahānipoti . Yu. Mahānimboti . [^484-4] ma. Uruvelā yasavā ca . Yu. Uruveḷo ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 485 -->
<a id="page-485"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 485]*

Cakkavattimaṇi nāma            yathā tapati yojanaṃ    
tatheva tassa ratanaṃ            samantā pharati yojanaṃ .

Navutivassasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Tevijjā chaḷabhiññāhi          balappattehi tādihi    
samākulamidaṃ āsi             arahantehi sāsanaṃ [^485-1] .

Tepi sabbe amitayasā         vippamuttā nirūpadhī    
ñāṇālokaṃ dassayitvā         nibbutā te mahāyasā .

Sumedho jinavaro buddho        medhārāmamhi nibbuto    
dhātu vitthārikaṃ āsi          tesu tesu padesatoti .

Sumedhabuddhavaṃso ekādasamo .

Dvādasamo sujātabuddhavaṃso    
[13] tattheva maṇḍakappamhi          sujāto nāma nāyako    
sīhahanu usabhakkhandho [^485-2]        appameyyo durāsado .

Candova vimalo suddho         sataraṃsīva patāpavā [^485-3]    
evaṃ sobhati sambuddho         jalanto siriyā sadā [^485-4] .

Pāpuṇitvāna sambuddho         kevalaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
sumaṅgalamhi nagare            dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 486 -->
<a id="page-486"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 486]*

Desento [^486-1] pavaraṃ dhammaṃ      sujāto lokanāyako    
asītikoṭī abhisamiṃsuṃ            paṭhame dhammadesane .

Yadā sujāto amitayaso        deve vassamupāgami    
sattattiṃsasatasahassānaṃ          dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā sujāto asamo [^486-2]       upagañchi pitu santikaṃ    
saṭṭhisatasahassānaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         sujātassa mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Abhiññābalappattānaṃ           appattānaṃ bhavābhave    
saṭṭhisatasahassāni [^486-3]          paṭhamaṃ sannipatiṃsu te .

Punāparaṃ sannipāte           tidivorohaṇe jine    
paññāsasatasahassānaṃ           dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Upasaṅkamma [^486-4] naravusabhaṃ [^486-5]     tassa yo aggasāvako    
catūhi satasahassehi            sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkami .

Ahantena samayena            catudīpamhi issaro    
antalikkhacaro āsiṃ           cakkavatti mahabbalo .

[6]    
catudīpe mahārajjaṃ            ratane satta uttame    
buddhe niyyādayitvāna         pabbajiṃ tassa santike .

Ārāmikā janapade           uṭṭhānaṃ paṭipiṇḍiya    
#[^486-1] ma. Yu. Desente ... Sujāte  lokanāyake . [^486-2] po. Ma. Yu. Asamasamo . [^486-3] yu.

#saṭṭhisatasahassānaṃ . [^486-4] ma. Yu. Upasaṅkamanto . [^486-5] ma. Narāsabhaṃ . Yu. Naravasabhaṃ .

#[^486-6] ma. Loke acchariyaṃ disvā abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ upagantvāna vandiṃ so sujātaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 487 -->
<a id="page-487"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 487]*

Upanenti bhikkhusaṅghassa         paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ .

Sopi maṃ tadā [^487-1] byākāsi     dasasahassamhi issaro    
tiṃsakappasahassānaṃ [^487-2]          ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 488 -->
<a id="page-488"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 488]*

nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo hāsaṃ jane ahaṃ    
adhiṭṭhahiṃ vattaṃ uggaṃ           dasapāramipūriyā .

Suttantaṃ vinayañcāpi           navaṅgasatthusāsanaṃ    
sabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitvāna          sobhayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ .

Tatthappamatto viharanto        brahmaṃ bhāvetvā bhāvanaṃ    
abhiññāsu pāramiṃ gantvā       brahmalokamagañchihaṃ .

Sumaṅgalaṃ nāma nagaraṃ           uggato nāma khattiyo    
mātā pabhāvatī nāma          sujātassa mahesino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 489 -->
<a id="page-489"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 489]*

Navavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
siri upasiri cando [^489-1]         tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tevīsatisahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sirinandā nāma nārī          upaseno nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         assayānena nikkhami    
anūnanavamāsāni              padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       sujāto [^489-2] lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          sumaṅgaluyyānamuttame .

Sudassano sudevo ca          ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
nārado nāmupaṭṭhāko         sujātassa mahesino .

Nāgā [^489-3] nāgasamānā ca      ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           mahāveḷūti vuccati .

So ca rukkho ghanakkhandho [^489-4]    acchiddo hoti pattiko    
uju vaṃso brahā hoti         dassaneyyo manoramo .

Ekakkhandho pavaḍḍhitvā        tato sākhā ca [^489-5] bhijjati    
yathā subandho morahattho       evaṃ sobhati so dumo .

Na tassa kaṇṭhakā honti        nāpi chiddaṃ mahā ahu    
vitiṇṇasākho aviraḷo          sandacchāyo [^489-6] manoramo .

Sudatto ceva citto ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
subhaddā ceva padumā ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

#[^489-1] ma. Nando . Yu. Nandā . [^489-2] yu. Sujāte . [^489-3] ma. Yu. Nāgā ca nāgasamālā ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 490 -->
<a id="page-490"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 490]*

Paññāsaratano āsi           uccattanena so jino    
sabbākāravarūpeto           sabbaguṇamupāgato .

Tassa pabhā asamasamā          niddhāvati samantato    
appamāṇo atuliyo [^490-1]        upamehi [^490-2] anūpamo .

Navutivassasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Yathāpi sāgare ummi          gagane tārakā yathā    
evaṃ tadā pāvacanaṃ           arahantehi cittakaṃ [^490-3] .

[tevijjā [^490-4] chaḷabhiññāhi      balappattehi tādihi    
samākulamidaṃ āsi             arahantehi tādibhi] .

So ca buddho asamasamo        guṇāni ca tāni atuliyāni    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Sujāto jinavaro buddho        selārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva [^490-5] cetiyo satthu      tīṇi gāvutamuggatoti .

Sujātabuddhavaṃso dvādasamo .

Terasamo piyadassibuddhavaṃso    
[14] sujātassa aparena            sayambhū lokanāyako    
durāsado asamasamo           piyadassī mahāyaso .

#[^490-1] yu. Atulayo ca . [^490-2] ma. Opammehi . Yu. Opamehi . [^490-3] po. Vicittitaṃ .

#ma. Cittitaṃ . Yu. Vicitaṃ . [^490-4] ma. Yu. Ayaṃ gāthā natthi . [^490-5] ma. Yu. Tattheva tassa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 491 -->
<a id="page-491"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 491]*

Sopi buddho amitayaso         ādiccova virocati    
nihantvāna tamaṃ sabbaṃ          dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi .

Tassāpi atulatejassa          ahesuṃ abhisamayā tayo    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Sudassano devarājā          micchādiṭṭhimarocayi    
tassa diṭṭhiṃ vinodento        satthā dhammamadesayi .

Janasannipāto atulo          mahā sannipatī tadā    
navakoṭisahassānaṃ [^491-1]          dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā doṇamukhaṃ hatthiṃ           vineti [^491-2] narasārathi    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         tassāpi piyadassino    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamo āsi samāgamo .

Tato paraṃ navutikoṭī           samiṃsu ekato munī    
tatiye sannipātamhi           asītikoṭiyo ahu .

Ahantena samayena            kassapo nāma māṇavo [^491-3]    
ajjhāyiko mantadharo          tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū .

Tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna          pasādaṃ janayiṃ ahaṃ    
koṭisatasahassehi             saṅghārāmaṃ amāpayiṃ [^491-4] .

Tassa datvāna ārāmaṃ         haṭṭho saṃviggamānaso    
saraṇe [^491-5] pañcasīle ca        daḷhaṃ katvā samādayiṃ .

#[^491-1] ma. Yu. Navutikoṭisahassānaṃ . [^491-2] ma. Yu. Vinesi . [^491-3] ma. Yu. Brāhmaṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 492 -->
<a id="page-492"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 492]*

sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       saṅghamajjhe nisīdiya    
aṭṭhārasakappasate            ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 493 -->
<a id="page-493"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 493]*

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ [^493-1]    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Sudhaññaṃ nāma nagaraṃ            sudatto nāma khattiyo    
sucandā [^493-2] nāma janikā       piyadassissa satthuno .

Navavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
sunimmalavimalagirigūhā           tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tettiṃsatisahassāni [3]       nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vimalā [^493-4] nāma sā nārī      kañcanāveḷaatrajo [^493-5] .

#[^493-1] yu. Pasādayi . [^493-2] po. Sucandanā . Ma. Candā nāmāsi . [^493-3] ma. Ca . [^493-4] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 494 -->
<a id="page-494"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 494]*

Nimitte caturo disvā         rathayānena nikkhami    
chamāsaṃ padhānacāraṃ            acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       piyadassiṃ mahāmuni    
vattacakko mahāvīro          usabhuyyāne [^494-1] manorame .

Pālito sabbadassī ca          ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sobhito nāmupaṭṭhāko         piyadassissa satthuno .

Sujātā dhammadinnā ca         ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           kakudhoti [^494-2] pavuccati .

Sandako [^494-3] dhammiko ceva      ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
visākhā dhammadinnā ca         ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Sopi buddho amitayaso         dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo    
asītihatthamubbedho            sālarājāva dissati .

Aggiyā candasuriyānaṃ          natthi tādisikā pabhā    
yathā ahu pabhā tassa          asamassa mahesino .

Tassāpi devadevassa          āyu tāvattakā [^494-4] ahu    
navutivassasahassāni            loke aṭṭhāsi cakkhumā .

Sopi buddho asamasamo         yugānipi tāni atuliyāni    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

(5) piyadassī munivaro        assatthārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa jinathūpo         tīṇi yojanamuggatoti .

Piyadassibuddhavaṃso terasamo .

#[^494-1] yu. Ussāvanuyyāne . [^494-2] yu. Kakuddhoti . [^494-3] ma. Sandhako dhammako ceva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 495 -->
<a id="page-495"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 495]*

Cuddasamo atthadassibuddhavaṃso    
[15] tattheva maṇḍakappamhi          atthadassī mahāyaso    
mahātamaṃ nihantvāna           patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Brahmunā yācito santo       dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi    
amatena tappayī lokaṃ          dasasahassī sadevakā [^495-1] .

Tassāpi lokanāthassa          ahesuṃ abhisamayā tayo    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā buddho atthadassī         carati devacārikaṃ    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Punāparaṃ yadā buddho          desesi pitu santike    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         tassāpi ca mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Aṭṭhanavutisahassānaṃ            paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
aṭṭhāsītisahassānaṃ            dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Aṭṭhasattatisahassānaṃ [^495-2]        tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
anupādāvimuttānaṃ            vimalānaṃ mahesinaṃ .

Ahantena samayena            jaṭilo uggatāpano    
susimo nāma nāmena          mahiyā seṭṭhasammato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 496 -->
<a id="page-496"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 496]*

dibbaṃ mandāravaṃ pupphaṃ          padumaṃ pārichattakaṃ    
devalokā āharitvā         sambuddhaṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       atthadassī mahāmuni    
aṭṭhārase kappasate          ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 497 -->
<a id="page-497"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 497]*

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          tuṭṭho [^497-1] saṃviggamānaso    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Sobhaṇaṃ nāma nagaraṃ            sāgaro nāma khattiyo    
sudassanā nāma janikā         atthadassissa mahesino [^497-2] .

Dasavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
amaragisuragigiribāhanā [^497-3]       tayo pāsādamuttamā .

#[^497-1] ma. Yu. Haṭṭho . [^497-2] ma. Yu. Satthuno . [^497-3] ma. Amaragirisugirivāhanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 498 -->
<a id="page-498"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 498]*

Tettiṃsatisahassāni [^498-1]         nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
visākhā nāma sā nārī        selo nāmāsi atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         assayānena nikkhami    
anūnaaṭṭhamāsāni             padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       atthadassī mahāyaso    
vattacakko mahāvīro          anomuyyāne narāsabho .

Santo ca upasanto ca         ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
abhayo nāmupaṭṭhāko          atthadassissa mahesino .

Dhammā ceva sudhammā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           campakoti pavuccati .

Nakulo [^498-2] ceva nisabho ca      ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
makilā ca sunandā ca          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Sopi buddho asamasamo         asītiratanamuggato [^498-3]    
sobhati sālarājāva           uḷurājāva pūrito .

Tassa pākaṭikā raṃsi           anekasatakoṭiyo    
uddhaṃ adho dasadisā           pharanti yojanaṃ sadā [^498-4] .

Sopi buddho naravusabho [^498-5]      sabbasattuttamo muni    
vassasatasahassāni             loke aṭṭhāsi cakkhumā .

Atulaṃ dassetvā [^498-6] obhāsaṃ    virocetvā sadevake    
sopi aniccataṃ patto          yathaggupādānasaṅkhayā .

#[^498-1] ma. Yu. Tettiṃsañca sahassāni . [^498-2] po. Ālā ceva nilabho ca . [^498-3] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 499 -->
<a id="page-499"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 499]*

Atthadassī jinavaro            anomārāmamhi nibbuto    
dhātuvitthārakaṃ āsi           tesu tesu padesatoti .

Atthadassibuddhavaṃso cuddasamo .

Paṇṇarasamo dhammadassibuddhavaṃso    
[16] tattheva maṇḍakappamhi          dhammadassī mahāyaso    
tamandhakāraṃ vidhamitvā          atirocati sadevake .

Tassāpi atulatejassa          dhammacakkappavattane    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā buddho dhammadassī         vinesi sañjayaṃ isī    
tadā navutikoṭīnaṃ             dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā sakko upagañchi          sapariso vināyakaṃ    
tadā asītikoṭīnaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Tassāpi devadevassa          sannipātā tayo āsuṃ [^499-1]    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .Pa    
yadā buddho dhammadassī         saraṇe vassaṃ upāgami    
tadā koṭisahassānaṃ [^499-2]        paṭhamo āsi samāgamo .

Punāparaṃ yadā buddho          devato eti [^499-3] mānusaṃ    
tadāpi satakoṭīnaṃ             dutiyo āsi samāgamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 500 -->
<a id="page-500"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 500]*

Punāparaṃ yadā buddho          pakāsesi dhute guṇe    
tadā asītikoṭinaṃ             tatiyo āsi samāgamo .

Ahantena samayena            sakko āsiṃ purindado    
dibbagandhena mālena          turiyena abhipūjayiṃ .

Sopi maṃ tadā [^500-1] byākāsi     devamajjhe nisīdiya    
aṭṭhārasakappasate            ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissati maṃ jinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 501 -->
<a id="page-501"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 501]*

khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Saraṇaṃ nāma nagaraṃ             saraṇo nāma khattiyo    
sunandā nāma janikā          dhammadassissa satthuno .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 502 -->
<a id="page-502"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 502]*

Aṭṭhavassasahassāni            agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
arajo virajo sudassano        tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Cattāḷīsahassāni [^502-1]          nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vicikolī [^502-2] nāma nārī        atrajo puññavaḍḍhano .

Nimitte caturo disvā         pāsādenābhinikkhami    
sattāhaṃ padhānacāraṃ           acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       dhammadassī narāsabho    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Padumo pussadevo [^502-3] ca       ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sudatto nāmupaṭṭhāko         dhammadassissa satthuno .

Khemā ca saccanāmā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           timbajāloti [^502-4] vuccati .

Subhaddo kaṭissaho ceva        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
sāḷisā [^502-5] ca kaḷissā ca      ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Sopi buddho asamasamo         asītihatthamuggato    
atirocati tejena            dasasahassamhi dhātuyā .

Suphullo sālarājāva          vijjuva gagane yathā    
majjhantikeva suriyo           evaṃ so upasobhatha [^502-6] .

Tassāpi atulatejassa          samakaṃ āsi jīvitaṃ    
vassasatasahassāni             loke aṭṭhāsi cakkhumā .

#[^502-1] ma. Yu. Ticattāḷīsasahassāni . [^502-2] yu. Vicitoḷī . [^502-3] ma. Yu. Phussadevo .

#[^502-4] ma. Yu. Bimbajālo . [^502-5] ma. Sāḷiyā ca kaḷiyā ca . Yu. Sāḷiyā ca vaḷiyā ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 503 -->
<a id="page-503"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 503]*

Obhāsaṃ dassayitvāna          vimalaṃ katvāna sāsanaṃ    
cavi [^503-1] candova gagane        nibbuto so sasāvakoti .

Dhammadassī mahāvīro           kesārāmamhi [^503-2] nibbuto    
tasseva [^503-3] so thūpavaro       tīṇi yojanamuggatoti .

Dhammadassibuddhavaṃso paṇṇarasamo .

Soḷasamo siddhatthabuddhavaṃso    
[17] dhammadassissa aparena          siddhattho lokanāyako [^503-4]    
nihantvāna [^503-5] tamaṃ sabbaṃ       suriyo abbhuggato yathā .

Sopi patvāna sambodhiṃ         santārento sadevakaṃ    
abhivassi dhammameghena          nibbāpento sadevakaṃ .

Tassāpi atulatejassa          ahesuṃ abhisamayā tayo    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Punāparaṃ bhīmaraṭṭhe            yadā [^503-6] āhani dundubhiṃ    
tadā navutikoṭīnaṃ             dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yadā buddho dhammaṃ desesi      vebhāre so naruttamo [^503-7]    
tadā navutikoṭīnaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         siddhatthassa [^503-8] mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

#[^503-1] yu. Virocayi . [^503-2] ma. Sālārāmamhi . [^503-3] ma. Tatthevassa thūpavaro . [^503-4] po.

#ma. Yu. - nāma nāyako . [^503-5] ma. Nīhinitvā . Yu. Nīharitvā . [^503-6] yu. Yadi āhani  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 504 -->
<a id="page-504"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 504]*

Koṭisatānaṃ navutiyā [^504-1]        asītiyāpica koṭinaṃ    
ete āsuṃ tayo ṭhānā       vimalānaṃ samāgame .

Ahantena samayena            maṅgalo nāma tāpaso    
uggatejo duppasaho          abhiññābalasamāhito .

Jambuto phalamāhatvā [^504-2]       siddhatthassa adāsahaṃ    
paṭiggahetvāna sambuddho       idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Passatha imaṃ tāpasaṃ            jaṭilaṃ uggatāpanaṃ    
catunavuti ito kappe          ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 505 -->
<a id="page-505"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 505]*

khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti sahanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Vebhāraṃ nāma nagaraṃ           udeno nāma khattiyo    
suphassā nāma janikā          siddhatthassa mahesino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 506 -->
<a id="page-506"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 506]*

Dasavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
kokāsuppalakokanudā [^506-1]       tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tisoḷasasahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sumanā [^506-2] nāma sā nārī      anupamo nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         sivikāyābhinikkhami    
anūnadasamāsāni              padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       siddhattho lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Sambalo ca sumitto ca         ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
revato nāmupaṭṭhāko         siddhatthassa mahesino .

Sīvalā ca surāmā ca          ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           kaṇṇikāroti [^506-3] vuccati .

Suppiyo ca sambuddho ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
rammā ceva surammā ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

So buddho saṭṭhiratanaṃ          ahosi nabhamuggato    
kañcanagghikasaṅkāso           dasasahassī virocati .

Sopi buddho asamasamo         atulo appaṭipuggalo    
vassasatasahassāni             loke aṭṭhāsi cakkhumā .

Vipulappabhaṃ [^506-4] dassayitvā       pupphāpetvāna sāvake    
vilāsetvā varasamāpattiyā [^506-5]  nibbuto so sasāvako .

#[^506-1] ma. -- kokanadā . [^506-2] ma. Somanassā . [^506-3] ma. Yu. Kaṇikāroti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 507 -->
<a id="page-507"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 507]*

Siddhattho munivaro buddho       anomārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa thūpavaro         catuyojanamuggatoti .

Siddhatthabuddhavaṃso soḷasamo .

Sattarasamo tissabuddhavaṃso    
[18] siddhatthassa aparena           asamo appaṭipuggalo    
anantasīlo [^507-1] amitayaso       tisso lokagganāyako .

Tamandhakāraṃ vidhamitvā          obhāsetvā sadevakaṃ    
anukampako mahāvīro          loke uppajji cakkhumā .

Tassāpi atulā iddhi          atulā [^507-2] sīlasamādhiyo    
sabbattha pāramiṃ gantvā        dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi .

So buddho dasasahassamhi        viññāpesi giraṃ suciṃ    
koṭisatāni [^507-3] abhisamiṃsu        paṭhame dhammadesane .

Dutiyo navutikoṭīnaṃ            tatiye [^507-4] saṭṭhikoṭiyo    
bandhanāto [^507-5] pamocesi       sampatte [^507-6] naramarū tadā .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         tissassa [^507-7] ca mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Khīṇāsavasatasahassānaṃ           paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
navutisatasahassānaṃ             dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

#[^507-1] ma. Anantatejo . [^507-2] ma. Atulaṃ sīlaṃ samādhi ca . Yu. Atulasīlasamādhī ca .

#[^507-3] yu. Koṭisatasahassāni samiṃs . [^507-4] ma. Tatiyo . [^507-5] yu. Bandhanā so .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 508 -->
<a id="page-508"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 508]*

Asītisatasahassānaṃ             tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           pupphitānaṃ vimuttiyā .

Ahantena samayena            sujāto nāma khattiyo    
mahābhogaṃ chaḍḍayitvā          pabbajiṃ isipabbajjaṃ .

Mayi pabbajite sante          uppajji lokanāyako    
buddhoti saddaṃ sutvāna         pīti me upapajjatha .

Dibbaṃ mandāravaṃ pupphaṃ          padumaṃ pārichattakaṃ    
ubho hatthehi paggayha         dhunamāno upāgamiṃ .

Pātuvaṇṇaparivutaṃ              tissaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
tamahaṃ pupphaṃ gahetvāna         matthake dhāraye [^508-1] jinaṃ .

Sopi maṃ tadā [^508-2] byākāsi     janamajjhe nisīdiya    
dvānavute ito kappe        ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 509 -->
<a id="page-509"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 509]*

imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 510 -->
<a id="page-510"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 510]*

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Khemakaṃ nāma nagaraṃ            janasando [^510-1] nāma khattiyo    
padumā nāma janikā           tissassa ca mahesino .

Sattavassasahassāni            agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
guṇaselānādiyanisabho [^510-2]       tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Samatiṃsasahassāni              nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
subhaddā nāma [^510-3] sā nārī     ānando nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         assayānena nikkhami    
anūnakaṃ [^510-4] aḍḍhamāsaṃ          padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       tisso lokagganāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          yasavatīdāyamuttame [^510-5] .

Brahmadevo ca udayo ca       ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
samago [^510-6] nāmupaṭṭhāko       tissassa ca mahesino .

Phussā ceva sudattā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           asanoti pavuccati .

Sambalo ca siri ceva          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
kīsāgotamī upasenā          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

#[^510-1] ma. Yu. Janasandho . [^510-2] ma. Guhaselanārisayanisabhā . Yu. G_haselanārīnisabhā .

#[^510-3] ma. Nāmikā . [^510-4] ma. Anūnaaṭṭhamāsāni . Yu. Anūnakaṃ aṭṭhamāsaṃ . [^510-5] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 511 -->
<a id="page-511"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 511]*

Sopi buddho saṭṭhiratano        ahu uccattane jino    
anūpamo asadiso             himavā viya dissati .

Tassāpi atulatejassa          āyu āsi anuttaro    
vassasatasahassāni             loke aṭṭhāsi cakkhumā .

Uttamaṃ pavaraṃ seṭṭhaṃ           anubhotvā mahāyasaṃ    
jalitvā aggikkhandhova         nibbuto so sasāvako .

Valāhakova anilena           suriyena viya ussavo    
andhakārova dīpena [^511-1]        nibbuto so sasāvako .

Tisso jinavaro buddho         nandārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa jinathūpo         tīṇi yojanasamussitoti .

Tissabuddhavaṃso sattarasamo .

Aṭṭhārasamo pussabuddhavaṃso [^511-2]    
[19] tattheva maṇḍakappamhi          ahu satthā anuttaro    
anūpamo asamasamo            pusso [^511-3] lokagganāyako .

Sopi sabbaṃ tamaṃ hantvā        vijaṭetvā mahājaṭaṃ    
sadevakaṃ tappayanto           abhivassi amatambunā [^511-4] .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattente         phusse nakkhattamaṅgale    
asītisatasahassānaṃ [^511-5]          paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

#[^511-1] ma. Padīpena . [^511-2] ma. Yu. Phussabuddhavaṃso . [^511-3] ma. Yu. Sabbattha phusso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 512 -->
<a id="page-512"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 512]*

Navutisatasahassānaṃ             dutiyābhisamayo ahu    
asītisatasahassānaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         pussassa ca [^512-1] mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Saṭṭhisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
paññāsasatasahassānaṃ           dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Cattāḷīsasatasahassānaṃ          tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
anupādā vimuttānaṃ           vocchinnapaṭisandhinaṃ .

Ahantena samayena            vijito [^512-2] nāma khattiyo    
chaḍḍayitvā mahārajjaṃ          pabbajiṃ tassa santike .

Sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       pusso lokagganāyako    
dvenavute ito kappe        ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 513 -->
<a id="page-513"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 513]*

imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 514 -->
<a id="page-514"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 514]*

Suttantaṃ vinayañcāpi           navaṅgasatthusāsanaṃ    
sabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitvāna          sobhayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ .

Tatthappamatto viharanto        brahmaṃ bhāvetvā bhāvanaṃ    
abhiññāsu pāramiṃ gantvā       brahmalokamagañchihaṃ .

Kāsikaṃ nāma nagaraṃ            jayaseno nāma khattiyo    
sirimā nāma janikā           pussassa ca [^514-1] mahesino .

Navavassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
garuḷahaṃsasuvaṇṇatārā [^514-2]        tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tevīsatisahassāni [^514-3]          nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
kīsāgotamī nāma [4]        ānando [^514-5] nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         hatthiyānena nikkhami    
sattāhaṃ padhānacāraṃ           acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       pusso lokagganāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Surakkhito dhammaseno [^514-6]       ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sabhiyyo nāmupaṭṭhāko         pussassa ca mahesino .

Cālā ca upacālā ca         ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           āmalakoti [^514-7] vuccati .

Dhanañjayo visākho ca          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
padumā sirināgā ca [^514-8]        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

#[^514-1] ma. Yu. Pi . [^514-2] ma. Yu. Garuḷahaṃsasuvaṇṇabhārā . [^514-3] ma. Tiṃsaïtthisahassāni .

#[^514-4] ma. Yu. Nārī . [^514-5] ma. Anūpamo . [^514-6] yu. Sukhito dhammaseno ca . [^514-7] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 515 -->
<a id="page-515"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 515]*

Aṭṭhapaṇṇāsaratanaṃ             sopi accuggato muni    
sobhati sataraṃsīva              uḷurājāva pūrito .

Navutivassasahassāni            āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Ovaditvā bahū satte         santāretvā bahū jane    
sopi satthā atulayaso         nibbuto so sasāvako .

Pusso jinavaro satthā         senārāmamhi nibbuto    
dhātuvitthārikaṃ āsi           tesu tesu padesatoti .

Pussabuddhavaṃso aṭṭhārasamo .

Ekūnavīsatimo vipassibuddhavaṃso    
[20] pussassa aparena             sambuddho dipaduttamo    
vipassī nāma nāmena          loke uppajji cakkhumā .

Avijjaṇḍaṃ [^515-1] padāletvā      patto sambodhimuttamaṃ    
dhammacakkaṃ pavattetuṃ           pakkāmi bandhumatīpuraṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattetvā         ubho bodhesi nāyako    
gaṇanāya na vattabbo          paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Punāparaṃ amitayaso            tattha saccaṃ pakāsayi    
caturāsītisahassānaṃ            dutiyābhisamayo ahu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 516 -->
<a id="page-516"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 516]*

Caturāsītisahassāni            sambuddhamanupabbajuṃ    
tesamārāmapattānaṃ           dhammaṃ desesi cakkhumā .

Sabbākārena bhāsato         ṭhatvā [^516-1] upanissā jino [^516-2]    
tepi dhammavaraṃ gantvā         tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         vippassissa mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Aṭṭhasaṭṭhisatasahassānaṃ          paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
bhikkhusatasahassānaṃ             dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Asītibhikkhusahassānaṃ            tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
tattha bhikkhugaṇamajjhe           sambuddho atirocati .

Ahantena samayena            nāgarājā mahiddhiko    
atulo nāma nāmena          puññavanto jutindharo .

Nekānaṃ nāgakoṭīnaṃ           parivāretvānahaṃ tadā    
vajjanto dibbaturiyehi         lokajeṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Upasaṅkamitvāna sambuddhaṃ        vipassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
maṇimuttāratanakhacitaṃ            sabbābharaṇavibhūsitaṃ    
nimantetvā dhammarājassa       suvaṇṇapīṭhamadāsahaṃ .

Sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       saṅghamajjhe nisīdiya    
ekanavuti ito kappe         ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 517 -->
<a id="page-517"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 517]*

ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assatthamūle sambodhiṃ          bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 518 -->
<a id="page-518"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 518]*

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhā titthaṃ gahetvā        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi [^518-1] vacanaṃ sutvā       bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Nagaraṃ bandhumatī nāma           bandhumo nāma khattiyo    
mātā bandhumatī nāma          vipassissa mahesino .

Aṭṭhavassasahassāni            agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
nando sunando sirimā         tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Ticattārisahassāni            nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sudassanā [^518-2] nāma nārī       samavattakkhandho [^518-3] nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         rathayānena nikkhami    
anūnaaṭṭhamāsāni             padhānaṃ padahī jino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 519 -->
<a id="page-519"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 519]*

Brahmunā yācito santo       vipassī lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Khandho ca tissanāmo ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
asoko nāmupaṭṭhāko         vipassissa mahesino .

Candā ca candamittā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           pāṭalīti pavuccati .

Punabbasumitto nāgo ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
sirimā uttarā ceva          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Asītihatthamubbedho            vipassī lokanāyako    
pabhā niddhāvatī tassa          samantā sattayojane .

Asītivassasahassāni            āyu buddhassa tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Bahudevamanussānaṃ             bandhanaṃ parimocayi    
maggāmaggañca ācikkhi         avasesaputhujjane .

Ālokaṃ dassayitvāna          dassetvā amataṃ padaṃ    
jalitvā aggikkhandhova         nibbuto so sasāvako .

Iddhivaraṃ puññavaraṃ             lakkhaṇacakkasumitaṃ [^519-1]    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Vipassī naravaro vīro          sumittārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa thūpavaro         sattayojanamussitoti .

Vipassibuddhavaṃso ekūnavīsatimo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 520 -->
<a id="page-520"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 520]*

Vīsatimo sikhibuddhavaṃso[^520-2]    
[21] vipassissa aparena            sambuddho dipaduttamo    
sikhivhayo [^520-1] nāma jino       asamo appaṭipuggalo .

Mārasenaṃ pamadditvā          patto sambodhimuttamaṃ    
dhammacakkaṃ pavattesi           anukampāya pāṇinaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattente         sikhimhi jinapuṅgave    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Aparaṃpi dhammaṃ desente        gaṇaseṭṭhe naruttame    
navutikoṭisahassānaṃ            dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ [^520-2]           dassayante sadevake    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         sikhissāpi mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Bhikkhusatasahassānaṃ             paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
asītibhikkhusahassānaṃ            dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Sattatibhikkhusahassānaṃ           tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
anupalitto padumaṃva            toyamhi sampavaḍḍhitaṃ .

Ahantena samayena            arindamo nāma khattiyo    
sambuddhappamukhaṃ saṅghaṃ           annapānena tappayiṃ .

#[^520-1] yu. Sikhisvahayo . Ma. Sikhivhayo āsi jino . [^520-2] ma. Yamakapāṭihāriyañca . Yu. Yamakaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 521 -->
<a id="page-521"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 521]*

Bahudussavaraṃ datvā            dussakoṭiṃ anappakaṃ    
alaṅkataṃ hatthiyānaṃ            sambuddhassa adāsahaṃ .

Hatthiyānaṃ nimminitvā          kappiyaṃ upanāmayiṃ    
pūrayiṃ mānasaṃ mayhaṃ            niccaṃ daḷhamupaṭṭhitaṃ .

Sopi maṃ buddho byākāsi       sikhī lokagganāyako    
ekattiṃse ito kappe        ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā [^521-1]      nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo  .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 522 -->
<a id="page-522"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 522]*

khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Nagaraṃ aruṇavatī nāma           aruṇo nāma khattiyo    
pabhāvatī nāma janikā          sikhissa ca mahesino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 523 -->
<a id="page-523"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 523]*

Sattavassasahassāni            agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
suvaḍḍhako giri nārīvāhano [^523-1]   tayo pasādamuttamā .

Catuvīsatisahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sabbakāmā nāma sā nārī      atulo nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         hatthiyānena nikkhamiṭa    
aṭṭhamāsaṃ padhānacāraṃ          acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       sikhī lokagganāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Abhibhū sambhavo nāma [^523-2]        ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
khemaṅkaro nāmupaṭṭhāko       sikhissa ca mahesino .

Makhilā [^523-3] ca padumā ca        ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           puṇḍarīkoti vuccati .

Sirivaḍḍho ca nando [^523-4] ca      ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
cittā ceva sucittā ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Uccattanena so buddho        sattatihatthamuggato    
kañcanagghikasaṅkāso           dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

Tassāpi byāmappabhā kāyā     niccharanti [^523-5] nirantaraṃ    
disodisaṃ niccharanti            tīṇi yojanāni so pabhā .

Sattativassasahassāni           āyu tassa mahesino    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

#[^523-1] ma. Sucandako giri vasabho . Yu. Sucando giri vahano . [^523-2] ma. Ceva . [^523-3] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 524 -->
<a id="page-524"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 524]*

Dhammameghaṃ vassitvāna [^524-1]       temayitvā sadevake    
khemataṃ pāpayitvāna           nibbuto so sasāvako .

Anubyañjanasampannaṃ            dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Sikhī munivaro buddho           assārāmamhi [^524-2] nibbuto    
tattheva tassa thūpavaro         tīṇi yojanamuggatoti .

Sikhibuddhavaṃso vīsatimo .

Ekavīsatimo vessabhūbuddhavaṃso    
[22] tattheva maṇḍakappamhi          asamo appaṭipuggalo    
vessabhū nāma nāmena         loke uppajji so [^524-3] jino .

Ādittaṃ ida [^524-4] rāgaggi       taṇhānaṃ vijitaṃ jānaṃ [^524-5]    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā        patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattesi           vessabhū lokanāyako    
asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Pakkante cārikaṃ raṭṭhe        lokajeṭṭhe narāsabhe    
sattatikoṭisahassānaṃ           dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Mahādiṭṭhiṃ vinodento         pāṭiheraṃ [^524-6] karoti so    
samāgatā naramarū             dasasahassīsadevake .

#[^524-1] ma. Pavassetvā . Yu. Dhammamegho pavassetvā . [^524-2] yu. Dussārānamhi . [^524-3] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 525 -->
<a id="page-525"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 525]*

Mahāacchariyaṃ disvā           abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
devā ceva manussā ca        bujjhare saṭṭhikoṭiyo .

Sannipātā tayo āsuṃ         vessabhussa mahesino    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Asītikoṭisahassānaṃ            paṭhamo āsi samāgamo    
sattatibhikkhusahassānaṃ           dutiyo āsi samāgamo .

Saṭṭhibhikkhusahassānaṃ            tatiyo āsi samāgamo    
jarādibhayabhītānaṃ              orasānaṃ mahesino [^525-1] .

Tassa buddhassa asamassa         cakkaṃ vattitamuttamaṃ    
sutvāna paṇītaṃ dhammaṃ           pabbajjamabhirocayi .

Ahantena samayena            sudassano nāma khattiyo    
annapānena vatthena          sasaṅghaṃ jinamapūjayiṃ .

Mahādānaṃ pavattetvā         rattindivamatandito    
pabbajjaṃ guṇasampannaṃ           pabbajiṃ jinasantike .

Ācāraguṇasampanno           vattasīlasamāhito    
sabbaññutaṃ gavesanto          ramāmi jinasāsane .

Saddhāpītiṃ uppādetvā        buddhaṃ vandāmi sattharaṃ    
pīti uppajjati mayhaṃ           bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Anivattamānasaṃ ñatvā          sambuddho etadabravi    
ekattiṃse ito kappe        ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 526 -->
<a id="page-526"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 526]*

ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 527 -->
<a id="page-527"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 527]*

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Anomaṃ nāma nagaraṃ            suppatito nāma khattiyo    
mātā yasavatī nāma           vessabhussa mahesino .

Chabbassasahassāni [^527-1]          agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
ruci surati vaḍḍhakā [^527-2]         tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Anūnatiṃsasahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sucittā nāma sā nārī        suppabuddho nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         sivikāyābhinikkhami    
chamāsaṃ padhānacāraṃ            acari purisuttamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 528 -->
<a id="page-528"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 528]*

Brahmunā yācito santo       vessabhū lokanāyako    
vattacakko [^528-1] mahāvīro       aruṇepi [^528-2] naruttamo .

Soṇo ca uttaro ceva        ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
upasanto nāmupaṭṭhāko        vessabhussa mahesino .

Rāmā ceva samālā nāma [^528-3]   ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           mahāsāloti vuccati .

Sotthiko ceva rammo ca       ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
gotamī ca sirimā ca           ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Saṭṭhiratanamubbedho           hemayūpasamūpamo    
kāyā niccharati raṃsi           rattiṃva pabbate sikhī .

Saṭṭhivassasahassāni            āyu tassa mahesino    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Dhammaṃ vitthārikaṃ katvā         vibhajjitvā mahājanaṃ    
dhammanāvaṃ ṭhapetvāna          nibbuto so sasāvako .

Dassaneyyaṃ sabbajanaṃ           vihāraṃ iriyāpathaṃ    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Vessabhū jinavaro satthā        khemārāmamhi nibbuto    
dhātuvitthārakaṃ āsi           tesu tesu padesatoti .

Vessabhūbuddhavaṃso ekavīsatimo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 529 -->
<a id="page-529"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 529]*

Dvāvīsatimo kukkusandhabuddhavaṃso [^529-1]    
[23] vessabhussa aparena           sambuddho dipaduttamo    
kukkusandho nāma nāmena       appameyyo durāsado .

Ugghāṭetvā sabbabhavaṃ         cariyāya pāramiṅgato    
sīhova pañjaraṃ bhetvā         patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattente         kukkusandhe lokanāyake    
cattāḷīsakoṭisahassānaṃ         paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Antalikkhamhi ākāse         yamakaṃ katvā vikubbanaṃ    
tiṃsakoṭisahassānaṃ             bodhesi devamānuse .

Naradevassa yakkhassa           catusaccappakāsane    
dhammābhisamayo tassa           gaṇanāto asaṅkhiyo .

Kukkusandhassa bhagavato          eko āsi samāgamo    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Cattāḷīsasahassānaṃ            tadā āsi samāgamo    
dantabhūmimanuppattānaṃ           āsavādigaṇakkhayā .

Ahantena samayena            khemo nāmāsi khattiyo    
tathāgate jinaputte           dānaṃ datvā anappakaṃ .

Pattañca cīvaraṃ datvā          añjanaṃ madhulaṭṭhikaṃ    
imetaṃ patthitaṃ sabbaṃ           paṭiyādemi varaṃ varaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 530 -->
<a id="page-530"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 530]*

Sopi maṃ muni [^530-1] byākāsi      kukkusandho vināyako    
imamhi bhaddake kappe         ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 531 -->
<a id="page-531"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 531]*

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Nagaraṃ khemavatī nāma           khemo nāmāsahaṃ tadā    
sabbaññutaṃ gavesanto          pabbajiṃ tassa santike .

Brāhmaṇo aggidatto ca       āsi buddhassa so pitā    
visākhā nāma janikā          kukkusandhassa mahesino [^531-1] .

Vasati tattha khemapure          sambuddhassa mahākulaṃ    
narānaṃ pavaraṃ seṭṭhaṃ           jātimantaṃ mahāyasaṃ .

Catuvassasahassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 532 -->
<a id="page-532"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 532]*

Kāmavaḍḍhakāmasuddhirativaḍḍhano [^532-1]  tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Samatiṃsasahassāni              nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
ropinī [^532-2] nāma sā nārī      uttaro nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         rathayānena nikkhami    
anūnakaṃ aṭṭhamāsaṃ [^532-3]          padhānaṃ padahī jino .

Brahmunā yācito santo       kukkusandho lokanāyako [^532-4]    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Vidhuro sañjīvanāmo [^532-5] ca      ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
buddhijo nāmupaṭṭhāko         kukkusandhassa mahesino [^532-6] .

Sāmā ca campanāmā [^532-7] ca     ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           sirisoti pavuccati .

Accuggato ca sumano          ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
nandā ceva sunnadā ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Cattāḷīsaratanāni             accuggato mahāmuni    
kanakappabhā niccharati [^532-8]        samantā dasayojanaṃ [^532-9] .

Cattāḷīsavassasahassāni         āyu tassa mahesino    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Dhammāpaṇaṃ pasāretvā         naranārīnaṃ sadevake    
#[^532-1] sī. Ruci suruci rativaddhananāmakā . Ma. Kāmakāmavaṇṇakāmasuddhināmā . Yu. Ruci suruci    
#vaḍḍhanā . [^532-2] ma. Rocīnī . Yu. Virocamānā . [^532-3] ma. Anūnaaṭṭhamāsāni . [^532-4] ma. Vināyako .

#[^532-5] ma. Vidhuro ca sañjīvo ca . [^532-6] ma. Yu. Satthuno . [^532-7] ma. Sāmā ca campā nāmā ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 533 -->
<a id="page-533"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 533]*

Naditvā sīhanādaṃva [^533-1]         nibbuto so sasāvako .

Aṭṭhaṅgavacanasampanno          acchiddāni nirantaraṃ    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Kukkusandho jinavaro           khemārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa thūpavaro         gāvutanabhamuggatoti .

Kukkusandhabuddhavaṃso dvāvīsatimo .

Tevīsatimo konāgamanabuddhavaṃso    
[24] kukkusandhassa aparena          sambuddho dipaduttamo    
konāgamano nāma jino        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho .

Dasadhamme pūrayitvāna          kantāraṃ samatikkami    
pavāhāya [^533-2] malaṃ sabbaṃ        patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattente         konāgamane [^533-3] vināyake    
tiṃsakoṭisahassānaṃ             paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Pāṭihīraṃ karonte ca          paravādappamaddane    
vīsatikoṭisahassānaṃ            dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Tato vikubbanaṃ katvā          jino devapuraṃ gato    
vasati tattha sambuddho          silāya paṇḍukambale .

Pakaraṇe satta desento       vassaṃ vasati so muni    
dasakoṭisahassānaṃ             tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

#[^533-1] yu. Sīhanādañca . [^533-2] ma. Yu. Pavāhiya . [^533-3] ma. Konāgamananāyake . Yu. Konāgamane$  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 534 -->
<a id="page-534"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 534]*

Tassāpi devadevassa          eko āsi samāgamo    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Tiṃsabhikkhusahassānaṃ             tadā āsi samāgamo    
oghānamatikkantānaṃ [^534-1]        bhijjitānañca maccuyā .

Ahantena samayena            pabbato nāma khattiyo    
mittāmaccehi sampanno        balabāhanamappakaṃ [^534-2] .

Sambuddhadassanaṃ gantvā         sutvā dhammamanuttaraṃ    
nimantetvā sajinaṃ saṅghaṃ        dānaṃ datvā yadicchakaṃ .

Pattuṇṇaṃ cīnapaṭañca            koseyyaṃ kambalaṃpica    
sovaṇṇapādukañceva           adāsi [^534-3] satthu sāvake .

Sopi maṃ muni byākāsi         saṅghamajjhe nisīdiya    
imasmiṃ bhaddake kappe         ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlasmiṃ            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ paggayha         nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ adi so jino    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimaṇḍamhi ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ    
assattharukkhamūlamhi            bujjhissati mahāyaso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 535 -->
<a id="page-535"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 535]*

imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti         apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 536 -->
<a id="page-536"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 536]*

Anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Sabbaññutaṃ gavesanto          dānaṃ datvā naruttamo [^536-1]    
ohāyāhaṃ mahārajjaṃ          pabbajiṃ jinasantike .

Nagaraṃ sobhavatī nāma           sobho nāmāsi khattiyo    
vasati tattha nagare            sambuddhassa mahākulaṃ .

Brāhmaṇo yaññadatto ca       āsi buddhassa so pitā    
uttarā nāma janikā          konāgamanassa satthuno .

Tīṇi vassasahassāni            agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
tusitasantusitasantuṭṭhā          tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Anūnasoḷasasahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
rucigattā nāma nārī          satthavāho nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         hatthiyānena nikkhami    
chamāsaṃ padhānacāraṃ            acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       konāgamano [^536-2] lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Bhiyyoso [^536-3] uttaro nāma     ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sotthijo nāmupaṭṭhāko        konāgamanassa yasassino [^536-4] .

#[^536-1] ma. Yu. Naruttame . [^536-2] ma. Konāgamananāyako . [^536-3] ma. Bhiyyaso . [^536-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 537 -->
<a id="page-537"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 537]*

Samuddā uttarā ceva         ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           udumbaroti vuccati .

Uggo ca somadevo ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā
