sīvalā ceva sāmā ca         ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Uccattanena so buddho        tiṃsahatthasamuggato    
ukkāmukhe yathā tambaṃ [^537-1]      evaṃ raṃsīhi maṇḍito .

Tiṃsavassasahassāni             āyu buddhassa tāvade    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Dhammacetiyaṃ [^537-2] samussitvā      dhammadussavibhūsitaṃ    
dhammapupphaguḷaṃ katvā           nibbuto so sasāvako .

Mahāvilāso tassa jano        siridhammappakāsano    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Konāgamano sambuddho         pabbatārāmamhi nibbuto    
dhātuvitthārikaṃ āsi           tesu tesu padesatoti .

Konāgamanabuddhavaṃso tevīsatimo .

Catuvīsatimo kassapabuddhavaṃso    
[25] konāgamanassa aparena         sambuddho dipaduttamo    
kassapo nāma nāmena [^537-3]      dhammarājā pabhaṅkaro .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 538 -->
<a id="page-538"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 538]*

Sa [^538-1] chaḍḍitaṃ kulamūlaṃ          bahūnaṃ [^538-2] pāṇapūjitaṃ    
datvāna dāyake dānaṃ         pūrayitvāna mānasaṃ    
usabhovāḷakaṃ [^538-3] bhetvā       patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattente         kassape lokanāyake    
vīsatikoṭisahassānaṃ            paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Catumāsaṃ yadā buddho          loke carati cārikaṃ    
dasakoṭisahassānaṃ             dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Yamakaṃ vikubbanaṃ katvā          ñāṇadhātuṃ pakittayi    
pañcakoṭisahassānaṃ            tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Sudhammadevapure [^538-4] ramme      tattha dhammaṃ pakittayi [^538-5]    
tīṇi koṭisahassāni            devānaṃ bodhayī jino .

Naradevassa yakkhassa           apare ca dhammadesane    
etesānaṃ abhisamayo [^538-6]       gaṇanāto asaṅkhiyo [^538-7] .

Tassāpi devadevassa          eko āsi samāgamo    
khīṇāsavānaṃ vimalānaṃ           santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Vīsatibhikkhusahassānaṃ            tadā āsi samāgamo    
abhikkantabhavantānaṃ [^538-8]         hirisīlena tādinaṃ .

Ahaṃ tadā māṇavako           jotipāloti vissuto    
ajjhāyiko mantadharo          tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū .

#[^538-1] ma. Yu. Sañchaḍḍitaṃ . [^538-2] ma. Bahvannapānabhojanaṃ . [^538-3] ma. Yu. Usabhova āḷakaṃ .

#[^538-4] ma. Sudhammā... . [^538-5] yu. Pakāsayi . 6-[^538-7] ma. Yu. Abhisamayā ... Asaṅkhiyā . [^538-8] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 539 -->
<a id="page-539"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 539]*

Lakkhaṇe itihāse ca          saddhamme pāramiṃ gato    
bhummantalikkhe [^539-1] kusalo       katavijjo anāmayo .

Kassapassa bhagavato            ghaṭikāro nāmupaṭṭhako    
sagāravo sappatisso          nibbuto tatiye phale .

Ādāya maṃ ghaṭikāro          upagañchi kassapaṃ jinaṃtha    
tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna          pabbajiṃ tassa santike .

Āraddhaviriyo hutvā          vattāvattesu kovido    
na kvaci parihāyāmi           pūremi [^539-2] jinasāsanaṃ .

Yāvatā buddhabhaṇitaṃ            navaṅgasatthusāsanaṃ    
sabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitvāna          sobhayiṃ jinasāsanaṃ .

Mama acchariyaṃ disvā           sopi buddho viyākari    
imamhi bhaddake kappe         ayaṃ buddho bhavissati .

Ahu kapilavhayā rammā         nikkhamitvā tathāgato    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            katvā dukkarakārikaṃ .

Ajapālarukkhamūlamhi            nisīditvā tathāgato    
tattha pāyāsaṃ samādāya [^539-3]     nerañjaramupehiti .

Nerañjarāya tīramhi           pāyāsaṃ paribhuñjiya    
paṭiyattavaramaggena            bodhimūlamhi [^539-4] ehiti .

Tato padakkhiṇaṃ katvā          bodhimaṇḍaṃ anuttaraṃ [^539-5]    
assatthamūle sambodhiṃ          bujjhissati mahāyaso .

#[^539-1] ma. Yu. Bhūmanta... . [^539-2] ma. Pūresiṃ . [^539-3] ma. Yu. Paggayha . [^539-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 540 -->
<a id="page-540"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 540]*

Imassa janikā mātā          māyā nāma bhavissati    
pitā suddhodano nāma         ayaṃ hessati gotamo .

Kolito upatisso ca          aggā hessanti sāvakā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         aggā hessanti sāvikā    
anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā .

Bodhi tassa bhagavato           assatthoti pavuccati    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggā hessantupaṭṭhakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggā hessantupaṭṭhikā    
āyu vassasataṃ tassa           gotamassa yasassino .

Idaṃ sutvāna vacanaṃ            asamassa mahesino    
āmoditā naramarū            buddhavījaṅkuro ayaṃ [^540-1] .

Ukkuṭṭhisaddā vattanti [^540-2]      apphoṭenti hasanti ca    
katañjalī namassanti            dasasahassī sadevakā .

Yadimassa lokanāthassa          virajjhissāma sāsanaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Yathā manussā nadiṃ tarantā      paṭititthaṃ virajjhiya    
heṭṭhātitthaṃ gahetvāna        uttaranti mahānadiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 541 -->
<a id="page-541"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 541]*

Evameva mayaṃ sabbe          yadi muñcāmimaṃ jinaṃ    
anāgatamhi addhāne          hessāma sammukhā imaṃ .

Tassāpi vacanaṃ sutvā          bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
uttariṃ vattamadhiṭṭhāsiṃ          dasapāramipūriyā .

Evamahaṃ saṃsaritvā            parivajjento anācaraṃ    
dukkaraṃ pakataṃ [^541-1] mayhaṃ         bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Nagaraṃ bārāṇasī nāma          kikī nāmāsi khattiyo    
vasati tattha nagare            sambuddhassa mahākulaṃ .

Brāhmaṇo brahmadatto ca      āsi buddhassa so pitā    
mātā [^541-2] dhanavatī nāma        kassapassa mahesino .

Duve vassasahassāni           agāraṃ ajjhāvasi so    
haṃso yaso siricando [^541-3]       tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Tisoḷasasahassāni             nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
sunandā nāma sā nārī        vijitaseno nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         pāsādenābhinikkhami    
sattāhaṃ padhānacāraṃ           acari purisuttamo .

Brahmunā yācito santo       kassapo lokanāyako    
vattacakko mahāvīro          migadāye naruttamo .

Tisso ca bhāradvājo ca       ahesuṃ aggasāvakā    
sabbamitto nāmupaṭṭhāko       kassapassa mahesino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 542 -->
<a id="page-542"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 542]*

Anulā ca uruvelā ca         ahesuṃ aggasāvikā    
bodhi tassa bhagavato           nigrodhoti pavuccati .

Sumaṅgalo ghaṭikāro ca         ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhakā    
vijitasenā ca bhaddā ca        ahesuṃ aggupaṭṭhikā .

Uccattanena so buddho        vīsatiratanamuggato [^542-1]    
vijjulaṭṭhiva ākāse          candova gahapūrito .

Vīsativassasahassāni            āyu tassa mahesino    
tāvatā tiṭṭhamāno so        tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Dhammataḷākaṃ māpayitvā [^542-2]      sīlaṃ datvā vilepanaṃ    
dhammadussaṃ nivāsetvā         dhammamālaṃ vibhajjiya [^542-3] .

Dhammavimalamādāsaṃ             ṭhapayitvā mahājane    
keci nibbānaṃ patthentā       passantu me alaṅkaraṃ .

Sīlakañcukaṃ datvāna            jhānakavacacammikaṃ [^542-4]    
dhammacammaṃ pārupitvā          datvā sannāhamuttamaṃ .

Satiphalakaṃ datvāna             tikhiṇañāṇakuntimaṃ    
dhammakhaggavaraṃ datvā           sīlasattuppamaddanaṃ [^542-5] .

Tevijjābhūsaṃ [^542-6] datvāna       āveḷaṃ caturo phale    
chaḷabhiññābharaṇaṃ datvā          dhammapupphapilandhanaṃ .

Saddhammapaṇḍaracchattaṃ           datvā pāpanivāraṇaṃ    
māpayitvā abhayaṃ pupphaṃ         nibbuto so sasāvako .

#[^542-1] ma. Vīsatiratanuggato . [^542-2] yu. Māpetvā . [^542-3] yu. Virājiya . [^542-4] ma. ...Vammitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 543 -->
<a id="page-543"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 543]*

Eso hi sammāsambuddho       appameyyo durāsado    
eso hi dhammaratano          svākkhāto ehipassiko .

Eso hi saṅgharatano          supaṭipanno anuttaro    
sabbaṃ samantarahitaṃ             nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Mahākassapo jino satthā       setabyārāmamhi nibbuto    
tattheva tassa jinathūpo         yojanubbedhamuggatoti .

Kassapabuddhavaṃso catuvīsatimo .

Pañcavīsatimo gotamabuddhavaṃso    
[26] ahametarahi sambuddho          gotamo sakyavaḍḍhano    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna            patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Brahmunā yācito santo       dhammacakkaṃ pavattayiṃ    
aṭṭhārasannaṃ koṭīnaṃ           paṭhamābhisamayo ahu .

Tato parañca desento        naradevasamāgame [^543-1]    
gaṇanāya na vattabbo          dutiyābhisamayo ahu .

Idhevāhaṃ etarahi            ovadiṃ mama atrajaṃ    
gaṇanāya na vattabbo          tatiyābhisamayo ahu .

Ekosi [^543-2] sannipāto me     sāvakānaṃ mahesinaṃ    
aḍḍhateḷasasatānaṃ             bhikkhūnāsi samāgamo .

Virocamāno vimalo           bhikkhusaṅghassa majjhato [^543-3]    
dadāmi patthitaṃ sabbaṃ           maṇīva sabbakāmado .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 544 -->
<a id="page-544"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 544]*

Phalamākaṅkhamānānaṃ            bhavacchandaṃ jahesinaṃ    
catusaccaṃ pakāsesi [^544-1]         anukampāya pāṇinaṃ .

Dasavīsasahassānaṃ              dhammābhisamayo ahu    
ekadvinnaṃ abhisamayo          gaṇanāto asaṅkhiyo .

Vitthārikaṃ bahujaññaṃ            iddhaṃ phītaṃ suphullitaṃ    
idha mayhaṃ sakyamunino          sāsanaṃ suvisodhitaṃ .

Anāsavā vītarāgā           santacittā samāhitā    
bhikkhū nekasatā sabbe         parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Idāni ye etarahi           jahanti mānusaṃ bhavaṃ    
appattamānasā sekkhā        te bhikkhū viññūgarahitā .

Ariyañjasaṃ thomayantā          sadā dhammaratā janā    
bujjhissanti jutimanto [^544-2]       saṃsārasaritā [^544-3] narā .

Nagaraṃ kapilavatthu me           rājā suddhodano pitā    
mayhaṃ janettikā mātā        māyādevīti vuccati .

Ekūnatiṃsavassāni             agāraṃ ajjhāvasihaṃ [^544-4]    
sucando [^544-5] kokanudo koñco   tayo pāsādamuttamā .

Cattāḷīsasahassāni            nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
yasodharā [^544-6] nāma nārī       rāhulo nāma atrajo .

Nimitte caturo disvā         assayānena nikkhamiṃ    
#[^544-1] ma. Pakāsemi . [^544-2] ma. Yu. Satimanto . [^544-3] ma. Saṃsārasaritaṃ gatā .

#[^544-4] ma. Yu. Ajjhahaṃ vasiṃ . [^544-5] ma. Rammo surammo subhako . Yu. Rāmo surāmo subhato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 545 -->
<a id="page-545"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 545]*

Chavassaṃ padhānacāraṃ            acariṃ dukkaraṃ ahaṃ .

Bārāṇasīisipatane            jino [^545-1] cakkaṃ pavattayiṃ    
ahaṃ gotamasambuddho           saraṇaṃ sabbapāṇinaṃ .

Kolito upatisso ca          dve bhikkhū aggasāvakā    
ānando nāmupaṭṭhāko        santikāvacaro mama .

Khemā uppalavaṇṇā ca         bhikkhunī aggasāvikā    
citto ca hatthāḷavako         aggupaṭṭhākupāsakā .

Nandamātā ca uttarā         aggupaṭṭhākupāsikā    
ahaṃ assatthamūlamhi            patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Byāmappabhā sadā mayhaṃ        soḷasahatthamuggato [^545-2]    
appaṃ vassasataṃ āyu           mametarahi [^545-3] vijjati .

Tāvatā tiṭṭhamānohaṃ          tāresiṃ [^545-4] janataṃ bahuṃ    
ṭhapayitvāna dhammokkaṃ [^545-5]       pacchimajanabodhanaṃ .

Ahaṃpi na cirasseva            saddhiṃ sāvakasaṅghato    
idheva parinibbissaṃ            aggivāhārasaṅkhayā .

Tāni [6] atulatejāni       imāni ca yasabalāni [^545-7] iddhiyo    
ahaṃ [^545-8] guṇadhāraṇo deho      dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇavicitto .

Dasadisā [^545-9] pabhāsetvā       sataraṃsīva chappabhā    
sabbā samantarahessanti        nanu rittā sabbasaṅkhārāti .

Gotamabuddhavaṃso pañcavīsatimo .

#[^545-1] ma. Yu. Cakkaṃ pavattitaṃ mayā . [^545-2] ma. ...Muggatā . [^545-3] ma. Yu. Idānetarahi .

#[^545-4] ma. Yu. Tāremi . [^545-5] ma. Dhammakkaṃ . [^545-6] ma. Yu. Ca . [^545-7] ma. Yu. Dasabalāni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 546 -->
<a id="page-546"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 546]*

Buddhappakiṇṇakakaṇḍo    
[27] aparimeyye ito kappe       caturo āsuṃ vināyakā    
taṇhaṅkaro medhaṅkaro         athopi saraṇaṅkaro    
dīpaṅkaro ca sambuddho         ekakappamhi te jinā .

Dīpaṅkarassa aparena           koṇḍañño [^546-1] nāma nāyako    
ekova ekakappamhi          tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Dīpaṅkarassa bhagavato           koṇḍaññassa ca satthuno    
etesaṃ antarā kappā        gaṇanāto asaṅkheyyā .

Koṇḍaññassa aparena          maṅgalo nāma nāyako    
tesaṃpi antarā kappā         gaṇanāto asaṅkheyyā [^546-2] .

Maṅgalo ca sumano ca          revato sobhito muni    
tepi buddhā ekakappe        cakkhumanto pabhaṅkarā .

Sobhitassa aparena            anomadassī mahāmuni [^546-3]    
tesaṃpi antarā kappā         gaṇanāto asaṅkheyyā .

Anomadassī padumo            nārado cāpi nāyako    
tepi buddhā ekakappe        tamantakaraṇā [^546-4] munī .

Nāradassa aparena            padumuttaro nāma nāyako    
ekakappamhi uppanno         tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Nāradassa bhagavato            padumuttarassa satthuno    
tesaṃpi antarā kappā         gaṇanāto asaṅkheyyā .

#[^546-1] yu. Koṇḍaññassa nāma . [^546-2] ma. Asaṅkhiyā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 547 -->
<a id="page-547"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 547]*

Kappasatasahassamhi             eko āsi mahāmuni    
padumuttaro lokavidū           āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi             duve āsiṃsu [^547-1] nāyakā    
sumedho ca sujāto ca         orato padumuttaro .

Aṭṭhārasakappasate            tayo āsiṃsu nāyakā    
piyadassī atthadassī            dhammadassī ca nāyakā .

Orato [^547-2] ca sujātassa       sambuddhā dipaduttamā    
ekakappamhi sambuddhā [^547-3]      loke appaṭipuggalā .

Catunavute ito kappe         eko āsi mahāmuni    
siddhattho so lokavidū         sallakkhato [^547-4] anuttaro .

Dvenavute ito kappe        duve āsiṃsu nāyakā    
tisso pusso ca sambuddho [^547-5]   asamo appaṭipuggalo .

Ekanavute ito kappe        vipassī lokanāyako    
sopi buddho kāruṇiko         satte mocesi bandhanā .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe        duve āsiṃsu nāyakā    
sikhī ca vessabhū ceva          asamā appaṭipuggalā .

Imamhi bhaddake kappe         tayo āsiṃsu nāyakā    
kukkusandho konāgamano        kassapo cāpi nāyako .

Ahametarahi sambuddho          metteyyo cāpi hessati    
#[^547-1] ma. Āsuṃ vināyakā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^547-2] yu. Oraso . [^547-3] ma. ... Te buddhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 548 -->
<a id="page-548"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 548]*

Etepime pañca buddhā        dhīrā lokānukampakā .

Etesaṃ dhammarājūnaṃ           aññesaṃ nekakoṭinaṃ    
ācikkhitvāna taṃ maggaṃ         nibbuto [^548-1] so sasāvakoti .

Buddhappakiṇṇakakaṇḍo niṭṭhito .

Dhātubhājanīyakathā    
[28] mahāgotamo jinavaro          kusināramhi nibbuto    
dhātuvitthārikaṃ āsi           tesu tesu padesato .

Eko ajātasattussa          eko vesāliyā pure    
eko kapilavatthusmiṃ           eko ca allakappake .

Eko ca rāmagāmamhi         eko ca veṭṭhadīpake    
eko pāveyyake malle      eko ca kosinārake .

Tumbassa [^548-2] thūpaṃ kāresi       brāhmaṇo doṇasavhayo    
aṅgārathūpaṃ kāresuṃ           moriyā tuṭṭhamānasā .

Aṭṭha sārīrikā thūpā          navamo tumbacetiyo [^548-3]    
aṅgārathūpo dasamo           tadāyeva patiṭṭhito .

[4]    
ekā dāṭhā tidasapure        ekā nāgapure ahu    
ekā gandhāravisaye          ekā kāliṅgarājino .

#[^548-1] ma. Yu. Nibbutā te sasāvakāti . [^548-2] ma. Yu. Kumbhassa . [^548-3] ma. Yu. Kumbhacetiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 549 -->
<a id="page-549"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 549]*

Cattāḷīsasamā dantā          kesā lomā ca sabbaso    
devā hariṃsu ekekaṃ          cakkavāḷaparamparā .

Vajirāyaṃ bhagavato             patto daṇḍo ca cīvaraṃ    
nivāsanaṃ kulaghare [^549-1]          paccattharaṇaṃ silavhaye [^549-2] .

Pāṭalīputtanagare             karakaṃ kāyabandhanaṃ    
campāyaṃ udakasāṭakā [^549-3]       uṇṇalomañca kosale .

Kāsāvakaṃ [^549-4] brahmaloke      veṭhanaṃ tidase pure    
[pāsāṇake [^549-5] padaṃ seṭṭhaṃ     yathāpi kacchataṃ puraṃ]    
nisīdanaṃ avantīsu [^549-6]           devaraṭṭhe [^549-7] attharaṇaṃ tadā .

Araṇi ca mithilāyaṃ             videhe [^549-8] parisāvanaṃ    
vāsī sūcigharañcāpi            indapatthapure [^549-9] tadā .

Parikkhārā 10 avasesā      janapadantake 1[^549-1] tadā    
paribhuttāni muninā            mahessanti manujā tadā .

Dhātuvitthārikaṃ āsi           gotamassa mahesino    
pāṇīnaṃ anukampāya            ahu porāṇikaṃ tadāti .

Dhātubhājanīyakathā niṭṭhitā .

Buddhavaṃso niṭṭhito .

#[^549-1] yu. Kusaghare . [^549-2] ma. Yu. Kapilhaye . [^549-3] ma. Campāyudakasāṭiyaṃ . Yu. Campāyaṃ    
#udakasāṭikā . [^549-4] ma. Yu. Kāsāvañca brahmaloke . [^549-5] yu. Pāsāṇake padaṃ seṭṭhaṃ    
#yañcāpi accuti padaṃ . [^549-6] yu. Avantipure . [^549-7] ma. Yu. Raṭṭhe ... .

#[^549-8] yu. Vedehi ... . [^549-9] ya. Indaraṭṭhe . 10 yu. Parikkhāraṃ avasesaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 550 -->
<a id="page-550"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 550]*

suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa  
cariyāpiṭakaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 551 -->
<a id="page-551"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 551]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa    
cariyāpiṭakaṃ    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

1. Dānapāramitā    
paṭhamaṃ akitticariyaṃ [^551-1]    
[1] kappe ca satasahasse          caturo ca asaṅkheyye    
etthantare yaṃ caritaṃ          sabbantaṃ bodhipācanaṃ .

Atītakappe caritaṃ             ṭhapayitvā bhavābhave    
imamhi kappe caritaṃ           pavakkhissaṃ suṇohi me .

Yadā ahaṃ brahāraññe         suññe vivanakānane [^551-2]    
ajjhogahetvā viharāmi        akitti [^551-3] nāma tāpaso .

Tadā maṃ tapatejena           santatto tidivābhibhū    
dhārento brāhmaṇavaṇṇaṃ       bhikkhāya maṃ upāgami .

Pavanā ābhaṭaṃ paṇṇaṃ           atelañca aloṇikaṃ    
mama dvāre ṭhitaṃ disvā        sakaṭāhena ākiriṃ .

Tassa datvānahaṃ paṇṇaṃ          nikujjitvāna bhājanaṃ    
punesanaṃ jahitvāna            pāvisiṃ paṇṇasālakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 552 -->
<a id="page-552"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 552]*

Dutiyaṃ divasaṃ tatiyaṃpi            upagacchi mamantike    
akampito anolaggo          evamevamadāsahaṃ .

Na me tappaccayā atthi        sarīrasmiṃ vivaṇṇiyaṃ    
pītisukhena ratiyā             vītināmemi taṃ divaṃ .

Yadi māsaṃpi dvimāsaṃ           dakkhiṇeyyaṃ varaṃ labhe    
akampito anolīno           dadeyyaṃ dānamuttamaṃ .

Na tassa dānaṃ dadamāno        yasaṃ lābhañca patthayiṃ    
sabbaññutaṃ patthayāno          tāni kammāni ācarinti .

Akitticariyaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ saṅkhabrāhmaṇacariyaṃ    
[2] punāparaṃ yadā homi           brāhmaṇo saṅkhasavhayo    
mahāsamuddaṃ taritukāmo         upagacchāmi paṭṭanaṃ .

Tatthaddasāmi [^552-1] paṭipathe       sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
kantāramaddhānaṃ paṭipannaṃ        tattāya kaṭhinabhūmiyā .

Tamahaṃ paṭipathe disvā          imamatthaṃ vicintayiṃ    
imaṃ [^552-2] khettaṃ anuppattaṃ       puññakāmassa jantuno .

Yathāpi kassako puriso         khettaṃ disvā mahārāmaṃ [^552-3]    
na tattha bījaṃ ropeti          na so dhaññena atthiko .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 553 -->
<a id="page-553"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 553]*

Evamevāhaṃ puññakāmo        disvā khettaṃ varuttamaṃ    
yadi [1] kāraṃ na karomi      nāhaṃ puññena atthiko .

Yathā amacco muddakāmo [^553-2]    rañño antepure jane    
na deti tesaṃ dhanadhaññaṃ         muddato [^553-3] parihāyati .

Evamevāhaṃ puññakāmo        vipulaṃ disvāna dakkhiṇaṃ    
yadi tattha [^553-4] dānaṃ na dāmi     parihāyissāmi puññato .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna          orohitvā upāhanā    
tassa pādāni vanditvā        adāsiṃ chattupāhanaṃ .

Tenāhaṃ [^553-5] sataguṇato         sukhumālo sukhedhito    
apica dānaṃ paripūrento        evaṃ tassa adāsahanti .

Saṅkhabrāhmaṇacariyaṃ dutiyaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kurudhammacariyaṃ [^553-6]    
[3] punāparaṃ yadā homi           indapatthe puruttame    
rājā dhanañjayo nāma         kusalehi dasahupāgato .

Kāliṅgaraṭṭhavisayā            brāhmaṇā upagacchu maṃ    
ayācuṃ maṃ hatthināgaṃ           dhaññaṃ maṅgalasammataṃ .

Avuṭṭhiko janapado            dubbhikkho chātako mahā    
dadāhi pavaraṃ nāgaṃ            nīlaṃ añjanasavhayaṃ .

#[^553-1] ma. Yu. Tattha . [^553-2] ma. Yu. Muddikāmo . [^553-3] ma. Yu. Muddito . [^553-4] ma. Yu. ... Tassa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 554 -->
<a id="page-554"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 554]*

Na me yācakamanuppatte        paṭikkhepo anucchavo    
mā me bhijji samādānaṃ        dassāmi vipulaṃ gajaṃ .

Nāgaṃ gahetvā soṇḍāya       bhiṅgāre ratanāmaye    
jalaṃ hatthe ākiritvā         brāhmaṇānaṃ adaṃ gajaṃ .

Tassa [^554-1] nāge padinnamhi      amaccā etadabravava    
kiṃ nu tuyhaṃ varaṃ nāgaṃ          yācakānaṃ ca dassasi .

Dhaññaṃ maṅgalasampannaṃ           saṅgāmavijayuttamaṃ    
tassa [^554-2] nāge padinnamhi      kinte rajjaṃ karissati .

Rajjampi me dade sabbaṃ        sarīraṃ dajjamattano    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā nāgaṃ adāsahanti .

Kurudhammacariyaṃ tatiyaṃ .

Catutthaṃ mahāsudassanacariyaṃ    
[4] kusāvatimhi nagare            yadā āsiṃ mahīpati    
mahāsudassano nāma           cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Tatthāhaṃ divase tikkhattuṃ        ghosāpemi tahiṃ tahiṃ    
ko kiṃ icchati pattheti         kassa kiṃ diyyatū dhanaṃ .

Ko chātako ko tasito        ko mālaṃ ko vilepanaṃ    
nānārattāni vatthāni         ko naggo paridahissati    
ko pathe chattamādeti         kopāhanā mudū subhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 555 -->
<a id="page-555"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 555]*

Iti sāyañca pāto ca         ghosāpemi tahiṃ tahiṃ    
na taṃ dasasu ṭhānesu           na hi [^555-1] ṭhānasatesu vā .

Anekasataṭhānesu             paṭiyattaṃ yācake dhanaṃ    
divā vā yadivā rattiṃ         yadi eti vaṇibbako .

Laddhā yadicchakaṃ bhogaṃ          pūrahatthova gacchati    
evarūpaṃ mahādānaṃ            adāsiṃ yāvajīvikaṃ .

Napāhaṃ dessaṃ dhanaṃ dammi        napi natthi nicayo mayi    
yathāpi āturo nāma          rogato parimuttiyā .

Dhanena vejjaṃ tappetvā       rogato parimuccati    
tathevāhaṃ jānamāno          paripūretuṃ asesato .

Ūnamanaṃ pūrayituṃ               demi dānaṃ vaṇibbake    
nirālayo apaccāso          sambodhimanupattiyāti .

Mahāsudassanacariyaṃ catutthaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ mahāgovindacariyaṃ    
[5] punāparaṃ yadā homi           sattarājapurohito    
pūjito naradevehi            mahāgovindabrāhmaṇo .

Tadāhaṃ sattarajjesu           yaṃ me āsi upāyanaṃ    
tena demi mahādānaṃ          akkhobhaṃ [^555-2] sāgarūpamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 556 -->
<a id="page-556"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 556]*

Na me dessaṃ dhanadhaññaṃ         napi natthi nicayo mayi    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā demi varaṃ dhananti .

Mahāgovindacariyaṃ pañcamaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ nemirājacariyaṃ    
[6] punāparaṃ yadā homi           mithilāyaṃ puruttame    
nemi nāma mahārājā         paṇḍito kusalatthiko .

Tadāhaṃ māpayitvāna           catusālaṃ catummukhaṃ    
tattha dānaṃ pavattesiṃ          migapakkhinarādinaṃ [^556-1] .

Acchādanaṃ sayanañca            annaṃ pānañca bhojanaṃ    
abbocchinnaṃ karitvāna         mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Yathāpi sevako sāmiṃ          dhanahetumupāgato    
kāyena vācāmanasā          ārādhanīyamesati .

Tathevāhaṃ sabbabhave           pariyesissāmi bodhijaṃ    
dānena satte tappetvā      icchāmi bodhimuttamanti .

Nemirājacariyaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

Sattamaṃ candakumāracariyaṃ    
[7] punāparaṃ yadā homi           ekarājassa atrajo    
nagare pupphavatiyā            kumāro candasavhayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 557 -->
<a id="page-557"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 557]*

Tadāhaṃ yajanā mutto          nikkhanto yaññavā [^557-1] tato    
saṃvegaṃ janayitvāna            mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃ .

Nāhaṃ pivāmi khādāmi          napi bhuñjāmi bhojanaṃ    
dakkhiṇeyye [^557-2] adatvāna      api chappañca rattiyo .

Yathāpi vāṇijo nāma          katvāna bhaṇḍasañcayaṃ    
yattha lābho mahā hoti        tattha [^557-3] taṃ harati bhaṇḍakaṃ .

Tatheva sakabhuttaṃpi [^557-3]          pare dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ    
tasmā parassa dātabbaṃ         satabhāgo bhavissati .

Etamatthavasaṃ ñatvā           demi dānaṃ bhavābhave    
na paṭikkamāmi dānato         sambodhimanupattiyāti .

Candakumāracariyaṃ sattamaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sivirājacariyaṃ    
[8] ariṭṭhasavhaye nagare          sivi nāmāsi khattiyo    
nisajja pāsādavare           evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Yaṅkiñci mānusaṃ dānaṃ          adinnaṃ me na vijjati    
yopi yāceyya maṃ cakkhuṃ        dadeyyaṃ avikampito .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya           sakko devānamissaro    
nisinno devaparisāya          idaṃ vacanamabravi .

#[^557-1] ma. Yaññavāṭato . Yu. Yaññavāṭako . [^557-2] yu. Dakkhiṇeyyaṃ . [^557-3] yu. Yatthāharati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 558 -->
<a id="page-558"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 558]*

Nisajja pāsādavare           sivi rājā mahiddhiko    
cintento vividhaṃ dānaṃ         adeyyaṃ so na passati .

Tathannu vitathannetaṃ            handa vīmaṃsayāmi taṃ    
muhuttaṃ āgameyyātha          yāva jānāmi taṃ manaṃ .

Pavedhamāno palitasiro         valigatto jarāturo    
andhavaṇṇo ca hutvāna         rājānaṃ upasaṅkami .

So tadā paggahetvāna        vāmaṃ dakkhiṇabāhuñca    
sirasmiṃ añjaliṃ katvā          idaṃ vacanamabravi    
yācāmi taṃ mahārāja          dhammika raṭṭhavaḍḍhana [^558-1]    
tava dānaratā kitti           uggatā devamānuse .

Ubhopi nettā nayanā         andhaüpahatā [^558-2] mama    
ekaṃ me nayanaṃ dehi          tvaṃpi ekena yāpaya .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          haṭṭho saṃviggamānaso    
katañjalī vedajāto           idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Idānāhaṃ cintayitvāna         pāsādato idhāgato    
tvaṃ mama cittamaññāya          nettaṃ yācitumāgato .

Ahome mānasaṃ siddhaṃ          saṅkappo me [^558-3] paripūrito    
adinnapubbaṃ dānavaraṃ           ajja dassāmi yācake .

Ehi sivaka uṭṭhehi           mā dandhayi mā pavedhayi    
ubhopi nayanaṃ dehi            uppāṭetvā vaṇibbake [^558-4] .

#[^558-1] yu. Raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ . [^558-2] ma. Yu. Andhā upahatā mama . [^558-3] ma. Yu. Mesaddonatthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 559 -->
<a id="page-559"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 559]*

Tato so codito mayhaṃ        sivako vacanaṅkaro    
uddharitvāna pādāsi          tālamiñjaṃva yācake .

Dadamānassa dentassa          dinnadānassa me sato    
cittassa aññathā natthi         bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Na me dessā ubho cakkhu      attāpi [^559-1] me na dessiyo    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā cakkhuṃ adāsahanti .

Sivirājacariyaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

Navamaṃ vessantaracariyaṃ    
[9] yā me ahosi janikā         phussatī nāma khattiyā    
sā atītāsu jātīsu           sakkassa mahesī siyā [^559-2] .

Tassā āyukkhayaṃ disvā [^559-3]     devindo etadabravi    
dadāmi te dasavare           varabhadde yadicchasi .

Evaṃ vuttāva [^559-4] sā devī     sakkaṃ punidamabravi    
kiṃ nu me aparādhatthi          kiṃ nu dessā ahantava    
rammā cāvesi maṃ ṭhānā       vātova dharaṇīruhaṃ [^559-5] .

Evaṃ vutatova [^559-6] so sakko    puna tassidamabravi    
na ceva te kataṃ pāpaṃ         na ca me tvamasi appiyā .

Ettakaṃyeva te āyu         cavanakālo bhavissati .

Paṭiggaṇha mayā dinne         vare dasa varuttame .

#[^559-1] ma. Yu. Attā na mena dessiyo . [^559-2] ma. Piyā . Yu. Ca mahesiyā . [^559-3] ma. Yu.

#ñatvā . [^559-4] ma. Yu. Vuttā ca sā devī . [^559-5] yu. Dharaṇiṃ ruhaṃ . [^559-6] yu. Evaṃ vutte ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 560 -->
<a id="page-560"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 560]*

Sakkena sā dinnavarā         tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā pamoditā    
mamaṃ abbhantaraṃ katvā          phussatī dasa vare vari .

Tato cutā sā phussatī         khattiye upapajjatha    
jetuttaramhi nagare           sañjayena samāgami .

Yadāhaṃ phussatiyā kucchiṃ         okkanto piyamātuyā8    
mama tejena me mātā        sadā dānaratā ahu .

Adhane āture jiṇṇe         yācake addhike [^560-1] jane    
samaṇe brāhmaṇe khīṇe        deti dānaṃ akiñcane .

Dasa māse dhārayitvā         karonte puraṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
vessānaṃ vīthiyā majjhe        janesi phussatī mamaṃ .

Na mayhaṃ mattikaṃ nāmaṃ          napi pettikasambhavaṃ [^560-2]    
jātettha [^560-3] vessavīthiyaṃ       tasmā vessantaro ahu .

Yadāhaṃ dārako homi          jātiyā aṭṭhavassiko    
tadā nisajja pāsāde         dānaṃ dātuṃ vicintayiṃ .

Hadayaṃ dadeyyaṃ cakkhuṃ           maṃsaṃpi rudhiraṃpi ca [^560-4]    
dadeyyaṃ kāyaṃ sāvetvā       yadi ko [^560-5] yācaye mamaṃ .

Sabhāvaṃ cintayantassa           akappitamasaṇṭhitaṃ [^560-6]    
akampi tattha paṭhavī            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Anvaḍḍhamāse pannarase        puṇṇamāse uposathe    
paccayaṃ nāgamāruyha           dānaṃ dātuṃ upāgamiṃ .

#[^560-1] yu. Paṭṭhike . [^560-2] yu. Mettikasambhavaṃ . [^560-3] yu. Jātomhi . [^560-4] yu. Ruhiraṃpica .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 561 -->
<a id="page-561"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 561]*

Kāliṅgaraṭṭhavisayā            brāhmaṇā upagacchu maṃ    
ayācuṃ maṃ hatthināgaṃ           dhaññaṃ maṅgalasammataṃ .

Avuṭṭhiko janapado            dubbhikkho chātako mahā    
dadāhi pavaraṃ nāgaṃ            sabbasetaṃ gajuttamaṃ .

Dadāmi na vikampāmi           yaṃ maṃ yācanti brāhmaṇā    
santaṃ nappaṭiguyhāmi           dāne me ramatī mano .

Na me yācakamanuppatte        paṭikkhepo anucchavo    
mā me bhijji samādānaṃ        dassāmi vipulaṃ gajaṃ .

Nāgaṃ gahetvā soṇḍāya       bhiṅgāre ratanāmaye    
jalaṃ hatthe ākiritvā         brāhmaṇānaṃ adaṃ gajaṃ .

Punāparaṃ dadantassa            sabbasetaṃ gajuttamaṃ    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Tassa nāgassa dānena         sivayo kuddhā samāgatā    
pabbājesuṃ sakā raṭṭhā        vaṅkaṃ gacchatu pabbataṃ .

Tesaṃ nicchubhamānānaṃ           akampitamasaṇṭhitaṃ    
mahādānaṃ pavattetuṃ           ekavaramayācihaṃ [^561-1] .

Yācitā sivayo sabbe         ekaṃ varamadaṃsu me    
sāvayitvā kaṇṇabheriṃ          mahādānaṃ dadāmahaṃ .

Athettha vattatī saddo         timulo [^561-2] bheravo mahā    
dānenimaṃ [^561-3] nīharanti         puna dānaṃ dadātayaṃ [^561-4] .

#[^561-1] ma. Ekaṃ varamayācisaṃ . Yu. ...Cissaṃ . [^561-2] ma. Yu. Tumulo . [^561-3] yu. Dānenamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 562 -->
<a id="page-562"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 562]*

Hatthī [^562-1] asse rathe datvā    dāsiṃ dāsaṃ gavaṃ dhanaṃ    
mahādānaṃ daditvāna           nagarā nikkhamiṃ tadā .

Nikkhamitvāna nagarā           nivattetvā vilokite    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            suneruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Catuvāhirathaṃ [^562-2] datvā         ṭhatvā catumahāpathe    
ekākiko [^562-3] adutiyo        maddiṃ [^562-4] devīdamabraviṃ .

Tvaṃ maddi kaṇhaṃ gaṇhāhi        lahukā esā kaniṭṭhakā [^562-5]    
ahaṃ jāliṃ gahissāmi           garuko bhātiko hi so .

Padumaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃva              maddī kaṇhājinaṃ [^562-6] gahi    
ahaṃ suvaṇṇabimbaṃ va            jāliṃ khattiyamaggahiṃ .

[7]    
abhijātā sukhumālā           khattiyā caturo janā    
visamasamaṃ akkamantā           vaṅkaṃ gacchāma pabbataṃ .

Ye keci manujā enti        anumagge paṭipathe    
maggante paṭipucchāma          kuhiṃ vaṅkatapabbato [^562-8] .

Te tattha amhe passitvā      karuṇaṃ giramudīrayuṃ    
dukkhante paṭivedenti         dūre vaṅkatapabbato .

Yadi passanti pavane           dārakā phalite dume    
tesaṃ phalānaṃ hetumhi          uparodanti dārakā .

Rodante dārake disvā       ubbhiggā [^562-9] vipulā dumā    
sayamevonamitvāna            upagacchanti dārake .

#[^562-1] ma. Hatthiṃ . [^562-2] ma. Yu. Catuvāhiṃ rathaṃ . [^562-3] ma. Yu. Ekākiyo . [^562-4] ma. Yu.

#maddideviṃ idamabraviṃ . [^562-5] ma. Kaniṭṭhikā . [^562-6] ma. Kaṇhājinaggahī . Yu ...Jinamagahī .

#[^562-7] yu. Jalaṃ hatthe ākiritvā brāhmaṇānaṃ adaṃ gajaṃ . [^562-8] ma. Vaṅkanta... . Ito paraṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 563 -->
<a id="page-563"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 563]*

Idaṃ acchariyaṃ disvā           abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
sādhukāraṃ pavattesi           maddī sabbaṅgasobhaṇā .

Accheraṃ vata lokasmiṃ          abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
vessantarassa tejena         sayamevonatā [^563-1] dumā .

Saṅkhipiṃsu pathaṃ yakkhā           anukampāya dārake    
nikkhantadivaseneva            cetaraṭṭhamupāgamama .

Saṭṭhi rājasahassāni           tadā vasanti mātulā [^563-2]    
sabbe pañjalikā hutvā        rodamānā upāgamama .

Tattha vattetvā sallāpaṃ       cetehi cetaputtehi    
te tato nikkhamitvāna         vaṅkaṃ agamu [^563-3] pabbataṃ .

Āmantayitvā devindo        vissukammaṃ mahiddhikaṃ    
assamaṃ sukataṃ rammaṃ            paṇṇasālaṃ sumāpaya .

Sakkassa vacanaṃ sutvā          vissukammo mahiddhiko    
assamaṃ sukataṃ rammaṃ            paṇṇasālaṃ sumāpayi .

Ajjhogahetvā pavanaṃ          appasaddaṃ nirākulaṃ    
caturo janā mayaṃ tattha         vasāma pabbatantare .

Ahañca maddī devī ca          jālī kaṇhājinā cubho    
aññamaññaṃ sokanudā           vasāma assame tadā .

Dārake anurakkhanto          asuññe homi assame    
maddī phalaṃ āharati [^563-4]         poseti sā tayo jane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 564 -->
<a id="page-564"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 564]*

Pavane vasamānassa            atthiko maṃ upāgami    
ayāci puttake mayhaṃ          jāliṃ kaṇhājinaṃ [^564-1] cubho .

Yācakaṃ upagataṃ disvā          hāso me upapajjatha    
ubho putte gahetvāna        adāsiṃ brāhmaṇe tadā .

Sake putte cajantassa         jūjake [^564-2] brāhmaṇe yadā    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Punadeva sakko oruyha        hutvā brāhmaṇasannibho    
ayāci maṃ maddiṃ deviṃ          sīlavatiṃ [^564-3] patibbataṃ .

Maddiṃ hatthe gahetvāna        udakañjali pūraya [^564-4]    
pasannamanasaṅkappo            tassa maddiṃ adāsahaṃ .

Maddiyā dīyamānāya           gagane devā pamoditā    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Jāliṃ kaṇhājinaṃ dhītuṃ [^564-5]        maddiṃ deviṃ patibbataṃ    
cajamāno na cintesiṃ          bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Na me dessā ubho puttā     madda devī na dessiyā    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā piye adāsahaṃ .

Punāparaṃ brahāraññe          mātāpitusamāgame    
karuṇaṃ paridevante            sallapante sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ .

Hirottappena garūnaṃ [^564-6]        ubhinnaṃ upasaṅkami    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

#[^564-1] yu. Jālikaṇhājine . [^564-2] yu. Yācake . [^564-3] ma. Sīlavantiṃ . [^564-4] ma. Pūriya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 565 -->
<a id="page-565"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 565]*

Punāparaṃ brahāraññā          nikkhamitvā sañātibhi    
pavisāmi puraṃ rammaṃ            jetuttarapuruttamaṃ .

Ratanāni satta vassiṃsu          mahāmegho pavassatha    
tadāpi paṭhavī kampi            sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

Acetanāyaṃ paṭhavī             aviññāya sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ    
sāpi dānabalā mayhaṃ          sattakkhattumakampathāti .

Vessantaracariyaṃ navamaṃ .

Dasamaṃ sasapaṇḍitacariyaṃ    
[10] punāparaṃ yadā homi           sasako pavanacāriko [^565-1]    
tiṇapaṇṇasākaphalabhakkho          paraheṭhanavivajjito .

Makkaṭo ca siṅgālo ca        suttapoto [^565-2] cahaṃ tadā    
vasāma ekasamaggā [^565-3]        sāyaṃ pāto ca dissare [^565-4] .

Ahante anusāsāmi           kiriye kalyāṇapāpake    
pāpāni parivajjetha           kalyāṇe abhinivissatha .

Uposathamhi divase            candaṃ disvāna pūritaṃ    
etesaṃ tattha ācikkhiṃ [^565-5]      divaso ajjuposatho .

Dānāni paṭiyādetha           dakkhiṇeyyassa dātave    
datvā dānaṃ dakkhiṇeyye [^565-6]    upavassathuposathaṃ .

#[^565-1] ma. Pavanacārako . [^565-2] ma. Uddapoto . [^565-3] ma. Yu. Ekasamantā . [^565-4] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 566 -->
<a id="page-566"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 566]*

Te me sādhūti vatvāna        yathāsatti yathābalaṃ    
dānāni paṭiyādetvā         dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesisuṃ .

Ahaṃ nipajja [^566-1] cintesiṃ        dānaṃ dakkhiṇanucchavaṃ    
yadihaṃ labhe dakkhiṇeyyaṃ         kiṃ me dānaṃ bhavissati .

Na me atthi tilamuggā         na māsā taṇḍulā ghataṃ    
ahaṃ tiṇena yāpemi           na sakkā tiṇadātave .

Yadi koci eti dakkhiṇeyyo     bhikkhāya mama santike    
dajjāhaṃ sakamattānaṃ           na so tuccho gamissati .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya           sakko brāhmaṇavaṇṇinā    
āsayaṃ me upagacchi           dānaṃ vīmaṃsanāya me .

Tamahaṃ disvāna santuṭṭho        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
sādhu khosi anuppatto         ghāsahetu mamantike .

Adinnapubbaṃ dānavaraṃ           ajja dassāmi te ahaṃ    
tuvaṃ sīlaguṇūpeto             ayuttante parahedhanaṃ .

Ehi aggiṃ padīpehi           nānākaṭṭhe samānaya    
ahaṃ pacissamattānaṃ            pakkaṃ tvaṃ bhakkhayissasi .

Sādhūti so haṭṭhamano          nānākaṭṭhe samānayi    
mahantaṃ akāsi cittakaṃ          katvānaṅgāragabbhakaṃ .

Aggiṃ tattha padīpesi [^566-2]        yathā so khippaṃ mahābhave    
phoṭetvā rajagate gatte      ekamantaṃ upāvisiṃ [^566-3] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 567 -->
<a id="page-567"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 567]*

Yadā mahākaṭṭhapuñjo          āditto dhamadhamāyati [^567-1]    
taduppatitvā papati [^567-2]         majjhe jālasikhantare .

Yathā sītodakaṃ nāma           paviṭṭhaṃ yassa kassaci    
sameti darathapariḷāhaṃ           assādaṃ deti pīti ca .

Tatheva jalitaṃ aggiṃ            paviṭṭhassa mamaṃ tadā    
sabbaṃ sameti darathaṃ            yathā sītodakaṃ viya .

Chavi cammañca maṃsañca           nhāru aṭṭhi hadayabandhanaṃ    
kevalaṃ sakalaṃ kāyaṃ            brāhmaṇassa adāsahanti .

Sasapaṇḍitacariyaṃ dasamaṃ .

Tassuddānaṃ    
akitti brāhmaṇo saṅkho       kururājā dhanañjayo    
mahāsudassano rājā          mahāgovindabrāhmaṇo .

Nemi [^567-3] candakumāro ca       sivi vessantaro saso    
ahameva tadā āsiṃ           yo te dānavare adā .

Ete dānaparikkhārā         ete dānassa pāramī    
jīvitaṃ yācake datvā          imaṃ [^567-4] pārami pūrayiṃ .

Bhikkhāya upagataṃ disvā         sakattānaṃ paraccajiṃ    
dānena me samo natthi        esā me dānapāramīti .

Dānapāramitā niṭṭhitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 568 -->
<a id="page-568"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 568]*

2. Sīlapāramitā    
paṭhamaṃ sīlavanāgacariyaṃ    
[11] yadā ahosiṃ pavane           kuñjaro mātuposako    
na tadā atthi mahiyā          guṇena mama sādiso .

Pavane disvā vanacaro         rañño maṃ paṭivedayi    
tavānucchavo mahārāja         gajo asati [^568-1] kānane .

Na tassa parikkhāyattho         napi āḷhakakāsuyā [^568-2]    
samāgahite soṇḍāya          sayameva idhehiti .

Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          rājāpi tuṭṭhamānaso    
pesesi hatthidamakaṃ            chekācariyaṃ susikkhitaṃ .

Gantvā so hatthidamako        addasa padumassare    
bhisamūḷālaṃ [^568-3] uddharantaṃ        yāpanatthāya mātuyā .

Viññāya me sīlaguṇaṃ           lakkhaṇaṃ upadhārayi    
ehi puttāti vatvāna         soṇḍāya mama aggahi .

Yaṃ me tadā pākatikaṃ          sarīrānugataṃ balaṃ(    
ajja nāgasahassānaṃ           balena samasādisaṃ .

Yadihantesaṃ pakuppeyyaṃ         upetaṃ [^568-4] gahaṇāya maṃ    
paṭibalo bhave tesaṃ           yāva rajjampi mānusaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 569 -->
<a id="page-569"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 569]*

Apicāhaṃ sīlarakkhāya           sīlapāramipūriyā    
na karomi citte aññathattaṃ      pakkhipantaṃ mamāḷhake [^569-1] .

Yadi te maṃ tattha koṭeyyuṃ      pharasūhi tomarehi ca    
neva tesaṃ pakuppeyyaṃ         sīlakhaṇḍabhayā mamāti .

Sīlavanāgacariyaṃ [^569-2] paṭhamaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ bhūridattacariyaṃ    
[12] punāparaṃ yadā homi           bhūridatto mahiddhiko    
virūpakkhena mahāraññā         devalokamagacchahaṃ .

Tattha passitvāhaṃ deve        ekantaṃ sukhasamappite    
taṃ saggaṃ gamanatthāya           sīlabbataṃ samādayiṃ .

Sarīrakiccaṃ katvāna            bhutvā yāpanamattakaṃ    
caturo aṅge adhiṭṭhāya        semi vammikamuddhani .

Chaviyā cammena maṃsena         nhārūhi aṭṭhikehi vā    
yassa etena karaṇīyaṃ          dinnaṃyeva harātu so .

Saṃsito akataññunā            ālambāno mamaggahi    
peḷāya pakkhipitvāna          kīḷeti maṃ tahiṃ tahiṃ .

Peḷāya pakkhipantepi          maṃ maddantepi [^569-3] pāṇinā    
ālambāne na kuppāmi        sīlakhaṇḍabhayā mama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 570 -->
<a id="page-570"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 570]*

Sakajīvitapariccāgo            tiṇatopi [^570-1] lahuko mayi    
sīlavītikkamo mayhaṃ            paṭhavī ubbattanā [^570-2] viya .

Nirantaraṃ jātisataṃ             cajeyyaṃ mama jīvitaṃ    
neva sīlaṃ pabhindeyyaṃ          catunnaṃ dīpāna hetupi .

Apicāhaṃ sīlarakkhāya           sīlapāramipūriyā    
na karomi citte aññathattaṃ      pakkhipantaṃpi peḷaketi .

Bhūridattacariyaṃ dutiyaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ campeyyacariyaṃ [^570-3]    
[13] punāparaṃ yadā homi           campeyyako mahiddhiko    
tadāpi dhammiko āsiṃ          sīlabbatasamappito .

Tadāpi maṃ dhammacāriṃ           upavuṭṭhaṃ uposathaṃ    
ahituṇḍiko gahetvāna         rājadvāramhi kīḷati .

Yaṃ [^570-4] so vaṇṇaṃ cintayati       nīlaṃ va pītalohitaṃ    
tassa cittānuvattento        homi cintitasannibho .

Thalaṃ kareyyaṃ udakaṃ            udakampi thalaṃ kare    
yadihantassa kuppeyyaṃ [^570-5]       khaṇena chārikaṃ kare .

Yadi cittavasī hessaṃ           parihāyissāmi sīlato    
sīlena parihīnassa             uttamattho na sijjhati .

#[^570-1] ma. Yu. Tiṇako lahuko mama . [^570-2] ma. Uppatanaṃ . Yu. Uppattanā . [^570-3] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 571 -->
<a id="page-571"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 571]*

Kāmaṃ bhijjatu yaṃ kāyo         idheva vikirīyatu    
neva sīlaṃpi [^571-1] bhindeyyaṃ       vikirante bhusaṃ viyāti .

Campeyyacariyaṃ tatiyaṃ .

Catutthaṃ cūḷabodhicariyaṃ    
[14] punāparaṃ yadā homi           cūḷabodhi susīlavā    
bhavaṃ disvāna bhayato           nekkhammaṃ abhinikkhamiṃ .

Yā me dutiyikā āsi         brāhmaṇī kanakasannibhā    
sāpi [^571-2] vaṭṭe anapekkhā     nekkhammaṃ abhinikkhami .

Nirālayā chinnabandhu           anapekkhā kule gaṇe    
carantā gāmanigamaṃ            bārāṇasiṃ upāgamama .

Tattha vasāma nipakā           asaṃsaṭṭhā kule gaṇe    
nirākule appasadde          rājuyyāne vasāmubho .

Uyyānadassanaṃ gantvā         rājā addasa brāhmaṇiṃ    
upasaṅgamma [^571-3] maṃ pucchi        tuyhesā kā kassa bhariyā .

Evaṃ vutte ahantassa         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
na mayhaṃ bhariyā esā         sahadhammā ekasāsanī .

Tassā [^571-4] sāratta [^571-5] gadhito   gāhāpetvāna ceṭake    
nippīḷayanto balasā           antepuraṃ pavesayi .

Odapattikiyā [^571-6] mayhaṃ        sahajātā [^571-7] ekasāsanī    
ākaḍḍhitvā nayantiyā         kopo me uppajjatha .

#[^571-1] ma. Yu. Neva sīlaṃ pabhindeyyaṃ . [^571-2] yu. Sā vivaṭṭe . [^571-3] ma. Yu. Upagamma .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 572 -->
<a id="page-572"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 572]*

Saha kope samuppanne         sīlabbatamanussariṃ    
tattheva kopaṃ niggaṇhiṃ         nādāsiṃ vaḍḍhitūpari [^572-1] .

Yadi taṃ brāhmaṇiṃ koci         koṭṭeyya tiṇhasattiyā    
neva sīlaṃ pabhindeyyaṃ          bodhiyā yeva kāraṇā .

Na me sā brāhmaṇī dessā    napi me balaṃ na vijjati    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā sīlānurakkhissanti .

Cūḷabodhicariyaṃ catutthaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ mahisarājacariyaṃ    
[15] punāparaṃ yadā homi           mahiso [^572-2] pavanacāraṇo    
pavaḍḍhakāyo balavā           mahanto bhīmadassano .

Pabbhāre giridugge ca         rukkhamūle dakāsaye    
hotettha ṭhānaṃ mahisānaṃ        koci koci tahiṃ tahiṃ .

Vicaranto brahāraññe         ṭhānamaddasa bhaddakaṃ    
taṃ ṭhānaṃ upagantvāna          tiṭṭhāmi ca sayāmi ca .

Athettha kapimāgantvā         pāpo anariyo lahu    
khandhe nalāte bhamuke         mutteti ohaneti taṃ .

Sakiṃpi divasaṃ dutiyaṃ             tatiyaṃ catutthaṃpica    
dūseti maṃ sabbakālaṃ           tena homi upadduto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 573 -->
<a id="page-573"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 573]*

Mamaṃ upaddutaṃ disvā           yakkho maṃ idamabravi    
nāsehetaṃ chavaṃ pāpaṃ          saṅgehi ca khurehi ca .

Evaṃ vutte tadā yakkhe       ahaṃ taṃ idamabraviṃ    
kiṃ tvaṃ makkhesi kuṇapena        pāpena anariyena maṃ .

Yadihantassa pakuppeyyaṃ         tato hīnataro bhave    
sīlañca me pabhijjeyya         viññū ca garaheyyu maṃ .

Hiḷitā jīvitā vāpi           parisuddhena mataṃ varaṃ    
kyāhaṃ jīvitahetūpi            kāhāmi parahedhanaṃ .

[1]    
hīnamajjhimaükkaṭṭhe            sahanto avamānitaṃ    
evaṃ labhati sappañño          manasā yathā patthitanti .

Mahisarājacariyaṃ pañcamaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ rurumigacariyaṃ    
[16] punāparaṃ yadā homi           sutattakanakasannibho    
migarājā ruru nāma           paramasīlasamāhito .

Ramme padese rammaṇīye       vivitte amanussake    
tattha vāsaṃ upagañchiṃ           gaṅgākūle manorame .

Atha upari gaṅgāya            dhanikehi paripīḷito    
puriso gaṅgāya patati          jīvāmi vā marāmi vā .

#[^573-1] ma. Yu. Na mevāyaṃ maññamāno        aññepevaṃ karissati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 574 -->
<a id="page-574"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 574]*

Rattindivaṃ so gaṅgāya         vuyhamāno mahodake    
ravanto karuṇaṃ ravaṃ            majjhe gaṅgāya gacchati .

Tassāhaṃ saddaṃ sutvāna         karuṇaṃ paridevato    
gaṅgāya tīre ṭhatvā [^574-1]       apucchiṃ kosi tvaṃ naro .

So me puṭṭho [2] viyākāsi  attano kāraṇaṃ tadā    
dhanikehi bhīto tasito          pakkhannomaṃ [^574-3] mahānadiṃ .

Tassa katvāna kāruññaṃ         cajitvā mama jīvitaṃ    
pavisitvā nīharintassa          andhakāramhi rattiyā .

Assatthakālamaññāya           tassāhaṃ idamabraviṃ    
ekantaṃ varaṃ yācāmi          mā maṃ kassaci pāvada .

Nagaraṃ gantvāna ācikkhi        pucchito dhanahetuko    
rājānaṃ so gahetvāna        upagañchi mamantikaṃ .

Yāvatā kāraṇaṃ sabbaṃ          rañño ārocitaṃ mayā    
rājā sutvāna vacanaṃ          usuṃ tassa pakappayi .

Idheva ghāṭayissāmi           mittadubbhiṃ anariyaṃ    
tamahaṃ anurakkhanto            nimminiṃ mama attanā .

Tiṭṭha te so mahārāja        kāmakāro bhavāmi te    
anurakkhiṃ mama sīlaṃ             nārakkhiṃ mama jīvitaṃ    
sīlavā hi tadā āsiṃ          bodhiyāyeva kāraṇāti .

Rurumigacariyaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 575 -->
<a id="page-575"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 575]*

Sattamaṃ mātaṅgacariyaṃ    
[17] punāparaṃ yadā homi           jaṭilo uggatāpano    
mātaṅgo nāma nāmena        sīlavā susamāhito .

Ahañca brāhmaṇo eko       gaṅgākūle vasāmubho    
ahaṃ vasāmi upari             heṭṭhā vasati brāhmaṇo .

Vicaranto anukūlamhi           uddhamme assamaddasa    
tattha maṃ paribhāsitvā          abhisapi muddhaphālanaṃ .

Yadihantassa pakuppeyyaṃ         yadi sīlaṃ na gopaye    
oloketvānahantassa         kareyyaṃ chārikaṃ viya .

Yaṃ so tadā maṃ abhisapi         kupito duṭṭhamānaso    
tasseva matthake nipati         yogena taṃ pamocayiṃ .

Anurakkhiṃ mama sīlaṃ             nārakkhiṃ mama jīvitaṃ    
sīlavā hi tadā āsiṃ          bodhiyāyeva kāraṇāti .

Mātaṅgacariyaṃ sattamaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ dhammadevaputtacariyaṃ    
[18] punāparaṃ yadā homi           mahesakkho [^575-1] mahiddhiko    
dhammo nāma mahāyakkho        sabbalokānukampako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 576 -->
<a id="page-576"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 576]*

Dasa kusalakammapathe            samādapento mahājanaṃ    
carāmi gāmanigamaṃ             samitto saparijano .

Pāpo kadariyo yakkho         dīpento dasa pāpake    
sopettha mahiyā carati         samitto saparijano .

Dhammavādī adhammo ca          ubho paccanikā mayaṃ    
dhure dhuraṃ ghaṭayantā           samimhā paṭipathe ubho .

Kalaho vattatī bhesmā [^576-1]      kalyāṇapāpakassa ca    
maggā ukkamanatthāya [^576-2]       mahāyuddho upaṭṭhito .

Yadihantassa pakuppeyyaṃ         yadi bhinde tapoguṇaṃ    
saha parijanantassa             rajabhūtaṃ kareyyahaṃ .

Apicāhaṃ sīlarakkhāya           nibbāpetvāna mānasaṃ    
saha janenukkamitvā [^576-3]        pathaṃ pāpassadāsahaṃ .

Saha pathato ukkante [^576-4]       katvā cittassa nibbutiṃ    
vivaraṃ adāsi paṭhavī            pāpayakkhassa tāvadeti .

Dhammadevaputtacariyaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

Navamaṃ jayadisacariyaṃ    
[19] pañcālaraṭṭhe nagaravare [^576-5]     kappilāyaṃ puruttame    
rājā jayadiso nāma          sīlaguṇamupāgato .

#[^576-1] yu. Asmā . [^576-2] ma. Yu. Okkamanatthāya . [^576-3] ma. Yu. Janenokkamitvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 577 -->
<a id="page-577"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 577]*

Tassa rañño ahaṃ putto        sutadhammo susīlavā    
alīnasatto guṇavā            anurakkhāparijano [^577-1] sadā .

Pitā me migavaṃ gantvā        porisādaṃ upāgami    
so me pitumaggahesi          bhakkhosi mama mā cali .

Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          bhīto tasitavedhito    
urukkhambho [^577-2] ahu tassa       disvāna porisādakaṃ .

Migavaṃ gahetvā muñcassu        katvā āgamanaṃ puna    
brāhmaṇassa dhanaṃ datvā        pitā āmantayī mamaṃ .

Rajjaṃ putta paṭipajja           mā pamajja [^577-3] puraṃ idaṃ    
kataṃ me porisādena          mama āgamanaṃ puna .

Mātupituñca vanditvā          nimminitvāna attānaṃ [^577-4]    
nikkhamitvā dhanana khaggaṃ          porisādamupāgamiṃ .(    
sasatthahatthūpagataṃ              kadāci so tasissati    
tena bhijjissatī sīlaṃ           parittāsaṃ kate mayi .

Sīlakhaṇḍabhayā mayhaṃ            tassa dessaṃ na byāhariṃ    
mettacitto hitavādī          idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Ujjalehi mahāaggiṃ           papatissāmi rukkhato    
supakkakālamaññāya [^577-5]         bhakkhaya [^577-6] tvaṃ pitāmahā .

Iti sīlavataṃ hetu             nārakkhiṃ mama jīvitaṃ    
pabbājesimahantassa           sadā pāṇātipātikanti .

Jayadisacariyaṃ navamaṃ    
#[^577-1] yu. Anuttaraparijano . [^577-2] yu. Uruthambho . [^577-3] ma. Yu. Pamajji . [^577-4] ma. Attanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 578 -->
<a id="page-578"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 578]*

Dasamaṃ saṅkhapālacariyaṃ    
[20] punāparaṃ yadā homi           saṅkhapālo mahiddhiko    
dāḍhāvudho ghoraviso          dvijivho uragābhibhū .

Catuppathe mahāpathe [^578-1]        nānājanasamākule    
caturo aṅge adhiṭṭhāya        tattha vāsamakappayiṃ .

Chaviyā cammena maṃsena         nhārūhi aṭṭhikehi vā    
yassa etena karaṇīyaṃ          dinnaṃyeva harātu so .

Addasaṃsu bhojaputtā           kharā luddhā akāruṇā    
upagacchacha mamaṃ tattha            daṇḍamuggarapāṇino .

Nāsāya vinivijjhitvā          naṅguṭṭhe piṭṭhikaṇṭhake    
kāje āropayitvāna         bhojaputtā hariṃsu maṃ .

Sasāgarantaṃ paṭhaviṃ             sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ    
icchamāno cahantattha          nāsavātena jhāpaye .

Sūlehi vijjhayantepi           koṭṭayantepi sattibhi    
bhojaputte na kuppāmi         esā me sīlapāramīti .

Saṅkhapālacariyaṃ dasamaṃ .

Tassuddānaṃ    
hatthināgo bhūridatto          campeyyo bodhi māhiso    
ruru mātaṅgadhammo ca          atrajo ca jayaddiso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 579 -->
<a id="page-579"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 579]*

Ete [^579-1] sabbe sīlabalā      parikkhārā padesikā    
jīvitaṃ parirakkhitvā            sīlāni anurakkhissaṃ .

Saṅkhapālassa me sato         sabbakālaṃpi jīvitaṃ    
yassa kassaci niyyantaṃ [^579-2]       tasmā sā sīlapāramīti .

Sīlapāraminiddeso niṭṭhito .

3. Nekkhammādipāramitā    
paṭhamaṃ yudhañjayacariyaṃ    
[21] yadāhaṃ amitayaso             rājaputto yudhañjayo    
ussāvabinduṃ suriyātape        patitaṃ disvāna saṃvijiṃ .

Tamevādhipatiṃ [^579-3] katvā        saṃvegamānubrūhayiṃ    
mātupituñca vanditvā          pabbajjamanuyācihaṃ .

Yācanti maṃ pañjalikā          sanegamā saraṭṭhakā    
ajjeva putta paṭipajja         iddhaṃ phītaṃ mahāmahiṃ .

Sarājake saorodhe          sanegame saraṭṭhake    
karuṇaṃ paridevante            anapekkho [^579-4] pariccajiṃ .

Kevalaṃ paṭhavīrajjaṃ             ñātiparijanaṃ yasaṃ    
cajamāno na cintesiṃ          bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Mātā pitā na me dessā     napi me dessā [^579-5] mahāyasā    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā rajjaṃ pariccajinti .

Yudhañjayacariyaṃ paṭhamaṃ .

#[^579-1] ma. Ete nava sīlabalā . [^579-2] ma. Niyyattaṃ . [^579-3] ma. Yu. Tanyevādhipatiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 580 -->
<a id="page-580"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 580]*

Dutiyaṃ somanassacariyaṃ    
[22] punāparaṃ yadā homi           indapatthe puruttame    
kāmito dayito putto         somanassoti vissuto .

Sīlavā guṇasampanno           kalyāṇapaṭibhānavā    
vuḍḍhāpacāyī hirimā           saṅgahesu ca kovido .

Tassa rañño paṭikaro          ahosi kuhakatāpaso    
ārāmaṃ mālāgacchañca         ropayitvāna [1] jīvati .

Tamahaṃ disvāna kuhakaṃ           thusarāsiṃva ataṇḍulaṃ    
dumaṃva antosusiraṃ             kadaliṃva asārakaṃ .

Natthimassa sataṃ dhammo          sāmaññā apagato ayaṃ    
hirisukkadhammajahito            jīvitavuttikāraṇā .

Kupito āsi [^580-2] paccanto      aṭavīhi carantihi [^580-3]    
taṃ nisedhetuṃ gacchanto         anusāsi pitā mama .

Mā pamajja tuvaṃ tāta          jaṭile [^580-4] uggatāpane    
yadicchakaṃ pavattehi            sabbakāmadado hi so .

Tamahaṃ gantvānupaṭṭhānaṃ         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
kacci te gahapati kusalaṃ         kiṃ vā te āharīyatu .

Tena so kupito āsi         kuhako mānanissito    
ghāṭāpemi tuvaṃ ajja          raṭṭhā pabbājayāmi vā .

#[^580-1] yu. So . [^580-2] ma. Ahu . Yu. Ahosi . [^580-3] yu. Aparantihi . [^580-4] ma. Yu. Jaṭilaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 581 -->
<a id="page-581"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 581]*

Nisedhayitvā paccantaṃ          rājā kuhakamabravi    
kacci [^581-1] khamanīyaṃ tava          sammāno te pavattito .

Tassa ācikkhatī pāpo         kumāro yathā nāsiyo [^581-2]    
tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          āṇāpesi  mahīpati .

Sīsaṃ tattheva chinditvā         katvāna catukhaṇḍikaṃ    
rathiyā rathiyaṃ dassetha          sā gati jaṭilahīḷitā .

Tattha [^581-3] te karaṇī gantvā     caṇḍā luddhā [^581-4] akāruṇā    
mātu aṅke nisinnassa         ākaḍḍhitvā nayanti maṃ .

Tesāhaṃ evamavacaṃ            bandhataṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ    
rañño dassetha maṃ khippaṃ        rājakiriyāni atthi me .

Te maṃ rañño dassayiṃsu         pāpassa pāpasevino    
disvāna taṃ saññāpesiṃ         mama ca vasamānayiṃ .

So maṃ tattha khamāpesi         mahārajjaṃ adāsi me    
sohaṃ tamaṃ dālayitvā          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Na me dessaṃ mahārajjaṃ        kāmabhogo na dessiyo    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā rajjaṃ pariccajinti .

Somanassacariyaṃ dutiyaṃ .

#[^581-1] ma. Yu. Kicci te bhante khamanīyaṃ . [^581-2] yu. Nāsito . [^581-3] ma. Tattha kāraṇikā gantvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 582 -->
<a id="page-582"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 582]*

Tatiyaṃ ayogharacariyaṃ    
[23] punāparaṃ yadā homi           kāsirājassa atrajo    
ayogharamhi saṃvaḍḍho           nāmenāsiṃ ayogharo .

Dukkhena jīvito laddho         sampīḷe patiposito    
ajjeva putta paṭipajja         kevalaṃ vasundharaṃ [^582-1] imaṃ .

Saraṭṭhakaṃ sanigamaṃ              sajanaṃ vanditvā khattiyaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna          idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Ye keci mahiyā sattā        hīnaükkaṭṭhamajjhimā    
nirārakkhā sake gehe        vaḍḍhanti sakañātihi [^582-2] .

Idaṃ loke uttariyaṃ           sampīḷe mama posanaṃ    
ayogharamhi saṃvaḍḍho           appabhe candasuriye .

Pūtikuṇapasampuṇṇā             muñcitvā mātu kucchito    
tato ghoratare dukkhe         puna pakkhittayoghare .

Yadihaṃ tādisaṃ patvā           dukkhaṃ paramadāruṇaṃ    
rajjesu yadi rañjāmi [^582-3]       pāpānaṃ uttamo siyaṃ .

Ukkaṇṭhitomhi kāyena         rajjenamhi anatthiko    
nibbutiṃ pariyesissaṃ            yattha maṃ maccu na maddiye .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna          vivarante [^582-4] mahājane    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā        pāvisiṃ [^582-5] kānanaṃ vanaṃ .

#[^582-1] ma. Yu. Vasudhaṃ . [^582-2] yu. Saha ñātibhi . [^582-3] ma. Yu. Rajjāyi . [^582-4] yu. Vivarantaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 583 -->
<a id="page-583"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 583]*

Mātā pitā na me dessā     napi me dessā [^583-1] mahāyasā    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā rajjaṃ pariccajinti .

Ayogharacariyaṃ tatiyaṃ .

Catutthaṃ bhiṃsacariyaṃ    
[24] punāparaṃ yadā homi           kāsīnaṃ puravaruttame    
bhaginī ca bhātaro satta         nibbattā sotthiye kule .

Etesaṃ pubbajo āsiṃ         hirisukkamupāgato    
bhavaṃ disvāna bhayato           nekkhammābhirato ahaṃ .

Mātāpitūhi pahitā            sahāyā ekamānasā    
kāmehi maṃ nimantenti         kulavaṃsaṃ dhārehīti .

Yantesaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ           gihidhamme sukhāvahaṃ    
tamme ahosi kaṭhinaṃ           tattaphālasamaṃ viya .

Te maṃ tadā ukkhipantaṃ         pucchiṃsu patthitaṃ mama    
kiṃ tvaṃ patthayasi [^583-2] samma       yadi kāme na bhuñjasi .

Tesāhaṃ evamavacaṃ            attakāmo hitesinaṃ    
nāhaṃ patthemi gihibhāvaṃ         nekkhammābhirato ahaṃ .

Te mayhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā         pituṃ mātuñca sāvayuṃ    
mātā pitā evamāhu         sabbepi pabbajāma bho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 584 -->
<a id="page-584"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 584]*

Ubho mātā pitā mayhaṃ        bhaginī [1] satta bhātaro    
amitaṃ dhanaṃ chaḍḍayitvā          pāvisimhā mahāvananti .

Bhiṃsacariyaṃ catutthaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ soṇanandapaṇḍitacariyaṃ    
[25] punāparaṃ yadā homi           nagare brahmavaddhane    
tattha kulavare seṭṭhe         mahāsāle ajāyihaṃ .

Tadāsiṃ [^584-2] lokaṃ disvāna       andhabhūtaṃ [^584-3] tamotthataṃ    
cittaṃ bhavato paṭikuṭati          tuttavegahataṃ viya .

Disvāna vividhaṃ pāpaṃ           evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
kadāhaṃ gehā nikkhamma         pavisissāmi kānanaṃ .

Tadāpi maṃ nimantiṃsu            kāmabhogehi ñātayo    
tesaṃpi chandamācikkhiṃ           mā nimantetha tehi maṃ .

Yo me kaniṭṭhako bhātā       nando nāmāsi paṇḍito    
sopi maṃ anusikkhanto          pabbajjaṃ samarocasi [^584-4] .

Ahaṃ soṇo ca nando ca        ubho mātā pitā mama    
tadāpi bhoge chaḍḍetvā       pāvisimhā mahāvananti .

Soṇanandapaṇḍitacariyaṃ pañcamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 585 -->
<a id="page-585"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 585]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ mūgaphakkhacariyaṃ    
[26] punāparaṃ yadā homi           kāsirājassa atrajo    
mūgaphakkhoti [^585-1] nāmena        temiyoti vadanti maṃ .

Soḷasitthīsahassānaṃ            na vijjati pumo tadā    
ahorattānamaccayena          nibbatto ahamekako .

Kicchā laddhaṃ piyaṃ puttaṃ         abhijātaṃ jutindharaṃ    
setacchattaṃ dhārayitvāna        sayane posesi maṃ pitā .

Niddāyamāno sayanavare        pabujjhitvānahaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ paṇḍaraṃ chattaṃ           yenāhaṃ nirayaṃ gato .

Saha diṭṭhissa [^585-2] me chattaṃ      tāso uppajji bheravo    
vinicchayaṃ samāpanno           kadāhaṃ imaṃ muñcissaṃ .

Pubbasālohitā mayhaṃ          devatā atthakāminī    
sā maṃ disvāna dukkhitaṃ         tiṃsu ṭhānesu yojayi .

Mā paṇḍiccayaṃ vibhāvaya         bālamato bhava sabbapāṇinaṃ    
sabbo tañjano ocināyatu      evaṃ tava attho bhavissati .

Evaṃ vuttāyahaṃ tassā         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
karomi te taṃ vacanaṃ           yaṃ maṃ bhaṇasi devate .

Atthakāmāsi me amma         hitakāmāsi devate    
tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā          sāgareva thalaṃ labhiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 586 -->
<a id="page-586"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 586]*

Haṭṭho saṃviggamānaso          tayo aṅge adhiṭṭhahiṃ    
mūgo ahosiṃ badhiro           phakkho [^586-1] gativivajjito .

Ete aṅge adhiṭṭhāya        vassāni soḷasaṃ vasiṃ    
tato me hatthe pāde ca      jivhaṃ sotañca maddiya    
anūnataṃ me passitvā          kāḷakaṇṇīti nindisuṃ .

Tato jānapadā sabbe         senāpatipurohitā    
sabbe ekamanā hutvā        chaḍḍanaṃ anumodisuṃ .

Sohaṃ tesaṃ matiṃ sutvā         haṭṭho saṃviggamānaso    
yassatthāya tapo ciṇṇo        so me attho samijjhatha .

Nhāpetvā anulimpetvā      veṭhetvā rājaveṭhanaṃ    
abhisiñcitvā chattena          kāresuṃ puraṃ padakkhiṇaṃ .

Sattāhaṃ dhārayitvāna          uggate ravimaṇḍale    
rathena maṃ nīharitvā           sārathi vanamupāgami .

Ekokāse rathaṃ katvā        sajjassaṃ hatthamuñcito [^586-2]    
sārathi khaṇatī kāsuṃ            nikkhātuṃ paṭhaviyā mamaṃ .

Adhiṭṭhitamadhiṭṭhānaṃ             tajjento vividhakāraṇā    
na bhindiṃva tamadhiṭṭhānaṃ          bodhiyāyeva kāraṇā .

Mātā pitā na me dessā     attā me na ca dessiyo    
sabbaññutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ           tasmā va tamadhiṭṭhahiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 587 -->
<a id="page-587"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 587]*

Ete aṅge adhiṭṭhāya        vassāni soḷasaṃ vasiṃ    
adhiṭṭhānena [1] samo natthi   esā me adhiṭṭhānapāramīti    
mūgaphakkhacariyaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

Sattamaṃ kapilarājacariyaṃ    
[27] yadā ahaṃ kapi āsiṃ           nadīkūle darīsaye    
pīḷito sasasumārena [^587-2]         gamanaṃ na labhāmahaṃ .

Yamhokāse ahaṃ ṭhatvā        orā paraṃ patāmahaṃ    
tatthacchi sattuvadhako           kumbhilo luddhadassano .

So maṃ asaṃsi ehīti           ahampemīti taṃ vadiṃ [^587-3]    
tassa matthakamakkamma           parakūle patiṭṭhahiṃ .

Na tassālikaṃ bhaṇitaṃ            yathā vācaṃ akāsahaṃ    
saccena me samo natthi        esā me saccapāramīti .

Kapilarājacariyaṃ sattamaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ saccasavhayapaṇḍitacariyaṃ    
[28] punāparaṃ yadā homi           tāpaso saccasavhayo    
saccena lokaṃ pālesiṃ         samaggaṃ janamakāsahanti .

Saccasavhayapaṇḍitacariyaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 588 -->
<a id="page-588"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 588]*

Navamaṃ vaṭṭakapotakacariyaṃ    
[29] punāparaṃ yadā homi           magadhe vaṭṭapotako    
ajātapakkho taruṇo           maṃsapesi kulāvake .

Mukhatuṇḍakena āharitvā        mātā posayatī mamaṃ    
tassā phassena jīvāmi         natthi me kāyikaṃ balaṃ .

Saṃvacchare gimhasamaye          davadāho padippati    
upagacchati amhākaṃ            pāvako kaṇhavattani .

Dhamadhamaṃ [^588-1] iti evaṃ          saddāyanto mahā sikhī    
anupubbena jhāpento         aggi mamamupāgami .

Aggivegabhayā bhītā           tasitā mātā pitā mama    
kulāvake maṃ chaḍḍetvā        attānaṃ parimocayuṃ .

Pāde pakkhe pajahāmi         natthi me kāyikaṃ balaṃ    
sohaṃ agatiko tattha           evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Yesāhaṃ upadhāveyyaṃ          bhīto tasitavedhito    
te maṃ ohāya pakkantā       kathaṃ me ajja kātave .

Atthi loke sīlaguṇo          saccaṃ soceyyanuddayā    
tena saccena kāhāmi         saccakiriyamuttamaṃ [^588-2] .

Āvajjitvā dhammabalaṃ          saritvā pubbake jine    
saccabalamavassāya             saccakiriyamakāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 589 -->
<a id="page-589"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 589]*

Santi pakkhā apatanā          santi pādā avañcanā    
mātā pitā ca nikkhantā       jātaveda paṭikkama .

Saha sacce kate mayhaṃ         mahāpajjalito sikhī    
vajjesi soḷasa karīsāni        udakaṃ patvā yathā sikhī    
saccena me samo natthi        esā me saccapāramīti .

Vaṭṭakapotakacariyaṃ navamaṃ .

Dasamaṃ maccharājacariyaṃ    
[30] punāparaṃ yadā homi           maccharājā mahāsare    
uṇhe suriyasantāpe          sare udakaṃ khīyatha .

Tato kākā ca gijjhā ca       kaṅkā [^589-1] kulalasenakā    
bhakkhayanti divā rattiṃ          macche upanisīdiya .

Evaṃ cintesahaṃ tattha          saha ñātīhi pīḷito    
kena nu kho upāyena         ñātī dukkhā pamocaye .

Cintayitvāna dhammatthaṃ          saccaṃ addasaṃ passayaṃ    
sacce ṭhatvā pamocesiṃ        ñātīnaṃ taṃ atikkhayaṃ .

Anussaritvā sataṃ dhammaṃ         paramatthaṃ vicintayaṃ    
akāsiṃ saccakiriyaṃ             yaṃ loke dhuvasassataṃ .

Yato sarāmi attānaṃ          yato pattosmi viññutaṃ    
nābhijānāmi sañcicca          ekapāṇaṃpi hiṃsitaṃ [^589-2] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 590 -->
<a id="page-590"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 590]*

Etena saccavajjena          pajjunno abhivassatu    
abhitthanaya pajjunna            nidhiṃ kākassa nāsaya .

Kākaṃ sokāya randhehi         macche sokā pamocaya    
saha kate saccavare           pajjunno abhigajjiya .

Thalaṃ ninnañca pūrento         khaṇena abhivassatha    
evarūpaṃ saccavaraṃ             katvā viriyamuttamaṃ .

Vassāpesiṃ mahāmeghaṃ           saccatejabalassito    
saccena me samo natthi        esā me saccapāramīti .

Maccharājacariyaṃ dasamaṃ .

Ekādasamaṃ kaṇhadīpāyanacariyaṃ    
[31] punāparaṃ yadā homi           kaṇhadīpāyano isi    
paropaññāsavassāni           anabhirato cariṃ ahaṃ .

Na koci etaṃ jānāti         anabhiratimanaṃ mama    
ahaṃpi kassaci nācikkhiṃ          arati [^590-1] me ca ratimānase .

Sabrahmacārī maṇḍabyo         sahāyo me mahāisi    
pubbakammasamāyutto           sūlamāropanaṃ labhi .

Tamahaṃ upaṭṭhahitvāna           ārogyamanupāpayiṃ    
āpucchitvāna āgañchiṃ         yaṃ mayhaṃ sakamassamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 591 -->
<a id="page-591"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 591]*

Sahāyo brāhmaṇo mayhaṃ       bhariyaṃ ādāya puttakaṃ    
tayo janā samāgantvā        āgacchacha pāhunāgataṃ .

Sammodamāno tehi saha        nisinno sakaassame    
dārako vaṭṭamanukkhipaṃ          āsīvisamakopayi .

Tato so vaṭṭagataṃ maggaṃ        anvesanto kumārako    
āsīvisassa hatthena           uttamaṅgaṃ parāmasi .

Tassa āmasane kuddho         sappo visabalassito    
kupito paramakopena           aḍaṃsi dārakaṃ khaṇe .

Saha daṭṭho ativisena          dārako papati bhūmiyaṃ    
tenāhaṃ dukkhito āsiṃ         mama vā hasitaṃ dukkhaṃ .

Tyāhaṃ assāsayitvāna         dukkhite sokasalline [^591-1]    
paṭhamaṃ akāsiṃ kiriyaṃ            aggaṃ saccavaruttamaṃ .

Sattāhamevāhaṃ pasannacitto    
puññatthiko acariṃ brahmacariyaṃ    
athāparaṃ yaṃ caritaṃ mamaṃ idaṃ    
vassāni paññāsasamādhikāni .

Ākāmakovāhi ahañcarāmi    
etena saccena suvatthi hotu    
hataṃ visaṃ jīvatu yaññadatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 592 -->
<a id="page-592"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 592]*

Saha sacce kate mayhaṃ         visavegena vedhito    
abujjhitvāna vuṭṭhāsi          arogo vāsi [^592-1] māṇavo    
saccena me samo natthi        esā me saccapāramīti .

Kaṇhadīpāyanacariyaṃ ekādasamaṃ .

Dvādasamaṃ sutasomacariyaṃ    
[32] punāparaṃ yadā homi           sutasomo mahīpati๋    
gahito porisādena           brāhmaṇe saṅgaraṃ sariṃ .

Khattiyānaṃ ekasataṃ            āvuṇitvā karatale    
ete sammamilāpetvā        yaññatthe upanayī mamaṃ .

Āpucchi maṃ porisādo         kiṃ tvaṃ icchasi nissajaṃ    
yathāmatiṃ te kāhāmi          yadi me tvaṃ punehisi .

Tassa paṭisuṇitvāna            pañhe āgamanaṃ mama    
upagantvā puraṃ rammaṃ          rajjaṃ niyyādayiṃ tadā .

Anussaritvā sataṃ dhammaṃ         pubbakaṃ jinasevitaṃ    
brāhmaṇassa dhanaṃ datvā        porisādaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Natthi me saṃsayo tattha         ghāṭayissati vā na vā    
saccavācānurakkhanto          jīvitaṃ cajitumupāgamiṃ    
saccena me samo natthi        esā me saccapāramīti .

Sutasomacariyaṃ dvādasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 593 -->
<a id="page-593"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 593]*

terasamaṃ suvaṇṇasāmacariyaṃ    
[33] sāmo yadā vane āsiṃ        sakkena abhinimmito    
pavane sīhabyagghehi           mettāyamupanāmayiṃ .

Sīhabyagghehi dīpīhi            acchehi mahisehi ca    
pasadamigavarāhehi             parivāretvā vane vasiṃ .

Na maṃ koci uttasati           napihaṃ bhāyāmi kassaci    
mettā balenupatthaddho        ramāmi pavane tadāti .

Suvaṇṇasāmacariyaṃ terasamaṃ .

cuddasamaṃ ekarājacariyaṃ    
[34] punāparaṃ yadā homi           ekarājāti vissuto    
paramaṃ sīlaṃ adhiṭṭhāya           pasāsāmi mahāmahiṃ .

Dasa kusalakammapathe            vattāmi anavasesato    
catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi            saṅgaṇhāmi mahājanaṃ .

Evaṃ me appamattassa         idha loke parattha ca    
dabbaseno upāgantvā        acchindanto puraṃ mama .

Rājūpajīve nigame            sabalaṭṭhe saraṭṭhake    
sabbaṃ hatthagataṃ katvā          kāsuyā nikkhaṇī mamaṃ .

Amaccamaṇḍalaṃ rajjaṃ            phītaṃ antepuraṃ mama    
acchinditvāna gahitaṃ           piyaputtaṃva passahaṃ    
mettāya me samo natthi       esā me mettāpāramīti .

Ekarājacariyaṃ cuddasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 594 -->
<a id="page-594"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 594]*

Paṇṇarasamaṃ mahālomahaṃsacariyaṃ    
[35] susāne seyyaṃ kappemi        chavaṭṭhikaṃ upanidhāya [^594-1]    
gāmamaṇḍalā upāgantvā       rūpaṃ dassenti nappakaṃ .

Apare gandhañca mālañca        bhojanaṃ vividhaṃ bahuṃ    
upāyanāni upanenti          haṭṭhā saṃviggamānasā .

Ye me dukkhaṃ upaharanti        ye ca denti sukhaṃ mama    
sabbesaṃ samako homi          dayā kopo na vijjati .

Sukhadukkhe tulābhūto           yasesu ayasesu ca    
sabbattha samako homi          esā me upekkhāpāramīti .

Mahālomahaṃsacariyaṃ paṇṇarasamaṃ .

Tassuddānaṃ    
yudhañjayo somanasso          ayogharabhisena ca    
soṇanando mūgaphakkho          kapirājā saccasavhayo .

Vaṭṭako maccharājā ca         kaṇhadīpāyano isi    
sutasomo pure [^594-2] āsi       sāmo ca ekarājā [^594-3] ca    
upekkhāpāramī āsi          iti vuttaṃ mahesinā .

Evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ            sampattiñca bahūvidhaṃ    
bhavābhave anubhavitvā          patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Datvā dātabbakaṃ dānaṃ         sīlaṃ pūretvā asesato    
nikkhammapāramiṃ gantvā         patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 595 -->
<a id="page-595"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 595]*

Paṇḍite paripucchitvā          viriyaṃ katvāna uttamaṃ    
khantiyā pāramiṃ gantvā        patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Katvā daḷhamadhiṭṭhānaṃ          saccavācānurakkhiya    
mettāya pāramiṃ gantvā       patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Lābhālābhe yasāyase         sammānanāvamānane    
sabbattha samako hutvā         patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Kosajjaṃ bhayato disvā         viriyārambhañca khemato    
āraddhaviriyā hotha           esā buddhānusāsanī  .

Vivādaṃ bhayato disvā          avivādañca khemato    
samaggā sakhilā hotha          esā buddhānusāsanī .

Pamādaṃ bhayato disvā          appamādañca khemato    
bhāvethaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          esā buddhānusāsanī .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ bhagavā attano pubbacariyaṃ sambhāvayamāno    
buddhāpadāniyaṃ nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsitthāti .

Cariyāpiṭakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

[1]    
#[^595-1] yu.   Yasmā pana ayaṃ pāli          paramparāya likkhitā    
#       vipallatthakkharā tasmā         sāyaṃ suṭṭhu visodhitā    
#       paññāsī hoti nāmena         anutherena dhīmatā    
#       sāsanṭaṭhitikāmena            bahugandhesu āgataṃ    
#       atthaṃ saṃsanditvā             mayā tassa tejena pāṇino    
#       samiddhasaṅkappā hontu         devā rakkhantu sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 596 -->
<a id="page-596"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 596]*

Samodhānakathā [^596-1]    
[36] dasete pāramī honti         dasete upapāramī    
paramatthā dasa honti          bodhiyā paripācanā .

Sivirājassa seṭṭhassa          dānapāramitā bhave    
vessantaro ca velāmo       bhavanti upapāramī .

Akittivisayhadānena           honti tā upapāramī    
kukkuṭasīlasaso bhūto           paramatthā dānapāramī .

Mahākapi ca chaddanto          nāgo ca mātuposako    
sīlapāramitā yanti            iti vuttaṃ mahesinā .

Campeyyako bhūridatto         sīlena upapāramī    
saṅkhapālo ca yo dhīro        paramatthā sīlapāramī .

Yudhañjayo ca govindo         hatthipālo ca yo gharo    
bhallāti yo susāmo ca        ..................

Maghadevo nimi ceva           hontetā upapāramī .

Mahosatho dhanaraṭṭho           kuṇḍalo cāpi taṇḍilo    
tittiro ca sakuṇo ca          hontetā upapāramī .

Vidhuraṃ sambhavaṃ paññāya          ubhotā honti pāramī    
yo sisso ācariyaṃ porā      suriyabrāhmaṇo mātaṅgo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 597 -->
<a id="page-597"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 597]*

sattubhastañca paramatthā         etā ve paññāpāramī    
sīlavā viriyo rājā          jātakaṃ jānakā vibhū .

Daḷhaparakkamo āsi           paramatthā viriyapāramī    
mahākapi pañca garukā          viriyā honti pāramī .

Dhammapālo kumāro ca         khantiyā honti pāramī    
upapāramīti vuccanti           dhammādhammikadaddavā .

Khantivādena paramatthā         khantipāramipūriyā    
buddhabhūmiṃ gavesanto           akāsi dukkaraṃ bahuṃ .

Tathūparisaccaguṇaṃ               sasavaṭṭakajātakaṃ    
saccenagginibbāpito          esā pāramitā bhave .

Udake maccho bhavitvāna        akāsi saccamuttamaṃ    
mahāmeghaṃ pavassesi           esā me saccapāramī .

Saccena nāvaṃ tāresi         dhīro supārapaṇḍito    
visaṃ saccena ghāṭesi          kaṇhadīpāyano isi .

Saccena vānaro hutvā        gaṅgā sotaṃ tarī tadā    
esā pāramitā satthu         honti tā upapāramī .

Sutasomo nāma rājā         rakkhanto saccamuttamaṃ    
moceti ekasataṃ khatye        paramatthā saccapāramī .

Adhiṭṭhānena kiṃ chando         pāramī bhavatī tadā    
duko mātaṅgayo ceva         adhiṭṭhānaüpapāramī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 598 -->
<a id="page-598"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 598]*

mugaphakkhena paramatthā          adhiṭṭhānaṃ āsi pāramī    
mahākaṇho ca mettāya        tattha ca rājasodhanā .

Esā duvidhā akkhātā        mettāpāramitā bhave    
sīlena brahmadatto ca         yo vāyaṃ gaṇḍitiṇḍako .

Akāsi nando piyassa          honti tā upapāramī    
ekarājena paramatthā         natthi mettāya īdiso .

Dve suvā upapāramī          paramatthā lomahaṃsanaṃ    
etā dasa pāramī mayhaṃ        paṭiveso aggabodhiyā .

Dasato paramā natthi           dasato natthi ūnakā    
anūnānadhikā sabbā           pūritā dasapāramīti .

Samodhānakathā niṭṭhitā    
cariyāpiṭakaṃ samattaṃ .
